śarīra' gatāḥ " bālatva yuvatva - sthaviratvādayaḥ, na ātmani sambadhyante | ātmagatāśca jñāna sukhādayo na śarīre | 'atha ca ' - 'devo jātaḥ' 'manuṣyo jātaḥ' tathā 'sa eva bālo yuvā sthaviraśva' iti vyapadeśaśca mukhyaḥ | bhūtasūkṣmaśarīrasyaiva kṣetrajñasya, deva- manuṣyādibhāvaḥ - iti,

' tadantara pratipattau
iti vakṣyate ' - iti ||

tat, anākalitasamyanyāyānugṛhītavedāntavākyagaṇasya, svamatiparikalpita- kutarkavijṛmbhitam | sarva eva hi ! vedāntāḥ, 'sthūlasya' sūkṣmasya cetanasyā'cetanasya ca 'samastasya, paramātmānaṃ prati śarīratvaṃ śrāvayanti |

subālopaniṣadi ' ca-

'yaḥ' pṛthivīmantare sañcaran yasya pṛthivī śarīram' ityārabhya tadvadeva cidacitoḥ sarvāvasthayoḥ, paramātmaśarīratvamabhidhāya,
'eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmā'pahatapāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇaḥ' iti, tasya sarvabhūtāni prati, ātmatvamabhidhīyate || smaranti ca -

'jagat sarva śarīraṃ te' 'yadambu vaiṣṇavaḥ kāyaḥ' 'tatsarva vai harestanuḥ' 'tāni sarvāṇi tadvapuḥ' 'so'mi 'dhyāya' śarīrāt svāt ' ityādi | bhūtadhūkṣmāt svāt śarīrādityarthaḥ ||
loke ca 'śarīra' śabdaḥ ghaṭādiśabdavat, ekākāradravyaniyatavṛttimanāsā' ditaḥ, krimi' - kīṭa- pataṅga - sarpa-nara- paśuprabhṛtiṣu atyanta 'vilakṣaṇākāreṣu' dravyeṣu, agauṇaḥ prayujyamāno dṛśyate | tena tasya pravṛttinimittavyavasthāpanaṃ, sarvaprayogānuguṇyainaiva
‘so'midhyāye’tyatra, cidacitoḥ śarīratvamaspaṣṭamiti, 'bhūtasūkṣmāt' iti tadvyācaṣṭe | 'āsīdidaṃtamo bhūtam' iti prakṛtameva, śarīratvenocyate ityarthaḥ |

ghaṭādīti | yathā ghaṭādiśabdāḥ ekākāravastuṣu mukhyāḥ prayujyante ; na tathā 'śarīra' śabdaḥ; api tu anekākāravastuṣu mukhyaḥ prayuktaḥ | ataḥ anugatatvāya hi krimikīṭādi- vyāpakaṃ lakṣaṇamuktam | tasyāpyavyāptatvāt sārvatrikaṃ lakṣaṇāntaraṃ svīkāryamiti bhāvaḥ | agauṇa iti | dvipātvaṃ manuṣyatvaṃ vā, śarīralakṣaṇatvena kimiti nocyate ; anyatrāpi mukhya- prayogadarśanāt | loka- vedayoḥ yatra yatra prayogo dṛśyate, tadanuyāyi lakṣaṇaṃ vācyam | na tu ananuyāyi lakṣaṇamuktvā anyatra gauṇatvaṃ kalpanīyam ityabhiprāyaḥ | pravṛttinimittalakṣaṇam – vācyabhūtaṃ samudāyaśaktinimittam | tacca tat lakṣaṇaṃ ca | tena

kāryam | 'tvaduktaṃ ' ca 'karma' phala' bhogahetuḥ' ityādikaṃ pravṛttinimittalakṣaṇa, na sarvaprayogānuguṇam ; yathokteṣu īśvaraśarīratayā'bhihiteṣu pṛthivyādiṣu avyāpteḥ || kiṃ ca - īśvarasya icchā vigraheṣu, muktānāṃ ca,

'sa ekadhā bhavati'

ityādi vākyāvagateṣu vigraheṣu, tat lakṣaṇam avyāptam ; karmaphalabhoganimittatvābhāvāt teṣām | paramapuruṣecchāvigrahāśca na pṛthivyādibhūtasaṅghātaviśeṣāḥ ;
'na bhūta' saṅghasaṃsthāno' deho'sya paramātmanaḥ' iti smṛteḥ | ataḥ, bhūtasaṃghātarūpatvaṃ ca śarīrasya, avyāptam |
pañcavṛttiprāṇādhīnadhāraṇatvaṃ ca sthāvaraśarīreṣu avyāptam | sthāvareṣu hi ! prāṇasadbhāve'pi tasya pañcadhā avasthāya, śarīrasya dhārakatvenā' svasthānaṃ nāsti | ahalyādīnāṃ karmanimitta śilā kāṣṭhādiśarīreṣu, indriyāśraya' tvaṃ, ' sukhaduḥkhahetutvaṃ cā'vyāptam ||

ataḥ, 'yasya cetanasya, yadravyaṃ sarvātmanā svārthe niyantuṃ dhārayituṃ ca śakyaṃ, vyutpattinimittasya upalakṣaṇasya ca vyāvṛttiḥ | gośabdasya gamidhātvarthaḥ, pṛthivī- śabdavācyasya pṛthivītvasyopalakṣaṇabhūtaṃ gandhavatvam | tadubhayamapi na pravṛttinimittam | vyutpatti- nimittam ||

tat lakṣaṇam – karmaphalopabhogahetutvam | paścavṛttīti | prāṇasadbhāvamātra vivakṣāyāṃ, cetaneṣu avyaktādiṣu ca īśvaraśarīrabhūteṣu avyāptirabhipretā | indriyāśrayatvam – jñānapravṛtti - jananapaṭuna rendriyāśrayatvam | indriya sadbhāvamātra vivakṣāyāṃ pūrvavadavyāptirabhipretā ||
ataḥ - anuyāyino lakṣaṇatvāt tvaduktasyānanuyāyitvāt | 'yasye 'tipadena, śarīrasya pitṛ-putrādivat sapratiyogikapadārthatvam ; na ghaṭādivat apratiyogika padārthatvamiti darśitam | 'sarvātmanā svārtheniyantuṃ śakyam' 'sarvātmanā svārthe dhārayituṃ śakyam' 'śeṣataika- svarūpam tattasya śarīram' lakṣaṇatrayam | 'eka' śabda- prayogaḥ 'sarvātmanā ' iti padasya sthāne | yāvaddravyabhāvitvaṃ vivakṣitam | 'dravya' miti, kriyā - guṇa | divyāvṛttyartham | svārthe svaviṣaye | 'sva' śabda: śarīraparaḥ | tattaccharīra-

taccheṣataikasvarūpaṃ ca ; tat tasya śarīram' iti, śarīralakṣaṇamāstheyam || rugṇaśarīrādiṣu niyamanādyadarśanaṃ, vidyamānāyā eva niyamanaśakteḥ pratibandhakṛtam agnyādeḥ śakti- pratibandhāt auṣṇyādyadarśanavat | mṛta śarīraṃ ca ' cetanaviyogasamaye eva viśaritumāra- bdham ; kṣaṇāntare ca viśīryate | 'pūrva' śarīratayā ' pariklapsa saṅghātaikadeśatvena ca, tatra- śarīratvavyavahāraḥ ||

152. bhoktrāpatterarāvaibhāgacet; syāllokavat || 2-1-14 ||

punarapi sāṅkhyaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate | yaduktam — sthūla sūkṣma cidacidvastu 'śarīrasya' parasya brahmaṇaḥ kārya-kāraṇarūpatvāt, jīva-brahmaṇoḥ svabhāvavibhāga upapadyate - iti | sa tu vibhāgo na sambhavati ; brahmaṇaḥ saśarīratve, tasya bhoktṛtvāpatteḥ - saśarīra tve jīvasyeva īśvarasyāpi saśarīratvaprayuktasukha-duḥkhayorbhoktṛtvasya avarjanīyatvāt |

nanu ca -

'sambhogaprāptiriti cenna vaiśeṣyāt ' . sū. 1-2-8)
ityatra, īśvarasya bhogaprasaṅgaparihāraḥ-uktaḥ !
punarapīti | 'na tu dṛṣṭāntabhāvāt' ityatra śarīragata bālatva-yuvatvādidharmāṇāṃ, śarīriṇi aprasaṅgaṃ dṛṣṭāntīkṛtya, paramātmani śarīrabhūtacidacidgatadoṣāprasaktiḥ uktā | atra - śarīra- saṃsargaprayuktabhoktṛtvaprasaṅgaḥ parihviyate ||

pūrvavadeva prathamo vicāraḥ | tadartha, kiṃ bhoktṛjīvaśarīrakasya brahmaṇaḥ, svaśarīrabhūtājjīvāt niratiśayānandasvabhāvatayā vilakṣaṇatvamasti ? na ? iti | tadartha, ki brahmaṇaḥ saśarīratve, jīvavat sukha-duḥkhabhoktṛtvaṃ prasajyate ! uta na ? - iti | duḥkhādibhoktṛtvaṃ kiṃ śarīraprayuktam ? uta anyaprayuktam ?–iti | 'na ha vai saśarīrasya' iti śrutau 'śarīra' śabdaḥ, kiṃ śarīra-
'brahmaṇo'khiladehasve bhoktṛtvaṃ jīvavadbhavet |
nāSto vibhāga iti cet dedditve'pi na bhoktṛtā ||

naivam ; tatra hi ! – upāsyatayā hṛdayāyatane saṃnihitasya, śarīrāntarvartitvamātreṇa bhogaprasaṅgo na vidyate — ityuktam | iha tu jīvavat brahmaṇo'pi 'saśarīratve', tadvadeva sukha-duḥkhayorbhoktṛtvaprasaṅgo durvāraḥ - ityucyate || dṛśyate hi ! saśarīrāṇāṃ jīvānāṃ, śarīragata bālatva' sthaviratvādi vikārāsambhave'pi śarīradhātusāmya-vaiṣamya- nimittasukha-duḥkhayogaḥ || śrutiśca -

'na ha vai saśarīrasya sataḥ priyāpriyayorapahatirasti; aśarīraṃ vāvasantaṃ
na priyāpriye spṛśataḥ'

iti ||

ataḥ — saśarīrabrahmakāraṇavāde jīveśvarasvabhāvavibhāgāsambhavāt", kevalabrahmakāraṇa- 'vāde' mṛt- suvarṇādivat jagatāpuruṣārthādisarva viśeṣāśrayatvaprasaṅgācca, pradhānakāraṇavāda eva jyāyān — iti cet !
mātraparaḥ ? uta puṇyāpuṇyanimittaśarīra para : : iti | atra caramo vicāraḥ prastutopayogena buddhisthatayā kṛtaḥ ; na tu asādhāraṇatayā kartavyatvāt ||

yadā śarīramātraparaḥ tadā duḥkhādibhogasya śarīritvaprayuktatvāt brahmago'pi tadbhoga prasaṅgena śarīrabhūtājjīvāt niratiśayānandatayā vilakṣaṇatvaṃ nāstīti syāt || yadā tasmin vākye 'śarīra' śabda: karmakṛtaśarīraparaḥ; tadā duḥkhādibhogasya śarīritvaprayuktatvābhāvāt, brahmaṇaḥ tadbhogāprasaṅgena śarīrabhūtājjīvāt... vailakṣaṇyamastīti sidhyati ||
'śarīrāntarvartitvamātreṇeti 'mātra' śabdena, śarīraṃ prati svāmyanivṛttiḥ | jīvavat .... saśarīratve ityanena śarīrasvāmyamabhipretam | yathā kāryavaśāt paragṛhaṃ praviṣṭasya gṛhasaṃnidhi - mātreṇa gṛhapatitvaprayuktānarthābhyudayabhāktvaṃ na bhavati tathā upāsyatvāya hṛdayasthasya śarīra- saṃnidhimātreṇa śarīritvaprayukta moktatvāpattirnāstīti 'sambhogaprāptiriti cet' ityatroktam | atra tu śarīrasvāmitvaprayukta bhoktṛtvamāśaṅkayamiti śaṅkāyā mukhabhedaḥ ityarthaḥ | sūtrabhedastu bhoktṛtvā- śaṅkādvārabhedanibandhanaḥ || 'asambhave'pī' tyanena, 'na tu dṛṣṭāntabhāvāt' ityatra parihṛtāṃśa uktaḥ | ata iti, aparihṛtāṃśamāha |

atrottaram - syāllokavat' – iti | syādeva vibhāgoṃ jīvaśvarasvabhāvayoḥ | na hi ! jīvasya śarīradhātusāmya-vaiṣamyanimittaṃ sukha - duḥkhayorbhoktṛtvaṃ saśarīratvakṛtam ; api tu puṇya pāparūpakarmakṛtam |

'na ha vai saśarīrasya'
ityapi, karmārabdhadehaviṣayam |
'sa ekadhā bhavati tridhā bhavati'
' sa yadi pitṛlokakāmo bhavati'
' sa tatra paryeti jakṣat krīḍan ramamāṇaḥ'
iti 'karmabandha'vinirmuktasya āvirbhūtasvarūpasya saśarīrasyaiva apuruṣārthagandhābhāvāt || apahatapāpmanastu paramātmanaḥ, sthūla sūkṣmarūpakṛtsna' jagaccharīratve'pi karmasambandha- gandho nāstīti, 'natarām apuruṣārthagandhaprasaṅgaḥ ||

lokavat - yathā loke rājaśāsanānuvartināṃ tadativartināṃ ca rājānugraha- nigrahakṛtasukhaduḥkhayoge'pi na śarīritva' mātreṇa śāsake rājyapi, śāsanānuvṛntyati- vṛttinimittasukhaduḥkhayorbhoktṛtvaprasaṅgaḥ | yathā''ha ' dramiḍa' bhāṣyakāra:

vyajanādīti, 'ādi' śabdena tattadaniṣṭanivāraṇasādhanāni vivakṣitāni |

gandhādīn aviśvajanopabhogyān 'dhārayati'; tathā'sau lokeśvaraḥ bhramatsvasāmarthya- cāmaraḥ doṣairnaspṛśyate, rakṣati ca lokān 'brahmalokādīna', bhogāṃva aviśva- janopabhogyān dhārayati — iti' ||
mṛt-suvarṇādivat brahmasvarūpapariṇāmastu naivā'bhyupagamyate ; avikāratva-nirdoṣa- tvādiśruteḥ |

yattu parai:- brahmakāraṇavāde bhoktṛ-bhogya vibhāgābhāvamāśaṅkaya, samudra- phena'. taraṅga-dṛṣṭāntena vibhāgapratipādanaparaṃ sūtraṃ vyākhyātam ;

antarbhāvitaśaktya vidyopādhikāt brahmaṇaḥ sṛṣṭimabhyupagacchatām,

tadayuktam ;

evamākṣepa- parihārayoḥ asaṅgatatvāt kāraṇāntargataśaktyavidyopādhyupahitasya bhoktṛtvāt, upādheśca bhogyatvāt ; vilakṣaṇayostayoḥ parasparabhāvāpattirhi ! na sambhavati ||

svarūpapariṇāmastu, tairapi nā'bhyupeyate ;
'na karmā'vibhāgāditi cennā'nāditvāt ' iti, kṣetrajñānāṃ tadgatakarmaṇāṃ cānāditvapratipādanāt |
atra cāmaragrahaṇam, aniṣṭanivāraṇānurūpa vastūnāmupalakṣaṇam |
saśarīrabrahmakāraṇatve, jīveśvara vibhāgābhāvaḥ parihṛtaḥ | kevalabrahmakāraṇavāde, pūrvokta- jagadgata! puruṣārthādiprasaṅgamanabhyupagamena, 'mṛt- suvarṇādivat' pariharati |
yatviti | 'bhoktṛ' śabdo bhoktṛtvaparaḥ iti tairapyuktam | paraiḥ bhogyasya bhoktṛtvāpatti, bhoktuḥ bhogyatvāpattiṃ cāśaṅkaya parihṛtam | tat, tadayuktam iti dūṣayati | kāraṇāntargateti, ākṣepaparihārayoḥ asaṅgatatvamupapādayati | ' upādheśce' tyatra upādhiśabdaḥ, śaktaya vidyopādhīnāṃ trayāṇāmapi vācakaḥ | yathā 'vāda' śabdaḥ, kathā sāmānyavācī kathā viśeṣa- vācī ca tadvat | yadvā 'upādhi' śabdena acidaśo vivakṣitaḥ | vilakṣaṇayoriti | upahitabrahmaṇo bhoktṛtvam upādherbhogyatvaṃ cā'stu ; ; tathāpi tayoḥ upādhi - brahmaṇoḥ avibhāgaḥ āśaṅkaya parihiyate
na karmeti | kṣetrajñānāditvābhyupagame, upahitasya brahmaṇaḥ kṣetrajñatvena, upādheśca bhogyatva- rūpeṇa nityatvābhyupagamāt, nityatvenā'bhyupagatayostayoḥ, avibhāgābhāvaḥ siddha evetyarthaḥ |

ārambhaṇādhikaraṇam ||2-1-6 ||
153. tadananyatvamārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ || 2-1-15||

'asaditi cenna pratiṣedhamātratvāt '

ityādiṣu, kāraṇa 'bhūtādbrahmaṇaḥ kāryabhūtasya jagataḥ ananyatvamabhyupagamya brahmaṇo jagatkāraṇatvam – upapāditam | idānīṃ - tadevā'nanyatvam, ākṣipya samādhīyate ||

INF

tatra kāṇādāḥ prāhuḥ na kāraṇāt kāryasya ananyatvaṃ sambhavati vilakṣaṇabuddhibodhyatvāt – na khalu ! tantupaṭa - mṛtpiṇḍaghaṭādiṣu kārya-kāraṇa- viṣayā buddhi:, ekarūpā |

ityādiṣu — sūtreṣvityarthaḥ | brahmaṇa: jagatkāraṇatvaṃ saṃbhavati ? na ? iti | kiṃ brahma- kāryatve jagataḥ śrutisiddhaṃ brahmaṇo'nanyatvaṃ sambhavati na ? iti | kiṃ'vācārambhaṇa' mityādi- vākyāni, kāraṇasyaiva avasthāntarāpattyā kāryatvaṃ bodhayituṃ prabhavanti na ? iti | kiṃ buddhi - śabdāntarādayaḥ, avasthābheda nibandhanāḥ | uta dravyabhedanibandhanāḥ ? iti |
yadā buddhibhedādayaḥ svarūpānyatvanibandhanāḥ tadā, kāryasya kāraṇādanyatayā jagato brahmānyatvāpatteḥ tadananyatayā vyapadiśyamānaṃ brahma, na kāraṇaṃ bhavatīti syāt || yadā buddhi- bhedādīnāmavasthāntara nibandhanatvaṃ tadā, kāryasya kāraṇādananyatvena jagato brahmānanyatvasiddheḥ, tadananyatayā vyapadiśyamānasya brahmaṇaḥ, jagatkāraṇatvaṃ sambhavatīti sidhyati || ||

?

'anamyaśvaṃ tu jagato brahmaṇaḥ kāraṇādiha |
vācārambhaṇamityādermṛtpiṇḍāddhavadbhavet ||'

śabda bhedāca ' nahi ! tantavaḥ paṭa ityucyante ; paṭo vā tantava iti | kāryabhedācca – na hi ! mṛtpiṇḍena udakamāhiyate ; ghaṭena vā kuḍyaṃ nirmīyate |
kālabhedācca - pūrvakālaṃ ca kāraṇam ; aparakālaṃ ca kāryam |
ākārabhedācca - piṇḍākāraṃ kāraṇam kārye ca pṛthubudhnodarākāram |
- tathā, satyā" meva' mṛdi, 'ghaṭo naṣṭaḥ' iti vyavahriyate |
saṅkhyābhedaśca dṛśyate — bahavastantavaḥ ; ekaśca paṭaḥ |

|

kārakavyāpāra vaiyarthya ca— kāraṇameva cet kārya; kiṃ kārakavyāpārasādhyaṃ syāt ?

satyapi kārye kāryopayogitayā kārakavyāpāreṇa bhavitavyaṃ 'cet'; sarvadā kāraka- vyāpāreṇa noparantavyam |
sarvasya sarvadā sacvena, nityānityavibhāgaśca na syāt ||
atha - kārya sadeva pūrvamanabhivyaktaṃ, kārakavyāpāreṇābhivyajyate | ataḥ, kāraka vyāpārārthavattvaṃ, nityānityavibhāgaśca – 'ucyate !'

m kiṃ ca kārakavyāpārasya abhivyañjakatve, ghaṭārthena kārakavyāpāreṇa karakāde- rabhivyaktiḥ 'prasajyate'; saṃpratipannābhivyañjakabhāveṣu dīpādiṣu, abhivyaṅganyaviśeṣa- niyamādarśanāt | na hi ! ghaṭārthamāropitaḥ pradīpaḥ karakādīn na abhivyanakti | ataḥ, asataḥ kāryasya utpattihetutvenaiva kārakavyāpārārthavattvam | ataśca satkārya- vādāsiddhiḥ ||

|

na ca niyatakāraṇopādānaṃ, sata eva kāryatvaṃ sādhayati ; kāraṇaśakti niyamādeva tadupapatteḥ ||

nanu - asa kāryavādino'pi, 'kāraka vyāpāro nopapadyate ; prāgutpatteḥ, kāryasya asabhvāt, kāryādanyatra kārakavyāpāreṇa bhavitavyam | tatra anyatvāviśeṣāt tantu- gatakārakavyāpāreṇa ghaṭotpattirapi prasajyate !
naivam ; yatkāryotpādanaśaktaṃ yat kāraṇam, tadgatakārakavyāpāreṇa tatkāryotpatti siddheḥ |

atrāhuḥ - kāraṇādananyat kāryam | na hi ! paramārthataḥ kāraṇavyatiriktaṃ kārya nāma vastvasti; avidyānibandhanatvāt sakalakārya tadvayavahārayoḥ | ataḥ, yathā kāraṇabhūtāt mṛddravyāt ghaṭādiṣu vikāreṣūpalabhyamānāt vyatiriktaṃ ghaṭa - śarāvādikārya,

na ca niyateti | mṛtpiḍe ghaṭo na vidyate cet !, asattvāviśeṣāt tanturapi ghaṭacikīrṣuṇā upādīyeta | naca tathā kriyate | ataḥ kāraṇaviśeṣopādānaniyamānupapattyā, kārya sadevetyarthaḥ | kāraṇeti | 'ghaṭo mṛtpiṇḍe eva vidyate ; na tantuṣu' iti niyamaḥ katham ? ; tadeva tatsadbhāvayogyam iti cet !, tadutpādanayogyaṃ tadeveti, tasyaivopādānaniyamo yuktaḥ—ityarthaḥ | etram upādānakāraṇaniyamānupapattirūpaṃ codyaṃ parihṛtam ||
naivamiti | ananyatvaśrutīnām amukhyatvamabhimatam ||
ataḥ yatheti | ‘vācārambhaṇa’ mityādivākyasyā'rthamabhipretya | vikāreṣūpalabhyamānāt

vyavahāramātrālambanaṃ mithyā kāraṇabhūtaṃ mṛddravyameva satyam tathā nirviśeṣasanmātrāt kāraṇabhūtāt brahmaṇo'nyaḥ ; ahaṅkārādivyavahārālambanaḥ kṛtsnaḥ prapañco mithyā ; kāraṇa bhūtaṃ sanmatraṃ brahmaiva satyam | tasmāt kāraṇavyatiriktaṃ kāryaṃ nāstīti, kāraṇādananyat kāryam ||

na ca vācyam - śuktikā - rajatādīnāmiva ghaṭādikāryāṇām asatyatvāprasiddheḥ, 'dṛṣṭāntānupapattiḥ iti yataḥ, tatrāpi yuktayā mṛdravyamātrameva satyatayā vyavasthā- pyate ; tadatiriktaṃ tu yuktyā bādhyate ||

kā punaratra yuktiḥ ?

mṛdravyamātrasya anuvartamānatvaṃ tadatiriktasya ca' vyāvartamānatvam | rajju- sarpādiṣu hi ! anuvartamānasya adhiṣṭhānabhūtasya rajjvādeḥ 'satyatā'; vyāvartamānasya ca sarpabhūlanāmbudhārādeḥ asatyatā 'dṛṣṭā' tathā anuvartamānamadhiṣṭhānabhūtaṃ mṛdravyameva satyam ; vyāvartamānāstu ghaṭa- śarāvādayaḥ asatyabhūtāḥ ||

kiṃ ca-sata ātmanaḥ vināśābhāvāt asataśca śaśaviṣāṇādeḥ upalabdhyabhāvāt, 'upalabdhi vināśayogi kārya, 'sadasadbhayām" anirvacanīyam" iti gamyate | anirvacanīyaṃ ca śuktikā - rajatādivat mṛṣaiva | tasya ca anirvacanīyatvaṃ pratīti- bādhābhyāṃ siddham ||

|

ityanena anuvartamānatva heturvivakṣitaḥ | tatheti, 'ekamevādvitīyam' ityasyārthamāha | ahaṅkārā- divyavahārālambanaḥ | ' ahamiti prathamo'dhyāsaḥ' iti hi ! teṣāṃ granthaḥ | 'ādi' śabdena jñeyamucyate | brahmavyatiriktaparamārthabhūtakāryābhāvaḥ 'tadananyatva' śabdārtha iti bhāvaḥ ||
yata iti | yathā 'jvālāvat kṣaṇikam' ityukte, jvālābhedasya apratyakṣatvena, na sādhyavaikalyaṃ tadvadityarthaḥ ||
kiṃ cetyādinā, sadasadvilakṣaṇatvaṃ nāma mithyātve hetvantaramāha | sata ātmanaḥ iti, kāryasya sadasadvilakṣaṇatve vyatirekiṇaṃ hetumāha | anirvacanīyaṃ cetyādinā, mithyātve sadasadvilakṣaṇatvam anvayiheturiti darśayati | tasya ceti, sapakṣasya śuktirajatādeḥ anirvaca- nīyatvaṃ darśayati |

kiṃ ca - kāryamutpādayat mṛdādikāraṇadravyaṃ kim avikṛtameva kāryamutpāda- yati' ? uta kañcana viśeṣa māpannam ?.

na tāvat adhikṛtam, utpādayatiḥ sarvadotpādakatvaprasaṅgāt | nā'pi viśeṣāntara- māpannam ; viśeṣāntara patterapi viśeṣāntarāpatti' pūrvakatvena' bhavitavyam, tasyā api tatheti, anavasthānāt |
adhikṛtameva, deśa-kāla- 'nimitta viśeṣasambaddha', kāryamutpādayati - iti cet, na devādi viśeṣa sambandho'pi hi ! avikṛtasya viśeṣāntaramāpanasya ca, pūrvavat na sambhavati ||

na ca vācyam - mṛt suvarṇa dugdhādibhyaḥ, ghaṭa- rūcaka- dadhyādīnām utpattirdṛśyate | śuktikārajatādivat deśa kālādipratipannopādhau bādhaśca na dṛśyate| ataḥ, pratīti- śaraṇānāṃ kāraṇātkāryotpattiḥ avaśyāśrayaṇīyā - iti ; vikalpāsahatvāt |
kiṃ hemādimātrametra svastikāderārambhakam ? uta rūcakādi 1 atha rūcakādyā- śrayo 'hemādiḥ ' ?

na tāvat, hemādimātramārambhakam ; hemavyatiriktasya kāryasya abhāvāt, svātmānaṃ prati ātmanaḥ ārambhakatvāsambhavācca |
kiṃ cetyādinā, vyāvartamānatvahetoḥ upādhiśaṅkāparihārāya kāryasya durnirūpatām, anumā ikata ke rūpeṇā 'SE | satyaṃ cet ! sunirūpaṃ syāt ityarthaḥ |

śrībhāpyam 2.1.6 ]

hemavyatiriktaṃ svastikaṃ dṛśyate iti cet ! na hemavyatiriktaṃ tat ; hemapratyabhijñānāt, tadatiriktavastvantarānupalabdheśca |
buddhi-śabdāntarādibhiḥ, vastvantaratvaṃ sādhitam - iti cet ! na'; anirūpita- vastvavalambanānāṃ 'buddhi-śabdāntarādīnāṃ śuktikārajata' buddhi śabdādivat bhrānti mūlatvena vastvantara sadbhāvasya asādhakatvāt ||
nā'pi rūcakādi svastikā derārambhakam ; svastike hi ! rūcakaṃ, paṭe iva tantavaḥ, bhavatā'pi nopalabhyate |
nā'pi rūcakāśrayabhūtaṃ hema ; rūcakāśrayākāreṇa hemnaḥ svastike anupalabdheḥ |
ataḥ mṛdādikāraṇātiriktasya kāryasya asatyatvadarśanāt, brahmavyatiriktaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat, "kāryatvena 'mithyābhūtam | tadidaṃ brahmavyatiriktamithyātva sukhapratipattaye, kālpanika mṛdādi satyatvamāśritya kāryasyā'satyatvaṃ pratipāditam | paramārthatastu, mṛtsuvarṇādi kāraṇamapi, ghaṭa- rūcakādikāryavat mithyābhūtam ; brahmakāryatvāviśeṣāt ||
'aitadātmyamidaṃ sarvaṃ tatsatyam'
tadatirikteti, hemapratyabhijñānaṃ samavāyikāraṇāṃśaviṣayamityāśaṅkaya | tarhi vyaktidvayamupalabhyeta ; na tathopalabdhirityarthaḥ |
nopalabhyata iti | kārye samavāyikāraṇānuvṛttyā tadupalabdhiḥ pareṇā'bhyupagamyate | paṭe tanta iva, svastike rucakamupalabhyetetyarthaḥ |
anupalabdheriti | rucakākāra viśiṣṭaṃ kāraṇaṃ cet ! svastike rucakākāra viśiṣṭa - hemopalambhaprasaṅga ityarthaḥ | evamatra kevalapramāṇāt tarkānugṛhītaṃ pramāṇaṃ prabalamiti, pratyakṣa- virodhaparihāra:' ityuktaṃ bhavati |

ata iti | kāraṇātiriktavastvanupalabdhyā na pratyakṣabādhaḥ | tata eva na sopādhikatvam ; pakṣepi sādhitatvena upādhitvābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | tadidamiti | na mṛttikāsatyatve tātparyam ; api tu brahmavyatiriktamithyātve evetyarthaḥ |

1 cet - da. sadbhāvāsādha-vā.

śabda-buddhayantarādīnāṃ ta. tha. 5 labhyante - vā. • tatkāryatvena - vā.

'neha nānāssti kiñcana | mṛtyoḥ sa mṛtyumāpnoti ya iha nāneva paśyati'

'yatra hi dvaitamiva bhavati, taditara itaraṃ paśyati' 'yatra tvasya sarvamātmaivābhūt, tat kena kaṃ paśyet' 'indro māyābhiḥ puru' rūpa īyate" ityevamādibhiḥ śrutibhiśva, brahmavyatiriktasya mithyātvamavagamyate ||

na ca – āgamāvagatārthasya pratyakṣavirodhaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ ; yathoktaprakāreṇa kāryasya sarvasya mithyātvāvagamāt pratyakṣasya sanmātraviṣayatvācca | virodhe satyapi, asambhāvita- doṣasya caramabhāvinaḥ svarūpasadbhāvādau pratyakṣādyapekṣatve'pi, pramitau nirākāṅkṣansya niravakāśasya śāstrasya balīyastvāt | ataḥ, kāraṇabhūtādrahmaṇo'nyat sarva mithyā ||

na ca - 'prapañca mithyāvena jīvamithyātvamāśaṅkanīyam; brahmaṇa eva jīvabhāvāt | brahmaiva hi ! sarvaśarīreṣu jīvabhāvamanubhavati ;
'anena jīvenā''tmanānupraviśya' 'eko devaḥ sarvabhūteṣu gūḍhaḥ'
'eko devo bahudhā ' niviṣṭaḥ "
śrutibhiśceti | 'aitadātmyamidaṃ sarvam' iti bādhārthe sāmānādhikaraṇyamiti bhāvaḥ |

'eṣa sarveṣu bhūteṣu gūḍho'tmā na prakāśate ' 'nā'nyo'to'sti draṣṭā'

ityevamādibhyaḥ ||

nanu - ekameva brahma, sarvaśarīreṣu jīvabhāvamanubhavati 'cet' ! " pāde me 'vedanā' śirasi me sukham " itivat sarvaśarīreṣu' sukha-duḥkhapratisandhānaṃ syāt | jīveśvara- baddhamukta śiṣyācārya- jñatvājñatvādivyavasthā ca na syāt ||

atra kecit advitīyatvaṃ 'brahmaṇo'bhyupayanta eva ', ' evaṃ ' samādadhate - ekasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ prativimvabhūtānāṃ jīvānāṃ, sukhitvādayaḥ ; ekasyaiva mukhasya prativimbānāṃ maṇi- kṛpāṇa - darpaṇādiṣūpalabhyamānānām, alpatva - mahattva - malinatva vimalatvādivat, tatta- dupādhivaśāt 'vyavasthāpyante' |

nanu -

'anena jīvenā''tmanā'nupraviśya' ityādiśruteḥ, 'na jīvāḥ brahmaṇo bhidyante - ityuktam !
satyaṃ paramārthataḥ | kālpanikaṃ tu bhedamāśritya, iyaṃ vyavasthocyate |
kasya punaḥ kalpanā ? na tāvadbrahmaṇaḥ ; tasya pariśuddhajñānātmanaḥ, kalpanāśūnya- tvāt | nāpi jīvānām itaretarāśrayatvaprasaṅgāt - kalpanādhīno hi ! jīvabhāvaḥ ; jīvāśrayā ca kalpaneti |
naitadevam ; avidyā- jīvabhāvayoḥ bījāṅkuranyāyena anāditvāt |
na syāt laukika-vaidikavyavasthānupapattiruktā |
satyaṃ paramārthataḥ – paramārthato na bhidyante iti satyam ; vayaṃ tu aupādhikabhedamāśritya

kiṃca - prāsādanigaraṇādivat anupapa 'nnataika' veṣāyām avastubhūtāyām avidyāyām, na itaretarāśrayatvādayo vastudoṣāḥ anavaklaptimāvahanti vastuto brahmāvyatiriktānāṃ jīvānāṃ svato viśuddhatve'pi, kṛpāṇādigatamukha pratibimbaśyāmatādivat aupādhikā- śuddhisaṃbhavāt, avidyāśrayatvopapatteḥ, kālpanikatvopapattiḥ | prativimba gataśyāmatādivat, jīvagatāśuddhirapi bhrāntireva; anyathā 'anirmokṣa' prasaṅgāt | jīvānāṃ bhramasya pravāhānāditvāt na taddheturanveṣaṇīyaḥ - iti ||

tadetat, aviditādvaitayāthātmyānāṃ bhedavādaśraddhālujana' sabahu' mānā 'valokana- lipsā vijṛmbhitam | tathāhi !

jīvasya akalpitasvābhāvikarūpeṇa avidyāśrayatve, brahmaṇa eva avidyāśrayatvamuktaṃ syāt | tadatiriktena tasmin kalpitenāsskāreṇa avidyāśrayatve, jaḍasya avidyāśrayatva- muktaṃ syāt | na khalu ! advaitavādinaḥ, tadubhayavyatiriktam ākāram abhyuṣa- gacchanti ||

śrī māpyam 2-1-6]

kalpitākāra viśiṣṭena svarūpeṇaiva avidyā' śrayatvam – iti cet ! " tanna' ; svarūpasya akhaṇḍaikarasasya, avidyāmantareṇa viśiṣṭarūpatvāsiddheḥ | avidyāśrayā kāra eva hi ! nirūpyate |

kiṃ ca bandha- mokṣādivyavasthāsiddhyarthaṃ hi ! jīvā' jñānavādā śrayaṇam | sā tu vyavasthā, jīvājñānapakṣe'pi na sidhyati | avidyāvināśa eva hi mokṣaḥ | tatra ekasmin mukte avidyāvināśāt, itare'pi 'vimucyeran ' |
anyasya amuktatvāt, avidyā tiṣṭhati - iti cet ! tarhi ekasyāpi amuktiḥ syāt ; avidyāyāḥ avinaṣṭatvāt |

pratijīvam avidyābhedaḥ kalpyate | tatra yasyā'vidyā 'vinaṣṭā', sa mokṣyate ; yasya tu avinaṣṭā, saḥ 'bhantsyate ' iti cet ! 'tanna'; 'pratijīvam' iti, jīva- bhedamāśritya | sa jīvabhedaḥ, kiṃ svābhāvikaḥ ? uta avidyākalpitaḥ ?

na tāvat svābhāvikaḥ ; anabhyupagamāt | bhedasiddhyarthasya 'ca' avidyā kalpanasya vyarthatvāt |
atha avidyākalpitaḥ ! tatra iyaṃ jīvabhedakalpikā avidyā, kiṃ brahmaṇaḥ 1 uta jīvānām ?
tanneti | avidyāśrayatve viśiṣṭatvam ; viśiṣṭākāra eva avidyāśraya iti, ātmā- śrayaṇamityarthaḥ |

kiṃ cetyādi | avidyā kim ekā ? uta bahvī ? iti vikalpamabhipretya ekatvapakṣe dūṣaṇaṃ tatra ekasmin mukte iti | (tathā) tatrā'pi kim avidyā naśyati | uta na naśyati ? iti vikalpamabhipretya nāśapakṣe ca dūṣaṇam |

anyasyeti, avināśapakṣaṃ dūṣayati |

pratijīvamiti, avidyā bahutva pakṣa manuvadati | bhantsyate - baddho bhaviṣyati | anabhyupagamāt — advaitahāniprasaṅgāt ||

2 na - i, ta, tha. 0 bhatsyate - a.

1 ṇāvidyā da. naṣṭā - ta, na.

brahmaṇaḥ – iti cet ! āgato'si madīyaṃ mārgam |

atha jīvānām ! kim - asyāḥ jīvabhedaklṛptisiddhyarthatāṃ vismarasi 1 ||
atha pratijīvaṃ baddhamuktavyavasthāsiddhyarthaṃ yāḥ avidyāḥ kalpyante ; tābhireva jīvabhedo'pi - iti manuṣe ! ; jīvabhedasiddhau, tāḥ siddhyanti ; tāsu siddhāsu jīvabheda- siddhiriti, itaretarāśrayatvam |
nacā'tra vījāṅkuranyāyaḥ sidhyati ; bījāṅkureṣu hi ! anyadanyadvījam anyasyā'nyasya(ṅkurasyotpādakam | iha tu yāmiravidyābhiḥ ye jīvāḥ kalpyante ; tānevāssśritya tāsāṃ siddhiriti, 'aśakanīyatā' |

atha – bījāṅkuranyāyena pūrvapūrvajīvāśrayābhiravidyābhiḥ, uttarottarajīvakalpanāṃ manyase ; tathā sati, jīvānāṃ bhaṅguratvam, akṛtābhyāgama-kṛta vipraṇāśādi' prasaṅgava | ata eva brahmaṇaḥ pūrvapūrvajīva (bhāvā) śrayābhira vidyābhiḥ, "uttarottarajīvabhāva kalpa- nam - ityapi nirastam | avidyā pravāhe abhyupagamyamāne, ta(ta) tkalpita 'jīvabhāva 'syā'pi

tadvat pravāhānāditā syāt na dhruvarūpatā | 'āmokṣācca' jīvasya dhruvatvam iṣṭaṃ na sidhyet ||

yaccoktam - avidyāyāḥ avastu rūpatvena' anupapannataika' veṣāyāḥ, na itaretarā- śrayatvādayo vastudoṣāḥ anavaklaptimāvahanti – iti | tathā sati, muktān paraṃ ca brahma' āśrayeda vidyā |

śuddhavidyāsvarūpatvāt, aśuddhirūpā na tatra prasajati' iti cet ! kim upapacyanuvartinī avidyā 1 evaṃ tarhi, uktābhirūpapattibhiḥ', jīvānapi nā''śrayet ||
kiṃ ca - jīvāśrayāyā avidyāyāḥ, tattvajñānodayāt nāśe sati jīvo naśyedvā ? na vā 1
yadi naśyet ; varūpocchittilakṣaṇo mokṣaḥ syāt | no cet; avidyānāśe'pi anirmokṣaḥ ; brahmasvarūpavyatiriktajīvatvāvasthānāt ||
yaccoktam – maṇi kṛpāṇa darpaṇādiṣūpalabhyamāna mukhamalinatvavimalatvādivat,

śuddhayaśuddhayādivyavasthopapattiḥ - iti |
tatredaṃ vimarśanīyam - alpatva- malinatvādayaḥ
naśyeyuḥ 1 - iti | kṛpāṇādyupādhyapagame – iti

'aupādhikāḥ doṣāḥ ", kadā

cet !; kiṃ tadā alpa- tvādyāśrayaḥ prativimbaṃ tiṣṭhati vā ? na vā ? | "tiṣṭhatīti cet ! ; taduttarottarajīvabhāvakalpanamityarthaḥ | āmokṣācceti, dharma- dharmikalpanapakṣayorubhayorapiṃ tulyaṃ dūṣaṇam | jīvasya -- jīvabhāvaviśiṣṭasya ||

cet ! ;

śuddhavidya svarūpatvāt śuddhajñaptirūpatvāt | upapattibhiḥ anyonyāśrayādibhiḥ || no cet...rmokṣa iti | avidyā kāryasya jīvatvasya avasthānāt anirmokṣa ityarthaḥ | dāntika paryālocanayā dūṣaṇamuktam |

tatsthānīyasya jīvasyā'pi sthitatvāt anirmokṣaprasaṅgaḥ | 'naśyati' cet ! tadvadeva jīvanāśāt, svarūpocchittilakṣaṇo mokṣaḥ syāt |
kiṃca - yasya hi apuruṣārtharūpa doṣapratibhāsaḥ, tasya taducchedaḥ puruṣārthaḥ | tatra kim aupādhikadoṣapratibhāsaḥ, vimvasthānīyasya brahmaṇaḥ 1 uta pratibimba sthānīyasya " jīvasya' ? uta anyasya kasyacit ?
ādyayoḥ kalpayoḥ, dṛṣṭānto'yaṃ na saṅgacchate ; mukhasya mukhapratibimbasya ca, alpatvādidoṣapratibhāsaśūnyatvāt | na hi ! mukhaṃ tatprativimbaṃ vā cetayate |

brahmaṇo doṣapratibhāse, brahmavidyā prasaṅgaśca ||
tṛtīyo'pi kalpo na kalpate; jīva-brahmavyatiriktasya draṣṭuḥ abhāvāt |

rika

kiṃ ca - avidyākalpyasya jīvasya kalpakaḥ kaḥ ? – iti 'nirūpaṇīyam" |
na tāvat avidyā; acetanatvāt | nā'pi jīvaḥ śuktikārajatādivat avidyākalpyatvācca jīvabhāvasya |

ātmāśrayadoṣaprasaṅgāt |

kiśca yasyeti | anuruṣārthapratibhāsasya nirasanīyatvāt, tannirāsāya hi vedāntārambhaḥ | bimba pratibimba sthānīyayo; jīva- brahmaṇoḥ dopapratibhāsāyogāt, tannirāsārtho vedāntaḥ anārabhyaḥ iti dūṣaṇam || utā'nyasyeti, dṛṣṭānte mukha-tatpratibimba-tadvyatirikta cetanasadbhāvāt, tadabhiprāyeṇa | na hīti | rūpavadacetana viśeṣasyaiva prativimbaṃ dṛṣṭam | tasmāt brahma- jīvayo: bimba pratibimbabhāvābhyupagame tayoḥ acetanatvamāpatatīti bhāvaḥ ||
kiścati | pūrvamavidyāśrayānupapattiruktā | idānīṃ avidyā kāryabhramāśrayo nirūpyate | kaḥ kalpakaḥ 1 ko amāśrayaḥ ? ātmāśrayeti | kalpakatvāt siddhiḥ kalpyatvāt asiddhiśceti, svasiddheḥ svāpekṣā | jīvaviṣayabhramāśraye siddhe hi ! jīvaklṛptiḥ | jīvasiddheḥ svaviṣayabhramāśrayabhūtena svenaiva sāpekṣatvāt ityarthaḥ | kalpyatvāditi |

brahmaiva kalpakam - iti cet !; 'brahmā' jñānamevā''yātam |

'kiṃca'- brahmājñānānabhyupagame, kiṃ brahma jīvān paśyati vā? navā ?
na paśyati cet' ! ' īkṣā pūrvikā vicitrasṛṣṭiḥ nāma-rūpavyākaraṇam' ityādi na syāt |
atha paśyati ! akhaṇḍaikarasaṃ brahma, na avidyāmantareṇa jīvān paśyatīti, brahmājñānaprasaṅgaḥ ||

māyāvidyayoḥ vibhāgavādasya nirāsa: ]

ata eva māyā'vidyā vibhāga' vādo'pi nirastaḥ | ajñānamantareṇa hi ! māyino'pi brahmaṇaḥ, jīvadarśitvaṃ na syāt | na ca māyāvī parānadṛṣṭvā, mohayitumalam | nāspi māyā, māyāvino darśanasādhanam ; dṛṣṭeṣu pareṣu tanmohana sādhanamātra' tvāt

tasyāḥ |

atha — brahmaṇo māyā tasya jīva 'darśitvaṃ kurvatī, jīva' mohanasya' hetuḥ iti manyase ! ; tarhi pariśuddhasya akhaṇḍaika' rasasvaprakāśasya brahmaṇaḥ paradarśanaṃ kurvatī māyā, māyāparaparyāyā avidhaiva syāt |
atha matam — viparitadarśanahetuḥ avidyā; māyā tu mithyābhūtaṃ brahmavyatiriktaṃ 'mithyātvenaiva darśayantī, na brahmaṇo viparītadarśana hetuḥ | ataḥ tasyāḥ na avidyātvam - iti |
avidyāmantareṇeti | aparamārthajīvapratītiḥ, avidyayā binā na saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ |
ata eva | īkṣāpūrvaka sṛṣṭyādyarthaṃ brahmaṇaḥ aparamārthajīvadarśanārthe ca avidyānvayābhyupaga- mādeva | 'māyā parabhramahetuḥ ; avidyā svabhramahetu:' iti tanmatam | 'māyinaṃ tu maheśvaram' iti śrutiḥ, etadvibhāgahetuḥ | brahmaṇo māyeti | asyāstu māyāyā ayaṃ viśeṣa iti | tarhīti | nāmani vivāda āvayorityarthaḥ |

naivam ; candraikatve jñāyamāne, dvicandradarśanahetorapi avidyātvāt | yadi ca mithyātvenaiva svavyatiriktaṃ jānāti na tarhi tanmohayati | na hi ! anunmattaḥ, mithyātvena jñātān 'mohayitumīhate' ||

brahma,

atha - apuruṣārthā paramārtha darśana hetuḥ avidyā | māyā tu brahmaṇaḥ na apuruṣārtha- "darśanahetuḥ " | ataḥ asyā na avidyātvam - iti matam !

tanna; dvicandrajñānasya duḥkhahetutvābhāvena apuruṣārthatvābhāve'pi ', taddhetuḥ avidyaiva | tannirasane ca ' prayasyanti' | yadi ca na apuruṣārthadarśanakarī māyā ; tarhi ! anucchedyatayā nityā brahmasvarūpānubandhinī syāt |
astu; ko doṣaḥ 1 – iti cet ! ; dvaitadarśanameva doṣaḥ |

'yatra hi dvaitamiva bhavati |

yatratvasya sarvamātmaivā'bhūt ; tat kena ke paśyet' ityādyadvaitaśrutayaḥ prakupyeyuḥ |

paramārthaviṣayāḥ advaitaśrutayaḥ ; māyāyāstu aparamārthatvāt avirodhaḥ – iti cet 1 aparicchinnānandaikasvarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ, aparamārthabhūtamāyādarśanaṃ tadvattā 'ca',

avidyāmantareṇa nopapadyate ||

jīvamohanam - iti cet ! apurūpārthena mohanena kiṃ prayojanam ! krīḍeti cet ! aparicchinnānandasya kiṃ krīḍayā ? paripūrṇabhogānāmeva krīḍā, puruṣārthatvena loke dṛṣṭā - iti cet !
naivamihopapadyate ; na hi ! aparamārthabhūtaiḥ krīḍopakaraṇaiḥ aparamārthatayā bhāsamānaiḥ niṣpannayā aparamārthabhūtayā krīḍayā, aparamārthabhūtena ca tatpratibhāsena, anunmattānāṃ krīḍāraso niṣpadyate |

māyāśrayatābhi

māyāśrayatayā'bhimatabrahmavyatirekeṇa avidyāśrayasya jīvasya kalpanāsambhavaśca, pūrvavadeva draṣṭavyaḥ | ataḥ brahmaiva anādyavidyāśabalaṃ, svagatanānātvaṃ paśyatīti, advitī- yatvaṃ brahmaṇo'bhyupayadbhiḥ, 'abhyupetyam' ||

yattu -- bandhamokṣavyavasthā nopapadyate iti na tat brahmājñāna' vādinaśrodyam | ekasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ ajñasya, svājñāna nivṛtyā mo (kṣa) kṣyamāṇatvāt baddha - muktā divyavasthāyā eva abhāvāt | vyavahriyamāṇāyāśca baddha-mukta- 'śiṣyācāryādi 'vyavasthāyāḥ, kālpanika- tvāt svamadarśina iva ca, ekasyaiva avidyayā sarvakalpanopapatteḥ | svapnadṛśā hi ! ekena dṛṣṭā śiṣyācāryādayaḥ tadavidyā kalpitā eva |
ata eva - bahvavidyā kalpanamapi na yuktimat | pāramārthikī bandhamokṣavyavasthā
pūrvavadeveti | anyonyāśraya | dibhirdūṣaṇairityarthaḥ |
yaviti | nedaṃ codyamaniṣṭamityarthaḥ | kālpanikatvāditi | svāmavyavasthāḥ yathā kālpanikatvāt ekāvidyayā upapadyante; tathā sarvāsāṃ vyavasthānāṃ kālpanikatvāt, ekā vidyayaiva tatkalpanopapattiḥ ityarthaḥ | sarvakalpanopapatteriti | anyathā'nupapannaṃ hi kalpakam | anyathopapannatvāt, na avidyā bhedaklaptiḥ nyāyyā ityarthaḥ |
ata eveti | āśrayakatve'pi sarvavyavasthopapattiḥ yena hetunā siddhā, tenaiva kālpanikatvena hetunā avidyābahutvakalpanamapi na yuktamityarthaḥ | pāramārthikītyādi | paramārtha kāryāṇāṃ hi | bahuhetvapekṣā | aparamārthānāṃ tu ekenaivā'lam ityabhiprāyaḥ |

svaparavyavasthā ca, jīvājñānavādinā'pi nā'bhyupeyate | apāramārthikī tu, ekasyaiva avidyayā 'upapadyate ||

'prayogaśca' – 1 ) bandha- mokṣavyavasthāḥ sva- paravyavasthāśca, svāvidyākalpitāḥ ; apāramārthikatvāt ; svapnadṛṣṭavyavasthāvat - iti | 2) śarīrāntarāṇyapi, mayaivā''tma- vanti ; śarīratvāt etaccharīravat | 3) śarīrāntarāṇyapi, madavidyākalpitāni + śarīratvāt, kāryatvāt, jaḍatvāt, kalpitatvādvā etaccharīravat| 4) vivādādhyā- sitaṃ cetanajātam, ahameva cetanatvāt, yat anaham tat acetanaṃ dṛṣṭam ; yathā ghaṭaḥ, ataḥ sva- paravibhāgaḥ baddhamukta - śiṣyācā yādivyavasthāśca ekasyā'vidyā kalpitāḥ ||

dvaitavādinā'pi, baddha-muktavyavasthā durupapādā; atītānā kalpānāmānantyāt, ekaikasmin kalpe ekaikamuktāvapi sarveṣāṃ mokṣasambhavāt, amuktānupapatteḥ |
anantatvāt ātmanām, amuktāśca santi - iti cet | kimidam anantatvam ? asakhyeyatvam – iti cet ! na bhūyastvāt alpajñaiḥ asaṅkhyeyatve'pi, īśvarasya sarvajñasya saṅkhyeyā eva | tasyā'pya' śakyatve, sarvajñatvaṃ na syāt |

3.

ātmanāṃ niḥsaṃkhyatvāt, īśvarasya avidyamānasaṅkhyā vedanābhāvaḥ, na asārvajñya- māvahati - iti cet ! bhinnatve, saṃkhyāvidhuratvaṃ nopapadyate |
ātmānaḥ saṃkhyāvantaḥ ; bhinnatvāt māṣa- sarpapa- 'ghaṭapaṭādivat | bhinnatve - cā''tmanām, ghaṭādivat jaḍatvam, anātmatvaṃ, kṣayitvaṃ ca prasajyate ; brahmaṇaśca
prayogaśceti | jīvājñānavādinaṃ prati prathama- tṛtīye anumāne | taṃ ca bhedavādinaṃ ca prati dvitīyahetuḥ prayujyate | tṛtīyaḥ pratyeka nirapekṣahetucatuṣṭayagarbhaḥ | turīyaṃ vyatirekyanumānam | asaṅkhyeyatvamiti, 'ananta' śabdena deśa - kālādyaparicchedasya vivakṣitatvavyāvṛttyarthaṃ -

anantatvaṃ na syāt | anantatvaṃ nāma - paricchedarahitatvam | bhedavāde ca vastvantarā- dvilakṣaṇatvena brahmaṇaḥ vastutaḥ paricchedarahitatvaṃ na śakyate vaktum | 'vastvantara'- bhāva eva hi ! vastutaḥ paricchedaḥ | vastutaḥ paricchinnasya, deśataḥ kālataśca aparicchinnatvaṃ ca na yujyate | vastvantarādvilakṣaṇatvena vastutaḥ paricchinnā 'eva ' ghaṭādayaḥ, deśataḥ kālataśca paricchinnā hi ! dṛṣṭāḥ | tathā sarve' cetanāḥ ' brahmaca, vastutaḥ paricchinnāḥ ; deśa kālābhyāmapi paricchidyante | evaṃ ca,

'satyaṃ jñānamanantam'

ityādibhiḥ sarvaprakāraparicchedarahitatvaṃ vadadbhirvirodhaḥ | utpatti - vināśādayaśca, jīvānāṃ brahmaṇaśca prasajyeran | kālapariccheda eva hi !" utpatti vināśabhāgitvam ||
ataḥ, 'ekasyaiva' aparicchinnasya brahmaṇaḥ, avidyāvijṛmbhitaṃ brahmādistambaṃ paryantaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat | sukha-duḥkhapratisandhāna vyavasthādayo'pi svāpnavyavasthāvat avidyā- svābhāvyāt upapadyante | tasmāt ekameva nityamuktasvaprakāśasvabhāvam, anādyavidyā- vaśāt jagadākāreṇa vivartata iti, paramārthato brahmavyatiriktābhāvāt tadananyatvaṃ jagataḥ iti ||

S

atrocyate - ' nirviśeṣa' khaprakāśa' mātraṃ brahma, anādyavidyātirohitasvasvarūpaṃ,

yathā ghaṭādiḥ ātmā na bhinnaḥ ajaḍatvāt yat bhinnaṃ; na tat ajaḍam | yathā ghaṭādiḥ | ātmā na bhinnaḥ ; akṣayitvāt | yat bhinnaṃ ; na tadakṣayi, yathā ghaṭādiḥ iti | vastvantarabhāva iti | vastvantaravatvaṃ vastutaḥ pariccheda ityarthaḥ | evaṃ ceti | nirūpapadam anantapadam asaṅkucitavṛtti trividhaparicchedarāhityamāheti bhāvaḥ | evam aparicchinnasya paricchinnatvaprasaṅga uktaḥ | atha avikārasya savikāratvaprasaṅgamāha – utpattīti | avikāratva- śrutivirodhaśvatyarthaḥ |

vivartate - abhyāsādhiṣṭhānaṃ bhavati ; na tu pariṇamatītyarthaḥ | tadananyatvamiti |
jagat brahmaṇaḥ paramārthabhūtamanyanna ityarthaḥ |

svagatanānātvaṃ paśyati' ityetat, 'prakāśasvarūpasya niraṃśasya prakāśanivṛttirūpatirodhāne, svarūpa nāśaprasaṅgena, tirodhānāsambhavā 'dibhyaḥ sakalapramāṇaviruddha 'svavacanaviruddhaṃ ca " iti, pūrvamevoktam |

" yat punaruktam – kāraṇa 'vyatiriktaṃ kārya, yuktivādhitatvena 'śuktikā 'rajatādivat bhramaḥ - iti | tadayuktam ; yukterabhāvāt |
yattu - anuvartamānasya kāraṇamātrasya satyatvam ; vyāvartamānānāṃ ghaṭa - śarāvādi- kāryāṇām asatyatvam - iti; tadapi, 'anyatra dṛṣṭasya anyatra vyāvartamānatā, na bādhikā' ityādibhiḥ pūrvameva parihṛtam |

yacca—upalabhyamānatva-vināśitvābhyāṃ sadasada nirvacanīyatvena, kāryasya mṛṣātvam - iti tadasat upalabdhi - vināśayogo hi ! na midhyātvaṃ sādhayati ; kintu anityatvam | yaddeśa - 'kāla' sambandhitayā yadupalabdham ; taddeśa- 'kāla' sambandhitayā bādhitatvameva hi | tasya midhyātve hetuḥ | deśāntara kālāntarasambandhitayā upalabdhasya anya deśakālasambandhitvena bādhitatvaṃ, deśāntara - kālāntarāvyāptimātraṃ sādhayati ;
niraṃśasyeti | tirodhāne'pi tadrahitāṃśa stiṣṭhanti iti na vaktuṃ yuktam ; niraṃśatvā- dityabhiprāyaḥ | svarūpanāśeti | tirodhānaṃ nāma prakāśanivṛttiḥ | tatra jaḍasya prakāśa- vyatiriktatvāt, prakāśanivṛttāvapi tasya sthitiḥ upapadyate | prakāśaikarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ, prakāśa- nivṛttirūpatirodhānaṃ nāma svarūpanivṛttirevetyarthaḥ | viruddhamiti | nityatvādisādhakavacana- viruddhaṃ nirviśeṣatvam | jñaptitvaviruddham asmatpratyayagocaratvam ityarthaḥ | pūrvamevoktam-- asmin prabandhe siddhāntinoktamityarthaḥ | ityādibhiriti | sopādhikatva kālātyayāpadiṣṭatva- vacanavyāhatibhiḥ pūrvameva parihṛtamityarthaḥ | yaddeśeti | yadi anityatvameva mithyātvam ; tarhi siddhasādhanaṃ mithyātvānumānam | yadi anityatvavilakṣaṇaṃ; tarhi sopādhikatvādi

śrī bhāṣyam 2-1-6]
na tu mithyātvam || pratiprayogaśca

ghaṭādikārya satyam ; deśa-kālādipratipannopādhau abādhitatvāt ātmavat ||

yaccoktam – kāraṇasvarūpāt avikṛtādvikṛtācca kāryotpattirna sambhavati - iti; tadasat ; deśa - kālādisahakārisamavahitāt kāraṇāt, kāryotpattisambhavāt | tatsamavadhānaṃ ca vikṛtasya avikṛtasya ca na sambhavati – iti, yaduktam ; 'tadayuktam ' ; pūrvamavikṛtasyaiva kālādisamavadhānasambhavāt ||
avikṛtatvāviśeṣāt pūrvamapi deśakālādisamavadhānaṃ prasajyate iti cet ! na deśa - kālādisamavadhānasya kāraṇāntarāyattasya etadāyattatvābhāvāt |

anaḥ, deśa-kālādisamavadhānarūpaviśeṣamāpanaṃ kāraṇaṃ, kāryamutpādayatīti na kiñcidavahīnam | kāraṇasya ca kārya prati ārambhakatvam abādhitaṃ dṛśyamānaṃ, na kenā'pi prakāreṇa apahnotuṃ śakyate ||

yathā ardāhāsya

yattu – hemādimātrasya, rucakādikāryasya etadāśrayasya vā hemādeḥ ārambhakatvaṃ na saṃbhavati - iti tadayuktam ; hemādimātrasyaiva yathoktaparikarayuktasya, ārambhakatva- saṃbhavāt' | na ca - ārambhaka' hema' vyatiriktaṃ kāryaṃ na dṛśyate iti vaktuṃ śakyam ; hemātiriktasya svastikasya darśanāt, buddhi-śabdāntarādibhiḥ vastvantarasya sādhi-

tatvācca |

na ca -- ayaṃ śuktikārajatādivat bhramaḥ ; utpatti - vināśayorantarāle upalabhya- mānasya, taddeśakālasambandhitayā vādhādarśanāt | na ca - asyā upalabdheḥ bādhikā, kācidapi yuktirdṛśyate | prāganupalabdha' svastikopalabdhivelāyāmapi hemapratyabhijñā, svastikāśrayatayā hemno'pyanuvṛtteḥ aviruddhā |
śrutibhistu prapañcamidhyātvasādhanaṃ pūrvameva nirastam | yaccānyadatra pratyakṣā- virodhādi prativaktavyam ; tadapi sarva, pūrvameva 'sūktam ||

yaccoktam – ekenā''tmanā sarvāṇi śarīrāṇi ātmavanti - iti ; tadasat ; ekasyaiva sarvaśarīraprayuktasukha-duḥkhapratisandhānaprasaṅgāt | saubhariprabhṛtiṣu hi !
yatviti | hemamātrasya, rucakādikāryamātrasya kāryāśritadravyamātrasya cā''rambhakatvaṃ na sambhavatītyuktam ! ityarthaḥ | yathoktaparikaraḥ- deśa - kālādiḥ |
na cāyamiti | pūrvapakṣiṇoktaṃ anirūpitavastvavalambanabuddhiśabdādīnām asādhakatvaṃ pratikṣipati | utpatti .... dhādarśanāt iti | anityatve sādhye, siddhasādhanatvam | pratipannopādhau bādhaśca t, tadanupalambhādeva nirastamiti bhāvaḥ |
nirastamiti | siddhāntinetyarthaḥ | yaccā'nyaditi | 'pratyakṣa sanmātragrāhi ; virodhe satyapi śāstra balīya:' ityādikaṃ tvaduktaṃ pūrvameva anena granthakāreṇa nirastamityarthaḥ |
ekasyaiveti, vyāptivirodhamāha | yat sampratipannaikātmakaṃ śarīram, tatra hi ! sukhādi- pratisandhānaṃ dṛṣṭamiti bhāvaḥ | saubharīti | pratisandhānābhāvaḥ śarīrabhedanibandhanaḥ ityatra

'ātmaikatvena', anekaśarīraprayuktasukhādipratisandhānam ekasya dṛśyate |
na ca – ahamarthasya jñātṛtvāt tadbhedāt pratisandhānābhāvaḥ na ātmabhedāt- iti vaktuṃ śakyam ; 'ātmā jñātaiva sa ca ahamartha eva | antaḥ karaṇabhūtastu ahaṅkāraḥ, jaḍatvāt karaṇatvācca śarīrendriyādivat na jñātā' ityupapāditatvāt |

- śarīratva - jaḍatva- kāryatva - kalpitatvaiḥ sarvaśarīrāṇām ekasyā'vidyākalpitatva- muktam ; tadapi — sarvaśarīrāṇāmavidyākalpitatvasyaivā'bhāvāt - ayuktam | tadabhāvaśca, abādhi tasya satya tvopapādanāt |
yacca — cetanādanyasya jaḍatvadarśanāt, sarvacetanānām ananyatvamuktam ; tadapi - sukha-duḥkhavyavasthayā bhedopapādanādeva – nirastam |

yattu – ' mayaivā''tmavanti, madavidyākalpitāni; ahameva sarva cetanajātam' iti ahamarthasya aikyamupapāditam tat ajñātasva siddhāntasya bhrāntijalpitam | ahaṃ- tvamādyartha vilakṣaṇaṃ cinmātraṃ hi ! ātmā tvanmate |
prabhṛtiśabdasya, vilakṣaṇajñānānāṃ keṣāṃcit śarīrabhede'pi pūrvajanmasukhādipratyabhi- jñānāditi bhāvaḥ | ātmaikatva iti | ekaśarīravatrtyātmana: ekatvaṃ sādhyate siddha- sādhanatvam | anekaśarīravartinaḥ dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhaḥ | paraśarīratacceṣṭādibhiḥ ātmā hyanumīyate | tacca anumānaṃ svasmādanyatvenaiva enaṃ darśayati | svātmā hi ! ' aham' iti aparokṣamavabhāsate | na hi paraśarīragataḥ ātmā aparokṣaḥ iti ||
ātmeti | antaḥkaraṇasya jñātṛtvameva nāsti ; tattu ātmanaḥ ityarthaḥ | jñātṛtve'pi tasma, pūrvapūrvakalpeṣu utpannavinaṣṭāntaḥ karaṇānāmapi sanakādīnāṃ tatpratisandhānasambhavāt na antaḥ bhedaḥ pratisandhānābhāvahetuḥ ||

tadapi . ayuktam iti dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvamuktaṃ bhavati | śarīrāṇāmavidyā - kalpitatve siddhe hi !, ekasyā'vidyā kalpitatva siddhiḥ | tadeva nā'stīti, sapakṣabhūtaśarīrasya avidyākalpitatvābhāvāt | tadapi .... nirastam iti, kālātyayāpadiṣṭatvaṃ dūṣaṇamuktaṃ bhavati ||
yatviti | baddhamuktā divyavasthānām ekāvidyā kalpitatvaṃ yaduktam ; tatrā'pi apāra- mārthikatva hetorasiddhiḥ, sopādhikatvaṃ cā'nusandheyam |

kiṃ ca - "nirviśeṣa cinmātrātireki sarva mithyā' iti vadataḥ, mokṣārthaśravaṇādi- prayatno niṣphalaḥ ; avidyākāryatvāt śuktikārajatādiṣu rajatādyupādānādiprayatnavat | 1) mokṣārtha' prayatnaḥ' vyarthaḥ ; kalpitācāryāyattajñāna kāryatvāt, śuka- prahlāda vāmadevādi prayatnavat | 2) tattvamasyādivākya janya jñānaṃ na bandhanivartakam ; avidyā kalpita- vākyajanyatvāt, svayamavidyātmakatvāt, avidyākalpitajñātrāśrayatvāt, kalpitā- cāryāyattaśravaṇa' janyatvādvā svāpnabandha' nivartana' vākyajanyajñānavat |

kiṃca - nirviśeṣacinmātraṃ brahma mithyā avidyā kāryajñānagamyatvāt avidyā- kalpitajñā' trāśraya' jñānagamyatvāt, 'avidyātmaka 'jñānagamyatvādvā | yadevaṃ tat tathā; yathā svāpnagandharvanagarādiḥ | na ca nirviśeṣacinmātraṃ brahma svayaṃ prakāśate yena na pramāṇāntaramapekṣate | yattu, ātmasākṣikaṃ svaprakāśajñānaṃ dṛśyate tattu jñeyaviśeṣa- siddhirūpaṃ, jñātṛgatameva dṛśyate iti pūrvamevoktam |

yāni ca tasya nirviśeṣatva ' sādhakāni ' yauktikāni jñānānyupanyastāni tāni ca anantaroktaiḥ 'avidyākāryatvāt' ityādibhiḥ anumānaiḥ nirastāni ||

na ca - nirviśeṣasya cinmātrasya, ajñānasākṣitvam ahaṅkārādijagadbhamaśca upapadyate |
avidyākāryatvāt ityādihetugaṇaiḥ aniṣṭaprasaṅgamāha | tattvamasyādīti | avidyā- mūlatvāśrayaṇe tastvamasya | divākyajanyajñānasya anivartakatvaprasaṅgaḥ ityarthaḥ ||

sākṣitvabhramādayo'pi hi ! jñātṛviśeṣagatā dṛṣṭāḥ na jñaprimātragatāḥ | na ca tasya 'prakāśatvaṃ', svāyattaprakāśatā vā sidhyati | prakāśo' hi !" kasyacit puruṣasya kaṃcana arthaviśeṣaṃ prati siddhi rūpo dṛśyate | 'tata' eva hi ! tasya svayaṃprakāśatā upapādyate bhavadbhirapi | na ca ' atādṛśasya' nirviśeṣasya 'prakāśatā sambhavati ||

yaḥ punaḥ – svagoṣṭhīṣu, 'aparamārthādapi paramārtha kārya dṛśyate' ityudghoṣaḥ ; so'pi, 'tāni kāryāṇi sarvāṇi, abādhitakalpāni vyāvahārikasatyāni, vastutastu avidyātmakānyeva' iti | svābhyupagamādeva nirastaḥ | asmābhirapi – sarvatra paramārthādeva kāraṇāt sarvakāryotpattim upapādayadbhiḥ pūrvameva – nirastaḥ |

na ca tvayā eṣāmanu' mānānāṃ śrutivirodho vaktuṃ śakyate; śruterapi avidyākāryatvena avidyātmakatvena ca, uktadṛṣṭāntebhyo viśeṣābhāvāt ||

yattu – brahmaṇaḥ apāramārthikajñānagamyatve'pi

paścāttanabādhādarśanāt brahma

satyameva - iti ;

tadasat duṣṭakāraṇajanyajñānagamyatve niścite sati paścāttanabādhādarśanasya akiñcitkaratvāt | yathā 'śūnyameva tatvam' iti vākyajanyajñānasya paścāttanabādhā- darśane'pi doṣamūlatvaniścayādeva tadarthasya asatyatvam | kiṃ ca-
atādṛśasya – nirviśeṣatvāt karma-kartṛrahitasyetyarthaḥ | tādṛśasya iti pāṭhe, karma-kartṛsahito yaḥ prakāśate, tasya nirviśeṣatve abhimate sā na sidhyatītyarthaḥ |
yaḥ punariti | aparamārthahetoḥ śuktirajatādyupādānādivat niṣphalatvaṃ pūrvamuktam ; tanna aparamārthādapi paramārthotpatteḥ iti viśeṣābhāvāditi | prabala virodhe hi ! daurbalyamiti bhāvaḥ |

kiṃ ceti | pratyakṣādipramāṇaṃ, vede'pi saviśeṣaparavākyajātaṃ ca vihāya, niṣedhaparavākya prābalyaṃ hi ! tvaduktam | tatra vidhirūpatvāt pūrveṣāṃ daurbalyam; niṣedharūpatvācca paścāttanasya prābalyam | tacca sarvaśūnyavādina evetyarthaḥ |

'neha nānā'sti kiñcana' 'vijñānamānandaṃ brahma'

iti, vijñānamātrātiriktasya kṛtsnasya vastujātasya niṣedhakatvena sarvasmātparatvāt', paścāttanabādhādarśanamucyate | 'śūnyameva tattvam' iti tasyā'pyabhāvaṃ vadataḥ, tasmātparatvena paścāttanabādha dṛśyate | sarvaśūnyatvātireki niṣedhāsambhavāt tasyaiva paścāttanabādhā- darśanam | doṣamūlatvaṃ tu, pratyakṣādīnāṃ vedāntajanmanaḥ sarvaśūnya jñānasyā'pi, aviśiṣṭam ||
'ataḥ - sarva vijñānajātaṃ pāramārthikajñātṛgatam, svayaṃ ca paramārthabhūtam artha- viśeṣasiddhirūpam | tatra kiṃcit jñānaṃ doṣamūlam ; doṣaśca paramārthaḥ | kiṃcicca 'nirdoṣa', "pāramārthika' sāmagrījanyam - iti yāvat na abhyupeyate ; na tāvat satya - mithyārtha- vyavasthā, lokavyavahāraśca - setsyati |
lokavyavahāro hi' ! 'pāramārthiko ' bhrāntirūpaśca pāramārthikajñātṛgatārtha- viśeṣasiddhirūpaprakāśapūrvakaḥ | nirviśeṣasanmātrasya tu, pāramārthikasya ca pratibhāsā- dehetutvāsaṃbhavāt, lokavyavahāro na sambhavati ||

yacca taiḥ – niradhiṣṭhānabhramāsaṃbhavāt, sarvādhyāsādhiṣṭhānasya sanmātrasya ' pāramārthikatvam – uktam ; tadapi doṣa- doṣāśrayatva jñātṛtva - jñānānām apāramārthye'pi bhramopapattivat, adhiṣṭhānāpāramārthye'pi bhramopapatteḥ - nirastam |
ataḥ ityasya na setsyati ityanvayaḥ | nirviśeṣeti | nirviśeṣajñānasya jñātṛtvā- bhāvāt, pratibhāsasya āśrayatayā hetutvaṃ nāsti | nirviśeṣatvādeva kāraṇatayā hetutvamapi nāstītyarthaḥ | pāramārthikāpāramārthikaśabdau paramārthā paramārthaviṣayatvaparau | pratibhāsāderiti ādiśabdaḥ vyavahāraparaḥ ||

atha - 'adhiṣṭhānāpāramārthyespi na kvacit bhramo dṛṣṭa iti, sanmātrasya pāramārthikatvam avaśyāśrayaṇīyam - iti manyase ! hanta tarhi, doṣa- doṣāśrayatva- 'jñātṛtva-jñānānām' apāramārthye'pi na kvaciddhamo dṛṣṭa iti, darśanānuguṇyena teṣāmapi paramārthyam avaśyāśrayaṇīyamiti ; na kaśvidviśeṣaḥ '; anyatra tatsaṃrambhāt ||

yattu – bheda' pakṣe'pi', atītakalpānāmānantyāt, sarveṣāmātmanā 'muktatvena baddhāsaṃbhavāt, baddhamuktavyavasthā na saṃbhavati – iti ; tat, ātmānantyena parihṛtam | yattu – ātmanāṃ bhinnatve māpa sarṣapa - 'ghaṭa-paṭa divat sakhyāvastvam avarjanīyam - iti, tatra ghaṭādīnāmapi anantatvāt dṛṣṭāntaḥ sādhyavikalaḥ sthāt |

'daśa ghaṭā : ' 'sahasraṃ māṣāḥ' iti saṃkhyāvaccaṃ dṛśyate iti cet ! satyam : tattu na ghaṭādi svarūpagatam | api tu deśa-kālādyupādhimadghaṭādigatam | tādṛśaṃ tu saṅkhyāvactvam, ātmanāmabhyupagacchāmaḥ |
na ca - tāvatā sarvamuktiprasaṅgaḥ ; ātmasvarūpānantyāt |
na kaścidviśeṣa iti | adhiṣṭhānasya apāramārthye, tasyā'pi adhiṣṭhānāntarāpekṣayā anavasthā — iti cet | doṣasya apāramārthye'pi anavasthā syāt | tatra svarūpānāditvena, pravāhānāditvena durghaṭatvena parihāraścet ! sa: [ atrā'pi ) tulyaḥ, ityabhiprāyaḥ |

ātmānantyeneti | yathā māṣa - sarṣapādiṣu ekaikajātīyā evā'nantāḥ samuditāścā- nantāḥ ; tathā, baddhāśvā'nantāḥ ; muktāścā'nantāḥ samuditāśca sarve cetanā anantāḥ | sarveṣāmātmanāṃ pratyekamekatvasaṃkhyā astyeva | ataḥ na saṃkhyāvattvamātraṃ niṣidhyate | dvitva- saṃkhyāyā avadhireva niṣidhyate ityarthaḥ | dṛṣṭānta iti | atītakalpānāmānantyāt, ekasmin kalpe ekaghaṭasadbhāve'pi ghaṭānāmānantyaṃ siddhamiti sādhyavikalatā dṛṣṭāntasya | abhyupagacchāma iti | pramāṇaṃ ca śāstram | tacca tān anantatvena pratipādayatīti, (sāvadhika) saṅkhyāvastvahetoḥ śāstrabādhāt, cetanānantyamabhyupagantavyamityarthaḥ | deśakālādīti, 'ādi' śabdena kartā vivakṣitaḥ; 'etatkulālanirmitā ghaṭāḥ daśa' ityapi vaktuṃ śakyatvāt | ānantyāditi | deśādyupādhirāhitye ātmasvarūpamātrasya asaṅkhyeyatvādityarthaḥ |

yattu — ātmanāṃ bhinnatve, ghaṭādivat jaḍatvānātmatvakṣayitvaprasaṅgaḥ - iti ; tadayuktam ; ekajātīyānāṃ bhedasya, tajjātīyānāṃ jātyantarīyatvānāpādakatvāt' | na hi ! 'ghaṭādīnāṃ bhedaḥ, teṣāṃ paṭatvamāpādayati ||
] brahmaṇi miśratvam ānantyavighaṭakamiti śaṅkā, tatprarihāraśca ]
yattu - bhinnatve, vastutaḥ paricchedāt, deśakālābhyāmapi paricchedo brahmaṇaḥ prasajyata iti, anantatvaṃ brahmaṇo na sidhyati – iti tadayuktam ; vastutaḥ paricchinnā- nāmapi deśakālaparicchedasya nyūnādhikabhāvena aniyamadarśanāt, deśakālasambandheyattāyāḥ- pramāṇāntarāyatta' nirṇayatvena', brahmaṇaḥ sarvadeśakālasambandhasyā'pi pramāṇāntarādāpatataḥ, virodhābhāvāt |

vastutaḥ paricchedamātrādapi, sarvaprakāraparicchedarahitatvābhāvāt, ānantyāsiddhiḥ nahīti | 'ghaṭo na bhinnaḥ ghaṭatvāt yadbhinnaṃ na tadbhaṭaḥ ; yathā paṭaḥ' ityukte, yathā kālātyayāpadiṣṭatvam ; tathā tvaduktavyatirekiṇāmapi kālātyayāpadiṣṭatvamityarthaḥ ||
pariccheda | diti | 'idam idaṃ na bhavati' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyānarhatvaṃ vastutaḥ pariccheda iti mṛṣāvādyabhimatamiti jñāpitam | siddhāntyabhimataḥ prakārāntareṇa vastuparicchedastu, mṛṣā- bādyanabhimata:; tadviparītarūpasya aparicchedasya, nirviśeṣatvavirodhāt | ataḥ mṛṣāvādinā svābhimatavastuparicchedasya deśa - kālapariccheda prayojakatvābhimānena tau prasañjitau | tadayuktamiti | prayojyā kāratāratamyaṃ, prayojakā kāratāratamyanibandhanaṃ dṛṣṭam | yathā patanatāratamyaṃ gurutvatāratamya- nibandhanam | ataḥ vastuparicchedasya deśa - kālaparicchedaprayojakatve sati nyūnādhikabhāvavatva- prasaṅgaḥ | na ca sa tathā dṛśyate | tasmāt tatprayojakatvaṃ tasya anupapannam | ataḥ vastutaḥ paricchinnasyā'pi brahmaṇaḥ, deśakālaparicchedābhāva upapannaḥ ityarthaḥ | pramāṇāntareti | vastuparicchedaḥ aprayojaka iti bhāvaḥ |
vastutaḥ paricchedamātrādapi iti, ānantyaśruteḥ saṅkocaṃ śaṅkate |

- iti cet ! ; tat bhavato'pi avidyāvilakṣaṇatvaṃ brahmaṇo'bhyupayataḥ samānam | ataḥ, sataḥ avidyāvilakṣaṇatvābhyupagamāt brahmaṇo'pi bhinnatvena, bhedaprayuktā doṣāḥ sarve tavāpi prasajyeran |

yadi - avidyāvilakṣaṇatvaṃ nā'bhyupeyate ! tarhi, avidyātma' kameva brahma' syāt | 'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'
iti lakṣaṇavākyamapi tata eva apārthakaṃ syāt ||

bhedatatvānabhyupagame hi ! svapakṣa- parapakṣasādhana- dūṣaṇādi vivekābhāvāt sarvam asamañjasaṃ syāt | ānantyaprasiddhiśva, deśa-kālaparichedarahitatvamātreṇa, 'na vastuto'pi ' paricchedarahitatvena'; tathāvidhasya śaśaviṣāṇāya' mānasya' anupalabdheḥ |
bhedavādinastu, sarvacidacidvastuśarīratvena brahmaṇaḥ sarvaprakāratvāt svataḥ parato'pi paricchedo na vidyate ||
yadītyādinā prasaṅgadāya prasañjakasya viparyaye aniṣṭamāha | satyaṃ jñānamiti | 'paramārthādanyatvaṃ vastupariccheda:' ityayuktam ; tacca bādhyatvam | bādhaphalaṃ ca kadāci - nivṛttiriti, tasya kālaparicchedāntarbhāvāt | ataḥ vilakṣaṇatvamātraṃ vastupariccheda iti, avidyāvilakṣaṇabrahmaṇaḥ vastuparicchedaprasaṅgo durvāraḥ ||
bhedatattveti | svapakṣo nirdūṣaṇa: prabala: ; parapakṣaḥ sadūṣaṇo durbala:, iti bhedāṅgīkāraḥ | bhedaḥ aparamārtha iti cet ! parapakṣa eva prabalaḥ syāt | sa evā'bhyupagataḥ syādityarthaḥ |
bhedavādinastviti | sākṣātsiddhāntipakṣe vastvaparicchedaḥ upapannaḥ ityarthaḥ | brahmaṇaḥ sarvaprakāratvāditi | 'idam idaṃ na bhavati' iti hi ! vastuparicchedaḥ | 'idam idaṃ bhavati' iti nirdeśayogyatvaṃ sarvavastusāmānādhikaraṇyārhatvaṃ vastvaparicchedaḥ ityarthaḥ | aneka viśeṣaṇā - nām ekaviśeṣyaparyantābhidhānaṃ hi ! sāmānādhikaraṇyakṛtyam | tacca vastunastathāvidhatvāt || yadvā brahmaṇo'pi vastvaparicchedo nāma svabhāvato'paricchedaḥ, guṇānantyamiti phalitam | svataḥ iti ghaṭā divyāvṛttiḥ | ghaṭādayo hi ! svataḥ paricchinnāḥ | parataḥ iti | dharma bhūtajñānavyāvṛttiḥ | taddhi parādhīnasaṅkocam | evaṃ deśa kālaparicchedābhāva uktaḥ ||

2 vastuto'pi - vā. 3 sve- ā, i, vā.

1 katvameva brahmaṇa:- - na. kameva - tha.

tadevaṃ kāraṇādbhinnasya kāryasya satyatvāt, brahmakārya kṛtsnaṃ jagat brahmaṇo'nyadeva-

iti prāpte,

tadananyatvamārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ-

tasmāt — paramakāraṇāt brahmaṇaḥ, ananyatvaṃ jagataḥ ārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ, tat upapādayadbhayaḥ avagamyate | 'ārambhaṇa' śabda : ādiḥ yeṣāṃ vākyānāṃ tāni ārambhaṇaśabdādīni—
'vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ mṛttiketyeva satyam'
'sadeva somyedamagra āsīdekamevādvitīyam' 'tadaikṣata bahusyāṃ prajāyeyeti, tattejo'sṛjata' 'anena jīvenā''tmanā'nupraviśya'
'sanmūlā : somyemāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ sadāyatanāḥ satpratiṣṭhā : . aitadātmyamidaṃ sarva, tat satyaṃ sa ātmā tat tvamasi śvetaketo'

ityetāni | prakaraṇāntarasthānyapi evaṃjātīyakāni, atrā'bhipretāni ||

etāni hi ! vākyāni cidacidātmakasya jagataḥ parasmād brahmaṇo'nanyatva- mupapādayanti || tathāhi !
'stabdho'si ! uta tamādeśamaprākṣyaḥ 1 yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavati ;
amataṃ matamavijñātaṃ vijñātam '
ārambhaṇetyādi | pūrvavākye ekavijñānena sarvavijñānaṃ pratijñātam | tatra dṛṣṭānta- śvoktaḥ | uktadṛṣṭānte'pi vaiśeṣikamatena kāraṇāt kāryasyā'nyatvāt, ekavijñānena sarva- vijñānānupapattimāśaṅkaya, tadupapādayati ; 'vācārambhaṇa' mityādivākyam | ataḥ idaṃ sūtreṇo- pātamityarthaḥ | vācā iti ārambhaṇam ityapi padāntaram ; natu vācārambhaṇamityekaṃ padam, iti darśayituṃ, 'ārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ' ityuktam ; na tu 'vācādibhyaḥ' iti | yeṣāṃ vākyānāṃ tāni iti samāsasya ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣatvavyāvṛtyartham |

śabdādibhyaḥ iti, ādiśabdopāttāni vākyāni sadevetyādīni |

iti, kṛtsnasya jagataḥ brahmaikakāraṇatvam, kāraṇāt kāryasya ananyatvaṃ ca hṛdi nidhāya, kāraṇabhūtabrahmavijñānena kāryabhūtasya sarvasya vijñāne pratijñāte sati, kṛtsnasya brahmaika kāraṇatām ajānatā śiṣyeṇa,
'kathaṃ nu bhagavaḥ sa ādeśa: ?'

iti, anyajñānena anya' jñātatā' sambhavaṃ coditaḥ, jagato brahmaikakāraṇatām upadekṣyan, laukikapratītisiddhaṃ kāraṇāt kāryasya ananyatvaṃ tāvat,
'yathā somyaikena mṛtpiṇḍena sarva mṛnmayaṃ vijñātaṃ syāt ' iti darśayati | yathā ekamṛtpiṇḍārabdhānāṃ ghaṭa - śarāvādīnāṃ tasmādanatiriktadravyatathā tajjñānena jñātatā ; ityarthaḥ |

atra kāṇādavādana kāraṇāt kāryasya dravyāntaratvamāśaṅkaya, lokapratītyaiva kāraṇāt – kāryasya, 'ananyatāmupapādayati ;
'vācāssrambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ mṛttiketyeva satyam' iti | 'ārabhyate - ālabhyate spṛśyate' iti ārambhaṇam' |
'kṛtyalyuṭo bahulam'

kṛtsnasyetyādinā, eka vijñānena sarvavijñānaṃ pratijānato'bhiprāyaḥ | eka mṛtpiṇḍārabdhānāmiti, 'mṛnmaya' padaṃ vyācaṣṭe | tasmādana tajjñāneneti, vākyābhipretārthamāha | 'kasminnu.... vijñātaṃ bhavati' 'ātmani khalvare .... idaṃ sarva viditam' ityādiśrutyantarānuguṇyāt, dṛṣṭānta-dāntikasāmarthyācca, 'tajjñānena' iti vyākhyātam | na hi mṛtpiṇḍasattayā ghaṭa - śarāvādi jñātaṃ bhavati | na hi ! parabrahmasadbhāvāt sarva jagat vijñātaṃ ca bhavati ||

....

ārabhyata iti | 'ārambhaṇa' śabdasya upādānārthatvamayuktam ; kāryamidhyātvapakṣe- pratītimātreṇa sataḥ kāryasya vāgārabdhatvābhāvāt vācaḥ pūrvamapi pratīyamānatvāt ; pariṇāmapakṣe ca— brahmakāryasya jagataḥ śabdapariṇāmatvābhāvācca | 'ālambha: sparśa - hiṃsayo:' iti nighaṇṭu-

iti karmaṇi lyuṭ | vācā - vākpūrvakeṇa vyavahāreṇa hetunā, ityarthaḥ | 'ghaṭena udakamāhara' ityādivākpūrvako hi ! udakāharaṇādivyavahāraḥ | tasya vyavahārasya siddhaye, tenaiva mṛddravyeṇa, pṛthu 'budhnodara 'tvādilakṣaṇo vikāraḥ - saṃsthāna viśeṣaḥ, tatprayuktaṃ ca 'ghaṭaḥ' ityādināmadheyam, spṛśyate - udakāharaṇādivyavahāraviśeṣasiddhyartha mṛdddravyameva saṃsthānāntara - ' nāmadheyāntara' bhāg bhavati | ataḥ 'ghaṭādyapi mṛttikā' ityeva 'satyam - mṛttikādravyamityeva satyam - pramāṇenopalabhyate, ityarthaḥ ; na tu dravyāntaratvena |

|

ataḥ tasyaiva mṛddhiraṇyādeḥ dravyasya, saṃsthānāntarabhāktva' mātreṇa buddhi - śabdānta- rādayaḥ upapadyante ; yathā ekasyaiva devadattasya, avasthābhedai: 'bālaḥ, yuvā, sthavira : ' iti buddhi' śabdāntarādayaḥ, kāryaviśeṣāśca dṛśyante |
kāryasya kāraṇādananyatve utpatti vināśātmaka bhāvābhāvayorvirodhaśaṅkā-tatparihārau ]

yaduktam – satyāmeva mṛdi, 'ghaṭo naṣṭaḥ' iti vyavahārāt kāraṇādanyat kāryam - iti ; tat utpatti-vināśādīnāṃ kāraṇabhūtasyaiva dravyasya avasthā 'viśeṣatvābhyupagamādeva parihṛtam | tattadavasthasya tasyaiva' ( ekasyaiva ) dravyasya, te te śabdāḥ tāni tāni ca kāryāṇīti yuktam |

kārāḥ | nāmadheyasya mṛdārabdhatvābhāvāt vikāra nāmadheyayorubhayorapi, 'ārambhaṇa' śabda - vācyatvasiddhaye sparśārthatvena vyākhyātam | 'sarirasya' ityatra 'sarira 'śabdena yathā salilamucyate tadvat ra-layoḥ abhedo vivakṣitaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | hetuneti | hetutvaṃ ca prayojanatayā ; na tu sādhanatayā ityarthaḥ | ataḥ ghaṭādīti | 'āste, śete' ityādiṣu avasthāntareṇā'pi buddhi - śabdāntarādayo dṛśyante; 'ghaṭaḥ, paṭa:' ityādiṣu vastvantaratvena buddhi śabdāntarādayo dṛśyante atra tu avasthāntaranibandhanatve pratyabhijñā pratyakṣameva niścāyakamiti śrutirāha [ ityāśayaḥ | | pramāṇenopalabhyate iti, satyaśabdaṃ vyācaṣṭe | na tu iti evakāraṃ vyācaṣṭe | vikārāsatyatvaṃ vivakṣitaṃ cet ! iti karaṇavaiyarthyaṃ syāt |
yathā ekasyaiveti | bāla. yuvādiśabdāḥ śarīradvārā śarīriṇamabhidadhate | tasya ca naśvaratvābhāvāt, cetana paryanta buddhi śabdāntarādayaḥ avasthānibandhanā eveti || tattadavasthasyeti, kāryabhedaṃ pariharati | avasthā - anityadharmaḥ |

.

dravyasya tattadavasthatvaṃ, kārakavyāpārāyattamiti, tasya arthavattvam | abhivyaktyanu- bandhīni codyāni, tasyā anabhyupagamādeva parihṛtāni | utpatyabhyupagame'pi satkārya- vādo na virudhyate ; sata eva utpatteḥ |

tathā,

'sadeva somyedamagra āsīdekamevādvitīyam'

iti | sadevedam — idānīṃ vibhaktanāma-rūpatvena nānārūpaṃ jagat, 'agre', nāma- rūpavibhāgābhāvena ekamevā''sīt sarvaśaktitvena adhiṣṭhātrantarāsahatayā advitīya ceti, ananyatvameva upapāditam | tathā,
'tadaikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeya'
iti, srakṣyamāṇatejaḥprabhṛti' vividhavicitra' sthiratra sarūpajagacvena ātmano bahubhavanaṃ saṅkalpya jagatsargābhidhānāt kāryabhūtasya jagataḥ paramakāraṇāt parasmādbrahmaṇo'nanyatvam | avasīyate |
sacchandavācyasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ, sarvajñasya satyasaṅkalpasya niravadyasyaiva, 'sadevedam' iti nirdeśārhajagaccam, sacchandavācya' syaiva ' ' jagataḥ ' nāma-rūpavibhāgā-
tathā.... ananyatvamevopapāditam ityanvayaḥ | 'sat' śabdaḥ, pramāṇasambandhārhatā vadati | 'sadeva' ityavadhāraṇena, kadācidasatyatvaṃ vyāvartitam | 'ekameva ' iti nāma- rūpa vibhāgābhāva uktaḥ | ' advitīya' padena adhiṣṭhātrantaraniṣedhaḥ ityarthaḥ | vibhakta...jagat iti, vākyastha midaṃśabdaṃ vyācaṣṭe | upapāditamiti | eṣāṃ padānām | uktārthaparatvam, eka vijñānena sarvavijñānapratijñopapādanārthatvādeva avagamyate | na hi ! sajātīya- vijātīyā divyāvṛtyā, pratijñopapādanopayogaḥ | tathetyādinā, eṣāṃ padānāmuktārthaikaparatvam | tejaḥprabhṛtīti | saṅkalpānantaraṃ tejaḥ prabhṛtisṛṣṭipratipādanāt 'bahusyām' iti 'bahu' śabdaḥ, tejaḥ prabhṛtirūpeṇa bahutvaraḥ | avasīyata iti | kāraṇabhūtasyaiva brahmaṇa: bahubhavanasaṅkarūpābhidhānāt, kārya- kāraṇayorananyatvaṃ labdhamityāśayaḥ |

sacchandetyādinā, vyaṣṭisṛṣṭisaṅkalpavākyavyudasanīyaḥ śaṅkāviśeṣaḥ | sacchabda sya brahmaparasve, 'idaṃ' śabdavācyajagatsāmānādhikaraṇyamanupapannam iti śaṅkābhiprāyaḥ | sacchabda- vācyasyaiveti | sacchabdavācyasya jagatparatve, 'tadaikṣate' tyādibhiḥ virodhaḥ ityabhiprāyeṇa | nāmarūpeti cetanāṃśasyaikyānupapattimabhipretya |

bhāvena ekatvam, adhiṣṭhātranta' ranirapekṣatvam', punarapi tasyaiva vicitrasthiratra sarūpa- jagatvena vahubhava saṅkalparūpekṣaṇam, yathāsaṅkalpaṃ sargaśca kathamupapadyate 1 – ityāśaṅkayāha- 'seyaṃ devataikṣata; hantā'hamimā stisro devatāḥ anena jīvenā''tmanā'nupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇīti

'tāsāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtam'

ityādi | ' tisro devatāḥ | iti kṛtsnam acidvastu nirdiśya, svātmakajīvānu- praveśena etat vicitranāma-rūpabhāk karavāṇi' ityuktam | 'anena jīvenā''tmanā - " madātmaka jīvena, ātmatayā anupraviśya, etat, vicitranāma - rūpabhāka karavāṇi ' ityarthaḥ | svātmanaḥ 'jīvasya ca ' ātmatayā anupraveśakṛtaṃ nāma-rūpabhāktvam - ityuktaṃ bhavati |
'tat sṛṣṭvā | tadevā'nuprāviśat | tadanupraviśya | sacca tyaccā'bhavat '
iti śrutyantareṇa spaṣṭam – 'sajīvaṃ jagat pareṇa brahmagā ātmatayā anupraviṣṭam ' – iti |

|

tadetat-kāryāvasthasya ca kāraṇāvasthasya ca cidacidvastunaḥ, 'sakalasya sthūlasya' sūkṣmasya ca ' parabrahmaśarīratvam parasya ca brahmaṇaḥ ātmatvam, antaryāmi brāhmaṇādiṣu siddhaṃ, – smāritam | anena pūrvoktāśaṅkā nirastā ||

acidvastuni sajīve' brahmaṇi ātmatayā avasthite, nāma-rūpa vyākaraṇa vacanāt cidacidvastuśarīrakaṃ brahmaiva jagacchandavācyamiti, "sadevedam agre eka- mevāsssīt' ityādi sarvam, upapannataram | śarīrabhūtacidacidvastugatāḥ sarve vikārāśca
ātmatayā - niyantṛtayā | gaganādivyāvṛttiḥ | svātmanaḥ jīvasya ceti | 'siṃhena bhūtvā mayā''ttāḥ' itivat 'jīvenātmane' tyasyā'rthaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | 'madātmaka- jīvene 'tyukte, 'mṛdātmako ghaṭaḥ' itivat syāditi śaṅkā | ātmatayā anupraviṣṭamiti śrutyantareṇa spaṣṭamityanvayaḥ | pūrvoktāśaṅkā – 'idaṃ' śabdasāmānādhikaraṇyena brahmaṇi jagadgata- doṣāśaṅkā | upapannataram - mukhyameva iti bhāvaḥ ||

apuruṣārthāceti, brahmaṇo niravadyatvaṃ, kalyāṇa' guṇākaratvaṃ' ca susthitam | tadetat-
'adhikaṃ tu bhedanirdeśāt'
iti anantarameva vakṣyati | tathā-
'aitadātmyamidaṃ sarvam'
iti, kṛtsnasya cetanācetanasya brahmatādātmyamupadiśati | tadeva ca,

'tattvamasi'
iti nigamayati ||

atredaṃ tatvam - cidacidvastuśarīratayā 'tatprakāraṃ brahmaiva', sarvadā sarvaśabdābhi- dheyam | tat kadācit, svasmāt svaśarīratayā'pi pṛthagvyapadeśānarhasūkṣmadaśāpana- cidacidvastuśarīram ; tat kāraṇāvasthaṃ brahma | kadācicca vibhaktanāma-rūpavyavahārāī- " sthūladaśā' pannacidacidvastuśarīram | tacca kāryāvasthamiti, kāraṇāt parasmādrahmaṇaḥ kāryarūpaṃ jagat 'ananyat ||

śarīrabhūtacidacidvastunaḥ śarīriṇo brahmaṇaca, kāraṇāvasthāyāṃ kāryāvasthāyāṃ ca, śrutiśata siddhayā svabhāvavyavasthayā guṇadoṣa' vyavasthā ca',

'na tu dṛṣṭāntabhāvāt '

-ityatroktā ||

ye tu kārya-kāraṇayorananyatvaṃ, kāryasya mithyātvāśrayaṇena varṇayanti ; na teṣāṃ kārya-kāraṇayorananyatvaṃ sidhyati satya- mityārthayoḥ aikyānupapatteḥ | tathā sati brahmaṇo mithyātvaṃ jagataḥ satyatvaṃ vā syāt ||

ye ca - kāryamapi pāramārthikam abhyupayanta eva, jīva- brahmaṇoḥ aupādhika- manyatvam | svābhāvikaṃ ca ananyatvam acidrahmaṇostu dvayamapi svābhāvikam - iti vadanti ;
teṣām upādhi brahma vyatiriktavastvantarābhāvāt niravayavasya akhaṇḍitasya brahmaṇa eva upādhisambandhāt brahmasvarūpasyaiva heyākārapariṇāmāt ' ;
atredamiti | viśiṣṭāt kāraṇāt viśiṣṭaṃ kārya jāyata iti, śarīra- śarīribhāvādi- rupapadyate ityarthaḥ | 'brahmaṇa:, cidacitorutpatti-pralayaśravaṇāt kāraṇāt kāryasthāna- nyatvāt, kāraṇāvasthāyāṃ bahutvābhāvāt, śarīra- śarīribhāvasya anupapannatvena, svabhāvavyavasthā na syāt ' ||
yeceti | brahmaṇi doṣasambandhajñāpanāyaṃ niravayavatvā diviśeṣaṇam |

śaktipariṇāmābhyupagame – 'śakti- brahmaṇoḥ ananyatvācca e, jīva- brahmaṇoḥ karma- vaśyatvāpahatapāpmatvādivyavasthāvādinyaḥ, acidrahmaṇośca 'pariṇāmāpariṇāma vādinyaḥ śrutayaḥ, vyākulībhaveyuḥ ( vyākupyeyuḥ) ||

'brahma vā idamagra āsīdekameva

tadekaṃ sanna vyabhavat

yānyetāni devatrā' kṣatrāṇi |

tacchreyorūpa' matya sṛjata ' kṣatram

indro varuṇaḥ somo rudraḥ parjanyo yamo mṛtyurīśāna iti || 'ātmā vā idameka evāgra āsīt | nā'nyat kiṃcana miṣat |
sa aikṣata lokān nu sṛjā iti |'
'eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt ; na brahmā neśāno neme dyāvāpṛthivī na nakṣatrāṇi, nāpo nāgnirna somo na sūryaḥ | sa ekākī na ra ( meta) mate tasya dhyānāntaḥ sthasya'
ityādibhiḥ, 'paramakāraṇaṃ, sarveśvareśvaro nārāyaṇa eva' ityavagamyate

sad- brahmā''tmaśabdā hi ! 'tulya' prakaraṇasthāḥ, tattulyaprakaraṇasthena 'nārāyaṇa'- śabdena viśeṣitāḥ, tameva avagamayanti |
'tamīśvarāṇāṃ paramaṃ maheśva' ram'
'sa kāraṇaṃ karaṇādhipādhipo nacā'sya kaścijjanitā na cā'dhipaḥ' iti, īśvarasyaiva kāraṇatvaṃ śrūyate | smṛtirapi mānavī-

' tataḥ svayambhūrbhagavān' iti prakṛtya, 'so'bhidhyāya śarīrāt svāt sisṛkṣurvividhāḥ prajāḥ |
apa eva sasarjāissaau tāsu' vīryamapā' sṛjat ||'

sa sisṛkṣuḥ sahasraśādasṛjat puruṣān dvidhā || ' 'viṣṇoḥ sakāśādudbhūtaṃ jagat tatraiva ca sthitam' ityādiṣu |

naca - īśvaraḥ sanmātrameva - iti vaktuṃ śakyam; tasya tadaṃśatvābhyupagamāt, śeṣatvācca |

savi-

na ca - tasya jñānānandādyanantakalyāṇaguṇayogaḥ kādācitkaḥ - iti vaktuṃ śakyate ; teṣāṃ svābhāvikatvena sadātanatvāt ;
'parā'sya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñānabalakriyā ca' 'yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit'

ityādibhyaḥ |

'tamīśvarāṇāṃ paramaṃ maheśvaram '

upasaṃhāradarśanādhikaraṇam || 2.18 ||
162. upasaṃhāradarśanānneti cenna kṣīravaddhi || 2-1-24||

parasya brahmaṇaḥ sarvajñasya satyasaṅkalpasya, sthūla sūkṣmāvastha sarva cetanācetanavastu śarīra- tayā sarvaprakāratvena, 'sarvātmatvaṃ' sakaletara vilakṣaṇatvaṃ ca aviruddham - iti sthāpitam | idānīṃ - 'satya' saṅkalpasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ, saṅkalpamātreṇa vicitrajagatsṛṣṭiyogaḥ, aviruddha: -- iti sthāpyate ||

nanu ca – parimitaśaktīnāṃ kārakakalā popasaṃhārasāpekṣatva' darśanena sarvaśakta- brahmaṇaḥ kārakakalāpānupasaṃhāreṇa jagatkāraṇatvavirodhaḥ kathamāśaṅkayate ?
ucyate: loke tattatkāryajananaśaktiyuktasyā'pi tattadupakaraṇā 'pekṣitva' darśanāt,
parasyeti | satyasaṅkalpatvādibhiḥ vailakṣaṇyaṃ pratipāditam | tatra satyasaṅkalpamātra pari- karatvam anupapannamiti, tasya satyasaṅkalpatvasya saṅkucitavṛttitvaśaṅkāyā utthānāt saṅgatirityarthaḥ | sarvātmatvam – sarvabhāvaḥ | kiṃ brahmaṇo jagatkāraṇatvaṃ saṃbhavati ? na ? iti | kiṃ kāryajana- nasamarthasya kāryajanakatvaṃ, niyamena sahakāryantarāpekṣam ? uta na ? – iti vicāraḥ |
yadā apekṣāniyamaḥ ; tadā sahakāriśūnyasya kāraṇatvāsaṃbhavaḥ | yadā na apekṣā niyamaḥ tadā kāraṇatvasaṃbhavaḥ iti siddham |

ucyata iti | brahmaṇa: saṅkalpamātra parikaratvamayuktamiti, anutthāna codya parihārasaṅgrahaḥ |

sūtrārthaḥ ; upasaṃhāradarśanāt – kārya jananapravṛtteṣu, sāmagrīsaṃpādanasya loke dṛṣṭatvāt, neti cet — kevalasya brahmaṇa: sraṣṭṛtvaṃ na yujyate iti śaṅkanam, nana sādhu, kṣīravat hi- dadhyātmanā pariṇāme kevalasya kṣīrasyeva, brahmaṇaḥ tatsaṃbhavāt ||

'samarthepi hi sāmagrīdarśanena na kartṛtā |

165. śrutestu śabdamūlatvāt ||2-1-27||

166. ātmani caivaṃ vicitrāśca hi || 2-1-28||
kiṃ ca — evam - vastvantarasambandhino dharmasya vastvantare ca āropaṇe sati, acetane "ghaṭādau dṛṣṭā dharmāḥ tadvisajātīye cetane nitye ātmanyapi prasajyante | tadaprasaktiśca, bhāvasvabhāvavaicitryāt ityāha- 'vicitrāśva hi' – iti | yathā agni jalādīnām anyonya- trisajātīyānām, auṣṇyādiśaktayaśca visajātīyā dṛśyante ; tadvat lokadṛṣṭavisajātīye pare brahmaṇi, 'tatra tatrā' dṛṣṭāḥ sahasraśaḥ śaktayaḥ santīti, na kiṃcidanupapannam |

172. vaiṣamya nairghṛṇye na sāpekṣatvāt tathā hi ! darśayati || 2-1-34||

173. na karmā'vibhāgāditi cennānāditvādupapadyate cā'pyupa- labhyate ca || 2-1-35||

ukta — 'jagajanmādikāraṇaṃ paraṃ brahma' – iti | tatra parairuddhāvitāca doṣāḥ parihṛtāḥ |
idānīṃ - svapakṣarakṣaṇāya parapakṣāḥ pratikṣipyante : itarathā, kasyacinmandadhiyaḥ, teṣāṃ pakṣāṇāṃ yuktyābhāsamūlatāmajānataḥ, prāmāṇikatvaśaṅkayā 'vaidika 'pakṣe kiṃcit śraddhāvaikalyaṃ jāyetāspi | ataḥ parapakṣa pratikṣepāya 'anantaraḥ pādaḥ pravartate ||

kiṃ brahmakāraṇavādaḥ ekāntataḥ śraddheya : ? uta na ? iti | tadarthaṃ pradhānaṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ ? na? iti | kiṃ pradhānasya puruṣasaṃnidhānāt paṅgusaṃnidhānāt andhasyeva pravṛttiḥ saṃbhavati ? na ? iti | kiṃ andhasya paṅgusaṃnidhānāt mārgāvalokana vyāpāravat, puruṣa saṃnidhānāt tacchāyāpacyā vā jñātṛtvaṃ saṃbhavati ? na ? iti |
yadā jñātṛtvasaṃbhavaḥ, tadā pravṛttisaṃbhavāt pradhānakāraṇatvam upapannamiti ; na ekāntato brahmakāraṇavādaḥ śraddheyaḥ | yadā jñātṛtvāsaṃbhavaḥ, tadā pravṛttyasaṃbhavāt pradhānakāraṇavādānupapatteḥ, brahmakāraṇavādaḥ ekāntataḥ śraddheya iti sidhyati ||

kuta: 1 ; kāryatvāt | kāryasya hi ! sarvasya, ' tatsarūpāt ' kāraṇaviśeṣāt vibhāgaḥ ; tasminneva avibhāgaśca dṛśyate | yathā ghaṭa- makuṭādeḥ kāryasya, tatsarūpāt mṛt-suvarṇādeḥ kāraṇāt vibhāgaḥ; tasminneva ca avibhāgaḥ |
ataḥ viśvarūpasya - jagataḥ, tatsarūpāt pradhānādutpattiḥ, tasminneva layaśceti, pradhāna' kāraṇakameva jagat | guṇatrayasāmyarūpaṃ pradhānameva, jagatsarūpaṃ kāraṇam ; sattva- rajas-tamomaya- sukha-duḥkha - mohātmakatvāt jagataḥ | 'yathā' mṛdātmano ghaṭasya mṛddravyameva kāraṇam || tadeva hi ! tadutpatyākhyapravṛttiśaktimat ; tathā darśanāt ||
avyaktasya guṇasāmyarūpasya deśataḥ kālataśca aparimitasyaiva kāraṇatvam ; bhedānāṃ – mahadahaṅkāratanmātrādīnāṃ parimitatvāt, avagamyate | mahadādīni ca - ghaṭādivat parimitāni, kṛtsnajagadutpattau na prabhavanti | ataḥ triguṇaṃ jagat,
guṇatrayasāmyarūpapradhānaika' kāraṇakam ' - iti niścīyate ||

ato viśvarūpasyeti, pakṣadharmatābalāt siddhamarthamāha |

176. payo'mbuvaccet tatrā'pi || 2-2-2 ||
yaduktam – pradhānasya prājñānādhiṣṭhitasya vicitrajagadracanānupapattiḥ iti,

tanna ; yataḥ payoṃ'buvat pravṛttirupapadyate | payasastāvat dadhibhāvena pariṇama- mānasya ananyāpekṣasya, ādyaparispanda' prabhṛtipariṇāmaparamparā svata evopalabhyate | yathā ca vāridavimuktasya ambunaḥ ekarasasya, nārikela-tāla-cūta- kapittha- nimba- tintriṇyādivicitrarasarūpeṇa pariṇāmapravṛttiḥ, svata eva dṛśyate'; tathā pradhānasyā'pi pariṇāmasvabhāvasya anyānadhiṣṭhitasyaiva pratisargāvasthāyāṃ sadṛśapariṇāmenā'vasthitasya, savasthāyāṃ guṇavaiṣamyanimitta 'vicitra pariṇāmaḥ upapadyate | yathoktam —
'pariṇāmataḥ salilavat pratipratiguṇāśrayaviśeṣāt'
iti | tadevam, avyaktam ' ananyā pekṣa' pravartate iti cet !

ata uttaram — 'tatrāpi' - iti | yat kṣīrajalādi dṛṣṭāntatayā nidarśitam ; tatrā'pi prājñānadhiṣṭhāne pravṛttirnopapadyate | tadapi pūrvatra pakṣīkṛtam - ityabhiprāyaḥ |

'upasaṃhāradarśanānneti cenna kṣīravaddhi'

177. vyatirekānavasthiteścā'napekṣatvāt ||2-2-3 ||
itaśca, satyasaṅkalpeśvarādhiṣṭhānānapekṣapariṇāmitve, sarga vyatirekeṇa' - pratisargāva-- sthayā, anavasthitiprasaṅgācca, na prājñānādhiṣṭhitaṃ pradhānaṃ kāraṇam | prājñādhiṣṭhitatve, tasya satyasaṅkalpatvena sarga pratisarga 'vicitra' sṛṣṭivyavasthāsiddhiḥ |

na ca vācyam prājñādhiṣṭhitatve'pi tasya avāptasa mastakāmasya paripūrṇasya anavadhikātiśayānandasya niravadyasya nirañjanasya sarga - pratisarga vyavasthā hetvabhāvāt,
na prājñetyādi | śarīrarūyatirikta daṇḍa- cakrādisthānī yā cidrapaparikārantara nairapekṣyamukta- miti, avirodha ityarthaḥ || 2 ||

viṣamasṛṣṭau nirdayatvaprasaṅgācca, samāno'yaṃ doṣaḥ - iti ; paripūrṇasyāpi 'līlārtha'- pravṛtti saṃbhavāt sarvajñasya tasya pariṇāmaviśeṣāpanaprakṛtidarśanarūpa 'sarga-pratisargaviśeṣa'- hetoḥ sambhavāt ; kṣetrajñakarmaṇāmeva viṣama' sṛṣṭi vyavasthāpakatvācca |

nanvevaṃ-kṣetrajñapuṇyāpuṇyarūpakarmabhireva, sarvāḥ vyavasthāḥ sidhyantīti kṛtam īśvareṇā'dhiṣṭhātrā | puṇyāpuṇyarūpānuṣṭitakarma saṃskṛtā prakṛtireva, puruṣārthānurūpaṃ tathā- tathā vyavasthayā pariṇasyate | 'yathā' viṣādidūṣitānām annapānādīnām, 'auṣadha' - viśeṣāpyāyitānāṃ ca sukha duḥkhahetubhūtaḥ pariṇāma viśeṣaḥ, deśa-kālavyavasthayā dṛśyate |
ataḥ sarga- pratisargavyavasthā, devādiviṣamasṛṣṭiḥ, kaivalyavyavasthā ca sarvaprakāra- pariṇāmaśaktiyuktasya pradhānasyaivopapadyate iti' |

anabhijño bhavān' puṇyāpuṇyakarma' svarūpayoḥ | puṇyāpuṇyasvarūpe hi ! śāstraka- samadhigamye | śāstra ca, anādinidhanāvicchinna " pāṭhasaṃpradāyānāghrātapramādādidoṣa- gandhavedākhyākṣararāśiḥ | tacca – 'paramapuruṣārādhana - tadviparyayarūpe karmaṇī puṇyāpuṇye | tadanugraha - "nigrahāyatte" ca tatphale sukha-duḥkhe' – iti vadati | tathāsse " dramiḍā :- cāryaḥ-

sarvajñasyeti | sarga - pratisargavyavasthā hetu:, īśvareṇā'nusandhīyamānā prakṛteḥ pariṇāma- viśeṣāpattiḥ | na ca tayā svātantryahāniḥ pratyuta svātantryapūrti reva | pariṇāmaviśeṣāpattau satyāmeva, sargādivyāpārasya tatsaṅkalpāyattatvāt | yathāsaṅkalpamanuṣṭhānaṃ hi svātatryamitibhāvaḥ | deśavyavasthā - uṣṇavarjite vā śītavarjite vā vartamānasya pathyāpathyavibhāgaḥ |

iti,

'tānahaṃ dviṣataḥ krūrān saṃsāreṣu narādhamān |
kṣipāmyajasramaśubhānāsurīṣveva yoniṣu' ||
'iti ca' | sa bhagavān puruṣottamaḥ, avāptasamastakāmaḥ, 'sarvajñaḥ sarveśvaraḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ, svamāhātmyānuguṇalīlā pravṛttaḥ, 'etāni karmāṇi samīcīnāni, etāni asamīcīnāni' iti karmadvaividhyaṃ saṃvidhāya, tadupādānocitadehendriyādikaṃ tanniyamanaśaktiṃ ca sarveṣāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ sāmānyena " pradiśya, svaśāsanāvabodhi śāstraṃ ca pradarśya, tadupasaṃhārārthaṃ ca antarātmatayā anupraviśya, anumantṛtayā ca niyacchaṃstiṣṭhati |

kaivalyavyavasthā - mokṣavyavasthā | iṣṭāpūrte śrautasmārte karmaṇī | bāhuvidhyaṃ devatā - phalādi - bhedena | bhuvanasya nābhiḥ 'tadyathā rathasyā'reṣu nemirarpitā' ityādi śrutiprakriyayā, antaryāmi- rūpeṇa sarvādhārabhūtaḥ ityarthaḥ | bibharti – ārādhanatayā svīkaroti | yataḥ pravṛttirityādi bhagavadgītāvacanaṃ, pāpasya tadaparādharūpatvajñāpanārtham | 'teṣāṃ satatayuktānām' iti vacanasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt, atra nopāttam ||

bhagavāniti | nirdoṣatvaṃ maṅgalaguṇatvaṃ ca bhagavacchabdārthaḥ | tābhyāṃ vailakṣaṇyaṃ- puruṣottamaśabdārthaḥ | sarvajñatvādīni vividhaphalapradāna- sargādyupayogīni | etānīti | anādipuṇya-pāpavyavasthā tadīyānādisvātantrya nibandhanetyarthaḥ | tadupādāneti | yathā pitṛ- myāmutpādya vardhitaḥ putraḥ, śikṣitaḥ, artha- gṛha kṣetradānādinā upakṛtaḥ, tairevā'rthādibhiḥ yajñādikaṃ vā karoti ; viṣayacāpalaṃ vā anuvartate | na tatra pitrordoṣaḥ | tadaghīna vaibhavatvaṃ ca putrasya na hīyate tadvaditi bhāvaḥ | sāmānyena pradiśyeti | dehendriyādikaṃ sarveṣāṃ dadāti | vaiṣamyaṃ tu karmakṛtam, ityabhiprāyaḥ | anumantṛtvam — pravṛttasya pravartakatvam |

kṣetrajñāstu, tadā hitaśaktayaḥ tatpradiṣṭakaraṇa -kalevarādikāḥ, tadādhārāśca svayameva svecchānuguṇyena puṇyāpuṇyarūpe karmaṇī upādadate | tataśca puṇyarūpakarmakāriṇaṃ svaśāsanānu' vartinaṃ' jñātvā dharmārtha-kāma-mokṣaiḥ vardhayate | | śāsanātivartinaṃ ca, tadviparyayaiḥ yojayati | ataḥ svātantryādivaikalya codyāni, na avakāśaṃ labhante ||

dayā hi ! nāma, svārthanirapekṣā paraduḥkhāsahiṣṇutā | sā ca svaśāsanātivṛtti- vyavasāyinyapi vartamānā, na guṇāyā'vakalpate | pratyuta 'apuṃstvamevā'vahati | tannigraha eva, tatra guṇaḥ | anyathā, śatrunigrahādīnām aguṇatvaprasaṅgāt | 'svaśāsanā' tivṛtti- 'vyavasāya' nivṛttimātreṇa anādyanantakalpopacitadurviṣahānantāparādhānaṅgīkāreṇa, nirati- śaya sukhasaṃvṛddhaye svayameva prayatate | yathoktam-

'teṣāṃ satatayuktānāṃ bhajatāṃ prītipūrvakam |
dadāmi buddhiyoga taṃ yena māmupayānti te' || 'teṣāmevānukampārthamahamajñānajaṃ tamaḥ |
nāśayāmyātmabhāvasthā jñānadīpena bhāsvatā' |
iti | ataḥ prājñānādhiṣṭhita ' pradhānaṃ na kāraṇam' ||3||

tadāhiteti | anumantṛtayā niyantṛtvāt dhārakatvāt sāmānyena dehendriyādi- pradatvācca sarvaṃ tadadhīnamityabhiprāyaḥ | svayameveti | bhagavataḥ upekṣakatvāt | upekṣakatvamapi svātantryaviśeṣa evetyabhiprāyaḥ | svātantryādītyādiśabdena, dayā- sarvasamatvavaikalyaṃ jīvānāṃ phalānvayarāhityaṃ ca vivakṣitam | etatkarmakāriṇe etat phalaṃ dāsyāmīti tasya saṅkalpaḥ | yathāsaṅkalpaṃ kurvataḥ svātantryapauṣkalyameva |
svārthanirapekṣeti, upajīvinām upajīvyāpadi yat duḥkhāsahiṣṇutvaṃ, tadvyāvṛttyartham || prītipūrvakaṃ dadāmītyanvayaḥ | satatayuktānām iti āśaṃsāyāṃ ktena upāsakaprīteḥ uktatvāt | bhajatām iti paunaruktyam | anapekṣitakathanam apekṣitākathanaṃ ca syātām | ata iti | īśvarasya svātantrya vaikalyādiprasaṅgābhāvena, karmaṇāṃ tadārādhanarūpatayaiva phalapradatvāt | sargādivyavasthāsiddhyarthaṃ brahmekakāraṇatvaṃ siddhamityarthaḥ || 3 ||

atha syāt - yadyapi, 'prājñānādhiṣṭhitāyāḥ prakṛteḥ, parispanda' pravṛttirapi na saṃbhavati, ityuktam ; tathāpi, 'anapekṣāyā' eva pariṇāmapravṛttiḥ saṃbhavati, tathā darśanāt | dhenvādinā 'upayukta' hi ! tṛṇodakādi, svayameva kṣīrādyākāreṇa pariṇama- mānaṃ dṛśyate | ataḥ prakṛtirapi svayameva jagadākāreṇa pariṇasyate— 'iti' | tatrāha—
178. anyatrābhāvācca na tṛṇādivat || 2-2-4|| naitadupapadyate ; tṛṇādeḥ prājñānādhiṣṭhitasya, pariṇāmābhāvāt dṛṣṭāntāsiddheḥ | kathamasiddhiḥ 1 – anyatrā'bhāvāt | yadi hi tṛṇodakādikam 'anaidā chupa- yuktam", prahīṇaṃ vā kṣīrākāreṇa paryaṇasyata, 'tataḥ ' ' prājñānādhiṣṭhitameva pariṇamate' iti vaktumaśakṣyata | na caitadasti | ataḥ dhenvādyupayuktaṃ prājña eva kṣīrīkaroti |
'payo'mbuvaccet tatrā'pi'
ityuktameva, atra prapañcitaṃ ; tatraiva vyabhicārapradarśanāya || 4 ||
dhenviti | kevaladārvādivyāvṛttyarthaṃ citsaṃnidhimātramevopagantavyam ; na tu saṅkalpa- viśeṣavatpuruṣādhiṣṭhitatvam | gavi sutāyāmapi tadupayuktatṛṇādeḥ kṣīrādibhāvadarśanāt |

kevalācinmātrasya pariṇāme dṛṣṭāntatvaṃ dūṣitam | anaḍuditi, citsaṃnidhimātreṇa pariṇāmo dūṣitaḥ | prājñeti | saṃkalpaviśeṣānvitacetana viśeṣādhiṣṭhānena pariṇāmitvaṃ tu parasyā'niṣṭamityabhiprāya: || prapaścitam - uktaśaṅkā viśeṣa parihārarūpaṃ prapañcanam | tatraiva payodṛṣṭānta eva vyabhicāraparihārapradarśanāyetyarthaḥ || 4 ||

179. puruṣāśmavāditi cet tathā'pi || 2-2-5 ||

'athocyeta 'yadyapi, caitanyamātravapuḥ puruṣaḥ, niṣkriyaḥ ; pradhānamapi dṛkchakti- vikalam ; tathā'pi, puruṣasaṃnidhānāt, acetanaṃ pradhānaṃ pravartate tathā darśanāt | gamanaśakti vikalahakachaktiyuktapaṅgusaṃnidhānāt taccaitanyopakṛtaḥ dṛkchattivikalaḥ pravṛtti- śaktaḥ andhaḥ pravartate | ayaskāntāśmasaṃnidhānācca, ayaḥ pravartate | evaṃ prakṛti- puruṣasaṃyogakṛtaḥ jagatsargaḥ pravartate | yathoktam --
'puruṣasya darśanārtha kaivalyārtha tathā pradhānasya |
pavandhavadubhayorapi saṃyogastaskṛtaḥ sargaḥ ||'

|

i

iti | puruṣasya pradhānopabhogārtha kaivalyārthaṃ ca puruṣasaṃnidhānāt pradhānaṃ sargādau pravartate- ityarthaḥ |

atrottaram - ' tathāpi ' – iti | evamapi pradhānasya pravṛtyasambhavaḥ, tadavastha eva | paṅgoḥ gamanaśakti vikalasyā'pi, mārgadarśana - tadupadeśādayaḥ kādācitkāḥ viśeṣāḥ, sahasraśaḥ santi | andho'pi cetanaḥ san tadupadeśā' dyavagamena' pravartate | tathā ayaskāntamaṇerapi, ayaḥ samīpāgamanādayaḥ santi | puruṣasya tu niṣkriyasya, na tādṛśavikārāḥ sambhavanti | saṃnidhānamātrasya, nityatvena nityasargaprasaṅgaḥ, nityamuktatvena bandhābhāvaḥ, apavargābhāvaśca || 5 ||
'upabhoga' śabdena 'darśana' śabdo vyākhyātaḥ pradhānasya darśanāpekṣayā karmatvāt karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhīti bhāva: | pravṛtyasaṃbhava iti | pūrvāpara sūtrasthānupapattiśabdānurodhena adhyāhāraḥ kṛtaḥ | nityanirvikārasya puruṣasya, paṅgoḥ mārgadarśanopadeśādivat na kādācitkaviśeṣo'sti | andhasya tadupadeśāvagativat na prakṛterviśeṣo'sti | ayaskāntasya samīpāgamanādivat, puruṣasya na vikārasaṃbhavaḥ | vibhutvāt samīpāgamanāpekṣā na cet ! nityasargaprasaṃga : ityarthaḥ ||5||

pagurandhamayaskānto'yaḥ prayuṅkte yathā tathā |

181. anyathānumitau ca jñaśaktiviyogāt || 2-2-7 || dūṣita prakārā| tiriktaprakārāntareṇa pradhānānumitau ca, pradhānasya jñātṛtvaśaktiviyogāt,
guṇānāmityādi | evaṃ paroktānumānasya prājñādhiṣṭhitatvaprasaṅgamukhena viṣamāvasthatva- prasaṅgamukhena ca viśeṣavirodha uktaḥ || 6 ||
anyathā .... viyogāditi | anumānāntare'pi uktadūṣaṇamatidiśati | jñātṛtve sati hi ! saṅkalpāt adhiṣṭhātrantaranirapekṣapravṛttyupapattiḥ | tacca nāsti | tasmāt nityasargādiprasaṅga
