182. abhyupagame'pyarthābhāvāt || 2-2-8 ||
anumānena pradhānasiddhayabhyupagame'pi pradhānena prayojanābhāvāt na tat anumātavyam |
'puruṣasya darśanārtha kaivalyārthaṃ tathā pradhānasya'
iti, pradhānasya prayojanaṃ, 'puruṣabhogāpavargī abhimatau | tau ca na saṃbhavataḥ | puruṣasya caitanyamātravapuṣo niṣkriyasya nirvikārasya nirmalasya, tata eva nityamuktasvarūpasya, prakṛtidarśanarūpo bhogaḥ tadviyogarūpo'pavargaśca na saṃbhavati |

evaṃrūpasyaiva prakṛtisaṃnidhānāt tatpariṇāma viśeṣasukha-duḥkhadarśanarūpabhogasaṃbhāva- nāyām, prakṛtisaṃnidhānasya nityatvena, kadācidapi apavargo na setsyati || 8 ||
183. vipratiṣedhāccā'samañjasam || 2-2-9 ||
ityarthaḥ | acetanatvādeva prājñādhiṣṭhitatvaṃ prasajediti bhāvaḥ | idaṃ viṣamāvasthatva- prasaṅgasyāpi pradarśanārthamuktam || 7 ||
anumāneneti | abhyupagamaḥ kasya ? ityapekṣāyāṃ pūrvasūtrapratyāsatyā anumitireva arthato buddhisthetyabhiprāyaḥ | pravṛttistu aneka sūtravyavahitatvāt anubandhaṃ nāītīti || 8|| nityanirvikārasyā'pi bhogāpavargābhyupagamaśaṅkāyām anantarasūtram - vipratiṣedhāditi |

vipratiṣiddhaṃ cedaṃ sāṅkhyānāṃ darśanam | tathāhi !

prakṛteḥ parārthatvena dṛśyatvena bhogyatvena ca, prakṛteḥ bhoktāram adhiṣṭhātāraṃ draṣṭāraṃ sākṣiṇaṃ ca puruṣam abhyupagamya, prakṛtyaiva sādhanabhūtayā, tasya kaivalyamapi prāpyaṃ vadanta eva tasya nityanirvikāracaitanyamātra svarūpatayā akartṛtvaṃ kaivalyaṃ ca svarūpa- 'mevāsshuḥ ' | tata eva, bandhamokṣasādhanānuṣṭhānaṃ 'mokṣa' prakṛtereva – ityāhuḥ | evambhūtanirvikārodāsīnapuruṣasaṃnidhānāt prakṛteḥ, itaretarādhyāsena sargādipravṛttiṃ puruṣa- bhogāpavargārthatvaṃ cā''huḥ |

'saṅghātaparārthatvāt triguṇādi viparyayādadhiṣṭhānāt |
puruṣo'sti bhoktṛbhāvāt kaivalyārthapravṛtteśca || tasmācca viparyāsāt siddhaṃ sākṣitvamasya puruṣasya | kaivalyaṃ mādhyasthyaṃ draṣṭṛtvamakartṛbhāvaśca' ||

iti |

'puruṣavimokṣanimittaṃ tathā pravṛttiḥ pradhānasya' ityuktvā, evamāhu:-
'tasmānna bādhyate'ddhā na mucyate nā'pi saṃsarati kaścit | saṃsarati badhyate mucyate ca nānāśrayā prakṛtiḥ' ||

iti | tathā,

'tasmāt tatsaṃyogādacetanaṃ cetanāvadiva liṅgam |
guṇakartutve ca tathā karteva bhavatyudāsīnaḥ || puruṣasya darśanārtha kaivalyārtha tathā pradhānasya |
paṅgbandhavadubhayorapi saṃyogastatkṛtaḥ sargaḥ ||

sākṣitva- draṣṭṛtva bhoktṛtvādayaḥ, nityanirvikārasya akartuḥ udāsīnasya kaivalyaika- 'svarūpasya' na sambhavanti | evaṃrūpasya tasya, adhyāsamū (lo) labhramo'pi na saṃbhavati ; adhyāsa-bhramayorapi vikāratvāt | prakṛteśva tau na sambhavataḥ tayoḥ cetanadharmatvāt |

adhyāso hi nāma - cetanasya anyasmin anyadharmānusandhānam | 'saca' cetanadharmaḥ, vikāraśca | na ca - puruṣasya prakṛtisaṃnidhimātreṇa, adhyāsādayaḥ sambhavanti; nirvikāratvādeva | sambhavanti cet ! nityaṃ prasajyeran | saṃnidheḥ akiścitkaratvaṃ ca, 'na vilakṣaṇatvāt '

ityatra pratipāditam |

prakṛtireva saṃsarati badhyate mucyate cet ! kathaṃ 'nityamuktasya puruṣasya upakāriṇī sā' ityucyate ? vadanti hi !

'nānāvidhairupāyairupakāriṇyanupakāriṇaḥ puṃsaḥ |
guṇavatyaguṇasya sataḥ tasyārthamapārthakaṃ carati ||'
iti | tathā prakṛtiḥ yena puruṣeṇa yathāsvabhāvā dṛṣṭā; tasmāt puruṣāt tadānīmeva

nivartate iti cā''huḥ—

'raṅgasya darśayitvā nivartate nartakī yathā nṛttāt |
puruṣasya tathā''tmānaṃ prakāśasya vinivartate prakṛtiḥ ||
prakṛteḥ sukumārataraṃ na kiṃcidastīti me matirbhavati |
yā dṛṣṭā'smīti punarna darśanamupaiti puruṣasya ||'

kadācidapi paśyati, na adhyasyati ca | svayaṃ ca svātmānaṃ na paśyati ; acetanatvāt | puruṣasya svātmadarśanaṃ, svadarśanamiti nā'dhyasyati ca ; 'svayama 'cetanatvāt | puruṣasya ca, darśanarūpavikārāsambhavāt |
atha – 'saṃnidhimātrameva darśanam' ityucyate !; saṃnidheḥ nityatvena, nitya-

darśanaprasaṅgaḥ ityuktam |

svarūpātiriktakādācitkasaṃnidhirapi ; nityanirvikārasya nopapadyate |

- mokṣa hetustu sva' saṃnidhānarūpameva ' darśana cet ! ; bandhaheturapi tadeveti " nityavat bandho 'mokṣaśca syātām |

ayathādarśanaṃ bandhahetuḥ ; yathāvatsvarūpadarśanaṃ mokṣahetuḥ - iti cet ! ; ubhaya- vidhasyā'pi darśanasya, saṃnidhānarūpatānatirekāt sadā ubhayaprasaṅga eva |
saṃnidheḥ anityatve ; tasya heturanveṣaṇīyaḥ, tasyā'pīti anavasthā |
atha - etaddoṣaparijihīrṣayā svarūpasadbhāva eva saṃnidhiriti ! ; tadā svarūpasya nityatvena, nityavat 'bandha-mokṣau' |
ataḥ evamādervipratiṣedhāt, sāṃkhyānāṃ darśanam asamañjasam ||

yespi – 'kūṭastha nitya nirviśeṣasva prakāśacinmātraṃ brahma, avidyāsākṣitvena apāra-

mārthikabandha - mokṣabhāk -- iti vadanti sādyasambhavāt ', asāmañjasyameva |

teṣāmapi uktanītyā avidyā sākṣitvādhyā-

iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ - ' sāṅkhyāḥ janana- 'maraṇa' pratiniyamādi vyavasthāsiddhyarthaṃ, puruṣa bahutvamicchanti | te tu tadapi necchanti' - iti, sutarām asāmañjasyam ||
athetyādinā, sannidheḥ svarūpānatiriktatve, svarūpātiriktatve ca dūṣaṇam | ye'pītyādinā, asmin sāṅkhyapakṣanirā se māyāvādināmanavakāśatvamāha | uktanītyeti | nirvikārasya avidyānubhavādhyāsāyogādityarthaḥ |

iyāṃstu iti, adhikaṃ dūṣaṇam |

yattu – prakṛteḥ pāramarthyāpāramārthyavibhāgena vaiṣamyamuktam ;
tadayuktam ; 'pāramārthikatve'pi apāramārthikatve'pi', nitya' nirvikārakha' prakāśai- karasacinmātrasya svavyatiriktasākṣitvādyanupapatteḥ | apāramārthikatve tu tasyāḥ dṛśyatva- bādhyatvābhyupagamāt sutarām asaṅgatam |
aupādhika' meda' vāde'pi, upādhisambandhino brahmaṇaḥ, ayamena svabhāva iti, upādhisambandhādyanupapatteḥ, asāmañjasyaṃ pūrvamevoktam || 9 ||

184. mahaddīrghavadvā hrasva-parimaṇḍalābhyām || 2-2-10||

'asamañjasam' iti vartate | 'vā' śabdaḥ, cārthe | hrasva-parimaṇḍalābhyām — dvayaṇukaparamāṇubhyām, mahaddīrghavat – 'tryaṇuko ' tpattivādavat anyacca tadabhyupagataṃ sarvam, asamañjasam | paramāṇubhyaḥ dvayaṇukādikrameṇa jagadutpattivādavat, anyadapi asamañjasamityarthaḥ |

na ca vācyam - avayavālpatva- mahaccābhyāṃ hi ! sarṣapa mahīdharayorvaiṣamyam ; paramāṇorapi anantāvayavatve, avayavānantya sāmyāt sarpapa- mahīdharayoḥ vaiṣamyāsiddheḥ, avayavāpakarṣakāṣṭhā avaśyābhyupagamanīyā iti paramāṇūnāṃ pradeśabhedābhāve sati, ekaparamāṇuparimāṇā 'tirekī' prathimā na jāyeteti, sarpapa- mahīdharayoreva asiddheḥ ||

kiṃ kurmaḥ 1 iti cet ! ; " vaidikaḥ pakṣaḥ parigṛhyatām ||

THE

yattu paraiḥ - brahmakāraṇavādadūṣaṇaparihāraparam - idaṃ sūtraṃ vyākhyātam ;
tat, asaṅgataṃ punaruktaṃ ca | brahmakāraṇavāde paroktān doṣān, pūrvasmin pāde parihṛtya, parapakṣapratikṣepo hi ! asmin pāde kriyate | cetanādbrahmaṇo jagadutpatti- sambhavaśva, 'na vilakṣaṇatvāt ' ityatraiva prapaścitaḥ | ataḥ hakha pari-
na cetyādi | sarṣapa - mahīdharavaiṣamyasiddhyartha, paramāṇūnāṃ niravayavatvamāstheyam | anyathā abayavānantyasāmyāt, tayorvaiṣamyā siddherityarthaḥ | paramāṇūnāmiti | niravayavatve, sahasra- paramāṇu saṃyoge'pi ekaparamāṇvanatirikta parimāṇatayā, sarṣapa mahīdharākhyadharmya siddhe, paramāṇūnāṃ sāṃśatvamā stheyamityarthaḥ |
evaṃ vaiṣamyākhyadharmasiddhyarthaṃ niravayatvamuktam | sarṣapa - mahīdharākhyadharmisiddhyarthaṃ sāvayava- tvamuktam |
kiṃ kurma iti | sāvayavatve, vaiṣamyāsiddhiḥ | niravayavatve, dharmyasiddhiriti, kimabalambanīyamityarthaḥ| vaidika ityādi | yuktibā dhāgocaraśāstraikasamadhigamyārthaḥ parigṛhyatām | tatra hi ! na hyalpena vipulasyotpattiḥ | daśaguṇaṃ bṛhato brahmaṇaḥ kāraṇāt, alpasya kāryatvaṃ hi ! pratipādyate | tattu na yuktibādhagocara ityarthaḥ ||

maṇḍalābhyāṃ mahaddīrghāṇuhvasvotpattivat, anyacca tadabhyupagataṃ sarvam asamañjasam - ityeva

sūtrārthaḥ || 10 ||

185. ubhayathā'pi na karmā'tastadabhāvaḥ || 2-2-11 ||
paramāṇu kāraṇavāde hi ; paramāṇugata karmajanitatatsaṃyogapūrvakadvayaṇu kādikrameṇa, jagadutpattiriṣyate | tatra - nikhilajagadutpattikāraṇabhūtaparamāṇugatam ādyaṃ karma, adṛṣṭa- kāritam – ityabhyupagamyate ' ;
'agnerūrdhvajvalanaṃ, bāyostiryaggamanaṃ, aṇu- manasoścādyaṃ karma ityaṣṭakāritāni'
iti | tadidaṃ paramāṇugata karma, svagatādṛṣṭakāritam ? ātmagatādṛṣṭakāritaṃ vā 1
ubhayathā'pi na saṃbhavati kṣetrajñapuṇyapāpānuṣṭhānajanitasya adṛṣṭasya, paramāṇu- gatatvāsambhavāt | saṃbhave ca, 'sado'tpādakatvaprasaṅgaḥ | ātma' gatasya ca ' adṛṣṭasya, paramāṇu'gata' karmotpattihetutvaṃ na saṃbhavati ||
atra' hrasva-parimaṇḍalābhyāṃ 'mahaddīrghāṇuhasvotpattivat' ityukteḥ, kāraṇadvayopādānaṃ, kāryadvayo- pādānaṃ ca spaṣṭamuktam | dvyaṇukāt tryaṇukotpattiḥ paramāṇoḥ dvyaṇukotpattirivetyarthaḥ || 10 ||

ātmagatasya ceti | vyadhikaraṇatvādityarthaḥ |

atha aṣṭavadātmasaṃyogāt aṇuṣu karmotpattiḥ ; tadā tasya adṛṣṭa pravāhasya nityatvena, nityasargaprasaṅgaḥ ||
nanu - adṛṣṭaṃ vipākāpekṣa phalāyālam | kānicit adṛṣṭāni tadānīmeva 'vipacyante'| kānicit janmāntare | kānicit kalpāntare | ataḥ vipākāpekṣatvāt, na sarvadotpādakatvaprasaṅgaḥ iti

naitat ; anantaiḥ 'ātmabhiḥ, asaṅketa' pūrvakam ayugapadanu ṣṭhita neka' vidha' karma- janitānām adṛṣṭānām, ekasmin kāle 'ekarūpa 'vipākasya, aprāmāṇikatvāt | ata eva yugapat sarvasaṃhāraḥ, dviparārdhakālam avipākenā'vasthānaṃ ca na saṅgacchate |
na ca - īśvarecchā hita viśeṣādṛṣṭasaṃyogāt aṇuṣu karma; ānumānikeśvarāsiddheḥ, 'śāstrayonitvāt' ityatra upapāditatvāt | ataḥ jagadutpatteḥ aṇugata karmapūrvakatvābhāvaḥ || 11 ||

186. samavāyābhyupagamācca sāmyādanavasthiteḥ || 2-2-12 || samavāyābhyupagamācca asamañjasam |

vipākaḥ _phalā haidaśā |

na ceti | saṃyogaśabdaḥ paramparayā sambandhaparaḥ | adṛṣṭavadātmasaṃyogādityarthaḥ | ānumāniketi | siddhe'pīśvare tasya nityecchāyāḥ parābhyupagāt nityasargaprasaṅgasya, pūrvoktayā diśā sphuṭatvāt tadanuktiḥ || 11 ||

kutaḥ ? sāmyādanavasthiteḥ | samavāyasyāspi 'avayavi' jātiguṇavat, upapāda- kāntarāpekṣā sāmyāt, upapādakāntarasyā'pi tatheti, anavasthiteḥ asamañjasameva |

187. nityameva ca bhāvāt || 2-2-13||
samavāyasya sambandhatvāt, sambandhasya nityatve, sambandhino jagataśca nityameva

bhāvāt,

asamañjasam || 13 ||

bhyupagamāt, abhimatanityatva- sūkṣmatva- niravayatvādiviparyayeṇa anityatva-sthūlatva-sāvayava- tvādi 'prasajyate'; rūpādimatāṃ ghaṭādīnām anityatva- tathāvidhakāraṇāntarārabdhatvādi- darśanāt | na hi ! darśanānuguṇyena adṛṣṭo'rthaḥ kalpyamānaḥ, svābhimata' viśeṣe vyavasthāpayituṃ śakyaḥ | darśanānuguṇyena hi ! paramāṇūnāṃ rūpādimadhvaṃ tvayā kalpyate | ato'pi asamañjasam || 14 ||

nā'bhyupagamyate ;

atha – etaddoṣaparijihīrṣayā paramāṇūnāṃ rūpādimattvaṃ

tatrāha-

189. ubhayathā ca doṣāt || 2-2 - 15 ||
na kevalaṃ paramāṇūnāṃ rūpādimadhvābhyupagama eva doṣaḥ rūpādivirahe'pi kāraṇaguṇapūrvakatvāt kāryaguṇānāṃ pṛthivyādayo rūpādiśūnyāḥ syuḥ | ' tatpari'- | jihīrṣayā rūpādimaccābhyupagame, pūrvoktadoṣa iti, ubhayathā ca doṣāt asa- mañjasam || 15 ||

190. aparigrahāccā'tyantamanapekṣā || 2-2 - 16||
kāpilapakṣasya śrutinyāyavirodhaparityaktasyā'pi satkāryavādinā kvacidaśe vaidikaiḥ parigraho'sti | asya tu kāṇādapakṣasya, kenā'pyaṃśena aparigrahāt, anupa- pannatvācca atyantamanapekṣaiva, niḥśreyasārthibhiḥ kāryā || 16 ||

iti mahaddīrghādhikaraṇam || 2||

samudāyādhikaraṇam || 2-2-3 ||
191. samudāya ubhaya hetu ke'pi tadaprāptiḥ || 2-2-17||

paramāṇu kāraṇavādino vaiśeṣikāḥ - nirastāḥ |

saugatāśca, jagataḥ paramāṇukāraṇatvam abhyupagacchantīti, anantaraṃ tanmate'pi jagadutpatti tadvayavahārādikaṃ nopapadyate ityucyate |

te caturvidhāḥ - kecit pārthivāpyataijasavāyavīya paramāṇu saṅghātarūpān bhūta bhautikān bāhyān, citta- caittarūpāṃśca ābhyantarān arthān, pratyakṣānumānasiddhān abhyupayanti' | anyetu, "bāhyārthān sarvān pṛthivyādīn vijñānānumeyān vadanti | apare tu, 'arthaśūnyaṃ vijñānameva 'paramārthasat', bāhyārthastu svāmārtha-
paramāṇviti | paramāṇūnām ekāntatva- cāturvidhyābhyupagamasāmyāt dhīsthatayā bauddhamata- nirāsa iti saṅgatiḥ | āhatamate, sthiratvā sthiratvādibhiḥ anaikāntyam, aikavidhyaṃ ca paramāṇūnāṃ hi svīkṛtam |

te iti | vaibhāṣika sautrāntikayoḥ paramāṇukāraṇavādābhyupagamena prastāve sati, bauddhatvasājātyāt, yogācāra - mādhyamikayorapi nirāsa: kriyate ityarthaḥ |
kiṃ kṣaṇikāḥ paramāṇavaḥ, jagadārambhakā ? na ? iti | kṣaṇikānāṃ teṣāṃ, saṅghātakāraṇatvaṃ saṃbhavati ? na ? iti | asthirādiṣu sthiratvādidhīrūpā avidyā paramāṇūnāṃ pṛthivyādisaṅghātahetutvamupapādayati ? na ? iti ||
sarveṣāṃ kṣaṇikasvoktyā samudāyo'ṇumūlakaḥ |

kalpāḥ' ityāhuḥ | trayopyete, svābhyupagataṃ vastu', kṣaṇikamācakṣate | uktabhūta- bhautika-citta-caittavyatiriktam ātmākāśādikaṃ, svarūpeṇaiva nānu manvate | anye tu sarvaśūnyatvameva saṅgirante' ||

tatra ye bāhyārthāstitvavādinaḥ; te tāvat,

nirasyante | te ca evaṃ manyante -

'rūpa-rasa- sparśa- gandhasvabhāvāḥ pārthivāḥ paramāṇavaḥ

rūpa-rasa-sparśasvabhāvāśca

āpyāḥ ; rūpa - sparśa' svabhāvāśca taijasāḥ; sparśa ' svabhāvāśca' vāyavīyāḥ pṛthivya- tejovāyurūpeṇa saṃhanyante | tebhyaśca pṛthivyādibhyaḥ, śarīrendriya viṣayarūpasaṅghātāḥ | bhavanti | " tatra ca', śarīrāntarvartī grāhakābhi' mānārūḍhaḥ ' vijñānasantāna eva, ātmatvenā'vatiṣṭhate | tata eva sarvo laukiko vyavahāraḥ pravartate iti |

tatrābhidhīyate – 'samudāya ubhayahetuke'pi tadaprāptiḥ' – | yo'yam aṇuhetukaḥ pṛthivyādibhūtātmakaḥ samudāyaḥ, yaśca pṛthivyādihetukaḥ śarīrendriya viṣayarūpaḥ- samudāyaḥ ; tasmin ubhayahetuke'pi samudāye, tatprāptiḥ nopapadyate ; jagadātmaka- samudāyotpattiḥ nopapadyate ityarthaḥ |

paramāṇūnāṃ pṛthivyādibhūtānāṃ ca kṣaṇikatvābhyupagamāt kṣaṇa' dhvaṃsinaḥ ' paramāṇavaḥ, bhūtāni ca kadā saṃhatau vyāpriyante ? ' kadā vā saṃhanyante ? ' kadā vijñāna' viṣaya 'bhūtāḥ ? kadā ca hānopādānādivyavahārāspadatāṃ bhajante ? ko vā ' vijñānātmā', kaṃ ca viṣayaṃ spṛśati ? kaśca vijñānātmā, kamartha kadā vedayate ? kaṃ vā viditamarthaṃ kava kadā upādatte ? spaṣṭā hi naṣṭaḥ | spṛṣṭaśca naṣṭaḥ | tathā veditā viditaśca naṣṭaḥ | " kathaṃ ca anyena spṛṣṭam anyo vedayate ? kathaṃ ca anyena viditamartham anya upādatte ? santānānāmekatve'pi santānibhyaḥ teṣāṃ vastuto vastvantaratvānabhyupagamāt na tannibandhanaṃ vyavahārādikamupapadyate |
ahamartha eva ātmā ; sa ca jñātaiva-iti ca, upapāditaṃ purastāt || 17 ||
192. itaretarapratyayatvādupapannamiti 'cenna' saṅghāta- bhāvānimittatvāt || 2-2-18 ||

vidyādīnām itaretara hetutvena, upapannaṃ saṅghātabhāvādikam - iti cet !
vyāpriyanta iti | vyāpāra:- calanam | kiṃ śabdaḥ kṣepe | viṣayaṃ spṛśati- indriyeṇa sambaddhaṃ karoti | indriyavyāpāraḥ jñānaṃ hānopādānādi ca nopapadyate ityarthaḥ | kadā na kadācit iti bhāvaḥ | santānānāmiti | sthiratvena anuyāyitvena santānasya vastvantaratvānabhyupagamāt ityarthaḥ |
ahamartha eveti | vijñānasya ca ātmatvamayuktamityāśayaḥ || 17 ||
pratyayaśabdaḥ hetuvācī | saṅghātabhāvādikam iti 'ādi' śabdena, vijñānaviṣayatvaṃ vyavahārāspadatvaṃ ca vivakṣitam |

itaretara hetutvādavidyāderjagadbhavet |
iti cenna tathā'pyasyāḥ saṅghātaśve na hetutā ||

etaduktaṃ bhavati - yadyapi, kṣaṇikāḥ sarve bhāvāḥ; tathāpi, avidya' yā etatsarvam upapadyate | avidyā hi nāma viparītabuddhiḥ, kṣaṇikādiṣu sthiratvādi ' gocarā | tayā", saṃskārākhyāḥ rāga-dveṣādayo jāyante | tataḥ, cittābhijvalanarūpaṃ vijñānam tataśca, nāmā' khyāḥ citta'- caittāḥ | ' tataśca pṛthivyādikaṃ ca rūpidravyam | tataḥ ṣaḍāyatanākhyam indriyaṣaṭkam | tataḥ sparśākhyaḥ kāyaḥ | tato vedanādayaḥ | tataśca punarapi avidyādayo yathoktāḥ iti, anādiriyam avidyādikā anyonyamūlā cakraparivṛttiḥ | etacca sarva pṛthivyādibhūta - bhautikasaṅghātamantareṇa nopapadyate | ataḥ saṅghātabhāvādikamupapannam - iti ||
tatrottaram - na saṅghātabhāvānimittatvāt -- iti |
naitadupapadyate, 'eṣām' avidya/dīnām pṛthivyādibhūta- bhautikasaṅghātabhāvaṃ prati, animittatvāt | na khalu ! asthirādiṣu sthiratvādibuddhayātmikā avidyā, tannimittāḥ rāga-dveṣādayo vā arthāntarasya kṣaṇikasya saṃhatihetutāṃ pratipadyante |

śuktikārajatādibuddhirhi ! na śuktayādyartha saṃhatiheturbhavati |
kiṃ ca yasya kṣaṇike sthiratvabuddhiḥ saḥ tadaiva naṣṭa iti, kasya rāgādayaḥ utpadyante ? saṃskārāśrayaṃ', sthiram ekaṃ dravyam anabhyupagacchatām, saṃskārānuvṛttirapi na śakyā kalpayitum || 18 ||

193. uttarotpāde ca pūrvanirodhāt || 2-2-19 ||
itaśca, kṣaṇikatvapakṣe jagadutpattiḥ nopapadyate ; uttarakṣaṇotpattivelāyāṃ pūrvakṣaṇasya vinaṣṭatvāt, tasya uttarakṣaṇaṃ prati hetutvānupapatteḥ |

abhāvasya hetutve, sarva sarvadā utpadyeta |

atha-pūrvakṣaṇavartitvameva hetutvaṃ - ityucyate ; evaṃ tarhi kaśvideva ghaṭakṣaṇaḥ, taduttara- kālabhāvināṃ sarveṣāmeva go- mahiṣāśva kuḍya pāṣāṇādīnāṃ trailokyavartināṃ hetuḥ syāt | atha - ekajātīyasyaiva pūrvakṣaṇavartinaḥ hetutvamiṣyate ! tathāpi, sarvadeśavartināṃ uttarakṣaṇabhāvināṃ ghaṭānām, eka eva pūrvakṣaṇavartighaṭaḥ hetuḥ syāt 1

atha – 'ekasyaiva hetuḥ ekaḥ' iti manuṣe ! tathāpi, kasyaikasya ko hetu: 1 iti na jñāyate |
atha - ' yasmin deśe yo ghaṭakṣaṇaḥ sthitaḥ, taddeśasambandhina eva ' uttaraghaṭa'- kṣaṇasya sa hetu : ' iti ! kiṃ deśasya sthiratvaṃ manuṣe ?

kiṃ ca cakṣurādi' saṃprayukta 'syārthasya jñānotpattikāle anavasthitatvāt, na kasyacidarthasya jñānaviṣayatvaṃ sambhavati || 19 ||
śuktiketi | na hi ! śuktikādau rajatādibhrame sati, śuktikādvayaṃ saṃhataṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ || 18 ||

194. asati pratijñoparodho yaugapadyamanyathā ||| 2-2-20 ||
asatyapi hetau kāryamutpadyate - cet ! ; sarva sarvatra sarvadā utpadyeta – ityuktam | na kevalam utpattivirodha eva pratijñā ca bhavatām upa' rudhyeta '
'adhipati sahakāryā' lambana' samanantarapratyayāḥ catvāro vijñānotpattau hetavaḥ' iti, vaḥ pratijñā | adhipatiḥ -- indriyam |

|

pūrvakṣaṇapratyayaḥ |

195. pratisaṅkhyā'pratisaṅkhyānirodhāprāptiravicchedāt || 2-2-21 || evaṃ tāvat, asata utpattiḥ nirastā | sataḥ niranvaya vināśo'pi nopapadyate - ityucyate |
kṣaṇikatvavādibhiḥ— mudgara (bhighātādyanantarabhābitayā upalabdhiyogyaḥ sadṛśasantā- nāvasānarūpaḥ sthūlo yaḥ ; sadṛśasantāne pratikṣaṇabhāvī ca upalabdhyanahaḥ sūkṣmaśca yaḥ niranvayo vināśaḥ, "pratisaṅkhyā nirodhā 'pratisaṅkhyā nirodha' śabdābhyām - abhidhī yete " tau na saṃbhavataḥ ityarthaḥ |
kutaḥ ? ; avicchedāt- sataḥ niranvayavicchedāsaṃbhavāt | asambhavaśca-

'sataḥ utpatti - vināśau nāma, avasthāntarāpattireva | avasthāyogi tu dravyam ekameva sthiram' iti kāraṇādananyatvaṃ kāryasya upapādayadbhiḥ asmābhiḥ ', ' tadananyatvam' ityatra 'pratipāditaḥ |

nirvāṇasya dīpasya niranvayavināśadarśanāt, anyatrā'pi vināśaḥ, niranvayo'nu- mīyate – iti cet ! na ; ghaṭa-śarāvādau mṛdādidravyānuvṛtyupalabdhyā, 'sato dravyasya avasthāpattireva vināśaḥ' iti niścite sati, pradīpādau sūkṣmadaśāpadhyā'pi anupalambhopa- patteḥ tatrāpi avasthāntarāpattikalpanasyaiva yuktatvāt || 21 ||

tadananyatvamityatreti | vastvantarānupalabdheḥ, buddhi-śabdāntarādīnāmanyathopapatteścetyarthaḥ | nirvāṇasyeti | abādhitapratyabhijñayā mṛdanuvṛttau satyāṃ, na tadapahnavo yuktaḥ | dṛṣṭenādṛṣṭakarūpanaṃ hi yuktam | na tu dṛṣṭānamityarthaḥ || 21 ||

196. 'ubhayathā' ca doṣāt || 2-2-22 ||

kṣaṇikatvavādibhiḥ abhyupetā tucchādutpattiḥ ; utpannasya tucchatāpattiśca na sambhavati — ityuktam | tadubhayaprakārābhyupagatau doṣaśca bhavati tucchādutpattau tucchātmakameva kārya syāt | yaddhi yasmādutpadyate, tadātmakaṃ dṛṣṭam ; yathā mṛt-suvarṇā- derutpannaṃ 'maṇika’-mukuṭādi, mṛt suvarṇādyātmakaṃ dṛṣṭam |
na ca jagat, tucchātmakaṃ bhavadbhirabhyupagamyate na ca pratīyate | sato niranvayavināśe sati, ekakṣaṇādūrdhvaṃ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ tucchatāpattireva syāt | paścāt tucchāt jagadutpattau, anantaroktaṃ 'tucchātmakatva 'meva syāt | ataḥ, ubhayathā'pi doṣāt, na bhavaduktaprakārau utpatti nirodhau || 22 ||

197. ākāśe cā'viśeṣāt || 2-2-23 ||
na ca pratīyata iti | na tucchatvena pratīyata ityarthaḥ |

śrīmāṣyam 2-2-4]

'bāhyābhyantara' vastunaḥ sthiratvapratipādanāya, pratisaṅkhyā'pratisaṅkhyā nirodhayoḥ tuccharūpatā nirākṛtā | tatprasaṅgena, tābhyāṃ saha tucchatvena saugataiḥ parigaṇitasya ākāśasyā'pi tucchatā pratikṣipyate |
ākāśe ca nirupākhyatā na yuktā | bhāvarūpatvenā'bhyupagatapṛthivyādivat, ākāśa' syāspi abādhitapratītisiddhatvāviśeṣāt | pratīyate hi ! ākāśaḥ, 'atra śyenaḥ patati, atra gṛdhraḥ' iti śyenādipatanadeśatvena |

na ca – 'pṛthivyādyabhāvamātram ākāśaḥ' iti vaktuṃ śakyam ; vikalpāsahatvāt | * pṛthivyādeḥ " prāgabhāvaḥ ? pradhvaṃsābhāvaḥ ? itaretarābhāvaḥ ? atyantābhāvo vā ? ākāśaḥ | sarvathā'pi ākāśapratītyanupapattiḥ syāt |
prāgabhāva - pradhvaṃsābhāvayoḥ ākāśatve, pṛthivyādiṣu vartamāneṣu ākāśapratītya- yogāt, nirākāśaṃ jagat syāt |
itaretarābhāvasya ākāśatve'pi, itaretarābhāvasya tattadvastugatatvena teṣāmantarāle ākāśapratītirna syāt |
atyantābhāvastu pṛthivyādīnāṃ na sambhavati |

tatprasaṅgeneti | kṣaṇikatva dūṣaṇopayuktatvābhāvāt, prasaṅgāt saṅgatirityarthaḥ | tābhyāṃ saheti | 'ākāśa dvau nirodhau ca nityaṃ niyama.. saṃskṛtam' iti saha parigaṇitasyetyarthaḥ |
śyenādīti | 'atra' iti śyenapatanādhāratayā pratīyamānastu, na ālokābhāvaḥ ; 'atrā''lokaḥ ' ' atrā'ndhakāraḥ' iti ālokāndhakārādhāratayā pratīyamānasya, ālokābhāva- -rūpatvāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ |

na ca .... śakyamiti | nibiḍadravyābhāvarūpāvakāśa eva 'ākāśa' śabdārtha iti na vaktuṃ śakyamityarthaḥ | atyantābhāvastviti | keṣāṃcidutpatsyamānānāṃ prāgabhāvasya, keṣāṃ cidutpannānāṃ dhvaṃsasya ca satvena, sarveṣāmatyantābhāvāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ ]

abhāvasya vidyamānapadārthāvasthāviśeṣatvopapādanācca, ākāśasya abhāvarūpatve'pi, na nirupākhyatvam | aṇḍāntarvartinaśca ākāśasya, trivṛtkaraṇopadeśapradarśitapañcīkaraṇena rūpavaccāt, cākṣupatve'pi avirodhaḥ || 23 ||
198. anusmṛteśca ||2-2-24||

pūrvaprastuta, vastunaḥ sthiratvameva upapādyate | anusmaraṇam - pūrvānubhūtavastuviṣayaṃ jñānam ; pratyabhijñānamityarthaḥ | ' tadevedam' iti, sarva vastujātam atītakālānubhūtaṃ pratyabhijñāyate | na ca bhavadbhiḥ – jvālādiṣviva sādṛśyanibandhanaḥ ayam ekatva - vyāmohaḥ - iti vaktuṃ śakyam ; vyāmuhyataḥ jñāturekasya anabhyupagamāt | nahi ! 'anyā 'nubhūtena ekatvaṃ sādṛśyaṃ vā svānubhūtasya, anyaḥ anusandhatte | ataḥ bhinna- kālavastvāśrayasādṛśyānubhavanibandhanam ekatvavyāmohaṃ vadadbhiḥ jñāturekatvam avaśyā- śrayaṇīyam |
na ca jñeyeṣvapi ghaṭādiṣu, jvālādiṣviva bhedasādhana pramāṇam upalabhāmahe ; yena sādṛśyanibandhanāṃ pratyabhijñāṃ kalpayema ||
yadapi vedamucyate - pratyakṣānumānābhyāṃ ghaṭādeḥ kṣaṇikatvaṃ sidhyati |
pratyakṣaṃ tāvat vartamānārthaviṣayam, avartamānādvastuno vyāvṛttaṃ svaviṣayamava- gamayati ; nīlamiva pītāt | evaṃ ca bhūta-bhaviṣyadbhayāṃ vartamānasya vastvantaratvam, avagata bhavati |
cākṣuṣatve'pīti | anena anumeyatvamapyanumatamityavagamyate || 23 ||

'anusmṛti' śabdenā'bhidhānaṃ smṛtisādṛśyāt | jvālādiṣviveti | utpatti-

anumānamapi - arthakriyākāritvāt saccācca

ghaṭādi: kṣaṇikaḥ | yat

akṣaṇikaṃ śaśaviṣāṇādi, tat anarthakriyākāri asacca | tathā antyaghaṭakṣaṇasaccāt pūrva ghaṭakṣaṇa saccāni, vināśīni ghaṭakṣaṇasattvāt, antyaghaṭakṣaṇa sanvavat - iti; tacca, kāryakāraṇabhāvānupapacyādibhiḥ pūrvameva nirastam |
kiṃ ca - pratyakṣagamyā vartamānasya avartamānādvayāvṛttiḥ, 'na vartamānasya' vastvantaratvamavagamayati ; api tu vartamānakālayogitāmātram | na ca tāvatā vastvanta -, ratvaṃ sidhyati ; tasyaiva kālāntarayogasambhavāt ||

yattu - 'saccāt arthakriyā' kāritvācca" iti kṣaṇikatve hetudvayam - uktam, 'tat abhimata 'viparītasādhanatvāt viruddham | saccāt arthakriyākāritvādvā, ghaṭādi sthāsnu | yat asthāsnu, tat asat anarthakriyākāri ca yathā śaśaviṣāṇam - " ityapi hi ! vaktuṃ śakyam |
kiṃ ca - arthakriyākāritvam, akṣaṇikatvameva sādhayet | kṣaṇadhvaṃsino hi ! vyāpārāsambhavāt, arthakriyākāritvaṃ na sambhavati — ityuktam |
vināśasāmagryanuvṛttyabhāvādityarthaḥ [ antyaghaṭakṣaṇeti | antyaghaṭakṣaṇāt pūrvaghaṭa- kṣaṇāḥ, vināśinaḥ ghaṭakṣaṇatvāt, antyaghaṭakṣaṇavat iti | ghaṭakṣaṇa- tatsatvayorabhedāt satvamiti vyavahṛtam |
nirastamiti | tarkānugṛhītapratyabhijñāpratyakṣeṇa tarkaviruddhamanumānaṃ bādhitamityarthaḥ | tasyaiveti | yathā vastuno nīlatvotpalatvayogo na vastvantaratvasādhakaḥ ; tathā kāladvayayogo'pi saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ ||
tadityādi | viparyayeṇā'pi vyāptisadbhāvāt, viparītasyā'pi sādhakamityarthaḥ | savāditi, prakaraṇasamatvamuktam |
vyāpārāsambhavāditi | vyāpārasya hetutvam āśrayatvaṃ ca na saṃbhavati ; utpattyanantara- kṣaṇa eva vinaṣṭatvāt | ato na abhimatakāryakaratvamityarthaḥ | sabhī hitaviparyayasādhano viruddha ḥ ' iti tallakṣaṇam |

tathā, antyaghaṭakṣaṇasya hetuto 'nāśa' darśanāt, itare'pi ghaṭakṣaṇāḥ hetvapekṣa- vināśāḥ syuriti, ā-mudgarādi hetūpanipātāt sthāsnutvameva |

na ca vācyam ; na mudgarādayo vināśahetavaḥ api tu kapālādivisadṛśa- santānotpattihetavaḥ iti 'kapālatvāvasthā' pattireva' ghaṭādīnāṃ vināśaḥ ' – ityupa- pāditatvāt | kapālotpattivyatiriktatvābhyupagame'pi vināśasya, vināśahetutvameva mudgarādeḥ, ānantaryāt yuktam |

199. nā'sato'dṛṣṭatvāt || 2-2-25 ||
evaṃ tāvat - vaibhāṣika - sautrāntikayoḥ bāhyārthāstitvavādinoḥ sādhāraṇāni dūṣaṇāni - uktāni | tatra yaduktam' – 'samprayukta ' syārthasya jñānotpattikāle anavasthita- na kasyacidarthasya jñānaviṣayatvaṃ sambhavati iti ; tatra sautrāntikaḥ

tvāt, pratyavatiṣṭhate-

na jñānakāle " anavasthānamarthasya, 'jñānāviṣayatva' hetuḥ ; jñānotpattihetutvameva hi ! jñānaviṣayatvam | na ca etāvatā cakṣurādeḥ jñānaviṣayatvaprasaṅgaḥ ; svākāra- samarpaṇena jñānahetoreva jñānaviṣayatvābhyupagamāt | jñāne svākāraṃ samarpya vinaṣṭo'pyarthaḥ, jñānagatena nīlādyākāreṇa anumīyate | na ca ' pūrva-pūrva' jñānena 'uttarottara' jñānākāra- siddhiḥ ; nīlajñānasantatau pītajñānānutpattiprasaṅgāt | ataḥ arthakṛtameva jñāna- vaicitryam — iti |

atrocyate- 'nāsato'dṛṣṭatvāt ' - iti | yo'yaṃ jñāne nīlādirākāraḥ upalabhyate ; saḥ vinaṣṭasya asato'rthasya, ākāro bhavituṃ nārhati

kutaḥ ? adṛṣṭatvāt | na khalu ! dharmiṇi vinaṣṭe, taddharmasya arthāntare saṅkramaṇaṃ

dṛṣṭam | prativimbādikamapi sthirasyaiva bhavati | tatrā'pi na dharmamātrasya | ataḥ arthavaicitryakṛtaṃ jñānavaicitryam, arthasya jñānakāle avasthānādeva ' bhavati' || 25 ||
'punarapi sādhāraṇaṃ dūṣaṇamāha-

200. udāsīnānāmapi caivaṃ siddhiḥ || 2-2-26||

evaṃ kṣaṇika cvāsadutpatya hetukavināśādyabhyupagame, udāsīnānām - anuguñjānā- nāmapi sarvārthasiddhiḥ myāt | iṣṭaprāptiḥ aniṣṭa nivṛttirvā prayatnādibhiḥ sādhyate | kṣaṇadhvaṃse hi ! sarveṣāṃ bhāvānāṃ pūrva pūrva vastu tadgato' vā 'viśeṣaḥ, saṃskārārādiko vidyādirvā uttaratra na kaścidanuvartata iti, prayatnādibhiḥ sādhyaṃ na kiñcidasti | evaṃ sati, ahetusādhyatvātsarvasiddhīnām, udāsīnānāmapi aihikāmuṣmika' phalaṃ mokṣaśca sidhyet || 26 ||

iti samudāyādhikaraṇam || 3 ||

prati vimbādikamiti | 'ādi' śabdena, sphaṭike japākusumasya chāyāpattirvivakṣitā | tatrāpīti | na kevalaṃ dharmamātrasya pratibimbo dṛṣṭaḥ ; dharmiṇa eva tu dṛṣṭaḥ ityarthaḥ || 25 ||
saṃskārādiḥ svargādihetubhūtā viśeṣaḥ | vidyādiḥ - mokṣaheturviśeṣaḥ || 26||

upalabdhyadhikaraṇam ||2-2--4 || sū. 27-29
201. nā'bhāva upalabdheḥ || 2-2-27 |
vijñānamātrāstitvavādino yogācārāḥ pratyavatiṣṭhante |

yaduktam — arthavaicitryakṛtaṃ jñānavaicitryam - iti; tannopapadyate ; arthavat jñānā- nāmeva ' sākārāṇāṃ svayameva vicitratvāt | tacca svarūpavaicitryaṃ, vāsanāvaśādeva upapadyate | vāsanā – vilakṣaṇapratyayapravāha eva | 'yad ghaṭākāra' jñānaṃ kapālākāra- jñānasya utpādakaṃ, tasya tathāvidhasyotpādakaṃ, tatpūrvaghaṭajñānam | tasya ca tathāvidha' syo'- tpādakaṃ tataḥ pūrvaghaṭajñānam' ityevaṃrūpaḥ pravāha eva, vāsanetyucyate |

nābhāva iti | kim arthaśūnyajñānavāde jagadutpattiḥ saṃbhavati ? na ? iti | sa pakṣaḥ samīcīnayuktimūla: ? na ? iti | ghaṭādyākāraḥ, jñānākāra : ? uta arthākāraḥ ? iti | kim arthāsādhāraṇyaṃ nāma, tattadarthasārūpyam ? uta arthasambandha: ? iti | kiṃ ghaṭādyākārātirekeṇa arthavyavahārānuguṇyaṃ nāma jñānākāra:, bhāsate ? na vā ? iti |
yadā ākārāntaraṃ na bhāsate; yadā ca arthasārūpyalakṣaṇam arthāsādhāraṇyam; sadā ghaṭādyākāraḥ, vijñānākāra iti, vijñānamātravādasya samyabhyuktimūlatvāt, tatra jagadudayasambhavaḥ | yadā jñānasya tattadarthavyavahārānuguṇyam ākārāntaraṃ bhāsate; yadā ca arthāsādhāraṇyaṃ tatsambandha- lakṣaṇam ; tadā ghaṭādyākārasya jñānākāratvābhāvāt yogācāramatasya yuktiśūnyatayā, tatpakṣe na jagadutpattisambhavaḥ | [ iti pūrvottarapakṣayoḥ phala-phalibhāvau |
yad ghaṭākāreti | vijātīyajñānasantāneṣu caramajñānaṃ vijātīyotpādanaśaktamityarthaḥ | yathā arthābhyupagame'pi vijātīyaghaṭādeḥ vijātīyakapālādyutpādakatvam ; tatheti bhāvaḥ |

kathaṃ-bahiṣṭhasarṣapa-mahīdharā (di) derākāraḥ, āntarasya jñāna ' syaivetyucyate ' 1
ittham ; arthasyā'pi vyavahārayogyatvaṃ jñānaprakāśāyattam ; anyathā, svapara- vedyayoḥ anatiśayaprasaṅgāt | prakāśamānasya ca jñānasya, sākāratvam avaśyāśraya- ṇīyam ; nirākārasya prakāśāyogāt | ekaśca ayamākāra upalabhyamānaḥ, jñānasyaiva | tasya ca bahirvadavabhāso'pi bhramakṛtaḥ | jñānārthayoḥ sahopalambhaniyamācca, jñānādavyati- riktaḥ arthaḥ |

kiṃ ca – bāhyamartham abhyupayadbhirapi, ghaṭa- paṭādivijñāneṣu jñānasya tattadarthā- sādhāraṇyaṃ, tattadarthasārūpyamantareṇa nopapadyate iti, avaśyaṃ jñāne 'arthasarūpaṃ rūpamā- stheyam || tāvataiva sarvavyavahāropapatteḥ tadvayatiriktārthakalpanā niprāmāṇikā | ataḥ vijñānamātrameva tattvam ; na bāhyārtho'sti – iti |

|

evaṃ prāpte, pracakṣmahe – 'nāḍabhāva upalabdheḥ ' - iti | "jñānātiriktasya arthasya, abhāvo vaktuṃ na śakyate |

kutaḥ 1 upalabdheḥ – jñāturātmanaḥ arthaviśeṣavyavahārayogyatāpādanarūpeṇa jñānasyopa- labdheḥ | eva ( mi ) meva hi ! sarve laukikāḥ pratiyanti'; 'ghaṭamahaṃ jānāmi' iti |
jñānārthayoriti | dvicandrajñāne candradvaya sahopalambhaniyamāddhi ! dvitīyacandrasya candrāda- vyatiriktatvam ; tadvaditi bhāvaḥ |

kiṃ cetyādi | jñānānāmeva vāsanāvaśāt vaicitryopapatteḥ, caramajñānasyaiva arthavat vijātīyotpādanaśaktatvācca jñānānāṃ sākāratayaiva prakāśamānatvena ākāradvayānupalabdhyā jñānasyaiva tattadākārabhākatvācca, sahopalambhaniyamācca, bāhyārthābhyupagame'pi jñānasya tattadarthā- sādhāraṇyasya tattadarthasārūpyanibandhanatvācca tāvataiva sarvavyavahāropapatteśca jñānamātrameva tattvam— ityarthaḥ |
jñāturā.... rūpeṇeti | anena 'nirākārasya prakāśā yogāt' iti hetuḥ pratyuktaḥ ; 'na nīlādiḥ jñānākāraḥ | tattannīlādyarthavyavahārānuguṇyameva jñānasyākāraḥ' ityasyārthasya uktatvāt ||

jñānavyati - vā. 4 pratīyante - i.

evaṃrūpeṇa sakarmakeṇa sakartṛkeṇa jñādhātvarthena, sarvalokasākṣikam aparokṣamavabhāsamānenaiva, 'jñānamātrameva paramārtha:' iti sādhayantaḥ sarvaloko paha | sopakaraṇaṃ 'bhavantīti', vedavāda- cchadmapracchanna bauddha nirākaraṇe nipuṇataraṃ prapaścitam ||

yattu - 'sahopalambhaniyamādabhedo nīla-taddhiyoḥ' – iti ; tat svavacanaviruddham ; sāhityasya arthabhedahetukatvāt | tadarthavyavahāra yogyataika svarūpasya "jñānasya, 'tena saholambhaniyamaḥ, tasmādavailakṣaṇyasādhanam' iti ca hāsyam |

niranvayavināśināṃ jñānānām anuvartamānasthira| kāra virahāt, vāsanā ca durupa- pādā| vinaṣṭena pūrvajñānena, anutpannam uttarajñānaṃ kathaṃ vāsyate ? ataḥ jñānavaicitryamapi artha vaicitryakṛtameva |

' tattadartha' vyavahāra yogyatāpādanarūpatayā sākṣātpratīya' mānasya jñānasya', tattadartha- sambandhāyattaṃ tattadasādhāraṇyam | sambandhaśca saṃyogalakṣaṇaḥ | jñānamapi hi ! dravyameva | prabhādravyasya pradīpaguṇabhūtasyeva jñānasyā'pi ātmaguṇabhūtasya, dravyatvamaviruddham - ityuktam | ataḥ na bāhmārthābhāvaḥ || 27 ||

202. vaidharmyācca na svapnādivat || 2-2-28 || svapnajñānavaidharmyāt, jāgaritajñānānām arthaśūnyatvaṃ na yujyate vaktum | svapnajñānāni hi !, 'nidrādidoṣaduṣṭa ' karaṇajanyāni, bādhitāni ca | jāgaritajñānāni tu, tadviparītānīti, teṣāṃ na tatsāmyam |

|

sarveṣāṃ ca jñānānām arthaśūnyatve, bhavadbhiḥ sādhyo'pyartho na sidhyati ; nirālamba' nānumāna' syā'pi arthaśūnyatvāt | tasyā'rthavatve, jñānatvasya anaikāntyāt, sutarām arthaśūnyatvāsiddhiḥ || 28 ||

203. na bhāvo'nupalabdheḥ || 2-2-29 ||
* na kevalasya arthaśūnyasya jñānasya bhāvaḥ sambhavati

kutaḥ 1 kvacidapyanupalabdheḥ | na hi ! akartṛkasya akarmakasya vā jñānasya "kvacidupalabdhiḥ' | svapna' jñānādiṣvapi na arthaśūnyatvamiti, khyāti nirūpaṇe pratipāditam || 29 ||

iti upalabdhyadhikaraṇam || 4 ||

kvacidapīti | suṣuptau kevaladharmisphuraṇameva ; na dharmabhūtajñānasyetyabhiprāyaḥ || 29||

svapnādivajjāgaritajñānasyā'pi na yujyate |
mithyātvaṃ, doṣarāhityavaidhamrmyādeva sidhyati ||

jñānasya cā'rthaśūnyasya sadbhāvo naiva vidyate |
karmakartṛviyuktasya jñānasyā'nupalabdhitaḥ ||
iti upalabdhyadhikaraṇam || 4 ||

• kvacidapyupalabdhiḥ - da.

sarvathānupapaśyadhikaraṇam || 225 || 204. sarvathā'nupapatteśca || 2-2-30 ||

atra 'sarvaśūnya' vādī mādhyamikaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate | śūnyavāda eva hi ! sugatamata- kāṣṭhā | śiṣyabuddhiyogyatānuguṇyena arthābhyupagamādinā kṣaṇikatvādaya uktāḥ |
vijñānaṃ, bāhyārthāca 'sarve na santi | śūnyameva taccam | abhāvāpattireva ca mokṣaḥ - ityeva buddhasyā'bhiprāyaḥ | tadeva hi ! yuktam ; śūnyasya ahetusādhyatayā, svataḥ siddheḥ | sata eva hi ! heturanveṣaṇīyaḥ | tacca sat, bhāvāt abhāvācca 'notpadyate | bhāvāt tāvat na kasyacit utpattirdṛṣṭā | na hi ! ghaṭādiḥ, anupamṛdite piṇḍādike jāyate |
nā'pi, abhāvādutpattiḥ sambhavati | naṣṭe piṇḍādike hi ! abhāvādutpadyamānaṃ ' ghaṭādikam, abhāvātmakameva syāt |
tathā, svataḥ parataśca utpattirna sambhavati ; svataḥ svotpattau, ātmāśrayadoṣaprasaṅgāt, prayojanābhāvācca | parataḥ parotpattau paratvāviśeṣāt sarveṣāṃ sarvebhya utpattiprasaṅgaḥ |

|

sarvatheti | kiṃ mādhyamikenoktaḥ sarvaśūnyavādaḥ saṃbhavati ? na ? iti | kiṃ sarva kārya bhāvarūpam ? uta abhāvarūpam ? iti | kiṃ sarvakāryeṣvapi bhāva eva kāraṇam ? uta abhāva : ? iti | kiṃ bhāvāntaram, abhāvarūpam ? uta tucchaṃ nāma arthāntaram ! |
yadā tucchaṃ nāma arthāntaram abhāva:; tadā sarvakāryeṣvabhāva evaṃ kāraṇamiti, kāryasyāpyabhāvātmakatayā, śūnyavāda upapannaḥ syāt | yadā bhāvāntaram abhāva:, tadā sarvakāryeṣu bhāva eva kāraṇamiti, kāryasyāpi bhāvarūpatvāt sarvaśūnyavādaḥ anupapannaḥ syāt | ahetviti | sato hetvabhāvādeva śūnyasiddhiḥ ; na tu sādhaka hetvantarāpekṣayetyarthaḥ | na hīti | vināśādeva jāyate ityarthaḥ | prayojanābhāvaḥ – svayaṃ vidyamānatvāt utpattinairapekṣyam ||

sarvaśūnyatvavādaśca na sidhyati bhavanmate |
sarvathā'nupapatteśca pramāṇādivirodhataḥ ||
1 sarvaśūnyatva - ā, i,ga,va. • na- tha. 3 nopapadyate - ga.da.

janmābhāvādeva, vināśasyā'pyabhāvaḥ | ataḥ śūnyameva tatvam || ataḥ janma- vināśa - sadasadādayaḥ, bhrāntimātram |

na ca - niradhiṣṭhānabhramāsambhavāt bhramādhiṣṭhānaṃ kiṃcit pāramārthikaṃ tattvam āśrayitavyam ; doṣa-doṣāśrayatva jñātṛtvādyapāramārthye'pi bhramotpattivat, adhiṣṭhānā- pāramārthye'pi bhramopapatteḥ | ataḥ śūnyameva tattvam ||

iti prāpte, ucyate – 'sarvathā'nupapatteśva' iti | sarvathā anupapatteḥ, sarva- śūnyatvaṃ ca bhavadabhipretaṃ na saṃbhavati |

kiṃ bhavān, 'sarva sat' iti vā pratijānīte ? asaditi vā 1 anyathā vā 1 sarvathā, tatrā'bhipreta tucchatvaṃ na saṃbhavati loke bhāvābhāvaśabdayoḥ tatpratītyośca, vidyamānasyaiva vastunaḥ avasthāviśeṣagocaratvasya pratipāditatvāt | ataḥ 'sarva śūnyam' iti pratijānatā, 'sarva sat' iti pratijānateva sarvasya vidyamānasya avasthāviśeṣa- 'yogitaiva' pratijñātā bhavatīti, bhavadabhimatā tucchatā, na kutaścidapi sidhyati |

'kiṃ ca - kutaścit pramāṇāt śūnyatvamupalabhya, 'śūnyatvaṃ siṣādhayiṣatā, tasya pramāṇasya "satyatvam' abhyupetyam | tasya " asatyatve,' sarva satyaṃ syāditi, sarvathā sarvaśūnyatvaṃ ca anupapannam || 30 ||

iti sarvathā'nupapattyadhikaraṇam || 5 ||
sarvathā śabdena pramāṇavikalpānupapattiḥ prameyavikalpānupapattiśca vivakṣitā |

210. adhiṣṭhānānupapatteśca || 2-2-36 ||
vedabāhyānām, anumānāddhi ! kevalanimiteśvarakalpanā tathā sati dṛṣṭānu- vākasya tu, upāsanavidhyādyanyaparatvābhāvāt upāsya viśeṣaika nirṇayaparatvamasti | ataḥ tadanu- guṇārthatvaṃ, śambhurākāśetyādivākyānāmabhyupagantavyamityarthaḥ | eko ha vā iti | sabrahmā''tma- śabdeṣu pūrvapūrve sāmānyaśabdā: ; uttarottare viśeṣaśabdāḥ | evaṃ nārāyaṇaśabdaṃ prati śambhu- śivādayaḥ sāmānyaśabdāḥ | tasmāt 'paśunā yajete 'tyatra 'chāgo vā mantravarṇāt ' iti nyāyāt viśeṣaparyavasānavat, sad-brahmā''tmādiśabdānāṃ nārāyaṇarūpaviśeṣaparyavasāyitvaṃ yuktamityarthaḥ || pratipāditamiti | 'yato vā imānī' tyā divākye asyārthasyā'bhipretattvāt, sūtrakā reṇā'pi tathā'bhipretamityarthaḥ | janmādisūtrabhāṣyeṇa 'parasmāt puṃsaḥ' ityantena ayamarthaḥ sūcitaḥ || 35 ||
adhīti | pauruṣeyavākyasya pramāṇamūlatve bubhutsite sati, atīndriyārthe śrutimūlatvam anumānamūlatvaṃ vā saṃbhavet | tatra śrutimūlatvaṃ śrutiviruddhaparā- varatastvapratipādanapradarśanāt apāstamiti, anumānamūlatvanirāsaparaṃ sūtratrayam | vedavādyānāmiti, anupapatteśca – aśarīre

aśarīrasyeśvarasya jagatsṛṣṭirna sidhyati |
kulālavadadhiṣṭhānānupapattervirodhata: ||

sāreṇa kulālādivat adhiṣṭhānaṃ kartavyam | na ca ' -kulālādeḥ mṛdādyadhiṣṭhānavat, paśupateḥ nimittabhūtasya pradhānādhiṣṭhānamupapadyate ; aśarīratvāt | saśarīrāṇāmeva hi ! kulālā dīnām, adhiṣṭhānaśaktirdṛṣṭā |

na ca - īśvarasya saśarīratvam abhyupagantavyam ; taccharīrasya sāvayavasya nityatve

anityatve ca,

'śāstrayonitvāt'

ityatra doṣasya uktatvāt || 36 ||

211. karaṇavacenna bhogādibhyaḥ || 2-2-37||

yathā, bhokturjīvasya karaṇa- kalevarādyadhiṣṭhānam, aśarīrasyaiva dṛśyate tadvat, maheśvarasyā'pi aśarīrasya ca pradhānādhiṣṭhānam upapadyate iti cet !
na; bhogādibhyaḥ | puṇya-pāparūpakarmaphalabhogārtha puṇya-pāparūpādṛṣṭakāritaṃ hi ! tadadhiṣṭhānam | tadvat paśupaterapi puṇya pāparūpādṛṣṭavattayā tatphalabhogādi sarvaṃ prasajyeta | ataḥ 'na adhiṣṭhānasambhavaḥ || 37 ||

212. antavattvamasarvajñatā vā || 2-2-38 ||
'vā' śabdaḥ, cārthe | paśupateḥ puṇya-pāparūpādṛṣṭavacce, jīvavat antavactraṃ sṛṣṭi- saṃhārādyāspadatvam, asarvajñatā ca syāditi, anādaraṇīyameva idaṃ matam |
'virodhetyanapekṣaṃ syāt ' ( pū. mī.

sū. 1-3-3)

ityādinā, veda viruddhasya anādaraṇīyatve siddhe'pi, paśupatimatasya vedaviruddhatākhyāpanārthaṃ 'patyurasāmañjasyāt' iti punarārambhaḥ |

yadyapi, pāśupata - śaivayoḥ, vedāvirodhina iva kecana dharmāḥ pratīyante vedaviruddhanimittopādānabhedakalpanā- 'parāvaratacca 'vyatyayakalpanā mūlatvāt,
sarvam asamañjasameveti, ' asamañjasyāt' ityuktam || 38 ||

tathā'pi,

iti paśupacyadhikaraṇam || 7 ||

utpanyasambhavādhikaraṇam || 2-2-8 ||
213. utpatyasambhavāt || 2-2-39 ||
kapilāditantra sāmānyāt, bhagavadabhihitaparama niḥśreyasasādhanāvabodhini pañcarātra- tantre'pi, aprāmāṇyam, āśaṅkaya nirākriyate |

tatra, evamāśaṅkayate-

'paramakāraṇāt paramabrahmabhūtāt vāsudevāt saṅkarṣaṇo nāma jīvo jāyate |

saṅkarṣaṇāt pradyumnasaṃjñaṃ mano jāyate | tasmāt aniruddhasaṃjñaḥ ahaṅkāro jāyate'
'āśaṅkaya' iti, pūrvapakṣasūtradvayārthaḥ | nirākriyate iti, siddhāntasūtradvayārthaḥ- vibhāgaḥ | pañcarātraṃ, kiṃ sāṅkhyādivat kvacit pramāṇam ? uta kātsmrtsnyena pramāṇam ? | kim asya kvacit śrutivirodhaḥ asti ? uta kvacidapi nāsti ? | iti | kimatra jīvotpa- śyādikaṃ vivakṣitam ? uta na ? iti | saṅkarṣaṇa- pradyumnādiśabdāḥ kiṃ jīvādiśabda viśeṣaṇatayā nirdiṣṭā: ? uta na ? iti | uta jīvādiśabdāḥ saṅkarṣaṇādiśabda viśeṣaṇatayā nirdiṣṭāḥ iti | |
saṅkarṣaṇādiśabdānāṃ jīvādiśabda viśeṣaṇatve jīvotpatyādikaṃ vivakṣitamiti, śruti- virodhasadbhāvāt, aviruddhāṃśe prāmāṇyāt tadekadeśasyopādeyatvam | jīvādiśabdānāṃ saṅkarṣaṇādiśabda viśeṣaṇatve, jīvotpattyādeḥ avivakṣitatvena kvacidapi śrutivirodho nāstīti, kārtsnyena pramāṇatvāt kārtsnyena niḥśreyasārthibhiḥ upādeyatvaṃ siddhayet ||
(sūtrārtha : ; utpatteḥ—jīvānāṃ (nirvikāratayā śrutisiddhānāṃ ) vikārarūpasya janmanaḥ, asambhavāt ayogāt, (vikāra (svarūpānyathābhāva) rūpa janmāvedakaṃ pāñcarātratantram apramāṇam ) || ]

jīvotpatyādivādaśca pāñcarātre tu dṛśyate |
utpatyasambhavaśrutyā virodhādasamañjasaḥ ||

iti hi ! bhāgavataprakriyā | atra jīvasyotpattiḥ śrutiviruddhā pratīyate | śrutayo hi !

jīvasya anāditvaṃ vadanti ;
'na jāyate mriyate vā vipaścit '

ityādyāḥ || 39 ||

214. na ca kartuḥ karaṇam || 2-2-40 ||
'saṅkarṣaṇāt 'pradyumna' sajña mano jāyate'
iti karturjīvāt karaṇasya manasaḥ utpattiḥ, na sambhavati ;
'etasmājjāyate prāṇo manaḥ sarvendriyāṇi ca'
iti, parasmādeva brahmaṇaḥ manaso'pi utpattiśruteḥ | ataḥ śruti' viruddhārtha' - pratipādanāt asyā'pi tantrasya prāmāṇyaṃ pratiṣidhyate iti || 40 ||

evaṃ prāpte, pracakṣmahe-

215. vijñānādibhāve vā tadapratiṣedhaḥ || 2-2-41|| 'vā' śabdāt pakṣo viparivartate | vijñānaṃ ca ādi ca' iti, parabrahma paramakāraṇāt' ityādiḥ, pañcarātragranthaḥ || 39 ||
'vā'śabdāditi | 'api vā kartṛsāmānyāt' 'ekaṃ vā saṃyoga- rūpa codanākhyā viśeṣāt itivaditi bhāvaḥ | vijñānaṃ cādi ceti | [ada-bhakṣaṇe' ityasmāt, avaśyakārye ṇinipratyaye, 'ādi' iti rūpaṃ tena ca nikhila -

karturjīvāśca karaṇaṃ manastu na hi jāyate |
saṅkarṣaṇāddhi jīvāttu manaso janmaśaṃsanāt ||

saṅkarṣaṇādikānāṃ tu paramātmadhvaniścayāt |
jīvābhimānyavatārāt na prāmāṇyaṃ niṣidhyate ||

vijñānādi | saṅkarṣaṇa- pradyumna niruddhānāmapi parabrahmabhāve sati tatpratipādanaparasya śāstrasya prāmāṇyaṃ na pratiṣidhyate |
etaduktaṃ bhavati bhāgavataprakriyām ajānatām idaṃ codyam ; yat ' jīvotpattiḥ viruddhā abhihitā' iti | 'vāsudevākhyaṃ paraṃ brahmaiva āśritavatsalaṃ, svāśritasamāśraya- ṇīyatvāya, svecchayā caturdhā avatiṣṭhate' iti hi ! tatprakriyā || yathā pauṣkara- saṃhitāyām -
'kartavyatvena vai yatra cāturātmyamupāsyate' |
kramāgataiḥ svasaṃjñābhirbrāhmaṇairāgamaṃ tu tat ||'
ityādi | tacca cāturātmyopāsanaṃ vāsudevākhyaparabrahmopāsanamiti sātvatasaṃhitāyā- muktam-
'brāhmaṇānāṃ hi saddbrahmavāsudevākhyayājinām |
vivekadaṃ paraṃ śāstra brahmopaniṣadaṃ mahat ||'
jagatsaṃhartṛtvamukhenaiva kāraṇatvaṃ pratipādyate ] caitanya svarūpiṇa' iti mantraliṅgāt, 'bhagavān vāsudeva: parā prakṛtiḥ' iti vacanācca, vāsudevasya jñānasvarūpatva-paramakāraṇatve prati- pādite | tasmādvijñānādiśabdena bhagavān vāsudeva: smāritaḥ 'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma' 'tasmādvā ... saṃbhūtaḥ ' 'yato vā... jāyante' iti jñānasvarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ paramakāraṇatvapratipādakavedāntaika- - kaṇṭhyadyotanārthaṃ ca vijñānaṃ ca ādi ca iti vyākhyātam |
etaduktamiti | saṅkarṣaṇetyādinā sūtrasya vākyārtha uktaḥ | ke punaḥ saṅkarṣaṇādayaḥ ? teṣāṃ parabrahmabhāvaḥ katham ? tathā brahmaṇa utpattirvā katham ? śāstraprāmāṇyaṃ vā katham ityatra

'kartavyatvena' ityādivacanam 'āgama' śabdanirvacanam | svasaṃjñābhiḥ - 'namaḥ' śabdādyupetaiḥ tattannāmabhiḥ | kramāgataiḥ upāsyavastupratipādanāt śāstrasya āgamatvam — iti ślokārthaḥ | 'cāturātmyamupāsyate' ityanena saṅkarṣaṇa | dicāturvidhyaṃ samāśrayaṇīyatva | yeti darśitam | cāturātmyeti | cāturātmyopāsanaparaśāstrasya vāsudevākhyaparabrahmopāsanaparatvadarśanāt, cāturvidhyaṃ vāsudevasyeti siddham |

iti | taddhi ! vāsudevākhyaṃ paraṃ brahma, sampūrṇaṣāḍguṇyavapuḥ, sūkṣma vyūha - vibhava medabhinnaṃ, yathādhikāraṃ bhaktaiḥ jñānapūrveṇa karmaṇā abhyarcitaṃ, samyakprāpyate |
'vibhavācanāt vyūhaṃ prāpya, vyūhārcanāt paraṃ brahma vāsudevākhyaṃ bhrūkṣmaṃ prāpyate' iti vadanti | vibhavo hi nāma rāmakṛṣṇādiprādurbhāvagaṇaḥ | 'vyūhaḥ ' vāsudeva- saṅkarṣaṇa- pradyumnāniruddharūpaḥ caturgṛhaḥ | sūkṣmaṃ tu kevalapāiguṇyavigrahaṃ vāsudevākhyaṃ 'paraṃ brahma' | yathā pauṣkare-
'yasmāt samyak paraṃ brahma vāsudevākhyamavyayam |
'asmāda'vāpyate śāstrājjñānapūrveṇa karmaṇā |
' ityādi | ataḥ saṅkarṣaṇādīnāmapi parasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ svecchāvigraharūpatvāt, 'ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyate'
iti śrutisiddhasyaiva āśrita vātsalya nimitta svecchā vigrahasaṅgraharūpa janmanaḥ abhidhānāt, tadabhidhāyiśāstra' prāmāṇyasya apratiṣedhaḥ – iti ||
tatra jīva- mano'haṅkāra tatvānām adhiṣṭhātāraḥ, 'saṅkarṣaṇa- pradyumnāniruddhā:' iti, teṣāmeva jīvādiśabdairabhidhānam aviruddham yathā ākāśa-prāṇādiśabdaiḥ brahmaṇo'bhi- dhānam | 41 ||

216. vipratiṣedhācca || 2-2-42 ||
vipratiṣiddhā hi ! jīvotpattiḥ, 'tasminnapi tantre | yathoktaṃ paramasaṃhitāyām —
' acetanā parārthā ca nityā satatavikriyā |
triguṇā karmiṇāṃ kṣetraṃ prakṛte rūpamucyate ||
vyāptirūpeṇa sambandhastasyāśca puruṣasya ca |
sānādiranantaśca paramārthena niścitaḥ ||'
iti | evaṃ sarvāsvapi saṃhitāsu jīvasya nityatvavacanāt jīvasvarūpotpattiḥ pañcarātra- "tantre pratiṣiddhaiva | janma-maraṇādivyavahārastu loka- vedayoḥ, jīvasya yathopapadyate ;

tathā,

'nāSStmā śruteḥ'

ityatra vakṣyate | ataḥ, jīvasyotpattiḥ tatrā'pi pratiṣiddhaiveti, jīvotpattivāda- nimittāprāmāṇyaśaṅkā 'dūrotsāritā' ||
vipratiṣedhācceti | jīvādiśabdaiḥ saṅkarṣaṇādīnāṃ viśeṣyatve anukūlaliṅgamuktam | saṅkarṣaṇādiśabdaiḥ jīvānāṃ viśeṣyatve, pratikūlaliṅgamucyate | viśeṣeṇa pratiṣedhaḥ - vipratiṣedhaḥ | vividhapratiṣedho vā | yathā 'śrutivipratipannā' ityatra, asmattaḥ śravaṇena viśeṣataḥ pratipannetyarthaḥ tadvat | yathā 'tacchāstrairvipratiṣiddham' ityatra vipratiṣedhaśabdaḥ ; tathā ayamapi niṣedhapara: | vyāghāta | sarvāsvapītyanena 'vi' śabdārtho vivakṣitaḥ | vakṣyata iti | kāraṇādyāśraya śarīra saṃyogakṛtajñānavikāsa saṅkoca- lakṣaṇau utpatti - vināśau iti vakṣyate ityarthaḥ |

|

yaścaiṣaḥ keṣāñciduddhoṣaḥ-

'sāṅgeṣu vedeṣu niṣṭhāmalabhamānaḥ śāṇḍilyaḥ pañcarātraśāstramadhītavān' iti, 'sāṅgeṣu vedeṣu puruṣārthaniṣṭhā 'na labdhā" iti vacanāt veda viruddhaṃ tantram - iti ;
so'pi, anāghrātavedavacasām anākalitatadupabṛṃhaṇa-nyāyakalāpānām, śraddhāmātra- vijṛmbhitaḥ | yathā-
'prātaḥ prātaranṛtaṃ te vadanti purodayājjuhvati ye'gnihotram ' iti anudita homanindā, udita homapraśaṃsārthetyuktam ; 'yathā ca ' bhūmavidyāprakrame nāradena, 'ṛgvedaṃ bhagavo'dhyemi yajurveda sāmaveda' mātharvaṇa' caturtham,

itihāsapurāṇaṃ pañcamam'
ityārabhya sarva vidyāsthānamabhidhāya,
'so'haṃ bhagavo mantravidevā'smi, nā''tmavit'
iti bhūmavidyāvyatiriktāsu sarvāsu vidyāsu 'ātmavedanā' lābhavacanaṃ vakṣyamāṇabhūma- vidyāpraśaṃsārthaṃ kṛtam |
athavā asya nāradasya, sāṅgeṣu vedeṣu yat parataccaṃ pratipādyate tadalābhanimittaḥ ayaṃ vādaḥ | evameva śāṇḍilyasyeti paścāt vedāntavedya vāsudevākhyaparabrahma taccābhi- dhānāt, avagamyate |

tathā vedārthasya durjñānatayā, sukhāvabodhārthaḥ śāstrārambhaḥ paramasaṃhitāyāmucyate- ' adhī' tā bhagavan' vedāḥ sāṅgopāṅgāḥ savistarāḥ |
śrutāni ca mayā'ṅgāni vākovākyayutāni ca || na caiteṣu samasteṣu saṃśayena vinā kvacit |
śreyomārga prapaśyāmi yena siddhirbhaviṣyati ||'

iti ;

'vedānteṣu yathāsāraṃ saṅgṛhya bhagavān hariḥ |

bhaktānukampayā vidvān sañcikṣepa yathāsukham ||'

iti ca ||

ataḥ sa bhagavān 'vedaika'vaidyaḥ, parabrahmābhidhāno vāsudevaḥ, nikhila heyapratyanīka- kalyāṇaikatānānantajñānānandādyaparimitodāraguṇasāgaraḥ, satyasaṅkalpaḥ, cāturvarṇya-cātu- rāśramyavyavasthayā avasthitān, avasthitān dharmārthakāmamokṣākhyapuruṣārthābhimukhān bhaktān
tathā—uktānuguṇamevetyarthaḥ | saṃśayeneti | saṃśayaḥ - anadhītaśākhāsu śrutārthavirodho'sti na bā ? ityevaṃ rūpaḥ | yadvā, 'pratipanneṣvartheṣu yat śreyaḥ, tanna jānāmi' ityevaṃ rūpaḥ |
vedānteṣviti | mokṣadharmeyaṃ vā keṣvanuvākeṣu niṣatsūpaniṣatsu ca | gṛṇanti satyadharmāṇa: satyaṃ satyeṣu sāmasu' iti | ekāyanaśākhāviṣaye pārameśvaraṃ vacanam – 'mahato vedavṛkṣasya mūlabhūto mahānayam | skandhabhūtā ṛgādyāste śākhābhūtāstathā mune' || ityādi draṣṭavyam |
ataḥ - vedāntasya duravabodhārthatvāt | bhagavān ityādiviśeṣaṇāni bhrama - vipralambhādi- saṃbhavaśaṅkāvyāvṛtyarthāni | bhagavacchabdena heyapratyanīka kalyāṇaikatānatve vivakṣite | vedaiketi, 'eka' śabdaprayogaḥ, 'puruṣatvāt sadoṣa:' ityādyanumānasya, dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhamabhipretya | nikhilaheyeti, bhagavacchabdasamākhyā pramāṇasiddhamarthamāha | satyasaṅkalpa iti, vakṣyamāṇa- vyavasthānuguṇaviśeṣaṇam | bhaktāniti, 'śuddhabhāvaṃ gato bhaktyā śāstrādvedmi janārdanam' iti

avalokya, 'apārakāruṇya' sauśīlyavātsalyaudārya mahodadhiḥ, svasvarūpa - 'svavibhūti - svārādhana-tatphalayāthātmyāvabodhino vedān ṛgyajuḥsāmātharva' bheda' bhinnān apari- mitaśākhān vidyarthavādamantrarūpān, svetarasakalasura naraduravagāhāṃva avadhārya, tadartha- yāthātmyāvabodhi paśñcarātraśāstra svayameva niramimīteti, – niravadyam ||

ityārabhya mahatā prabandhena pañcarātraśāstraprakriyāṃ pratipādya,

'idaṃ śatasahasrāddhi bhāratākhyāna vistarāt |
āvidhya matimanthānaṃ dadhno ghṛtamivoddhṛtaṃ ||' 'navanītaṃ yathā dadhno dvipadāṃ brāhmaṇo yathā |
āraṇyakaṃ ca vedebhya oṣadhībhyo yathā'mṛtam ||' 'idaṃ mahopaniṣadaṃ caturvedasamanvitam |
sāṃkhya yoga' kṛtāntena' pañścarātrānuśabditam ||' 'idaṃ śreya idaṃ brahma idaṃ hitamanuttamam ' 'ṛgyajuḥsāmabhirjuṣṭamatharvāṅgirasaistathā'
'bhaviṣyati pramāṇaṃ (vai) vā etadevā'nuśāsanam ' iti | 'sāṅkhya- yoga' śabdābhyāṃ jñānayoga- karmayogāvabhihitau | yathoktam - 'jñānayogena sākhyānāṃ karmayogena yoginām'

iti | bhīṣmaparvaṇyapi,
'brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyairvaiśyaiḥ śūdraiśca kṛtalakṣaṇaiḥ |
arcanīyaśca sevyaśca pūjanīyaśca mādhavaḥ ||
'sātvataṃ vidhimāsthāya gītaḥ saṅkarṣaṇena yaḥ'
iti | katham evaṃ bruvāṇoṃ bādarāyaṇaḥ, vedavidagresaraḥ, vedāntavedya parabrahmabhūtavāsudevo - 'pāsanārcanādi 'pratipādanaparasya sātvataśāstrasya, aprāmāṇyaṃ brūyāt ? ||

nanu ca

'sāṅkhyaṃ yogaḥ pañcarātraṃ vedāḥ pāśupataṃ tathā'

'kimetānyekaniṣṭhāni pṛthaniṣṭhāni vā mune ?'
ityādinā, sākhyādīnāmapi ādaraṇīyatocyate | 'śārīrake tu', sāṃkhyādīni pratiṣidhyante | ataḥ idamapi tantraṃ tattulyam !
netyucyate ; yataḥ tatrā'pi imameva śārīra koktanyāyamavatārayati |
'kimetānyekaniṣṭhāni pṛthaṅa niṣṭhāni vā ?' iti praśnasya, ayamarthaḥ - kiṃ sāṃkhya- yoga - pāśupata - veda- pañcarātrāṇi, ekatattvapratipādanaparāṇi 1 pṛthaktatvapratipādanaparāṇi vā ? 'yadā' ekatatvapratipādanaparāṇi kiṃ tat ekaṃ tattvam ? yadā tu pṛthaktatvapratipādana- parāṇi tadā eṣāṃ paraspara viruddhārthapratipādanaparatvāt', vastuni vikalpāsambhavācca, ekameva pramāṇamaṅgīkaraṇīyam ; kiṃ tadekam 1 - iti ||

asya uttaraṃ bruvan,

'jñānānyetāni rājarṣe ! viddhi nānāmatāni vai
sāṃkhyasya vaktā kapilaḥ'

235. jño'ta eva || 2-3-19||

viyadādivat jīvo notpadyate ityuktam | tatprasaṅgena jīvasvarūpaṃ nirūpyate | kiṃ sugata - kapilābhimatacinmātrameva, ātmanaḥ svarūpam ? uta kaṇabhugabhimata- pāṣāṇa kalpasvarūpam 'acitsvabhāva' meva ' āgantuka' caitanyaguṇakam ? atha jñātṛtvameva asya svarūpam ? – iti ||

kimiti | kiṃ jīvaḥ caitanyamātravapuḥ uta jaḍasvarūpa eva āgantuka caitanyaḥ ? uta svato jñātā ? – iti | kiṃ jñānamātravapuṣchre jñānasvarūpatva taddharmakatva para sarvaśrutisāmañjasyam ? uta āgantukacaitanya jaḍasvarūpatve ? uta svato jñātṛtvaikasvarūpatve ? iti | jñātṛtvasya svarūpatve sarvatra upalabdhiḥ prasajyate ? na ? iti | tadarthaṃ kiṃ jīvaḥ sarvagataḥ ? uta aṇuḥ ? iti | aṇutve viprakṛṣṭārthagrahaṇāgrahaṇe saṃbhavata: ? na ? iti | ekajātīyavyayoḥ niyatadharma- dharmi- bhāvaḥ upapadyate ? na ? iti |
yadā nopapadyate ; tadā viprakṛṣṭārthagrahaṇāsaṃbhavena, sarvagatatvāt sarvatropalabdhiprasaṅgena, jñātṛtvasya svarūpatve sarvaśrutisāmañjasyābhāvāt, pakṣāntarayoranyataram svīkārya syāt | yadā upapadyate ; tadā viprakṛṣṭārthagrahaṇāgrahaṇasaṃbhavena aṇutve siddhe, sarvatropalambhāprasaṅgāt, sarvaśrutisāmañjasyena jātṛtvasvarūpa ātmeti sidhyati ||

cinmātramiti bhāvapradhāna nirdeśaḥ, jñānatvamityarthaḥ | mātracā jñātṛtvādivyāvṛttiḥ | parasya ca avabhāsaprati sambandhitvam |

'tat tvamasi'
'ayamātmā brahma'

'brahma dāśā brahma dāsā brahmame kitavā:'

na ca bhedavyapadeśānāṃ pratyakṣādi prasiddhārthatvena, anyathāsiddhatvam ; brahma- sṛjyatva-tanniyāmyatva-taccharīratva- taccheṣatva- tadādhāratva- tatpālyatva- tatsaṃhāryatva tadupāsa- katva-tatprasādalabhyadharmārthakāmamokṣa rūpapuruṣārtha bhāktatvādayaḥ, tatkṛtaśca jīva brahmaṇorbhedaḥ, pratyakṣādyagocaratvena ' ananyathāsiddhaḥ | ataḥ, na jagatsṛṣṭayā divādinīnāṃ pramāṇāntara- siddhabhedānuvādena mithyārthopadeśaparatvam ||

na ca - akhaṇḍaikarasa cinmātrasvarūpeṇa brahmaṇā, 'ātmanaḥ atadbhāvānu' sandhānaṃ, bahubhavanasaṅkalpapūrvaka viyadādisṛṣṭiṃ, jīvabhāvena tatpraveśaṃ, vicitranāma-rūpa 'vyākaraṇa' tatkṛtānanta viṣayānubhava nimittasukhaduḥkhabhāgitvam, abhoktṛtvena tatra sthitvā tanniyamanena antaryāmitvaṃ, jīvabhūtasya svasya kāraṇatrahmātmabhāvānusandhānaṃ, saṃsāramokṣaṃ, tadupa deśaśāstra' ca kurvāṇena, bhramitavyam — ityupadiśyate ; tathā sati, unmattapralapitatvāpātāt ||
tataśceti | utkṛṣṭaḥ sāmānādhikaraṇye, abhedasya aupacārikatvaśaṅkā syāt | iha tu dāśa - kitavādinikṛṣṭa sāmānādhikaraṇyam, abhedasya tātvikatvaṃ dyotayatītyarthaḥ |
brahmaṇā .... bhramitavyam iti anvayārthaḥ | pramāṇāntarāgocarārthopadeśo hi ! parasya- bodhanārthaḥ | ataḥ pramāṇāntarāgocaraṃ vadan hi vedaḥ, 'tvam evaṃ viddhi' iti bodhayati | tasmāt aparamārthabhramamupadiśatā vedena 'brahmaṇā bhramitavyam' ityuktaṃ bhavati | na ca tadyukta- mityarthaḥ | tathā satīti | bhramanivṛtyā lokojjīvanapravṛttena vedena, adhikabhramāntarāpādanaṃ vyāhṛtamityarthaḥ | kurvāṇena bhramitavyamiti | kurvāṇatvena bhramitavyam; 'kurvāṇamiva brahma bhrāmyati' iti nopadiśyate iti paryavasito'rthaḥ || |

'atha yo hai tānanantānupāste'
iti upāsanaśravaṇāt, upāsya prāṇa' viśeṣaṇabhūtakārya' bāhulyābhiprāyā || 6 ||
275. śreṣṭhaśca || 2-4-7||

prāṇasaṃvāde, śarīrasthitihetutvena śreṣṭhatayā nirṇītaḥ 'mukhya' prāṇaḥ,
' ānīdavātaṃ svadhayā tadekam ' iti mahāpralayasamaye svakāryabhūtaprāṇana sadbhāvaśravaṇāt, 'etasmājjāyate' iti janma - śravaṇasya, jīvajanmaśravaṇavat upapatteḥ, na utpadyate - ityāśaṅkaya,

prāk sṛṣṭeḥ ekatvāvadhāraṇādivirodhāt,
'etasmājjāyate prāṇaḥ'

iti pṛthivyāditulyotpattiśravaṇāt utpattiniṣedhābhāvācca, jāyata eva śreṣṭhaśca prāṇaḥ— ityucyate |
' ānīdavātam' iti tu na jaiva' śreṣṭhaprāṇamabhipretyocyate ; api tu parasya brahmaṇa ekasyaiva vidyamānatvamucyate; 'acātam' iti tatraiva śravaṇāt |
pūrveṇaiva tulyanyāyatve'pi pṛthagyogakaraṇam 'uttara' cintārtham || 7 ||
iti prāṇāṇutvādhikaraṇam || 3 ||

vāyukriyādhikaraṇam || 2-4-4 ||
276. na vāyu- kriye pṛthagupadeśāt || 2-4-8||

so'yaṃ 'śreṣṭhaḥ prāṇaḥ', kiṃ mahābhūtadvitīyavāyumātram ? tasya vā spanda rūpā- kriyā' ? athavā vāyureva kaṃcana viśeṣamāpannaḥ 1 - iti viśaye ;

vāyureva - iti prāptam ;

'yaḥ prāṇaḥ sa vāyuḥ'

iti vyapadeśāt | yadvā-vāyu' mātre prāṇatvaprasiddhyabhāvāt, ucchvāsa - niśvāsādi- vāyukriyāyāṃ prāṇaśabdaprasiddheśva, tatkriyaiva ||

| siddhāntarūpaṃ sūtrottarārdhavarṇanam ]

iti prāpte ; 'na vāyumātraṃ na ca tatkriyā' – ityucyate | 'kutaḥ ? pṛthagupadeśāt ' ;
'etasmājjāyate prāṇo manaḥ sarvendriyāṇi ca | khaṃ vāyuḥ'

na vāviti | kiṃ mukhyaprāṇo vāyumātram uta vāyukriyā ? uta avasthā viśeṣa panno vāyuḥ ? - iti || śrutei lokaprasāddha- pṛthagupadeśa- tattvamakhyā prasiddhīnāṃ kasmin makṣe sāmañjasyam ? iti | vāyumātratvapakṣe vāyukriyātvapakṣe vā sāmañjasyamasti cet | tayoranyatarasya siddhiḥ | avasthā viśeṣānnavāyupakṣe sāmañjasyaṃ cet ! sa eva siddhāntaḥ | etasmāditi | 'khaṃ vāyuḥ '

iti | tata eva pṛthagupadeśāt, vāyukriyā'pi na bhavati prāṇaḥ | na hi ! tejaḥ prabhṛtīnāṃ kriyā, taiḥ saha pṛthagdravyatayā upadiśyate | 'yaḥ prāṇaḥ sa vāyuḥ' iti tu 'vāyureva avasthāntaramāpannaḥ prāṇaḥ na tejaḥprabhṛtivat taccāntaram' iti jñāpanārtham | ucchvāsa- niśvāmādāvapi, 'prāṇaḥ spandate' iti kriyāvati dravya eva, 'prāṇa' śabda-

'prasiddhi:'; na kriyāmātre || 8 ||

kimayaṃ prāṇaḥ, vāyorvikāraḥ san agnivad bhūtāntaram ? netyāha- 277. cakṣurādivattu tatsahaśiṣṭyādibhyaḥ || 2-4-9||
na ayaṃ bhūtaviśeṣaḥ api tu cakṣurādivat jīvopakaraṇaviśeṣaḥ | tacca upakaraṇatvam, upakaraṇa' viśeṣabhūtaiḥ' indriyaiḥ saha śiṣṭayādibhyaḥ avagamyate | cakṣurādibhiḥ saha ayaṃ prāṇaḥ śiṣyate, prāṇasaṃvādādiṣu tatsajātīyatve hi ? taiḥ saha śāsanaṃ yujyate |
iti pṛthagupadeśāt, na vāyumātramityarthaḥ | tata eveti kriyātvapratikṣepaḥ | taiḥ sahetyādi | dravyotpattiprakaraṇatvāt tejaḥ prabhṛtīnāṃ kriyā: pṛthagupadiśyante cet ! dravyatayopadiśyeran | na ca tadyuktam | ataḥ kriyāyā adravyatvena pṛthagupadeśāsambhavāt pṛthagupadiṣṭaḥ prāṇaḥ na vāyukriyetyarthaḥ || 8 ||
kimayamiti | 'avasthāviśeṣāpanno vāyuḥ prāṇaḥ' ityuktam | avasthāntarāpattiśca agnyāderiva vāyutvāvasthā prahāṇenaiva syāt ! iti hi ! śaṅkā syāt | prāṇasaṃvādādiṣvityādi | śrutibhedena saha śiṣṭayo bahvayaḥ ; viśiṣyā'bhidhānāni ca bahūnītyabhiprāyaḥ | anantarasūtra- vivariṣyamāṇakaraṇatulyopakārasūcanārthe vā bahuvacanam | tatsajātīyatve hīti, agryaprāya- nyāya mabhipretya |

bāyyavasthābheda iti nā'yaṃ bhūtāntaraṃ bhavet |
indriyaiḥ saha śiṣṭavādeḥ prāṇasaṃvadanādiṣṭha ||

'prāṇa' śabdaparigṛhīteṣu karaṇeṣu asya viśiṣyā'bhidhānam, 'ādi' śabdena

gṛhyate |

'atha ha ya evāyaṃ mukhyaḥ prāṇaḥ'
'yo'yaṃ madhyamaḥ prāṇaḥ'
ityādiṣu 'viśiṣyā' 'bhidhānāt || 9 ||

cakṣurādivat 'asyāspi' karaṇatve, 'tadvat asyā'pi jīvaṃ prati upakāraviśeṣa- kriyayā bhavitavyam | sā tu na dṛśyate | ataḥ nāḍyaṃ cakṣurādivad bhavitumarhati - iti cet ! tatrā''ha
278. akaraṇatvācca na doṣastathāhi darśayati ||2-4-10||
akaraṇatvāt - karaṇaṃ kriyā ; 'akriyatvāt | asya prāṇasya jītraṃ prati upakāra- viśeṣarūpakriyārahitatvācca yo doṣa udbhāvyate, sa nā'sti | yataḥ upakāraviśeṣa' rūpāṃ śarīrendriyadhāraṇādirūpāṃ kriyāṃ darśayati śrutiḥ ;

' yasminnutkrānte, idaṃ śarīraṃ pāpiṣṭhatara mitra dṛśyate, sa 'vaḥ' śreṣṭhaḥ '

karaṇatvam || 10 ||

nanvevaṃ—– nāmabhedāt kāryabhedācca, prāṇāpānādayaḥ taccāntarāṇi syuḥ ! tatrāha 279. pañcavṛttirmanovad vyapadiśyate || 2-4 -11 || yathā kāmādivṛttibhede tatkāryabhede'pi, na kāmādikaṃ manasastattvāntaram ; 'kāmaḥ saṅkalpo vicikitsā śraddhā'śraddhā dhṛtiradhṛtihrīrdhīrbhīrityetatma mana eva'

280. aṇuśca || 2-4 - 12 ||
aṇuśvā'yam, pūrvavat utkrāntyādiśravaṇāt, 'tamutkrāmantaṃ prāṇo'nūtkrāmati'

'sama ebhistribhirlokaiḥ samo'nena sarveṇa'

'sama ebhistribhi:' ityādivākyāni naikatra kramapaṭhi ni | 'śreṣṭhaśva' iti sūtre tyā pūrvapakṣo thāne'pi parihāraḥ pūrvādhikaraṇena tulyaḥ | ekatvāvadhāraṇādibhiḥ utpamive siddha sṛṣṭeḥ prāgavastha' navā divākyasya brahmaparatvopapādanāt, 'aṇavaśca' ityānantyaśrutaḥ upamanārthatayā nirvāha: kṛta: | na tathā'sya nirvāha iti im adhikaraṇāntaram || 12 ||

jyotirādyadhikaraṇam || 2-4-6 || 281. jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānaṃ tu tadāmananāt
prāṇavatā śabdāt || 2-4-13||

saśreṣṭhānāṃ prāṇānāṃ brahmaṇa utpattiḥ, iyattā, parimāṇaṃ ca - uktam | teṣāṃ prāṇānām annyādidevatā' dhiṣṭhitatvaṃ ca', pūrvameva
'abhimāni vyapadeśastu viśeṣānugatibhyām '
ityanena sūtreṇa prasaṅgādupapāditam | 'jīvasya ca' svabhogasādhanānāmeṣāmadhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ loka siddham |
'evamevaiṣa 'etān prāṇān gṛhītvā sve śarīre yathākāmaṃ parivartate'

ityādiśrutisiddhaṃ ca |

tadidaṃ jīvasya agnyādidevatānāṃ ca prāṇaviṣayam adhiṣṭhānaṃ, kiṃ svāyattam 1 uta paramātmāyattam ? – iti viśaye,

iyattā - saṅkhyā | parimāṇam — saṅkhyātānā pratyekaṃ parimiti | jīvasya agnyādidevatānāṃ ca prāṇaviṣayam adhiṣṭhānam kiṃ svāyattam uta paramātmāyattam ? | teṣāṃ -tatra nairapekṣyapratītiḥ samīcīnā ? uta bhrāntimūlā ? iti |
samīcīnatve svāyattatā | bhrāntimūlatve paramātmādhīnatā |

jyotirādestu jīvena saṅkalpāt paramātmanaḥ |
indriyāderadhiṣṭhānaṃ śabdādevāvagamyate ||

nairapekṣyāt svāyattam ;

khārthavarṇanam ]

iti prāpte, ucyate – 'jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānam' – iti | prāṇavatā - jīvena saha, jyotirādīnām – agnyādidevatānāṃ prāṇaviṣayam adhiṣṭhānam, tadāmananāt - tasya 'paramātmanaḥ ' āmananāt bhavati | āmananam - ābhimukhyena mananam | paramātmanaḥ saṅkalpādeva bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
kuta etat ? śabdāt | indriyāṇāṃ sābhimāni devatānāṃ, jīvātmanaśca "svakāryeṣu paramapuruṣamananā' yattatva' śāstrāt | yathā antaryāmitrāhmaṇādiṣu–
'yo'gnau tiṣṭhan agnerantaraḥ, yamagnirna veda, yasyā'gniḥ śarīram, yognimantaro- yamayati, sata ātmā'ntaryāmyamṛtaḥ'

'yo vāyau tiṣṭhan'
'ya āditye tiṣṭhan '
'ya ātmani tiṣṭhan '
'yaścakṣuṣi tiṣṭhan'
ityādi | yathā ca -
'bhīṣā'smādvātaḥ pavate | bhīṣodeti sūryaḥ ||
bhīṣā'smādagniścendraśca | mṛtyurdhāviti pañcamaḥ ||'

iti | " tathā "

'etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi ! sūryācandramasau vidhṛtau tiṣṭhataḥ '

ityādi || 13 ||

282 tasya ca nityatvāt || 2-4-14 ||

283. 'ta indriyāṇi' tadvayapadeśādanyatra śreṣṭhāt ||2-4-15||

kiṃ sarve 'prāṇa' śabda nirdiṣṭāḥ indriyāṇi ? uta śreṣṭhaprāṇavyatiriktā eva 1 -

iti viśaye,

iti prāpte, ucyate śreṣṭhaprāṇavyatiriktā eva prāṇāḥ indriyāṇi | kutaḥ ? śreṣṭhādanyeṣveva prāṇeṣu tadvayapadeśāt |
'indriyāṇi daśaikaṃ ca pañca cendriyagocarāḥ' ityādibhirhi' ! cakṣurādiṣu samanaskeṣveva ' 'indriya śabdo vyapadiśyate || 15 ||
284. bhedaśrutervailakṣaṇyācca || 2-4-16 ||
ta indriyāṇīti | kiṃ sarve prāṇāḥ indriyāṇi ? uta mukhyaprāṇavyatiriktāḥ ? iti | kiṃ śrotrādiṣu mukhyaprāṇe ca 'prāṇa' śabdaḥ, eka nimittena vartate ? uta nimittabhedena ? – iti | yadā eka nimittena tadā sarve prāṇāḥ indriyāṇi | yadā nimittabhedena tadā mukhyaprāṇavyatiriktāḥ indriyāṇi || 15 ||

karaṇatve'pi sarveṣāṃ śreṣṭhādanyatra śaṃsanāt |
indriyāṇīti, na śreṣṭha indriyaṃ bhavati hyasau ||

samanaskendriyebhyo'sya bhedenotpattiśaṃsanāt |
suṣuptau ca prāṇavṛtterupalabdhyā viśeṣataḥ ||

dibhi: - na. ' samanaskeṣu - da.

indriyāṇi tu -- śra.

'etasmājjāyate prāṇo manaḥ sarvendriyāṇi ca'
ityādiṣu indriyebhyaḥ prāṇasya pṛthak śravaṇāt, prāṇavyatiriktānāmeva indriyatvamavagamyate | manasaḥ pṛthak śravaṇe'pi, 'tasya anyatra' indriyāntarbhāva uktaḥ ;

'manaḥṣaṣṭhānīndriyāṇi'

ityādau |

vailakṣaṇyaṃ ca cakṣurādibhyaḥ śreṣṭhaprāṇasya upalabhyate | suṣuptau hi ! prāṇasya vṛttirupalabhyate ; cakṣurādīnāṃ tu vṛttiḥ nopalabhyate | kārya ca cakṣurvāgādīnāṃ " samanaskāna, jñāna- karmasādhanatvam | prāṇasya tu śarīrendriyadhāraṇam |

prāṇādhīnadhāraṇatvāttu indriyeṣu 'prāṇa' śabdavyapadeśaḥ | tathā ca śrutiḥ - " ta etasyaiva sarve rūpamabhavan tasmādeta 'etenāsskhya yante' iti | rūpamabhavan – śarīramabhavan' | tadadhīnapravṛttayo'bhavan – ityarthaḥ || 16 || iti indriyādhikaraṇam ||7||
'bhedaśruteriti | ' ityādī' ityatra 'ādi' śabdena, 'indriyāṇi daśakaṃ ca pañca cendriya- gocarā:' ityādi vivakṣitam ||

suṣuptāvityādi | suṣuptau vṛttyupalambhavailakṣaṇyaṃ śarīrendriyadhāraṇarūpakāryavailakṣaṇyaṃ, 'prāṇa' śabda pravṛttinimittavailakṣaṇyaṃ cetyarthaḥ || cakṣurādīnāṃ prāṇaśabdabodhyatvaṃ lākṣaṇikam ; mukhyaprāṇasya tu mukhyam | bhedaśrutervailakṣaṇyāccetyekaṃ sūtramiti yuktam | vailakṣaṇyānugṛhīta- śrutereva hetutvāt | hetubhede hi ! sūtrabhedopapattiḥ | manasa iva gobalīvardanyāyena bhedanirdeśa- sambhavāt | tadvyāvartakākārāntareṇa vailakṣaṇye siddhe hi ! bhedaśrutiḥ stheyasī syāt || 16 ||

2. bhedaśruteḥ ]

saṃjñāmūrtiklatyadhikaraṇam ||2-4-8 ||
285. saṃjñāmūrtiklaptistu trivṛtkurvata upadeśāt || 2-4-17||

bhūtendriyādīnāṃ samaṣṭisṛṣṭiḥ, jīvānāṃ kartṛtvaṃ ca parasmād brahmaṇaḥ - ityukta purastāt | jīvānāṃ svendriyā 'dhiṣṭhānaṃ ca parāyattam - iti ca, anantaraṃ sthirīkaraṇāya smāritam |
purastāt - tṛtīye pāde | anantaram - asmin pāde pūrvasmādapi pūrvādhikaraṇe | sthirīti | devānāmapi parāyattapravṛttitvanirṇayena jīvānāṃ parāyattaṃ kartṛtvaṃ sthiraṃ bhavati | tadarthaṃ tadeva dṛṣṭāntatayā smāritamityarthaḥ | tṛtīyapādādārabhya itaḥ pūrvamekā peṭikā | tatra bhūtendriya- samaṣṭisṛṣṭinirūpaṇam | atha vyaṣṭisṛṣṭinirūpaṇamiti peṭikāsaṅgatiḥ | vyaṣṭeśca jīvopakaraṇa- rūpatvāt tatsṛṣṭinirūpaṇamiti pādasaṅgatiḥ | pūrvādhikaraṇe samaṣṭisṛṣṭeḥ paramātmakartṛkatvamuktam | atha vyaṣṭisṛṣṭiḥ jīvakartṛkatayā pratīyata iti, śaṅkayā avāntarasaṅgatiḥ | pūrvapādoktasamaṣṭi- sṛṣṭinirūpaṇena vyaṣṭisṛṣṭinirūpaṇasya saṅgatiḥ | anyānyadhikaraṇāne tu prasaktānuprasaktikayā nirūpitānīti etat kaṇṭhoktam ||
brahmaṇaḥ sākṣātsarvakāraṇatvaṃ śrutam upapadyate ? uta na ? iti saṃśayaḥ | tadarthaṃ vyaṣṭināma- rūpavyākaraṇaṃ, kiṃ hiraṇyagarbhamātrakartṛkam ? uta tadavasthabrahmakartṛkam ? iti | tadartham 'anena jīvena' iti jīvāsāmānyakartṛkatvaśrutiḥ, kiṃ jīvasamaṣṭyā śraya ke valahiraṇyagarbhakartṛkatvamavagamayati ? uta jīvamātraviśiṣṭaparamātmakartṛkatvam ? iti | tadartha kevala hiraṇyagarbhakartṛkatve trivṛtkaraṇa--

yātviyaṃ nāma 'rūpavyākaraṇātmikā' prapañścavyaṣṭisṛṣṭiḥ; sā kiṃ samaṣṭi- 'jīva rūpasya ' hiraṇya' garbhasyaiva karma ? uta tejaḥprabhṛtiśarīra kasya parasya abādisṛṣṭivat, hiraṇyagarbhaśarīrakasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ 1 - iti idānīṃ cintyate |

kutaḥ ?

api tu svāṃśabhūtena jīvarūpeṇa 'anena jīvenā''tmanā ' iti vacanāt |

nanvevaṃ – 'cāreṇā'nupraviśya parabalaṃ saṅkalayāni itivat, vyākaravāṇīti 'uttamapuruṣaḥ', kartṛsthakriyaśca praviśatiḥ, lākṣaṇikaḥ syāt |

naivam – tatra rāja- cārayoḥ svarūpabhedāt lākṣaṇikatvam | iha tu jīvasthāpi svāṃśatvena svarūpatvāt tena rūpeṇa praveśaḥ | vyākaraṇaṃ ca ātmana eveti, na lākṣaṇikatvaprasaṅgaḥ |
na ca – sahayogalakṣaṇā iyaṃ tṛtīyā; kārakavibhaktau sambhavantyām, upapada-

vibhakteḥ anyāyyatvāt |

na ca karaṇe tṛtīyā ; brahmakartṛkayoḥ praveśa-vyākaraṇayoḥ, jīvasya sādhaka- tamatvābhāvāt |
na ca - jīvasya kartṛtvaṃ praveśamātre paryavasyati | nāma-rūpavyākaraṇaṃ tu brahmaṇa eva – iti śakyaṃ vaktum ; tvāpratyayena samānakartṛkatvapratīteḥ | jīvasya svāṃśatve na svarūpatve'pi parasvarūpavyāvṛtyarthaḥ, 'anena jīvene 'ti "parātvena' parāmarśaḥ | ataḥ hiraṇyagarbhakartṛkā iyaṃ nāma rūpavyākriyā ||
kartṛsthakriya iti | 'vyākaraṇakartṛsthakriyaḥ' iti vivakṣayā, 'samānakartṛkaḥ' ityarthaḥ | praviśatiriti | nā'tra dhātoḥ lākṣaṇikatvamabhimatam api tu taduttarapratyayasya ;- 'kartṛsthakriyaḥ' iti viśeṣaṇāt 'lyabantapraviśatiḥ' ityarthalābhāt ||]

naivamiti | vastuto jīvasya paramātmāṃśatvāt tasya kartṛtvaṃ paramātmana eva bhavati | tasmānmukhya koṭirityarthaḥ |
na ca saheti | 'tṛtīyāyāḥ sahayogādyarthāntaraparatve, paramātmanyeva kartṛtvaṃ syāt ' ityāśaṅkaya (idamuktam ) kārakavibhaktiḥ 'kartṛkaraṇayostṛtīyā' ityādibhiḥ padāntaramanapekṣya vihitā vibhaktiḥ | upapadavibhaktiḥ - padāntarasāhacarye vihitā vibhaktiḥ | anyāyyatvāditi | padāntarasāhacarya sāpekṣatayā vilambitavṛttitvāt anyāyyatvamabhipretam ||

1 kartṛtva - vā. ' uktaṃ paramapuruṣaḥ -mā.

ata 'eva ca ' smṛtiṣu caturmukha' kartṛka' sṛṣṭiprakaraṇe, nāmarūpavyākaraṇaṃ saṅkīrtyate- 'nāmarūpaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ kṛtyānāṃ ca prapañcanam |

evaṃ prāpte, abhidhīyate – saṃjñā - mūrtiklaptistu – iti | 'tu'śabdaḥ pakṣaṃ vyāvarta - yati | saṃjñāmūrtiklaptiḥ – nāma-rūpavyākaraṇam | tat trivṛtkurvataḥ 'parasyaiva' brahmaṇaḥ ; tasyaiva nāma-rūpavyākaraṇopadeśāt | trivṛtkaraṇaṃ kurvata eva hi ! nāma-rūpavyākaraṇa- -mupadiśyate-

iti, 'tejaḥprabhṛti śarīrakaṃ' parameva brahma abhidhīyate | ataḥ jīvasamaṣṭibhūta hiraṇyagarbha- śarīrakasya parasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ karma, nāma rūpavyākaraṇam |

evaṃ ca – 'praviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi' iti praviśatiḥ, uttamapuruṣaśca akliṣṭau mukhyārthāveva bhavataḥ, praveśa-vyākaraṇayoḥ samānakartṛkatvamapyupapadyate | caturmukhaśarīrakasya parasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ karma, devādivicitrasṛṣṭiriti caturmukha'kartṛkasṛṣṭi prakaraṇe nāma- rūpavyākriyopadeśaśca upapadyate ||

ataḥ, 'seyaṃ devate 'tyādivākyasya, ayamarthaḥ – imāḥ – tejo'vannarūpāḥ, tisro devatāḥ, 'anena jīvena' - jīvasamaṣṭiviśiṣṭena, ātmanā anupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākara- vāṇi – devādivicitra' sṛṣṭiṃ tannāmadheyāni ca ' karavāṇi | tadartham, anyonya- saṃsargamaprāptānām eṣāṃ tejo'vannānāṃ viśeṣasṛṣṭayaṃ samarthānāṃ tatsāmarthyāya ekaikāṃ " trivṛtaṃ trivṛtaṃ karavāṇi iti | ataḥ parasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ karma idaṃ nāma rūpa-

vyākaraṇam || 17||

286. māṃsādi bhaumaṃ yathāśabdamitarayośca || 2-4-18||

yaduktam – aṇḍasṛṣṭayuttarakālaṃ caturmukhasṛṣṭadevatādiviṣayo'yam, 'tāsāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtamekaikāmakarot' iti trivṛtkaraṇopadeśaḥ - iti ;
trivṛtkaraṇasya aṇḍasṛṣṭyarthatvaṃ na sambhavati | ataḥ trivṛtkaraṇaṃ caturmukha kartṛkamiti, tadekakartṛkaṃ nāma-rūpavyākaraṇaṃ caturmukha kartṛkamityarthaḥ ||

tannopapadyate ; 'annamaśitaṃ tredhā vidhīyate' ityatra, māṃsa- manasoḥ purīṣāt aṇutvena aṇīyastvena ca vyapadiṣṭayoḥ, kāraṇānuvidhāyitvena āpya - taijasatvaprasaṅgāt, 'āpaḥ pītā:' ityatrāpi, mūtraprāṇayoḥ sthaviṣṭhāṇīyasoḥ 'pārthivatva' - taijasatvaprasaṅgācca ||
na caivamiṣyate ; 'māṃsādi hi !" "bhauma' miṣyate | purīṣavat māṃsa-manasī api bhaume-pārthive iṣyete; 'annamaśitaṃ tredhā' iti prakramāt |

yathāśabdamitarayośca – itarayorapi, 'āpaḥ pītāḥ' 'tejo'śitaṃ' iti paryāyayoḥ, yathāśabdaṃ 'vikārāḥ iṣyante |
'āpaḥ pītāstredhā vidhīyante'
iti, apāmeva ' tredhā' pariṇāmaḥ śabdātpratīyate | " tathā ",
'tejo'śitaṃ tredhā vidhīyate' ityapi, tejasa eva ' tredhā' pariṇāmaḥ śabdātpratīyate | ataḥ, purīṣa- māṃsa-manāṃsi pṛthivīvikārāḥ ; mūtra-lohita- prāṇāḥ abvikārāḥ ; asthi, majjā, vācaḥ tejovikārāḥ - iti pratipattavyam ||

kāraṇeti | atrivṛtkṛtānāmapi pṛthivyaptejasāṃ sthūlatvāṇutvā ṇīyastvāt trivṛtkṛteṣvapi tattadbhūtāṃśānāṃ svakāraṇānuguṇasthūlatvā ṇīyastvaṃ syāt | tasmāt 'annamaśita 'mityādivākye trivṛtkaraṇaṃ pradaśyate cet ! sthaviṣṭhaṃ purīṣaṃ pārthivam; aṇu māṃsam āpyam ; aṇīyo manaśca taijasaṃ syāt | na tathopapadyate ; māṃsa-manasoḥ pārthivatvāt | pārthivatvaṃ ca 'annamaśita ' miti prakramādavagatamityarthaḥ | evam itaraparyāyadvaye'pi |

na caivamiṣyate ityantena, prathama- dvitīya paryāyayoraniṣṭaṃ darśitam | tṛtīye tu na prasaktam | tatrā'yaṃ bhāvaḥ pratiparyāyaṃ caramopātteṣu aniṣṭasya prasañjanīyatvāt | tṛtīyaparyāye carame prasañjanīyasya aniṣṭatvābhāvāt na tat prasañjanīyamiti || tṛtīye'pi asthimajjā pārthivāpya- tvaniṣṭatā paścādvakṣyate | māṃsādi hīti | māṃsa-manasoḥ ādhyatva-taijasatve aniṣṭe 1

dvitīye mūtraṃ pārthivam ; lohitam āpyam ; prāṇa: taijasaḥ syāt | tatra mūtra - prāṇayoḥ pārthivatva- taijasatve aniṣṭe | 'annamaśitam āpaḥ pītā:' iti ca prakrameṇa māṃsa-manasoḥ pārthivatvāt ;

'annamayaṃ hi ! somya manaḥ, āpomayaḥ prāṇaḥ, tejomayī vāk' iti vākyaśeṣāvirodhācca | ataḥ,
'tāsāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtamekaikāmakarot '
' ityuktaḥ ' trivṛtkaraṇaprakāraḥ, 'annamaśita 'mityādinā na pradarśyate | tathā sati, manaḥ- prāṇavācāṃ trayāṇāmapi, aṇīyastvena taijasatvāt,
'annamayaṃ hi somya ! manaḥ' '

ityādirvirudhyate |

prāgeva trivṛtkṛtānāṃ pṛthivyādīnāṃ puruṣaṃ prāptānām, 'annamaśita' mityādinā, ekaikasya tredhā pariṇāma ucyate | aṇḍasṛṣṭeḥ 'prāgeva ca', tejo'vannānāṃ trivṛtkaraṇena bhavitavyam ; atrivṛtkṛtānāṃ teṣāṃ kāryārambhāsāmarthyāt | anyonyasaṃyuktānāmeva hi ! kāryārambhasāmarthyam | tadeva ca trivṛtkaraṇam | tathā ca smaryate-

287. vaiśeṣyāttu tadvādastadvādaḥ || 2-4 -19 ||
vaiśeṣyam – viśeṣabhāvaḥ | trivṛtkaraṇena trirūpe'pi ekaikasmin annādyādhikyāt, pūrva bhāvitvaṃ dṛṣṭam | upabṛṃhaṇe ca tat viśadīkṛtam | tasmāt ' nāma rūpe vyākaravāṇi 'trivṛtaṃ trivṛtamekaikāṃ karavāṇi' iti saṅkalpavākye trivṛtkaraṇaphalasya nāma-rūpavyākaraṇasya pūrvapratipattau trivṛtkaraṇasya pūrva bhāvitvaṃ siddham | 'agnihotraṃ juhoti' 'yavāgūṃ pacati' ityādiṣu arthakrameṇa pāṭhakramabādhadarśanāt | saṅkalpavākyasvābhāvyāt, prākaraṇika saṅkalpavākyāntare paścā- dbhāvinaḥ phalasya pūrvabhāvadarśanāt upabṛṃhaṇavacanasvārasyācca, 'nāma-rūpe vyākarot ' 'tāsāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtamekaikāmakarot' iti pāṭhakramaḥ arthakrameṇa bādhyata ityarthaḥ ||
kriyate | trivṛtkaraṇapradarśana miti śeṣaḥ || 18 ||
vaiśeṣyamiti | 'viśeṣa' śabda: 'vikāra' śabdavat dharmiparo dharmaparaśca | atra tu

trivṛtkṛteṣu bhūteṣu cādhikyaṃ yasya vai bhavet |
tattadvācakaśabdena tattadvastūcyate budhaiḥ ||

brahma paramātmabhūtaṃ vastu, jagatāṃ na hetuḥ — jagatkāraṇaṃ na bhavitumarham, anaghīta- vedārthaviśadīkaraṇapravṛttasya, smṛteḥ pradhāna kāraṇatkhaparakā pilatantrākhyaśāstrasya virodhāt - anavakāśatāpādanarūpād virodhāt itīdam - evamākāraṃ śaṅkanam asāram - adṛḍham, tāvikāṃśa cintanena pravṛttam anyasmṛtibhiḥ virodhāt – jagadveṣajatayā śrutisiddhābhiḥ manvādīnāṃ smṛtibhiḥ saha vyāitiprasaṅgāt | teṣāṃ anāptistu — tādṛśasmṛtikartṝṇāṃ manvādīnāṃ yathārthavāditva-yathāvasthitārthadarśitvobhayātmakasya āptatvasya abhāvastu, na ca śakyaśaṅkā- naiva śanasyāpyana || 1 ||

tattādṛk .... siddhiḥ – vilakṣaṇa vicāraparaparyāyatarkapradhāne mīmāṃsākhyatantrīya prakāraviśeṣe kuśalānāṃ medhāvināṃ (tatvārthanirṇayakṛta) harṣaprāptiḥ, uktena- ādyādhyāyapratipāditena samyagjñānena arthajātena labhyā sambādyā bhavitumarhā, tadapi tathāpi, mṛdudhiyām –- lalitamatīnām, haitukā skandaśaṅkī – śrutyananuguṇatarkamātraparaiḥ vāvadūkaiḥ ākramaṇaṃ sambhāvayan sūtrakāraḥ kathitam – pūrvapratipāditameva, brahmaṇaḥ kāraṇatvam - paramātmanaḥ jagadabhinnanimittopādānakāraṇatvam, yasya – jagadantargatasya cetanasya acetanasya vā, kāryatvam- avasthāntaralābhātmakaṃ janma, yādṛk – svabhāvagataṃ svarūpagataṃ vā vikārarūpaṃ, śrutibhiravagatam - atīndriyārthanirṇayapravṛttaiḥ vedavākyaiḥ pratipannam tasya — ātmākāśa | deḥ

sṛjyajātasya

tattādṛk — avaiparītyena tādṛśameva sthūṇākhātakrameṇa — sthūṇā nikhanananyāyena, sthirayati - svayameva sambhāvanīyakṣobhapradarśanapūrvakaṃ apanītibhirakampyaṃ karoti, ( draḍhayati ) || 1 ||
pādadvandvaṃ dvitīye pariharati pare kāraṇe bāhyapīḍāṃ
kāryadvāreṇa pādāntara 'yugamudaya' tyāntarakṣobhaśāntyai |
hetutvāyogabhaṅgaḥ prathamamiha vibhostasya sārvatrikatvā-
yogakṣepaḥ parastāt phalati sa ca bhavecchrauta nityaṃ vihāya || 2 ||

dvitīye pādadvandvam - dvitīyādhyāyasambandhi ādyaṃ pādadvayam pare kāraṇe-parama- kāraṇabhūtaparabrahmaviṣaye, bāhyapīḍāṃ – avaidikaiḥ udbhāvyamānāṃ bādha-pratirodhātmikāṃ bādhām, bodhayati | pādāntarayugam - dvitīyaṃ pādadvayam, kāryadvāreṇa

ākāśādikaṃ kārya dvārīkṛtya, āntarakṣobhaśāntyai āntaraiḥ vaidikāntaḥ pātibhiḥ, āntareṣu vedāntavākyeṣu vā viṣaye sambhavataḥ kṣobhasya nivṛtyai, udayati- pravartate | (tathā) iha prathamam – etadadhyāyīyādyapādadvike, vibhoḥ - paramātmanaḥ, hetutvāyogabhaṅgaḥ – parairāpādyamāna- kāraṇatvānvayavirodhadūrīkaraṇam parastād paścāttane pādadvaye, tasya – īśvaragata kāraṇatvasya, sārvatrikatvāyogakṣepaḥ sarvajagannirūpitatvānanvaya nirāsaḥ, phalati - vicāraphalatayā siddhyati, sa ca – kāraṇatvāyoganirāsaśca śrautanityaṃ vihāya – nityatvena śrutipratipannaṃ prakṛti-puruṣa- kālādikaṃ tattvajātaṃ nirūpakatvena agṛhītvā bhavet — avaśiṣṭanirūpitatayā paryavasānaṃ labhate || 2 ||

tantracchāyānidāne svayamupaniṣadāmānyaparye 'niṣiddhe' tantrebhyo durbalatvāt tadanusaraṇamityujjihīte 'paro'dya' | itthaṃ satyatra tattatsmṛtinaya' pṛtanātikramaṃ tattada hai:
pratyastrairvārayitvā draḍhayati calitaṃ pādataḥ prācyamartham || 3 ||

tantracchāyā nidāne kāpilatantra sādṛśyadarśanataḥ śaṅkayamāne, upaniṣadāṃ ānyaparye— vedāntavākyānāṃ brahmetarakāraṇatvatātparye, svayaṃ – śarīrakasūtrakṛtaiva, niṣiddhe – ānumānikādhi- karaṇāditaḥ nirākṛte, adya - śārīraka dvitīyādhyāye, paraḥ – sāṅkhyaḥ, tantrebhyaḥ – upabṛṃhaṇatayā avaśyānusaraṇīyebhyaḥ sāṅkhyāditantrebhyaḥ, durbalatvāt – upabṛṃhaṇānāṃ vedāntānāṃ tatprāmāṇya- vighaṭakatārūpabalābhāvāt, tadanusaraṇamiti kā pilatantrānurovyarthakatvaṃ vedāntānāṃ abhyupa- gamyamiti, ujjihīte – pratyavasthito bhavati, itthaṃ sati - evamavasthite, tattatsmṛtinaya pṛtanāti- kramam — vedāntānanurodhi-smṛti-tarkātmaka virodhivarūthakṛtaṃ sīmollaṅghanam, tattada hai: pratyatra :- tattatsmṛti-tarkanirasanakṣamaiḥ pratismṛtitarkaiḥ, vārayitvā – nirudhya cakitam — apanītibhiḥ kampitam, prācyamartham – pūrva pratipāditaṃ brahmaṇa eva sarvakāraṇatvarūpamartham atra pādataḥ- etadadhyāyīyādyacaraṇena draḍhayati–pratiṣṭhāpayati || 3 ||

nirṇītaṃ karmakāṇḍe smṛtinayavihatau niścalatvaṃ śrutīnāṃ

carcā tatsiddhaye'sau punariti viphalaḥ syād dvitīyādyapādaḥ | maivaṃ gambhīranānāśrutiśikharaparicchedyadurbodhatāyā-
mātoktayā tarkataśca kṣamamanusaraṇaṃ paśyato hyatra bhaṅgaḥ || 4 ||

śrutīnāṃ - vedavācām, smṛti - nayavihatau smṛtyā tarkeṇa vā virodhe prāpte sati, niścalatvam – abādhyārthavatvam, karmakāṇḍe – karmavidhāyaka śrutivākyārtha vicārapare jaiminīya- tantre, nirṇītam — virodhādhikaraṇato dṛḍhīkṛtam | tatsiddhaye tasyaivārthasya nirṇayāya, asau- asminnadhyāye kriyamāṇā, punaścarcā - carvitacarvaṇātmikā cintā, iti-ato hetoḥ, dvitīyādyapādaḥ (smṛtipādanāmā ) dvitīyādhyāyīyaḥ prathamaścaraṇaḥ, viphalaḥ syāt — niṣprayojana eva bhavet (iti śaṅkane) ; maivam evaṃ na yujyate, gambhīra.... durbodhatāyām – gūḍhābhiprāyaka- vividhavedāntabākyanirṇeyārthānām durakhagamatvaprasaktau satyām, āptoktayā - yathārthadarśināṃ

kapilādīnāṃ vācā, tarkataśca – tadupakārakaiḥ tadīyapravartitaiḥ tarkaiśca, sahakṛtaṃ, anusaraṇam - paścātkālikaṃ śrutyarthacintanam kṣamaṃ paśyataḥ– yuktamiti āpātaghiyā niścinvato janasya, atra hi-asmin pāda eva khalu bhaṅgaḥ – anaucityakhyāpanayatnaḥ kriyate || 4 ||

dvābhyāṃ smṛtyā virodhaṃ pariharati tatastvaṣṭabhistarkabādhaṃ
tenopādānabhāvaṃ draḍhayati tu vibhoḥ kartṛtāṃ tadvayaṃ ca |
tattatkṣepāt tulāgra 'dvayanamana' samunnāmanītyā pravṛtte
śaṅkāvarge parīkṣāsamanihitamatiḥ pakṣapātaṃ ruṇaddhi || 5 ||

dvābhyām-prathamadvitīyādhikaraṇābhyām, smṛti- yogapratyuktirūpābhyāṃ smṛtyā virodham- yathākramaṃ kāpilasmṛtyā pātañjalasmṛtyā ca bādhitārthakatvam, tataḥ aṣṭabhistu - tadanantara- pravṛttaiḥ prayojanavatvāntaiḥ aṣṭādhikaraṇaiḥ param tarkabādham — brahmaṇa upādānatve āpātapratīyamānaṃ yuktivirodham (tena aprāmāṇyaṃ) ca, pariharati - vastuto'vidyamānatayā jñāpayati | tena- kṛtena ca virodhaparihāreṇa, vibhoḥ – īśvarasya, upādānabhāvam – pariṇāmikāraṇatvarūpaṃ viśvopādānatvam, kartṛtām – sṛṣṭyaupayikasaṅkalpāśrayatvarūpaṃ nimittakāraṇatvam | tadddvayaṃ ca- nimittatvopādānatvobhayamapi ca, draḍhayati – (kvacidadhikaraṇe) sthirayati | tattatkṣepāt tadvā- dyukteṣvartheṣu yathāyogyam ekaikasyārthasya ayuktatvakhyāpanāt, tulāgradvayanamana - samunnāmanītyā- tulākhyadaṇḍasambandhikoṭidvayānyataranimnnonnatabhāvaprāptikrameṇa, pravṛtte - samunmeṣaṃ prāpte, śaṅkāvarge- uttarottara pravṛttā kṣepastome, parīkṣāsamanihitamatiḥ - sādhvasādhutva nirṇaya cintayā avaiṣamyena dattadṛṣṭiḥ sūtrakāraḥ, pakṣapātam — ekaikasmin pakṣe svanikṛṣṭatvabuddhirūpamabhimāna viśeṣam, ruṇaddhi— pratibadhnāti || 5 ||
smartā śrutyaiva gīta: kapilaṛṣirasau vāsudevāṃśabhūtaḥ
khyāto rāmāyaṇādau praṇidhi' nipuṇadhīrvakti' vedāntatattvam | tasmādasmadvidūre śrutiśirasi taduktyaiva niṣkarṣaṇaṃ syā-
nna syādekārthamanvādyanaghabahugirā tatra tattvārthasiddheḥ || 6 ||
smartā—sāṃkhyasmṛteḥ kartā, asau kapilaḥ – prastutaḥ kapilākhyaḥ, śrutyaiva- śvetāśvataroniṣadaiva, ṛṣigītaḥ - atīndriyārthayāthātmyadarśitvena kīrtitaḥ ('ṛṣi prasūtaṃ

|

2 niyatadhīrveti -

kapilam' iti), rāmāyaṇādau - rāmāyaṇa - bhārata - bhāgavateṣu ( itihāsa - purāṇeṣu) vāsudevāṃśabhūtaḥ khya (taḥ — niratiśayajñānādyaiśvaryasampannasya vāsudevākhyasya viṣṇoḥ aṃśarūpo'vatāra iti ca prasiddhaḥ, praṇidhinipuṇadhī : - cittapraṇidhānarūpe yoge pariniṣṭhitamatiḥ, vedāntatattvaṃ vakti- trayyantārthatattvameva svīyayā smṛtyā viśadayati, tasmāt — evaṃ kapilasya āptatamatvāt asmadvidūre—carmacakṣuṣāmasmākaṃ dugrahārthake, śrutiśirasi - ( upabṛṃhaṇasāpekṣatayaivārthanirṇāyake ) vedāntavākyajāte, niṣkarṣaṇam - arthaniścayasampādanam, taduktyaiva syāt – kapiloktimavalambyaiva ghaṭate ; nā'nyathā (ityevaṃ śaṅkane prāte), ekārthamanvādyanaghabahugirā - vedāntena samānārthikā mithaścāviruddhārthikaḻāyā manvādyaṣīṇāṃ nirhṛṣṭā gīḥ nānāsthānasthā vāk tayā tatra — asmadvidūre'pi vedāntavacasi tatvārthasiddheḥ – prāmaṇikārthaniścayalābhāt ; ( upabṛṃhaṇārthaṃ kāpilasmṛtirnā'nu- dhāvanīya| ) || 6 ||
