adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ (yoga- adhi)

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ (vila - adhi)

viśvaṃ traiguṇya 'vatvāt ' triguṇata uditaṃ nā'samādityayuktaṃ sarvākāreṇa sāmyaṃ kacidapi na bhavet kenacit sāmyamiṣṭam | bhagnā hetuvyavasthocitaguṇasamatā gomayāvṛścikā dau

sthūlatvaṃ yāti ceśaḥ prakṛtitanurataḥ sarvacodyopamardaḥ || 8 || jagat traiguṇyavattvāt — samvādiguṇatrayāśrayatvāddhetoḥ,

viśvam — idaṃ -

triguṇata

uditam — traiguṇyavataḥ pradhānākhyatatvādeva jātam, asamāt – vilakṣaṇa : brahmaṇaḥ, noditam - na jātam, ityayuktam - īdṛśaṃ śaṅkanaṃ yuktidūram kvacidapi yasmin kasminnapi vā vastuni sāmyam – samānalakṣaṇatvamityetat sarvākāreṇa prakārībhūtaṃ sarvāṃśamapyādāya, na bhavet naiva sambhāvitam, iṣṭaṃ sāmyam – bubodhayiṣitaṃ sālajhaṇyamapi, kenacit – yatkiñcitprakāramādāyaiva | hetu vyavasthocitaguṇasamatā – (bhavatsaṃmatā ) kāraṇatvanirṇayaupayikī guṇataḥ salakṣaṇatā ca, gomayācikādau gopurīṣaprabhave droṇākhye kīṭaviśeṣe, tathā anyatra ca, bhannā— naṣṭaniyamā, prakṛtitanuḥ– sūkṣmāvasthacidaciccharīrako'vasthitaḥ, īśaḥ – paramātmā, sthūlatvaṃ yāti — sthūla- cidaciccharīrakatāṃ pratipadyate, ataḥ uktāddhetoḥ, sarvacodyopamardaḥ sarvākṣepanirāso bhavati ||8||

0-

nayasaṅgatimālikā (yoga aghi)
yogasmṛtyapavāde tu pūrvanyāyātideśataḥ |
yoga pratyuktatyadhikṛtiḥ saṅgatistvātideśikī || 73 || iti yogapratyuktatyadhikaraṇam || 2 ||

yat — ghaṭādikaṃ yadvastu, yatkāryam - yādṛśamṛdādyupādānakam, tat — mṛdādikāryarūpaṃ ghaṭādikam, tatpratibhaṭaguṇakaṃ na - kāraṇabhūtamṛdvṛttidharmavirodhidharmakaṃ na bhavati, brahma ca idaṃ viśvaṃ ca - upādānatayā pratipannaṃ brahma tathā upādeyatayā pratipannaṃ jagacceti dvayam jñatvā jñatvādidharmaiḥ – jñānāśrayatva - tadanāśrayatva pramukhaiḥ dharmaiḥ mitho viruddhasvarūpam, atha īkṣaṇāt cetanatvaṃ cet — teja ādiṣu śrutāt cetanāsādhāraṇāt īkṣaṇāddharmāt cetanatvaṃ saṃmanyate yadi, tat — īkṣaṇasamānādhikaraṇaṃ cetanatvam, tāsāṃ devatānām – anādipadavācyānāmabādyabhimāni- devatānāṃ sambandhyeva; na kevalābādisambandhi iti tu — īdṛśaṃ śaṅkanaṃ tu na caturam – na kauśalakṛtam, mākṣikādeḥ - madhvāderdravyāt, kṛmyādidravyabhedo dṛśyate—madhuno vijātīyaḥ kṛmyādirūpo dravyaviśeṣaḥ utpadyate ityetat lokasiddhaṃ khalu tat — sajātīyatvanaiyatyamityetat, kārya- hetvoḥ kāryakāraṇayoḥ, yadi na bhavati nāsti cet, alaṃ syāt – (nirapekṣatayā ) kāraṇatvaṃ siddhayet || 3 ||

īkṣā tākū bahusyāmiti sati paṭhitā tejaso'pāṃ ca draṣṭā sālakṣaṇyaṃ tataḥ syājjagata iti mṛduprajñapārśvastha codye |
tattanmūrteḥ parasyekṣaṇamidamiti tadvākyabhāvāpalāpī
sāmānyenābhimānivyavaharaṇamiha vyāharat pūrvapakṣī || 9 ||

sati paṭhitā — sacchabdavācyabrahmaviṣaye āmnātā, bahu syāmiti – bahurūpaṃ bhaveya- mityā kārikā, yā, īkṣā - saṅkalpātmakā īkṣaṇakriyā, tādṛk - tādṛśī mukhyaiva saṅkalparūpekṣaṇa- kriyā, tejasaḥ apāṃ ca draṣṭā - tejo jalagatatayāpi 'teja aikṣata', 'āpa aikṣanta' iti paṭhitā | tataḥ tasmāt (jagato brahmaṇaśca mukhyekṣaṇavatvāt', jagataḥ sālakṣaṇyamiti viśvasya brahmaṇā saha samānalakṣaṇatvameva na vilakṣaṇatvamiti, mṛduprajñapārśvasthacodye- mandamateḥ nikaṭavartinaḥ śaṅkane, pravṛtte, tattanmūrtaḥ– jala- teja ādiśarīrakasya parasya – paramātmana eva, idamīkṣaṇa- miti - 'āpa aikṣanta', 'teja aikṣata', ityuktaṃ saṅkalpanamitīdṛśaṃ tadvākyabhāvāpalāpī- tādṛśavākyāśayama potukāmaḥ, pūrvapakṣī etadadhikaraṇapūrvapakṣī --- sāmānyena - viśeṣarūpaṃ vihāya, abhimānivyavaharaṇam – abhimānitvarūpasāmānyadharmanirdeśarūpatvam

iha — asmintradhikaraṇe, vyāharat – uktavān || 9 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
saṃvādā' dakṣapāda' kṣapaṇaka - kaṇabhug- bhikṣupakṣeṣvaṇūnāṃ viśvaṃ taddhetukaṃ syāditi mṛdumatibhiḥ śvāvarāha' kra moktau | anyonyavyāhatārtha sthapuṭita kuhanā yuktidoṣāpanutyai
bhāti trayyantasūryaḥ pratimatatimira stoma kukṣimbharirnaḥ || 10||

akṣapāda .... bhikṣupakṣeṣu gautama - jina kaṇāda sugatānāṃ mateṣu, aṇūnāṃ saṃvādāt- paramāṇu kāraṇatāviṣaye abhiprāyaikyāt, viśva–jagat, taddhetukaṃ syāt – paramāṇu kāraṇakameva bhavet, iti mṛdumatibhiḥ śvāvarāhakramauktau — evamākārakamandaprajñakartṛkāyāṃ śvāvarāhāvalambinyāṃ vāci pravṛttāyāṃ satyām ( svayūthakalahaṃ vismṛtya svaśatruṣu sambhūya pratibhaṭanaṃ - śvāvarāhanyāyaḥ) anyonya.... panutyai—mithoviruddhārthakṛta vaiṣamyavatkapaṭayuktirūpāyā: niśāyāḥ pratimata....mbhariḥ aupaniṣada virodhimatātma katamojāla kavalana (nāśana)kṣama:, trayyantasūrya:- vedāntākhyaḥ ādityaḥ, naḥ asmākaṃ purataḥ, bhāti - ujvalaḥ san prakāśate || 10 ||

apahārāya,

bhoktrāpattyadhikaraṇam || 2-1-5 ||
nayasaṅgatimālikā

kośabhājām - prameyārthānāṃ sarveṣāṃ surakṣitasaṅgraharūpāṇāṃ śrutīnāṃ sambandhinīm jīveśasīmām- jīvaparamātmanoḥ svarūpasvabhāvamaryādām, apalapitumanāḥ – apahnotuṃ pravṛtto janaḥ, iha — jīva- parayordehasambandhavatorviṣaye, samrāḍ- bhṛtyādinītyā - ekasmin rājabhavane eva nivasato: rāja-tadbhṛtyayoḥ śāsya-śāsakabhūtayoḥ nyāyamanusṛtya, sāmyavaiṣamyadarśī – śarīrāśritatvena. sāmyam, niyantṛtva-niyāmyatvādinā vaiṣamyaṃ ca paśyan śamam — paramātmanaḥ śarīrasambandha-- mātreṇa bhoktatvāpādanayatnavarjanarūpaṃ śamam labhatām - prāpnutām || 11 ||

bhedakaiḥ - itaretara bhedajñāpakaiḥ, buddhayā gaṇanaiḥ kāryakāraṇaviṣayakaiḥ vilakṣaṇabuddhi- vilakṣaṇākāra - vilakṣaṇavācakaśabda - vilakṣaṇavyavahāra - vilakṣaṇa saṃkhyānarūpairathaiḥ, kāryahetvo:- paṭatantvādyātmakayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhūtayorarthayoḥ, ekatvaṃ na siddhayet — mitho'nanyatvaṃ naiva niścetu- maham, kārakānarthatā ca – kāryasya kāraṇānanyasya pūrvameva siddhatayā tantuvāyādikārakavyāpāra- vaiphalyamapi iti tu — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ tu na caturam-na nipuṇacintanam, yena – yasmāt kāraṇāt, sad.... ātmādiśabdaiḥ abhihitam - (kāryabhūtaṃ jagat ) kāraṇabhūtabrahmavācibhiḥ śabdaiḥ 'sadevedam', 'brahmaivedam', 'ātmā vā idam ityādibhiḥ pratipāditam ; tasmāt, apṛthak — kāraṇādananyadeva kāryam, śabdādibheda: kāraṇāpekṣayā kārye vilakṣaṇaśabdādiśva, dravyāvasthāvalambī dravyagataṃ avasthā vailakṣaṇyamavalambya pravartate, tataḥ tasmādeva hetoḥ, (avasthābhedasya sādhyatvādeva ca, iha - kāryagate avasthābhede jananīye, kārakānarthatā ca na bhavet — kāraka vyāpārāvalambanavaiphalyaṃ ca naiva sambhāvyam || 5 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
kārya dharmairviruddhaiḥ 'kaṭa' iva śakaṭāt kāraṇadravyato'nyad- vyāpāraḥ kārakāṇāṃ viphala itarathetyardhavaināśi' ko ktau " dravyaikye'pyastu sarvaṃ tadabhimatadaśā bhedato'sacchratiśce-
tyadhyakṣāllāghavācca śrutikathitajagadbrahmatādātmyamuktam || 12 ||

kāryam – utpādyaṃ vastu, viruddhaiḥ dharmaiḥ – buddhiśabdāntarādibhiḥ mithovyāvṛttaidharmaiḥ jñāpakabhūtaḥ, kāraṇadravyato'nyat — utpādakāt dravyādatiriktam, virudvaiḥ dharmaiḥ – upaveśana- sādhanatva - yānasādhanatvādibhiḥ mithovyāvartakaiḥ dharmaiḥ kaṭa iva śakaṭāt — kaṭarūpo'rtho yathā śakaṭarūpādarthādanyaḥ tadvat itarathā—kāryasya kāraṇādananyatve, kārakāṇāṃ vyāpāraḥ viphala iti kāryanirvāhakatvena siddhānāṃ kartṛkaraṇādikārakāṇāṃ kriyāviśeṣaḥ aprayojanaḥ syādityevam, ardhavaināśikoktau tārkikasya vacane pravṛtte dravyaikye'pi kāryakāraṇadravyayoḥ paṭa - tantvādyoḥ ananyatve'pi tadabhimatadaśā medataḥ tārkikāṇāmapyabhimatāt kāryagatāt avasthā viśeṣādeva, sarvamapi astu – buddhiśabdāntarādi sarvamapi nirvyūḍhaṃ bhavatu nāma, asacchratiśceti – 'asadeveda- magra āsīt' iti śrutirapi tatheti hetoḥ, (uttaratra jāyamānāvasthāviśeṣeṇa asatvāt ) adhyakṣāt

paṭatvāvasthāyāmapi tantorananyatāyāḥ paṭe pratyakṣatvāt, lāghavācca - kārye kāraṇadravya bhedāvasthā- bhedo bhayamakalpayitvā avasthā bhedamātrasya kalpane ' dharmikalpanāto varaṃ dharmakalpanā' itīdṛśanyāyānu- rodhakṛtāt lāghavādapi śrutikathita.... tādātmyam – 'sadeva somyedaṃ' iti śrutipratipāditaṃ brahma-jagatorananyatvam, uktam - ' tadananyatva.... ' iti sūtre viśadīkṛtam || 12 || māyopādhisvaśakti vyati karitaparabrahmamūlaḥ prapañco

yeṣāṃ te'pyadvitīyaśrutimavitathayantyatra tattadviśiṣṭe |
aprādhānyāt tathā na prakṛtipuruṣayorantarātmapradhāne
vākye'smin sthūla-sūkṣmānvaya iti jagato'nanyabhāvopapattiḥ || 13 ||

mate,

vyaktayullāsau – ( kāraṇa tirohitatayā vidyamānasyaiva) prādurbhāvam, mukulasya puṣpatvamiva vikāsaviśeṣam, visṛṣṭim – (bhastrāyāmantaravasthitasya vastuno bahiravasthāpanamiva) nigīrṇasya bahirvisarjanam vikṛtim – ( mṛdūpeṇāvasthitasya ghaṭarūpa- tāpattimiva ) brahmaṇo jagadrūpatāpattim, tattvapaṅktau – tatvaśreṇipraviṣṭe vastujāte, aniyatāṃ sṛṣṭim - niyamarahitāṃ (kālaviśeṣe tejaso jalasya, kālaviśeṣe ca jalāt tejasa iva ca) sṛṣṭimapi ca tattadvā... kharasa iti tasmiṃstasmin śrutivākyakhaṇḍe akleśena pratipanno'rtha iti ca mudhā kalpayantaḥ - śrutyanabhimatamapi svabuddhayā āropayantaḥ, mugdhāstu — mandaprajñāstu, sarvaśru .... praṇayibhiḥ – nikhilopaniṣadai kakaṇṭhyabaddhā daraiḥ, tatvavidbhiḥ – anāropitārthacintakaiḥ, adharīcakrire – udāsitāḥ tatvajñā iti nābhyupagatāḥ || 14 ||

prasavam

acidākhyaśaktiviśeṣasya

yataḥ yena hetunā, tattvamasyādivākyam – 'tatvamasi', 'ayamātmā brahma' ityādi- śrutivākyam, jīvasya – jīvātmana eva brahmabhāvam – brahmasvarūpatām, vyapadiśati – spaṣṭaṃ brūte, tasmāt — tena hetunā, duḥkhaikakandam – duḥkhamātra nidānam jagat – viśvam, na hi sṛjati naiva khalu utpādayati ( īśvaraḥ ), kaḥ ko vā prekṣāvān, khaduḥkhābhilāṣī – ātmano duḥkhe icchāvān, ( evaṃ śaṅkane prāpte) etat naiva-uktamidaṃ naivocitam yena yena hetunā, tayo: ....vākyam - ' tayoranya: piplulaṃ svādvatti anaśnannanyo abhicākaśīti' ityevamādi śrutivākyam, bhedaṃ prakaṭayati- jīvātmanaḥ brahmaṇo'tiriktatāmeva spaṣṭayati, yathā cetanaikyopadeśaḥ –cetanabhūtayoḥ jīveśvarayoḥ aikyopadeśaḥ yathā śrutau vartate tadvat, dehātma- tvādibhedaśravaṇamapi – śarīra - śarīribhāvātmakasya bhedasyāpi upadeśa: śrutāveva śrūyate || 6 ||

nirṇayāt, tadapi — tatsthatvamapi brahmaṇa:, cidacitoḥ– cetanācetanayoḥ, taccharīratvasiddheḥ- paramātmānaṃ prati śarīratāyāḥ antaryāmibrāhmaṇāditaḥ pratipatteḥ || 15 ||

- -0-

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ (upasaṃ - adhi)
śaktau kartṛ - prakṛtyorupakaraṇagaṇopasthitau kāryakṛtvaṃ tasmāda sadekaṃ kimupakaraṇayedityasacchaktibhedāt | kṣīrāyaskāntalatā tridaśamunimukhān vīkṣya toṣṭavyamasmin

saṅkalpādeva jīvo nudati nijavapurviśvarūpastatheśaḥ || 16 ||

śaktau —samarthatāyāṃ satyām, upakaraṇagaṇopasthitau – sahakārikāraṇakalāpasamavadhāne ca sati, kartṛ - prakṛtyoḥ – nimittopādānakāraṇayoḥ, kāryakṛtvaṃ svakāryajanakatvaṃ loke dṛṣṭamasti, natvanyathā ; tasmāt — kāraṇasāmānyasya upakaraṇasāpekṣatvāt, agre-pralaye, sṛṣṭeḥ prākkāle, sat-satpadā bhidheyaṃ brahma, ekam - svetararāhityenāvasthitam kimupakaraṇayet — kiṃ vā vastu svopakaraṇatayā upādātuṃ śaknuyāt : (ato brahma naiva jagataḥ kāraṇam ), ityasat — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ naiva sādhu, śaktibhedāt – upakaraṇāntaranairapekṣye'pi kāryajananānukūlyarūpāyāḥ vilakṣaṇaśakterapi satvāt, kṣīrayaskā... mukhān - kṣīraṃ dadhyātmanā pariṇāmi, ayaskāntaṃ ayasa ākarṣakam, lūtā mukhāt tantuprasaraṇaśīlā, devāḥ ṛṣayo'pi ca svasaṅkalpamātreṇa vicitrakāryakaraṇasamarthāḥ, tānetān vilakṣaṇaśaktimataḥ, vīkṣya – samyak pariśīlya, asmin - brahmaṇaḥ abhinnanimitto- pādānatve, toṣṭavyam — niḥśaṅkatayā sthātavyam jīvaḥ – sādhāraṇaḥ cetano'pi, nijavapuḥ - svīyaṃ śarīram saṅkalpādeva - mānasika vyāpāramātrādeva, nudati - prerayati khalu, tathā- tadvat, viśvarūpaḥ - viśvarūpatvāddhetoḥ īśaḥ – īśvaro'pi saṅkalpādeva viśva sṛjati || 16 ||

-0-

kṛtsnaprasaktyadhikaraṇam || 2-1-9||

śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ

brahma syāt kāryaśeṣaṃ nikhilamavayavābhāvataḥ sāṃśatā cedū-
vyākopaḥ syādidānīmavayavavirahaśrāviṇīnāṃ śrutīnām | hetutvaṃ tasya tasmād vihatamubhayathetyetadapyāttasāraṃ
bailakṣaṇyātprakārācchratibhirapi tathā jñāpanācchaktimatvāt || 8 ||

(brahmaṇo jagatkāraṇatve) avayavābhāvataḥ tasya niraṃśatvāt, nikhilaṃ brahmakṛtsna- mapi brahma, kāryaśeṣaṃ syāt - kāryabhūta jagadātmanaiva avaśiṣyeta ; (dhyeyatvādyarha naiva śiṣyeta ) sāṃśatā cet ! —tadarthaṃ tasya sāvayavatvopagamaścat kriyeta, idānīm - asyāmavasthāyām, avayava.... ṇīnām - niraṃśatvapratipādakānābhū, śrutīnām - 'niṣkalam' ityādīnāṃ vedagirām, vyākopaḥ syāt – bādhitārthatā bhavet tasmāt uktahetoḥ, ubhayathā - sāvayavatve niravayavatve ca, tasya hetutvaṃ vihatam - brahmaṇa: jagatkāraṇatvaṃ bhagnam ityetadapi āttasāram evaṃ śaṅkanamapi durbalam, vailakṣaṇyāt – itaravisajātīyatā rūpāt prakārāt — brahmāsādhāraṇāddharmāt (ukta- doṣāprasakteḥ) śrutibhirapi — brahmasvarūpavarṇanaparābhiḥ vedāntavāgbhirapi tathā jñāpanāt- sakaletaravisajātīyatayaiva brahmasvarūpasyopadeśāt śaktimatvāt – loke vede'pi ca sarveṣā - madhyarthānāṃ mitho vilakṣaṇaśaktimatvadarśanāt, brahmago'pi sarvādhika sāmarthyaśālitvāt | ato jagatkāraṇatā nirbādheti ) || 8 ||

pariṇamati tadā, niṣkalatvaśrutivihatiḥ - brahmaṇo niravayavatāvodhakānāṃ śrutīnāṃ 'niṣkalam " ityādīnāṃ bādhaḥ syāt, idam - kṛtsnaikadeśavikalpāyattaṃ dūṣaṇam, viśiṣṭe'pi tasmin syāt – cenanācetanaviśiṣṭe māyāviśiṣṭe vā brahmaṇi jagadupādānatayā svīkṛte'pi samānatayā prāpnuyādeva | evaṃ uktadoṣāparihāre, brahmopādānatā na ghaṭate - brahmaṇaḥ viśvaṃ prati pariṇāmikāraṇatvātmakamupādānatvaṃ na susthaṃ bhavati iti cet na evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na yuktam, svapakṣeṣu sāmyāt - pradhāna-paramāṇvādikāraṇatāvādināṃ pakṣeṣvapi asya doṣasya samatvāt, tanmānāt tadgṛhītau — pradhāna- paramāṇvādeḥ jagatkāraṇatvasādhanaupayikāt anumānāt kāraṇatva- niścaye kartavyatayā āpatite sati, 'tattadarthasādhakapramāṇāt tattadarthaniścayaḥ kāryaḥ' ityaṃśe sudṛḍhe sati (iti vā ), tadapi — brahmaṇa eva jagadupādānatvamapi śrutimitam — pramāṇatve sarvādhikayā śrutyā nirṇītatvādeva, lokavat -- pratyakṣadṛṣṭamiva abādhena, svīkuruṣva – abhyupagaccha | ( na tatra vimatiṃ kuru) || 17 ||

saṃyogākhyaṃ hi kāryaṃ vibhu taditarayoḥ syādaṇūnāṃ mitho vā
kāsmrtsnyenāṃśena vā tadvihṛtamiti vadan śūnyavāde nimajjet sāṃkhyo'pi prāha vibhvīṃ prakṛtimiti kathaṃ nyūnasṛṣṭistataḥ syāt
māyādiṣvevamūhyaṃ nigamanigaditā tvakṣatā paddhatinaḥ || 18 ||
saṃyogākhyaṃ kāryam—saṃyogapadābhidheyaṃ kāryam, hi – yataḥ, vibhu taditarayoḥ pari- cchedarahitasya īśvarasya tadbhinnasya aṇvāderapi, (adhiṣṭhātradhiṣṭhānabhāvena) aṇūnāṃ vā mithaḥ syāt - paramāṇūnāmeva vā parasparaṃ sambhavet, tat - saṃyogākhyaṃ kāryam, kārtsnyena aṃśena vā vihatam — (vibhuṣvaṇuṣu ca ) sarvāṃśeneti kiñcidaśeneti ca bādhitameva bhavati, (sarvāṃśeneti cet aṇorapi vibhutvāpattiḥ ; kiñcidaṃśeneti cet vibhorapyaṇutvāpattiriti ) iti vadan - evaṃvādī vaiśeṣikaḥ śūnyavāde nimañjet — mādhyamika bauddhānāṃ sarvaśūnyatāvāde eva svasiddhāntaṃ parisamāpa- yet, sāṃkhyo'pi – kapilānusāryapi, prakṛtim – pradhānāparaparyāyāṃ mūlaprakṛtim vibhvīṃ prāha- aparicchinnāmeva vadati, iti-anena hetunā, tataḥ - vibhvyāḥ prakṛteḥ, (pañcamī) nyūnasṛṣṭiḥ kathaṃ syāt paricchinnapariṇāmasya kāryajātasyotpattiḥ yuktisiddhā kathaṃ bhavet ? māyādiṣu—māyā-śakti-upādhiṣu brahmaviśeṣaṇatāsvīkartṝṇāṃ mateṣvapi, evamūhyam – sāṃkhyā di- nirasanavidhayaiva nirasanaṃ ālocanīyam | naḥ paddhatistu brahmaṇo nimittopādānatāṃ abhyupa- gacchatāṃ asmākaṃ saraṇistu, nigamanigaditā akṣatā - śrutyaiva nirṇītā satī nirābādhā || 18 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

kṛtsnaprasaktayadhikaraṇe - niravayavasya brahmaṇaḥ kāraṇatve, kṛtsnasyāpi brahmaṇaḥ ekakāryopayuktatva- "prasaṅgāt brahmakāraṇatvaṃ asamañjasam — ityāśakya,

brahmaṇo niravayavatvasya vicitra jagatkāraṇatvasya ca śrutipratipannatvāt śrutipratipannārthasya yuktayā bādhāyogācca, brahmakāraṇatvaṃ samañjasam — ityuktam ||

-0-

viṣamasṛṣṭau karmasāpekṣatālābhasya, anāditvataḥ aniṃdaprathamatvena hetunā, vaiṣamyādi ca nāsti —– jīvaviśeṣe pakṣapātaḥ niṣkaruṇatvaṃ ityāderabhāvo'pi sphuṭaḥ || 9||

-0-

viśvam — jagat sadā-sarge sarge, duḥkhaikatānaṃ viṣamamapi — prātikūlyamayatayā vaiṣamya bahulatayāpi ca nirmimāṇasya sṛjata īśvarasya, līlā sṛṣṭyātmikā līlā, asamañjakramataḥ —— asamañjanāmnaḥ sagaraputrasya hiṃsāvihārasadṛśī, sañjāyeteti — pariṇatā syāditi, nirdayatvādidoṣo bhavet – (sraṣṭuḥ īśvarasya) akāruṇyapakṣapātau prasajyeyātām, - iti śaṅkane,

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

prayojanavatvādhikaraṇe—brahmaṇa: avāptasamasta kāmasya prayojanābhāvāt jagatsraṣṭṛtvaṃ na

sambhavati ityukte,

avāptasamasta kāmasyāpi ·lī|lārūpaprayojanasambhavāt sraṣṭṛtvaṃ saṃbhavati iti parihāraḥ |

niṣprayojanatvataḥ, utthitam - āpatitaṃ ākṣepam paryaharat – (smṛtyadhikaraṇamārabhya prayojana- vastvādhikaraṇāntaiḥ daśabhiradhikaraṇaiḥ parihṛtavān sūtrakāraḥ || 10-11 ||
dvābhyāṃ smṛtyavirodhaḥ syāccheṣairnyāyāvirodhitā | caturthapañcamau tatra tṛtīyoktau prasaṅgataḥ || 12 ||
tasmāt ṣaṣṭhaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca pāde'smin saṅgate mate |
ṣaṣṭhādayastu catvāraḥ saṅgacchante yathākramam || 13 ||

dvābhyām - smṛti- yogapratyuktayadhikaraṇābhyām, smṛtyavirodhaḥ syāt — kāpila- hairaṇyagarbhasmṛtivirodhasya vedāntārthapratirodhakatvābhāvaḥ bhavet | śeṣaiḥ – avaśiṣṭaḥ aṣṭabhiradhi- karaṇaiḥ, nyāyāvirodhitā vedāntārthasya yuktivirodhakṛtabādhyatvābhāvaḥ tatra - eteṣvaṣṭasu, caturtha - paścamau – śiṣṭāparigraha - bhoktā pattinyāyau, tṛtīyoktau – vilakṣaṇatvādhikaraṇoktāveva, prasaṅgataḥ prasaktārthavivaraṇātmakatayā yujyete || 12 ||

tasmāt pūrvoktāddhetoḥ, ṣaṣṭhaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca - ārambhaṇa- vilakṣaṇatvādhikaraṇe ubhe api, asmin pāde – ādye smṛtipāde, saṅgate mate- ākṣepākhyasaṅgatyā mithaḥ sāṅgatyayukte samate | ṣaṣṭhādayaḥ catvārastu - ārambhaṇādhikaraṇata ārabhya kṛtsnaprasakttayantāḥ nyāyāstu yathākramaṃ saṅgacchante—vinyāsakramamanurudhyaiva saṅgatā bhavanti || 13 ||
tacchāyavākyairutthānaṃ tathānyāyena codbhavaḥ |
iti pūrveṇa pādena saṅgatiḥ syāt suśobhanā | 14 |
tacchāyavākyaiḥ—sāṃkhyābhimatārthapratipādanaparairiva bhāsamānaiḥ vedāntavākyaiḥ brahmaṇo jagatkāraṇatvavighaṭakatvena śaṅkayamānaiḥ (prathamādhyāyāyasya tu pādasya ) utthānaṃ - udaya:, tathā- tādṛśyaiva rītyā, nyāyena kāryasau sādṛśyasya pradhāne eva vidyamānatayā brahmakāraṇatvapratirodhakaṃ yuktyābhāsaṃ hetukṛtya, (dvitīyādyapādasya ) udgamaḥ – udaya:, iti evaṃ rītyā, pūrvapādena- ādyaturyeṇa, saṅgatiḥ – dvitīyādyapādasya kāryaikyarūpā saṅgatiḥ, suśobhanā syāt – atyantaṃ samīcīnā bhavet ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ

sāṃkhyasmṛtyā virodhāt vidhimatavihateḥ kāryavairūpyato'smin
ekārthāne katantroditavihatatayā dehabhogāviyuktyā |
kāryopādānabhedāt svahitavihatitaḥ kārakastomahāne:
kṛtsnāṃśādyūhabādhāt kṛtiviphalatayā'pyutthitaṃ pratyavidhyat || 22 ||

sāṃkhyasmṛtyā virodhāt - ( vedāntavākyānāṃ) kāpilasmṛtyā saha virodhāt vidhi- matavihateḥ hiraṇyagarbhasaṃmatena yogaśāstreṇa virodhāt, kāryavairūppataḥ sṛjyasya jagataḥ brahmaṇaḥ sakāśāt acetanatvakṛtavailakṣaṇyāt, ekārthānekatantroditavihatatayā - ( jagatkāraṇa viṣaye ) samānābhiprāyaka bahudarśanoktārthavyāhatārthakatayā, dehabhogā viyuktyā – śarīrasambandhāyattasukhaduḥkha- saṃvedanasya brahmaṇi avarjanīyatayā, kāryopādānabhedāt – upādeyopādānabhūtayorarthayoḥ paṭa- tantvoriva mithobhedasya āvaśyakatvāt, strahitavihatitaḥ - jīvā bhinnena brahmaṇā duḥkhamaya jagatsṛṣṭau svahitavighātaprasaṅgāt, kārakastomahāneḥśaktasya kāryajanane āvaśyaka sāmagryanupādānakṛtāt kāraṇatvavighaṭanāt, kṛtsnāṃśāha bādhāt - brahmaṇaḥ jagatpariṇāmaḥ kṛtsnataḥ ? ekadeśato vā ? iti vikalpakṛtāt ubhayānupapannatvāt, kṛtivikalatayāpi - brahmaṇo jagadvyāpārataḥ svapara- prayojanābhāvenāpi ca, utthitaṃ pravṛttaṃ brahmaṇo jagatkāraṇatvābhāvaśaṅkanaṃ pratyavidhyat - sūtrakṛt nirākarot || 22 ||

|| śrīḥ ||

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ | śriyai namaḥ | śrīdharāya namaḥ |
dvitīyādhyāye dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ

racanānupapattyadhikaraṇam || 2-2-1 ||

śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ

punarapi vītarāgaḥ khyātyādivimukhaḥ, atra asmin dvitīye pāde, kim- kimartham, paraparīvādaṃ vadati – tantrāntarīyān prati dūṣaṇān kathayati, ityayuktam — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na sādhu, prakhyātaiḥ prācyaiḥ – prasiddhaiḥ pūrvakālataḥ pravṛttaiḥ, naikaiḥ pratisamayaiḥ – nānāvidhaiḥ viruddha pakṣaiḥ, bhavataḥ — utpatsyataḥ, mandasandehasya śāntyai— alpaprajñānāṃ viparītakoṭiprāmāṇikatā saṃśayasya niravaśeṣatvāpattaye, tulyatvabhrānti siddhā – itaratantrāṇāṃ vedāntena sākaṃ samaprāmāṇyabhramato niṣpannā, prakaraṇasamatā - śrutyukte'rthe satpratipakṣitatvaśaṅkā, tarkapādena vāryā tarkapādanāmnā dvitīyādhyāya dvitīya caraṇena pariharaṇīyā bhavati || 1 ||

ṣaḍviṃśālambi sāṃkhyaṃ sphuṭatarapaṭhitaṃ bhārate yogatulyaṃ

tatsthatvāt pañcaviṃśastviti ca 'nigaditaḥ sarvatattvāntarātmā | tasmāt seśānatantraṃ śrutisamadhigataṃ sthāpayitvātha sūtraiḥ

pakṣaṃ tvīśānapekṣaṃ pratibadati nayairutkaṭa pratyavāyam || 2 ||

bhārate— (pañcamavedākhye) mahābhārate, paḍḍvaṃśāvalambi – ṣarviśakaṃ paramapuruṣamadhya - lambamānam, sāṃkhyam – pañcaviṃśatirūpaniyāmyatatva saṃkhyānataḥ pravṛttam sāṃkhyaśāstram, yoga- tulyam – yogaśāstreṇa samatayā, sphuṭaparipaṭhitam - asandigdhaṃ kaṇṭhata evoktamāste | tatsthatvāt – pañcaviṃśa ke jīvatatve śarīritayā avasthitatvāddhetoḥ, sarvāntarātmā - sarva jagadanta-- ryāmī paramātmā, pañcaviṃśastviti – tattvasaṃkhyāne pañcaviṃśaka eveti, nigaditaḥ niścitya pratipāditaḥ, tasmāt – ukta ddhetoḥ, seśānatantram – seśvaraṃ sāṃkhyatantram, tatsadṛśaṃ yogatantra ca, śrutisamadhigataṃ sthāpayitvā vedapratipannameveti samarthya, atha nayaiḥ sūtraḥ etatpādīyaiḥ adhikaraṇasūtraiḥ, īśānaṣekṣaṃ pakṣa tu — nirīśvarasāṃkhyapakṣameva, utkaṭapratyavāyaṃ prativadati- anatilaṅghanīyapāpāvahatayā tyājyamiti pratyākhyāti bādarāyaṇaḥ || 2 ||

avyaktādīn padārthānanumitimukhataḥ ' sthāpayantaḥ svabuddhayā ṣaṭtriṃśattatvavādaprabhṛtiṣu na kathaṃ saṅgamicchanti sāṃkhyāḥ | tulyākṣepopattī: śrutiniyatimucā kalpanāḥ saṃmṛśantaḥ
dṛṣṭaṃ nāpanuvīran laghumanuminuyuḥ śeṣamicchanti śāstrāt || 3 ||

avyaktādīna padārthān – 'mūlaprakṛtiḥ, mahān, ahaṅkāraḥ, tanmātrāṇi' ityevamādīn

parigaṇyamāṇān tasvātmakān padārthān, anumiti mukhataḥ anumānaṃ dvāratayopādāya, svabuddhayā - śrutyananurodhinyā, svacchandayā dhiyā sthāpayantaḥ samarthayantaḥ sāṅkhyā:- sāṅkhyatayā loke prathitāḥ tāntrikāḥ, ṣaṭtriṃśattatvavādaprabhṛtiṣu - pāśupata - vaiśeṣikā dipari- gṛhīteṣu ṣaṭtriṃśattatvavāda - saptapadārthavādādiṣu anyānya tattatsamaya siddheṣvartheṣu saṅgam— svābhyupagamarūpaṃ sambandham, necchanti katham - nābhilaṣanti ityetat kathaṃ vā yujyate ?, tulyākṣepopattīḥ- ākṣepa samādhānayoḥ sāmyavatīḥ, śrutiniyatimucāṃ kalpanāḥ vedamaryādāti- laṅghināṃ bāhyasāmayikānāṃ tāstāḥ kalpanāḥ saṃmṛśantaḥ - samānayogakṣematayā bhāvayantaḥ vedāvalambinaḥ, dṛṣṭaṃ na apanuvīran - pratyakṣato jñeyamarthaṃ na apalapeyuḥ, laghum - indriyā vedyaṃ kalpanālāghavayutamartham, anuminuyuḥ – anumānato nirṇayeyuḥ śeṣam – pratyakṣānumānābhyāma- nirṇeyaṃ cārthaviśeṣam, śāstrāta alaukikārthanirṇaye samarthān veda- tadupabṛṃhaṇarūpādāgama- pramāṇāt icchanti – nirṇetumabhilaṣanti || 3 ||

kārya kiñcit kuvindaprabhṛtiviracitaṃ dṛśyate'nyacca sarvaṃ

kartrāyattaṃ śrutaṃ tat kvacidapi na parādhīnatābhaṅgadṛṣṭiḥ |
pabandha-kṣīrapāthastṛṇajalada taṭidvāyvayaskāntapūrvaiḥ

dṛṣṭāntairna tvadiṣṭaṃ phalati tadakhilaṃ cetanādhiṣṭitaṃ naḥ || 4 ||

-- nanvatrācetanānāṃ svasamucitavidhau kartradhīnatvamuktaṃ
śāstrārambhe vidhāturvyanudadanumiti kiṃ paraḥ sūtrakāraḥ ? | pṛccheyaṃ nātitucchā śruṇu tadavahitaḥ sarvakṛnnānumātuṃ
nāpahmotuṃ ca śakyastadubhayaniyamādarśanādityamaṃsta || 5 ||
atra — asminnadhikaraṇe, acetanānām — cetana bhinnānāmarthānām, svasamucitavidhau- svakāryotpādane, kartradhīnatvam – kṛtimaccetanāpekṣatvanaiyatyama, uktaṃ nanu – yuktitaḥ samarthitaṃ khalu, śāstrārambhe– śārīrakādyapeṭikāgate śāstrayonitvādhikaraṇe, sūtrakāraḥ– śārīrakasūtrakartā kiṃparaḥ – kimabhiprāyakaḥ san vidhātuḥ – jagatkarturīśvarasya,

anumitim - anumānāyattāṃ siddhim, vyanudat – nira karot iyaṃ pṛcchā- īdṛśaḥ praśnaḥ, atitucchā na atyantaṃ na laghuḥ, tat – uttarayogyatvāddhetoḥ, avahitaḥ śrṛṇu - sāvadhānamanāḥ niśāmaya, sarvakṛt - sarvakartā īśvaraḥ, na anumātu śakyaḥ sādhitumapi nāhaḥ, na apahnotuṃ śakyaḥ – bādhitumapi nāī:, tadubhayaniyamādarśanāt – īśvara - tadabhāvavyāghyoḥ kutrāpi dṛṣṭānte anupalambhāt, ityamasta evaṃ niścitavān || 5 ||

'prakṛteḥ jaḍatvarūpe'cetanatve, puruṣasya caitanyānāśrayatve'cetanatve ca vidyamāne, kathaṃ ghaṭeta- adhyāsasya caitanaikadharmatvāt kathaṃ vā yujyeta ? || 6 ||
puṃsāṃ bhogāpavargaprabhṛti phalamidaṃ tacca sarva pradhāne
draṣṭṛtvādeśca 'klṛptiḥ' puruṣa iha pṛthag draṣṭṛtādiśca buddhau | muktayai baddhasya śāstra munirakṛta tato nitya'mukto'smi' cetyā-
dhanyonyavyāhatoktiṃ vṛṣalapariṇaye jainabhaktā japantu || 7 ||

idaṃ - śāstradṛṣṭam, bhogāpavargaprabhṛti – saṃsāramokṣa bandhātmakam puṃsāṃ phalam - puruṣasambaddhaṃ prayojanam, tacca sarva pradhāne – puruṣaprayojanabhūtaṃ sarvamapi prakṛtāvevāṅgīkṛtaṃ sāṅkhyaiḥ, draṣṭṛtvādeva – prakṛtau paridṛśyamānasya draṣṭṛtvādhyāsasya anyathānupapattyā draṣṭṛtvāderāśrayatayā puruṣe klaptiḥ pṛthak – puruṣaviṣayiṇī kalpanā pārthakyena

draṣṭṛtādiśca buddhau – vastutaścintya- māne tu draṣṭṛtvādi dharmaḥ buddhāveva, muniḥ – kapilaṛṣiḥ, śāstram – sāṃkhyaśāstram, baddhasya muktayai —bandhāspadasya puruṣasya bandhavigamāya, akṛta — nirmitavān tataḥ sāṃkhyaśāstrāt, nityamukto'smi - svābhāvikamuktimāṃścāham, ityādyanyonyavyāhatoktim - evamādimitho - viruddha vāgjālam, vṛṣalapariṇaye – mithoviruddhavākprayogasthānabhūte śūdrāvivāhe, jainabhaktāḥ – sarvānaikāntyavā dijainanītimantaḥ sāṃkhyāḥ, japantu – upāṃśu uccārayantu na tu prekṣāvatā purataḥ spaṣṭam, apahāsanairbharyāt || 7 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā (racanā - aghi)
evaṃ bādhaḥ parihṛtaḥ pratipakṣo nirasyate | pārśvasthasaṃśayocchatyai dvitīye caraṇe punaḥ || 83 || viphalā citpravṛttiścat tādṛśyevācito varam |
iti śaṅkā podyate'tra racanāsambhavāt punaḥ || 84 || iti racanānupapattyadhikaraṇam

paramamahat — vibhvātmakaṃ sāṅkhyasaṃmataṃ pradhānatatvam adhikṣipya – (aprāmāṇikatvena hetunā pūrvādhikaraṇe) nirasya, dṛṣṭāt samadhikavapuṣaḥ dṛśyatrasareṇyapekṣayā adhikasaṃkhyā kasvarūpavataḥ, nānāvidhāṇūpādānaughasya - pṛthivītvāstvā dijātibheda-guṇavaividhyavadaṇvātmaka samavāyikāraṇasto- masya, kalpane—anumānataḥ sādhane, aniṣṭaṃ āha— tārkikānabhimatārthaprasañjanarūpaṃ doṣaṃ, asminnadhikaraṇe sūtrakṛt pratipādayati, prāk – pūrvādhikaraṇe, prājñādhiṣṭhānaśūnya prakṛtipariṇati:- cetanaviśeṣānadhiṣṭhitāyāḥ acetanāyāḥ prakṛteḥ pariṇāmavādasaraṇiḥ, nirastā - pramāṇadūratayā nirūpitā | atha—asminnadhikaraṇe seśam – īśvarāyattatvavāda garbham, sūkṣmāṇu.... pakṣam - paramāṇvākhyadravyakāraṇakadvyaṇu kādikāryaprapañca tpattivādasaraṇim, pinaṣṭi - sarvāṃśaśodhanataḥ aprā- māṇikaṃ bodhayati || 8||

prāgevārambhaṇoktāvapahṛtaviṣayā prāgasadddravyaklaptiḥ

kāṇādānāmidānīṃ kṣipati bahumukhaṃ kāraṇaprakriyāṃśam |
tredhā hetau vibhakte hyanumitiśaraṇaistattadaṃśe yathārha
vyāghātādīn vikalpakramavividhagatīn vyāharatyatra sūtraiḥ ||9||

prāgeva – pūrvasmin smṛtipāde eva, ārambhaṇoktau — ārambhaṇaśabdaghaṭite adhikaraṇe, kāṇādānaḥsvaiśeṣikānāṃ saṃmatā, prāgasadravyaklṛptiḥ – pūrva ma vidyamāna dravyotpatti kalpanavidhā, apahṛtaviṣayā-bādhitatvena pratipāditā, idānīm – tarkapādīye asminnadhikaraṇe, kāraṇa- prakriyāṃśam — kāraṇasāmagrī sambandhinaṃ anirastaṃ kāṇādābhimataṃ śiṣṭāṃśam, bahumukhaṃ kṣipati - nānāprakāreṇa nirasyati, anumitiśaraṇaiḥ - anumānarasikaiḥ tārkikaiḥ, tredhā vibhakte hetau samavāyyasamavāyinimittabhedena vibhajya nirūpite kāraṇajāte, tattadaṃśe - trividhakāraṇa- kalpanādhīnasiddhi ke atirikta vayanyādyaṃśe yathā—yathāyogyam, vikalpakramavividhagatīn- vikalparītyānuguṇyena vividhaprakārān, vyāghātādīn – svavyāhṛtya pasiddhāntādidoṣān, atra sūtraḥ – etadadhikaraṇīyaiḥ sūtraiḥ vyāharati- vivicya bodhayati bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ ||9||

_

dṛṣṭasyāṇo: prasūrti dvyaṇukamaṇumapi sthāpayanto'numityā
dṛṣṭākārānusārānna niravayavatādyatra vaktuṃ kṣameran |
sarvaṃ sahyeta sūkṣme prakṛtiparava'tā' jālakā lokalakṣye
tadbhāgān khyāpayedvā smṛtira phalatayā tvānya paryeṇa neyā || 10 ||

dvayaṇukaṃ aṇumapi - ( paramāṇudvayārabdhaṃ ) dvyaṇukākhyaṃ dravyam, (tathā) tato'pi sūkṣmaṃ paramāṇvākhyaṃ dravyaṃ ca, dṛṣṭasya aṇoḥ - cakṣurvedyaṃ trasareṇvākhyaṃ sūkṣmadrayaṃ prati praśtutim – kāraṇatvena, anumityā sthāpayantaḥ - anumānākhyapramāṇādhīnajña ( nāyattasiddhitayā samarthayantaḥ vaiśeṣikāḥ, dṛṣṭākārānusārāt-vagatamākāramanuruddhayaiva adṛṣṭakalpanāddhetoḥ, atra – kalpanīye paramāṇau, niravayavatādi - avayavāsametanityatvādikam vaktum - svavacanataḥ sthāpayitum, na kṣameran – samarthā na syuḥ, pramitiparavatām – pramāṇāyattajñānaparatantrāṇāṃ dārśanikānām, jālakā lokalakṣye - vātāyanapravṛtta sūrya marīcigocare, sūkṣme - aṇudravye, sarva sota - niravayavatvādi nikhilam kathañcidabhyupagamyeta, (dṛṣṭe kalpanābhāvāt ) smṛtiḥ paramāṇuparimāṇapradarśanaparatvena āpātataḥ pratīyamānā 'jālasūrye 'tyādirūpā śilpaśāstramadhyagā smṛtiḥ, tadbhāgān khyāpayedvā- trasareṇoraṃśān sthāpayituṃ śaknuyādvā ? naiva, aphalatayā - tadaṃśasthāpane prayojanābhāvena, ānya- paryeṇa tu tatprakaraṇoktavedhyādiva kyaśeṣatva kṛtātparyāntaravattayaiva, neyānirvāhyā, na tu paramāṇvādisthāpakatayā || 10 ||

viśrāntirna kacidavayavanivahānantyato mānasāmyaṃ

māṣa- kṣoṇībhṛto: syāditi yadi tadasat tāratamyādanante | vaiṣamyaṃ pakṣamāsaprabhṛtiṣu niyataṃ na hyananteṣvaniṣṭaṃ
parāparyaṃ ca jātyorna kimanuka thiṃta vyaktayanantatvasābhye || 11 ||

kvacit – kutracidvastuni viśrāntiḥ na cet — avayavadhārāyāḥ virāmaḥ naivāsti yadi, māṣa kṣoṇībhṛto:- alpaparimāṇasya māṣasya, mahāparimāṇasya mahīdharasya ca avayava- nivahānantyataḥ – aparimitasaṅkhyā kāvayava stomabattākṛtam, mānasāmyam - parimāṇātāratamyam, syāditi yadi - prasajyeteti tārkikaiḥ yadyucyate, tadasat tarhi tadasādhveva, anante tāratamyāt — asaṅkhyāte'pi tāratamyasya avarjanīyatvāt ; hi yataḥ, pakṣa- māsaprabhṛtiṣu - pakṣa-māsādyātmakeṣu kālābayaveṣu ananteṣu — iyattayā anirdeśyasaṅkhyā keṣu satsvapi, niyatam - avicālanīyatayā'vasthitam vaiṣamyam - saṅkhyātāratamyam, na aniṣṭam – kasyāpi na asaṃmatam vaiṣamyam—saṅkhyātāratamyam, api tu sarvapratipannameva, vyaktānantatvasāmye - dravyatvapṛthivītvā dijātyāśrayabhūtānāṃ vyaktīnāma- nantatve samāne'pi, jātyoḥ - tadāśritayoḥ dravyatva - pṛthivītvādyo: sāmānyayoḥ, pārāparya ca- mithovyāpyatva-vyāpakatvābhyāmavasthānamapi kiṃ na anukathitam – bhavadbhiḥ na siddhāntitaṃ

kiñcidanyacchrutisaraṇijuṣāṃ devatānugrahāde- ranyatve tasya tajjñairadhikayatanavatklaptirādau vyudastā |
yatne yatnānapekṣāṃ na kimanumanute syādadṛṣṭe'pi tadvat
bhūtasthādṛṣṭavāde dvyaṇukṛdaṇugatādṛṣṭakalpo'tra lūnaḥ || 12||

śruti saraṇijupām – vedamārgāvalambinām, devatānugrahāderanyat — paramapuruṣaprītya- prītibhyāṃ bhinnam, adṛṣṭam — adṛṣṭapadābhidheyaṃ yatkiñcidvastu, na – naiva vidyate, tasya anyatve - adṛṣṭasya jībagatatayā atirikta svarūpatve kalpyamāne, tajjñaiḥ – tādṛśādṛṣṭasvarūpaniṣṭhāvadbhiḥ kriyamāṇā, adhikayatanavatklaptiḥ adṛṣṭātiriktajagannirmāṇānuguṇayatna viśeṣavataḥ īśvarasya anumānataḥ kalpanā, ādau – śārīrakārambhe śāstrayonitvādhikaraṇe, vyudastā — nirākṛtā bādarāyaṇena, yatne — ātmagate kriyājanake guṇaviśeṣe, yatnānapekṣām – yatnāntarāpekṣā- rāhityam kiṃ nānumanute ? – bhavān nā'ṅgīkaroti kim ? adṛṣṭe'pi tadvat syāt - yatne yatnāntarāpekṣārāhityamiva adṛṣṭasya nirvāhakāpekṣā rāhityamapi syādeva bhūtasthādṛṣṭavāde- adṛṣṭasya bhūtagatatvapakṣe janatārkikādyabhyupagate, dvaṇukadaṇugatādṛṣṭakalpaḥ dvayaṇukotpādaka- paramāṇugatādṛṣṭavādināṃ pakṣaḥ, atra lūna:- mahaddīrghādhikaraṇe niravaśeṣitaḥ || 12 ||

}-

nityaṃ sambandhame ke nijagadurapṛthaksiddhasarvānuvṛttaṃ

nityaṃ nityeṣvanityeṣvapi katicidimaṃ tāvadāyuṣkamāhuḥ |
tattaddvandvasvabhāvapratiniyatimucāṃ syānna tena vyavasthā
gurvī tvanyasya klṛptiḥ kathamadhikajuṣāṃ nānavasthādidoṣaḥ || 13 ||

eke vaiśeṣikā : apṛthaksiddhasarvānuvṛttam - niyataparamparāśrita bahvarthānugatam, na vyavasthā syāt — asaṅkīrṇavyavahāro naiva bhavet, anyasya klaptiḥ sambandhidvayāti- riktasyārthasya kalpanā, gurvītu – gauravākhyadoṣagrastaiva, adhikajuṣām – adhikārthābhyupagantṛṇām,

anavasthādidoṣaḥ - kalpanā vighaṭaka ḥ aviśrāntirūpo doṣaḥ, kathaṃ na kena vā prakāreṇa

nodiyāt || 13 ||

nirdhūte sūtrakārairavayavi- paramāṇvātmake dravyavarge

'viśvavyāptyeka' lakṣyaṃ paramamahadasat syādathā'dravyameva |
naivaṃ dṛṣṭāṇu tatsaṃhati tadubhayasambandhasiddherabādhāt
āgantukṣoṇitādyaiḥ śrutirapi jagadārambhaṇaṃ bambhaṇīti || 14||

sūtrakārai:- brahmasūtrakṛdbhiḥ bādarāyaṇaiḥ, avayavi-paramāṇvātmake upādānātirikteḥ paṭādyavaya visvarūpe, (tathā) trasareṇubhinne paramāṇusvarūpe ca dravyavarge vedāntyanabhyupagate dravyastome, nirdhūte - ārambhaṇādhikaraṇena mahaddīrghādhikaraṇena ca nirākṛte sati, viśvavyāptyeka- lakṣyam—sarvamūrtadravyasaṃyogitā rūpa vibhutvamātra viśrāntam paramamahat— paramamahaditi svīkṛtaṃ kālākāśādikaṃ dravyameva, syāt — aṅgīkṛtaṃ bhavet, (athavā ) asat syāt - paramamahadapi avayavyādikamiva asatkalpaṃ syāt, atha – athavā, adravyameva syāt - sarvadravya - nirāsaparyavasāne adravyameva prameyatayā abhyupagatamā padyeta ! (ityākṣepe sūtrakāraṇa | mupari pravṛtte ), maivam — evamākṣepo na sādhuḥ, dṛṣṭa( ṇu... siddheḥ – pratyakṣadṛṣṭasya trasareṇoḥ teṣāṃ saṃghātasya, saṃhatyananyathāsiddhāṇudvayasambandhasya ca avagamasya, abādhāt – sūtrakṛdbhiranirākaraṇāt, āgantu- kṣoṇitādyaiḥ - mūlaprakṛteḥ pariṇāmataḥ sambhaviṣyadbhiḥ pṛthivītvādidharmaiḥ jagadārambham - pañcīkṛtajagatsṛṣṭim, śrutirapi bambhaṇīti - śrutireva punaḥ punarbhaṇati || 14 ||

samudāyādhikaraṇam || 2-2-3 ||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ

saṅghairaṇūnāṃ hi ' dharādayaḥ syuḥ

'ghaṭādibhūtaiḥ karaṇādayaśca |
ityapyasāraṃ kṣaṇikāḥ padārthāḥ
ka saṃhatāḥ syuḥ ka ca kāryasiddhiḥ || 17 ||

nirdhūte — nirākṛte sati, vaināśikānāṃ pakṣān – niranvayavināśavādināṃ bauddhānāṃ saṃmatānarthān, jina giriśamatakṣepataḥ prāk - āheta. pāśupata matanirasanataḥ pūrvam, kṣiṇoti - nirākaroti (sūtrakāraḥ) || 15 ||

buddho'sau svāvatāraiḥ saha parigaṇitaḥ śrīdhareṇa svaśāstra

sarvajño 'naiva' muhyenna ca nikhilasuhṛd vipralipseta kaṃcit | tasmād buddhoktabhaṅge bhajati bhiduratāṃ sātvatādīti cenna ±svābhīṣṭa’pratyanīkapramathanamanasā mohanādipravṛtteḥ || 16 ||

asau buddhaḥ – nirasanīyamatapravartaka : sugataḥ, śrīdhareṇa - śrīmatā nārāyaṇena, svaśāstra- svīye pāñcarātratantre, svāvatāraiḥ saha — lokānugrahāthaiḥ svīyaiḥ avatārābhidhaiḥ janmabhiḥ saha, parigaṇitaḥ – anyatamatayā saṃkhyātaḥ, sarvajñaḥ – nirupādhika sārvajñyavān dhṛ buddhaśarīra : bhagavāṃśca, naiva muhyet —na kathañcidapi tattvārthaviplavavān bhavitumarhati nikhilasuhṛt – nirupādhika- sarvabhūtasauhārdavāṃścāyam, kaścit na ca vipralipseta – uddhighīrṣitaṃ yaṃ kamapi vā cetanaṃ naiva pratārayitumicchet, tasmāt uktahetoḥ, (bauddhavaiṣṇavatantrayoḥ ekapravaktṛkatvāt ) buddhoktabhaṅge– buddhaproktārthasya (śrutyanuguṇaiḥ nyāyaiḥ) nirākaraṇe āvaśyake sati sātvatā di- bhagavacchāstrādikamapi, bhiduratāṃ bhajati - nirasanīyatāmāpnotyeva, iti cet na evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ asādhu, svasaṃmatānāṃ sādhūnāṃ virodhino ye asādhavaḥ taddamanasaṅkalpena hetubhūtena, mohanādipravṛtteḥ tattvārthaviplavapravṛtteḥ (paramātmani) udayāt || 16||

durvārā moha necchā nikhila janayituḥ kena moho'nyathā naḥ ! tasmāt karmānurūpaṃ diśati phalamasau tattvabodhaṃ bhramaṃ vā
svoktyādeḥ siddhametannirupadhiviṣamo naiṣa tasmāt samoktiḥ
kāruṇyāt vipralipsā na yadi vṛjinajaṃ neśanighnaṃ phalaṃ syāt ||17||

nikhilajanayituḥ–sarvakāraṇasya paramātmanaḥ, mohanecchā durvārā— mohotpādana saṅkalpaḥ avidyamānatayā varṇayitumaśakyaḥ, anyathā — bhagavataḥ mohanasaṅkalpābhāve, naḥ mohaḥ asmākaṃ atatvārthāvagāhī ghīviśeṣaḥ, kena kiṃ hetuko bhavet ? tasmāt – bhagavata eva mohanasaṅkalpava svāt, asau – bhagavān karmānurūpam — tattaccetanagatakarmānuguṇaṃ, tatvabodhaṃ bhramaṃ vā-

vāstavikārthajñānaṃ, tadanyathājñānaṃ vā phalaṃ diśati – phalatvena cetanebhyaḥ prayacchati, etat- pramāpra me prati bhagavata eva janakatvam, svoktyādeḥ " mattaḥ smṛtirjñānamapohanaṃ ce "ti bhagavadvacanādeva, siddham – nirṇītam, eṣaḥ - bhagavān, nirupadhiviṣamo na jīveṣu -svabhāvato vaiṣamyavān na, tasmāt – svābhāvikavaiṣamyābhāvādeva, samoktiḥ – bhagavataḥ sarvān prati samatvavācoyuktirghaṭate, kāruṇyāt – anaupādhikīṃ duḥkhaprahāṇecchā nimittīkṛtya, vipralipsā na yadi bhagavataḥ asādhumohanecchā naiva saṃmanyeta cet, tarhi, vṛjinajaṃ phalam - pāpāyattaṃ phalam, īśanimnaṃ na syāt – īśvarādhīnamiti vaktuṃ naiva śakyeta || 17 ||

kalpyo boddheti sarve sugatamatavido bodhyamadhyakṣameke

buddhyākārānumeyaṃ katicana katicid bodhya mithyātvamāhuḥ | tattulyāyato'nye ghiyamapi jagaduḥ saṃvṛtereva siddhāṃ
tān sarvāṃstarkamānairghati ditijagurū 'na'dya vaibhāṣikādīn || 18 ||

sugatamatavidaḥ sarve buddhāśayavedinaḥ sarve'pi, (caturvidhā api), boddhā -jñātṛ- bhūto'rthaḥ kalpyaḥ – kalpanāmātrasiddhaḥ ; na vāstavika:, iti āhuḥ - iti vadanti, (sarveṣāṃ vairāgyavāde viśeṣāt ) eke - tadekadeśinaḥ vaibhāṣikākhyāḥ, bodhyaṃ adhyakṣamāhuḥ – jñeyamartha pratyakṣasiddhameva kathayanti, katicana — tanmatīyā eva sautrāntikanāmānaḥ kecana, buddhyākārānu- meya āhuḥ – (baudhyamartha) buddhigatena ākāraviśeṣeṇa anumeyasvarūpaṃ varṇayanti, katicit- tadantaḥpātina eva yogācārākhyāḥ kecit bodhyamidhyātvamāhuḥ – jñeyasyārthasya avāsta- vikatāmeva ūcuḥ, anye—tadīyā eva mādhyamikasaṃjñāḥ itare, dhiyamapi – (jñeyamiva ) jñānamapi, tattulyanyāyataḥ—–jñeyakalpanasadṛkṣanyāyena, saṃvṛtereva siddhām — avidyaikakalpitām, jagaduḥ- varṇayāmāsuḥ, tān sarvān ditijagurūn – tānetānakhilān daityācāryān, atha – parapakṣa- pratikṣepaprastāve tarkamānaiḥ – saugatīya kalpanā nirāsakai starkaiḥ, tadanugṛhītaiḥ pramāṇaiśca sādhana- tayā'valambitaiḥ, dyati — śakalīkaroti sūtrakāraḥ || 18 ||
eka ṭryādisvabhāvairyadaṇubhirathavā tattadekasvabhāvaiḥ

kṣoṇī- dehādipuñjaprasṛtiriti samābhāṣi vaibhāṣikeṇa |
jñānādānādi tatra kṣaṇabhiduratayā bodhya. buddherna sidhyet
nirbādhā pratyabhijñāvyanumitimathanī śeṣamanyatra sūktam || 19 ||

eka-dvayādisvabhāvaiḥ sparśaikasvabhāvaiḥ, sparśarūpadvisvabhāvaiḥ, sparśa-rūpa-rasa- trisvabhāvaiḥ, sparśa-rūpa-rasa-gandhākhyacatuḥ svabhāvaiśva, aṇubhiḥ yathākramaṃ vāyutejo jala- pṛthivyākhya- caturbhūtīyaiḥ paramāṇubhiḥ, athavā yadvā tattadekasvabhāvaiḥ aṇubhiḥ - sparśa rūpa-rasa-gandhā- khyaikaikamātrasvabhāvaiḥ uktairaṇubhiḥ, kṣoṇī prasṛtiriti — pṛthivyādibhūtasaṅghātasya tathā dehādibhautikasaṅghātasya ca prasaraṇātmikā sṛṣṭiriti vaibhāṣikeṇa yat samābhāṣi–vaibhāṣika kṛtaṃ ca sautrāntikakṛtaṃ ca yat svasiddhāntavarṇanabhasti, tatra – tasmin siddhānte, bodhya-buddhayoḥ - jñeyasya aṇoḥ tadviṣayajñānasya ca, kṣaṇa bhiduratayā - kṣaṇamātradhvaṃsitayā, jñānādānādi - viṣayīkāra- tadgrahaṇādikam na siddhayet — nirvyūḍhaṃ na bhavet, nirvādhā – uttarakālikabādhakapratyayavidūrā, pratyabhijñāpi — saṃskārasacivendriyasaṃnikarṣajā dhīrapi, ( sa evāyamiti) anumitimathanī- 'yat sat tat kṣaṇikam' iti kṣaṇikatvānumiteḥ anutpādakarī, (jāgarti), śeṣam – avaśiṣṭaṃ saugata dūṣaṇam, anyatra - sthānāntare granthāntare ca sūktam – samyak pratipāditam,

1:

bodheṣvākārabhedaṃ nijamuparitaneṣvarpayan prāktano'rthaḥ

tadvaicitryānumeyaḥ kvacidapi na tadrādhyakṣateti prajalpan |
prāk paścācca pravṛttairvihatibahulatāṃ gauravaṃ ca bruvāṇaiḥ

śikṣā dakṣasyūyairdamita upaśamaṃ yātu sautrāntikākhyaḥ || 20 ||

prāktanaḥ – svagrāhiṇo jñānāt pūrvakālavartī artha:- ghaṭādirviṣayaḥ uparitaneṣu- svānantarakālikeṣu, bodheṣu – jñāneṣu nijam ākārabhedam – svagataṃ ākāraviśeṣam, arpayan – svabhāvata eva yojayan tadvaicitryānumeyaḥ – tattajjñānagatena viṣayitāvailakṣaṇyena kalpyasvarūpo bhavati, (ata eva ) tadādhyakṣatā jñeyānāmarthānāṃ pratyakṣatā, kvacidapi na kīdṛśajñānasthale'pi naiva sampāditā, iti prajalpan– evaṃ niravapratayā yatkiñcidbhāṣamāṇaḥ, sautrāntikākhyaḥ – sautrāntikanāmā (bauddhasayūthyaḥ) prāk paścācca pravṛttaiḥ sayūthyaiḥ- svasmāt pūrvaṃ svamatapracārāyodyuktaiḥ yogacārākhyaiḥ, svasmādanantaramudyuktaiḥ vaibhāṣikākhyaiśca bauddhakoṭipraviṣṭeḥ cihatibahulatām — vyāghātapracuryam (naṣṭenārthena anutpanne jñāne svākārasamarpaṇādi) gauravaṃ ca jñāne ākāraviśeṣābhyupagamanibandhanaṃ gauravamapi, bruvāṇaiḥ spaṣṭaṃ ghoṣayadbhiḥ, (ata eva ) śikṣādakṣaiḥ sayūthyaiḥ – svayūthamadhyageṣu nyūnātireka- cintanapaṭubhiḥ sadbhiḥ damitaḥ pratiruddhasvācchandyaḥ san upaśamaṃ yātu - (samprāptaśṛṅkhala : hastīva dauṣṭyaṃ vihāya ) śānti prāpnotu || 20 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā (samu-adhi
saṅghātavāde prārambhavādadoṣo na vidyate | samudāyanaye cāpi śaṅkaṣā pratyudīryate || 86 || iti samudāyādhikaraṇam || 3 ||

kvacidapi – svāne jāgare kasminnapi vā pratyaye, grāhya grāhakau – jñeyagrahītṛbhūtau bāhyāvarthī, na staḥ naiva vidyete, adya saṃsaraṇāvasthāyām buddhyātmā–jñānamātrātmaka : āntaro'rthaḥ vividhānādisantānacitraḥ nānāprakārānādikālapravṛttavāsanā kṛtavaicitryavān san (ata eva tryātmakaḥ jñeya-jñāna jñātrātmakatayā trirūpa iva sphurati — bhāsate, bhavadaśātikrame saṃsaraṇāvasthāyāḥ atilaṅghane, svātmanaiva kevalajñaptisamma tirūpeṇaiva avabhāsate, itthaṃ yogācāroktiḥ īdṛśī yogācārasya svābhimatārthavarṇana rītiḥ, viṣaya viṣayiṇoḥ-- jñeya-jñānayorviṣaye, bodha- bādhau bodhasādhikāṃ pratīti tadbādhikāṃ kalpanāṃ ca samānau bodhabādhabodhasādhikāṃ manvānaiḥ tulyatayā vimṛśadbhiḥ prājñaiḥ vāraṇīyā tattvopaplavaśamanārthaṃ pratikṣepyā, atra yogācārasaṃmate'rthe rūḍhe sati, kvāpi kasminnapi viṣaye, svapara vibhajanādi na siddhayet - svīya-parakīyavibhāgādikaṃ naiva labhyeta sarvopaplavāt || 21 ||

buddhayaikyaṃ bodhya-boddhorna ghaṭana iha te satyayogyayorvā bhinnatve grāhyalakṣmakṣatimabhimanuṣe nātra citraikyamarthyam | citraṃ dravyaṃ guṇaṃ vā kimapi na ca viduḥ ke'pi bhinnaikarūpaṃ

tenātmakhyātivādaḥ ' sthitimiha bhajatu svaprakāśatvamātre || 22 ||

iha – jñāna jñeya - jña' tṛrūpeṣvartheṣu, bodhya-boddhoḥ - jñeya - jñātrorarthayoḥ, satyayoḥ anyayorvā vastubhūtayoḥ taditarayo (avāstavikayorvā abhyupagatayorapi te buddhayaikyam- tavābhimataṃ buddhyā saha abhinnatvam, na ghaṭate - tvadabhimatasādhakatayā saṅgataṃ na bhavati, (satyayoścat ) apalāpāyogāt asatyayoścet ! asatyā bhinnāyā buddherapi asatyatvāpātāt, bhinnatve – etaddoṣanivāraṇāya tayoḥ buddheḥ sakāśādbhede svīkṛte sati, grāhyalakṣmakṣatim — ( grāhyaviṣaye ) yat tattajjñānaviṣayatārūpaṃ tattajjñānābhinnatvaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ tasya bhaṅgaṃ tvaṃ cintayasi, citraikyam — dṛṣṭavisajātīyaṃ bhedasahā bhedarūpaṃ bhinnābhyāmabhedarūpaṃ vā aikyam, atra-- jñānāditritayamadhye, na arthyam - vivakṣāyogyaṃ na bhavati kespi ye ke'pi vā dārśanikāḥ, dravyaṃ guṇaṃ vā kimapi - dravyarūpaṃ adravyarūpaṃ vā yatkiñcidapi vastu, citraṃ na ca viduḥ - mithoviruddha- bhinnābhinnobhayānātmakaṃ naiva abhyupagaccheyuḥ, (tathā) bhinnaikarūpaṃ ca na viduḥ - bhedānupamaddaryaikatvavadvā naiva saṃmanyeyuḥ tena uktena vaighaṭyena, iha – yogācāramate dṛśyamānaḥ, ātmakhyātivādaḥ - jñānasya svātmana eva jñeya - jñātrātmanā khyātiḥ ityevaṃ paridṛśyamāno vādaḥ, svaprakāśatvamātre-

ghaṭādivaditarāpekṣa svaprakāśatāṃ vihāya svāyattasvaprakāśatārūpatve eva sthitiṃ bhajatu paryavasānaṃ

prāpnotu || 22 ||

-0-

hāneḥ anavasthāpattirūpāddoṣāt vikṛtabhavanamapi prāptavikārāt kāraṇāt kārya jananamitye--

tadapi, yuktimattāṃ naiva aśnute tarkānurodhitāṃ naiva labhate tasmāt - vikṛtāt avikṛtāca hetusvarūpāt kāryodayasya ayogāt, bhuvanam – jagat, tucchāt- nirupākhyāt śūnyā deva, prabhūtam - utpannam, (ataḥ śūnyameva ca ) iti na sat - īdṛśaṃ mādhyamikānāṃ vacanam, naiva sādhu, kasyacit vastunaḥ– yasya kasyāpi vā pramāṇasambandhārhasya arthasyeva, bhāvābhāva- vyavasthā—bhāvatvasya vā abhāvatvasya vā avasthā viśeṣasya niyamaḥ syāt – avarjanīyaḥ, tena- uktena hetunā, taditaraviṣaye – bhāvaśabdabuddhayoḥ abhāvaśabda-buddhayośca gocare, tucchatvahāniḥ - tucchatvasya sayoga eva |

prāk paścāta satvahānergaganakusumavat syānna madhye'pi kārya

maivaṃ tatraiva dṛṣṭena yadi kathamasau madhyakāryādiśabdaḥ | kāryārambhe nidānaṃ vikṛtimaduta netyādicintā'pi vandhyā sāmagryā kāryasiddherbhajati ca guṇatāṃ kāraṇasyānavasthā || 24 ||

kāryam utpattibhāk vastu, prāk – svotpatteḥ pūrva, paścāt – vināśānantaraṃ ca, paścāt–vināśānantaraṃ saccahāneḥ astivyavahāragocaratāyāḥ bhraṃśāt madhye'pi — utpattivināśāntarite kāle'pi gaganakusumavat-khapuṣpasadṛśaṃ sat śūnyameva syāt - aṅgīkārya bhavati, (iti śaṅkane) maivam - īdṛśaṃ śaṅkanaṃ nocitam, tatra – madhyakāle, dṛṣṭereva - santvāvyabhicāriṇyāḥ upalabdhereva, na yadi savajñāpikā upalabdhiḥ tatprayojyaṃ sattvaṃ vā yadi na syāt tarhi asau madhya- kāryādiśabdaḥ utpattivināśakālavyāvartakaḥ 'madhya' śabdaḥ, ajanyavyāvartakaḥ 'kāryādiḥ ' śabdo vā bhavatā prayujyamānaḥ, katham – kayā vā rītyā sādhurbhavet ? nidānam - kāraṇabhūtaṃ vastu mṛtpiṇḍādi, kāryārambhe kārye utpādanīye, vikṛtimat uta na – upamardādirūpakriyā- sampannameva kāryajanakam ? aprāptavikāramapi kāraṇaṃ vā ? ityādi cintā'pi — evamādyā vicikitsāpi, vandhyā– viphalaiva, sāmagryā – samagrakāraṇasampattyā, kāryasiddheḥ – kāryāṇāṃ svarūpalābhāt, kāraṇasyānavasthā – kāraṇaparamparāyāḥ aviśrāntiḥ guṇatāṃ ca guṇatvameva, -na doṣatām bhajati - sampadyate || 24 ||

sādhyaṃ hetustadaṅgaprabhṛti ca yadi vaḥ saṃvṛtereva sidhye-
dasmadvākyānurodhādiha na kathamasiddhyādidoṣā bhaveyuḥ |
tatra prāmāṇyabuddhirna yadi paṭhata tanmānamityasmadukti

vastusthityā na mānaṃ taditi yadi samaṃ tvanmatasthāpake'pi || 25 ||
(mādhyamikena sarvāpalāpāt ( pramāṇānāmapyapalāpāt ) śūnyākhyasya prameyasyāsiddhiḥ ; prameyasiddheḥ pramāṇādhīnatvāt iti (bauddhoparyākṣepe) sāṃvṛtikasiddhikāṅgakalāpavatā anumānena śūnyatvādisiddhiriti bauddhāśayaḥ, taddūṣaṇaṃ ca vitriyete anena)

sādhyam – anumeyaṃ sarvaśūnyatvam hetuḥ tatsādhanabhūto'rthaḥ tadaṅgaprabhṛti ca- hetvaṅgabhūtavyāpti pakṣadharmatvādi ca vaḥ - bauddhānāṃ yuṣmākam saṃvṛtereva - saṃvṛtyākhyāt doṣa- viśeṣādeva, sidhyedyadi—labhyeta yadi tarhi, asmadvākyānurodhāt – bhavadddaṣṭyā sāṃvṛti- kāntargatāsmadīyavacanānusārataḥ, iha - śūnyatvarūpaprameyasādhake pramāṇe anumānarūpe prāpyamāṇāḥ,

asiddhayādidoṣāḥ hetvābhāsatvā pādakā : asiddhi - bādhaprabhṛtayo doṣāḥ, kathaṃ na bhaveyuḥ - kathaṃ vā na prāpnuyuḥ ? prāpnuyureva tatra – asmadukte āpādane, yadi na prāmāṇyabuddhi:- pramāṇatvadhīḥ yadi na jāyeta tarhi tanmānamiti asmaduktim– 'tadvākyaṃ pramāṇam' ityeva - marthakaṃ madīyaṃ vacanam paṭhata - punaḥ punarabhimānena vadata, (bauddhopadeśasyāssvṛttimātreṇa bhavanmatasiddhivan ) tat — madīyaṃ tadvākyam, vastusthityā - vāstavikyā rītyā, na mānam iti yadi na pramāṇamiti bhavataḥ āśayo yadi, tvanmatasthāpake'pi bhavadīyamatasthāpana- pravṛtte 'sabai śūnyam' iti vākye'pi samam – (sāṃvṛtikatvāt ) vastusthityā apramāṇatvaṃ tulyameveti tadapi naiva siddhaṃ bhavati || 25 ||
akhyā tistvanyathādhīrviṣayarahita 'dhīścā'nadhiṣṭhānabuddhi-
rbāhyārthākārayogaḥ sadasaditaradhīḥ śūnyaghīrātmadhīśca |
bhrāntau sarvatra tattatparamatakathakai hatāḥ pakṣabhedāḥ

prāyo buddhiryathārthā śrutividabhimatā vā'pi bhedāgrahādi || 26 ||

bhrāntau sarvatra bhrama sāmānye, tattatparamatakathakaiḥ – prābhākarāditattanmatīyaiḥ vādibhiḥ, ādṛtāḥ - svīkṛtāḥ, pakṣabhedāḥ – pakṣaviśeṣā ete, akhyātiḥ – agrahaṇam (dharmiṇi (prakārāsaṃsargasya), anyathādhīḥ- dharmiṇyavidyamānena prakāreṇa dharmigrahaṇam, viṣayarahitadhīḥ vikalpāparaparyāyā nirviṣayakhyāti, anadhiṣṭhānabuddhiḥ – āropadharmiṇaṃ vinaiva dharmāropākhyā niradhiṣṭhānakhyātiḥ, bāhyārthākārayogaḥ– arthasaṃsargajākārakhyātināmno nirākāre jñāne ghaṭā- dyākārollekhinī ghī, sadasaditaradhīḥ- vivartāparaparyāyā anirvacanīya khyātiḥ, śūnyadhīḥ- śūnyāparaparyāyasya abhāvasya bhāvarūpajagadātmanā khyātiḥ, ātmadhīśva ātmāparaparyāyasya jñānasyaiva bāhyābhyantarasarvaprakāreṇa khyātiścati, prāyo buddhi: - viśiṣṭā pratītiḥ sarvā'pi, yathārthā—yathārthakhyātirityeva śrutividabhimatā - vedavitsaṃmatā, kvāpi – yāthārthyopapādanā- nupapattisthale kvacittu pratyakṣaviśeṣe pramāyāmapramāyāṃ ca bhedāgrahādi - bhedasya agrahaṇarūpā, asaṃsargasya agrahaṇarūpā ca akhyātirbhavati || 26 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ (sarvathānu aghi)

sarvathānupapattyadhikaraṇe sattvāsattvayoḥ avasthā viśeṣarūpatvāt, asaditi pratijñāyāmapi, saditi pratijñāyāmiva mādhyamikābhimatatucchatvānupapatteḥ, tanmatam anupapannam — ityuktam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā (sarvathānu adhi )
na hi sarvābhāvavāde ko'pi doṣa: prasajyate | iti mādhyamiko vādaḥ pratyudāhṛtisaṅgateḥ || 88 || iti sarvathānupapattyadhikaraṇam || 5 ||

śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ
sarvaṃ ca sattvādi- 'paṭādi 'bhāvai- bhinnaṃ tvabhinnaṃ sthiramasthiraṃ ca |
ityapyasad yena viruddha dharmo

"naikāśrayā' vanya' kṛtā pratītiḥ || 20 ||
sarvaṃ ca nikhilameva vastu, sacvādibhāvaiḥ abhinaṃ sthiraṃ ca

satva- dravyatvādīn
paṭatva- ghaṭatvādīn

sāmānyadharmān dvārīkṛtya, bhedarahitaṃ cirāvasthāyi ca paṭādibhāvaiḥ viśeṣadharmān dvārīkṛtya mithobhedavat, āśuvināśi ca bhavati, (ataḥ anekāntavāda evocitaḥ) ' ityapi asat — evaṃ cintanamapi asādhu, yena yena hetunā, viruddhadharmo—mithaḥ prati- dvaṃdvinau dvau dharmoṃ, na ekāśrayau naiva samānādhikaraṇau syātām, pratītiḥ – ekasminneva dharmiṇi viruddhadharmāvagāhinī ghīḥ param anya kṛtā- deśa - kālādyupādhyantarakṛtā na tu anaikāntyakṛtā || 20 ||

kvāpi bhāge mānavānāṃ sāmānyadharmabhūte ahiṃsādau kiñcidaṃśe, jinamunivacasaḥ

saṃvādāt – jinākhyarṣeḥ vākyasya śrutisaṃpratipatteḥ śeṣamapi mānamastu – jaināgamoktaṃ avaśiṣṭa- mapi vacaḥ pramāṇatayā sampratipannaṃ bhavatu tasmāt — pramāṇatvena sāmyasiddheḥ tena - jinamune - rvacasā, upaniṣat uparodhyeti vedāntagīḥ kacidvādhyāpi bhavediti, kacolluñchanānām- keśaluñchataniṣṭhānāṃ jainānām, durāśā — duṣṭā apekṣā cejjā yeta ; sā na saphalā bhavet, vaighaṭyasyāpi – jinavacaḥ su vaidikārthaviparītabodhakatvasyāpi dṛṣṭeḥ upalambhāt (sarvāṃśasaṃvādā- bhāvāt ), na yadi vedavirodhe'pi jinavācā maduṣṭatve, evameva – jinoktiriva sugatavāk – śākyamunibhāṣāpi, satyāstu – abhyupagataprāmāṇyā bhavatu, tena vedaviruddhatvāśe aviśeṣeṇa, puruṣavacanayoḥ - puruṣoktayoḥ jina- sugatagiroḥ, anyonyaṃ nirodhāt – mithovirodhādvādhaprāpteḥ, naḥ śrutiḥ vaidikānāmasmākaṃ pramāṇabhūtā apauruṣeyī vaidikī bāka, agrakampyā – acālanīya- prāmāṇyā satī jāgarti || 27 ||

saccā sacca dvayaṃ ca dvitayasamadhikaṃ tacca pūrvaiḥ saheti

syādastītyādivācā parihita gaganairgīyate saptabhaṅgī |
vyāghāta 'stairyadīṣṭa: ' svasamayavihatiryadyaniṣṭaḥ parokte-
'stadvākyairna kṣatiḥ syānna ca nirupadhikaḥ kāpyasatvā diyogaḥ ||28||
parihitagaganaiḥ – gaganavāsaskaiḥ digambara jainaiḥ, saptabhaṅgī - abhyupagata saptavidhabhaṅga

( anaikāntya) vatī svasiddhāntasaraṇiḥ, syādastītyādivācā - ' syādasti, syānnāsti, syādasti
ca nāsti ca syādavaktavyam, syādasti cāvaktavyaṃ ca

syānnāsti cāvaktavyaṃ ca syādasti ca nāsti cāvaktavyaṃ ca' ityevaṃrūpayā bhāpayā, sacca asacca sattāvadapi sattāvadanyadapi, dvayaṃ ca- sattvāsatvobhayavadapi, dvitayasamadhikam – uktobhayavadvilakṣaṇamapi (anirvācyam) pūrvaiḥ saha tacca — uttaretaiḥ saha tatsarvavailakṣaṇyarūpā nirvācyatvavadapi iti gīyate - ityevamarthatayā pratipādyate, taiḥ saptabhaṅgīvādibhirjanaiḥ, vyāghātaḥ vyāghātanāmā kathakairudbhāvyo doṣaḥ, iṣṭo yadi - doṣatayā svairapyaṅgīkṛto yadi tarhi svasamaya vihatiḥ - svasiddhāntacyutiḥ (saptabhaṅgayāḥ vyāitimayatvāt ) yadi aniṣṭaḥ sadoṣatayā na saṃmataścet tvadvākyaiḥ doṣodbhāvanārthaiḥ bhavadīyaiḥ vacobhiḥ, parokteḥ na kṣatiḥ tvatprativādyukta yāḥ tatsiddhāntavā ca: maiva bhaṅgo bhavitā, kvā'pi yatra kutrāpyekasminnarthe, nirupadhikaḥ deśakālādyupādhibhedānāyattaḥ (svābhāvikaḥ),

asaccādiyogaḥ na ca - (svagatasacvena sākaṃ ) asattvāderviruddharmasya samāveśo naiva bhavitumarhaḥ || 28 ||
vṛddhiha sau yathāī pratitanu bhavināṃ deha bhane vimānaṃ
muktau nityordhvayānaprabhṛti gurutayā nityapātaṃ kṣamādeḥ | dharmādervyāpakatvaṃ gaganavadathavā ' tādṛśa' pudgalatvaṃ

dustarkaiḥ kalpayantaḥ śrutinaya kuśalairdū gmutsāraṇīyāḥ || 29 ||

bhavinām—saṃsaratāṃ jīvānām, pratitanu – pratideham (dehe - dehe ), yathārham - yathā- yogyam, vṛddhihāsau — upacayāpacayau (mahadalpapariṇāmatve) avajyoṃ, dehabhaṅge - dehasambandhocchede sati, vimānam -- parimāṇasāmānyabhāvaḥ (bhāvapradhāna nirdeśāt ), muktau – mokṣāvasthāyām, nityordhvayānaprabhṛti — satatordhvagamanādikam (evam) kṣamāde: - bhūgrahamaṇḍalādeḥ, gurutayā- patanahetugurutvākhyadharmavatvena hetunā, nityapātam - patananairantaryam, dharmādeḥ – dharmasyā dharmasya ca gaganavat - ākāśasāmyena, vyāpakatvam - sarvadeśasarva kālavyāptim athavā - yadvā, atādṛśam - vyāpakatvaviruddham, pudgalatvam -- paricchinnaparimāṇatvam, paricchinnaparimāṇatvam, dustakaiḥ - śrutiviruddhaistakaiḥ, kalpayantaḥ—apramāṇaṃ niścinvantaḥ ete jaināḥ, śrutinayakuśalaiḥ – vedanītinipuṇaiḥ, dūra- mutsāraṇīyāḥ - apunaḥ prarohaṃ nirasanīyāḥ || 29 ||

paśupatyadhikaraṇam || 2-2-7 ||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ

patyuḥ paśūnāṃ matamādarāī
sarvajñabhāvācca parigrahācca |
ityapyasad vedaviruddhadharma-
parāvarādipratipādanena || 21 ||

paśūnāṃ patyuḥ matam - paśupateḥ śivasya upadeśataḥ pravṛttaṃ pāśupatākhyaṃ matam, sarvajñabhāvācca tadupadeṣṭuḥ śivasya sarvatattvārthavettatvāt, parigrahācca – vaidikaireva kaiścit upadeśānuṣṭhānādidvārā ( pramāṇatayā ) tatkṛtasya āgamasya svīkṛtatvadarśanādapi jñāpakāt, ādarārha vedopabṛṃhaṇabhūtāgamapravṛttatayā abhyupagamārham ityapi asat — evaṃ cintanamapi asādhu, vedaviruddha .... pādanena - śrutyanabhimatanimittatvopādānatvavattayā īśvara-pradhānākhyatastvayoḥ, bodhanena (asya matasya vaidikairanādaraṇīyatvāt ) || 21 ||

yataḥ, daityapramathanarucinā – danujamardanā bhilāṣiṇā, devena bhagavatā mahāviṣṇunā, moha- śāstrāṇi kuru iti asuramohakārīṇi śāstrāṇi pravartayeti, ādiṣṭaḥ- ājñaptaḥ san,

eṣaḥ - paśupatisaṃjñaḥ śivaḥ, nijatantram - svīyaṃ pāśupataṃ tantram, akṛta — aracayat, tataḥ—tasmāddhetoḥ, tat —asuramohanārtha pāśupataṃ tantram, śiṣṭopajīvyaṃ na sacchāstrara (taiḥ) tānāṃ pramāṇatayā nā'valambanīyam || 30 ||

prājāpatye hi vākye prakaṭamupaniṣat prāha dehātmavādaṃ
cakre lokāyataṃ tat suggururabhajanmo 'hanatvaṃ' mukundaḥ | kaṇvasthāne ca lokāyatikaparivṛḍhā bhārate'pi pragītāḥ
'kāryārtho vipralambhastadiha paśupatestadvadevopapanna: || 31 ||
upaniṣat - chāndogyaśrutiḥ, prājāpatye vākye prajāpatinā indra- virocanau prati upadiṣṭe ātmasvarūpabodhanapare vākye (daharavidyāśeṣabhūte), dehātmavādam – dehasyaiva ātmatā- bodhakaṃ vākyam, prakaṭaṃ hi āha - spaṣṭameva vadati, tat - prajāpatyuktameva dehātmatvam, suraguruḥ- devācāryo bṛhaspatiḥ lokāyataṃ cakre - lokāyatanāmnā prathitaśāstratayā racitavān, mukundaḥ – muktidāyako janārdana eva, mohanatvam - mohinyākhyaṃ rūpam, buddhādirūpaṃ vā mohanaupayikam, abhajat – prāptavān aṅgīcakāra vā, kaṇvasthāne ca kaṇvasya ṛperāśrame'pi, lokāyatikaparivṛḍhāḥ-lokāyata matīya gresarāḥ, bhārate'pi pragīta : - mahābhārate'pi spaṣṭīkṛtāḥ, tat — uktāddhetoḥ, iha— pāśupatatantrepi tadvadeva prājāpatyā divākyeṣviva paśupateḥ- paśupatināmnaḥ śivasya, vipralambhaḥ prakṛtiviśeṣamuddiśya mohanavyāpāraḥ kāryārthaḥ bhagavataḥ satyasaṅkalpa nirvahaṇaprayojanakaḥ san upapannaḥ – yukta eva || 31 ||

śaiva ( dyākhyāviśeṣaiḥ paśupatisamayaḥ syāccaturghā'nyathā vā
śrutyā'nyonyaṃ ca bādhaḥ sphuṭa iha tadasau śāpaduṣṭāī uktaḥ | agrāhyān vaidikānāmanusṛta nigamāḥ sasmarastatpraviṣṭān
tastve'pyatrā'yathātvaṃ sugata jinamatānantaroktiḥ kramāptā || 32 ||
paśupatisamayaḥ — paśupateḥ śivasya siddhāntaḥ, śaivādyākhyāviśeṣaiḥ śaiva- pāśupata-

kāpāla kālāmukheti saṃjñābhedaiḥ, caturdhā syāt bhedacatuṣṭayavān varteta, anyathā vā syāt ekaprakāravāneva vā varteta, (asmākaṃ tatra na ādaraḥ) iha-pāśupate tantre, śrutyā anyonyaṃ ca bādhaḥ sphuṭa: – vedena saha svasminneva miśraśca viruddhārthakatvaṃ vivecakānāṃ sujñeyaḥ tasmāt - uktāddhetoḥ, asau – pāśupataḥ samayaḥ, śāpaduṣṭāḥ – gautamasya muneḥ prāptāt śāpāt dūṣitānāmeva sevanayogya : ; na vaidikānāmiti uktaḥ - vaidikairniścito vartate, anusṛta- nigamāḥ – vedamārgāvalambino mantrādayaḥ, tatpraviṣṭān - pāśupata samayapraviṣṭān vaidikānāṃ agrāhyān — vedamārgapraviṣṭānāṃ anupādeyatvena, sasmaruḥ – svīyāsu smṛtiṣu bodhayāmāsuḥ, atra—asmin pāśupate, tatve'pi mumukṣubhirnirṇeyārthaviṣaye'pi, anyathātvam - śrutiviparyasta - tvam ; ( na kevalamācārāṃśe) sugata-jinamatāntaroktiḥ - asya tantrasya bauddhāṃtamata nirāsapaścādbhāvena nirasanavā coyuktiḥ,

kramāptā- - vaidiketaratve

tāratamyakramamanurudhya

siddhā || 32 ||

niṣṭhā sarveṣu nārāyaṇa iti vacanāddhetvahantavya tokte-

manitvoktayā ca tantrāntaramapi mahitaṃ vedavad bhāratādau | nāsto bauddhādivat tannirasanamiti cet satyamaṃśe tu bādhaḥ
syānnā'sau pañcarātre kvacidapi tadiha svīkṛtirvedatulyā || 33 ||

sarveṣu – sāṅkhyā disarvatantreṣvapi niṣṭhā nārāyaṇa iti – paryavasānabhūmiḥ nārāyaṇaḥ eveti, bhāratādau pramāṇatayā sarvasampratipanneṣu mahābhārataprabhṛtītihāsapurāṇeṣu vacanāt- 'niṣṭhā nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ' iti varṇanāt jñāpakāt hetvahantavyatokteḥ – 'pāśupataṃ tathā na hantavyāni hetubhiḥ' iti kevalābhiryuktibhirabādhyatvanirṇayāt, mānatvoktayā ca - 'ātma- pramāṇānyetānī'ti pramāṇatāyāśca vyavasthāpanāt tantrāntaramapi pañcarātrādanyadapi tantram, vedavat mahitam - vedasadṛśatayā utkarṣavadeva, ataḥ uktahetubhyaḥ, bauddhādivat – saugata- jainādisamānatvabuddhyā, tannirasanaṃ na iti cet kāpilāditantrakhaṇḍanaṃ naiva yuktam iti śaṅkane, satyam – iyamuktiḥ kiñcidaśe aṅgīkārthaiva, aṃśe tu bādhaḥ syāt – īśvarasya nimitta- mātratvānupādānatvādi kiñcidaṃśe eva vedavirodho'napahvanīya eva, paścarātre –nārāyaṇaprokte pañcarātrākhye tantre, asau—śrutivirodhaḥ kvacidapi na syāt – kiñcidaṃśe'pi naiva sambhāvana- mapyarhati tat - tato hetoḥ, iha - pañcarātre, svīkṛtiḥ vedatulyā - vaidikagrāhyatā vedasāmye- naiva na tāratamyeneti || 33 ||

dṛśyante saṅgṛhītā jagati hi samayāste ca rājñānupālyā-

stasmānnaḥ pakṣapātaḥ kvacidanucita ityarbhakaprāyacodyam |
mugdhairanye gṛhītā bhavatu samaya saṃrakṣaṇoktiśca dharmye
niṣṭhaikyoktistadanyagraha' vihati 'parā tadviruddhoktidṛṣṭeḥ || 34 ||

hi-yata:, jagati – loke, samayāḥ – anye'nye siddhāntāḥ, saṅgrahītāḥ dṛśyante grantharūpeṇa ekatra niveśitāḥ, (taistaiḥ tīrthaṅkaraiḥ) upalabhyante, te ca-te ca sarve siddhāntāḥ rājñā anupālyāḥ – pārthivena (prajā saṃrakṣaṇavadeva) saṃrakṣaṇīyāḥ tasmāt - rājñāpi saṃrakṣaṇīyatvāddhetoḥ, naḥ– asmākamapi kvacit kasmiṃścidekasmin darśane, pakṣapātaḥ anucitaḥ – viśiṣṭe abhimānaḥ asādhuḥ, iti arbhakaprāyacodyam – evaṃ bāliśatulyānā - mevāśaṅkanam, anye avaidikāḥ samayā, mugdhaiḥ gṛhītāḥ prāptamohaireva upāttāḥ (na vivekibhiḥ) samayasaṃrakṣaṇoktiśva – ' sarve siddhāntāḥ anupālanīyāḥ' iti pravādastu, dha bhavatu dharmāvirodhisiddhāntaviṣayaka iti niścito bhavatu, niṣṭhaikyoktiḥ 'sarveṣā- meva tantrāṇāṃ niṣṭhā nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ' iti nārāyaṇe ekasminnaiva paryavasānavācoyuktiḥ, tadanyagrahavihatiparā – nārāyaṇetarasmin (devatāntare) paratvabuddhiniṣedhoddeśataḥ pravṛttā, tadviruddhoktidṛṣṭeḥ – bhagavadekaparatva virodhivacasāṃ purāṇāntareṣu janeṣu ca upalabhyamānatvāt tannirākaraṇasya vivekibhiḥ kartavyatvāt || 34 ||

utpattyasambhavādhikaraṇam || 2-2-8 ||

śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ
jīvaḥ parasmāttu manastu jīvā-

dityādibhirvedavirodhadṛṣṭeḥ |
syāt pañca'rātraṃ' na sadityasāraṃ
vyūhābhidhānāt pratibodhataśca || 22 ||

parasmāt tu — paramakāraṇāt vāsudevāt brahmaṇa eva, jīvaḥ syāt – jīvātmā jāyate, manastu – mana indriyaṃ tu jīvāt syāt – jīvātmano jāyate, ityādibhiḥ- evamādyarthakaiḥ śrīpañcarātragatairvāvayaiḥ, vedavirodhadṛṣṭeḥ – jīvanityatvavādinyā manasaḥ paramātma- kāraṇakatvavādinyā ca śrutyā vipratipatterdarśanāt pañcarātraṃ na sat - pañcarātrākhyaṃ tantraṃ na sādhu (na pramāṇabhūtam ) ityasāram – evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na yuktimat vyūhābhidhānāt - teṣāṃ vākyānāṃ jīvatatvābhimānibhagavadvyūhā virbhāvapratipādanaparatvāt manastatvābhimānibhagavad- vyūhā virbhāvapratipādanaparatvācca, pratiṣedhataśca pāñcarātre eva 'sānādiranantaśce' ti jīvotpatteḥ kaṇṭhoktyaiva nivāraṇācca jñāpakāt || 22 ||

kapila-kaṇabhug 'buddhā' rhanto hatāḥ saha śambhunā kuliśa niśitairetaistarkera karkaśabuddhayaḥ |
api ca bhagavaddṛṣṭā śrīpāñcarātragatistviyaṃ
na khalu sadṛśī bāhyairanyairiti pratisādhitam || 23 ||

kapila-kaṇabhug buddha- arhantaḥ – kapila - kaṇāda - sugata- jinā: sāṃkhya-vaiśeṣika- bauddha- jainasamayācāryāḥ, śambhunā saha — pāśupatācāryeṇa paśupatinā sākam, akarkaśabuddhayaḥ- atīkṣṇamatitvāddhetoḥ, etaiḥ – śārīra ke pratibodhitaiḥ, kuliśaniśitaiḥ — vajravat tīkṣṇavattaraiḥ, tarkaiḥ—tarkaiḥ sādhanatayā avalambitaiḥ, hatāḥ- tattanmata nirasanato nirastāḥ ; api ca- kiñca, iyaṃ śrīpāñcarātragatistu – prastutā śrīpāñcarātraprakriyā tu, bhagavaddddaṣṭā – vāsudevena bhagavatā sākṣātkṛtā, (ataḥ) ba(hyaiḥ– vedabahiṣkṛtaiḥ itaraiḥ tantraiḥ sākam, na khalu sadṛśīti naiva sameti, pratisādhitam — samyaṅnirṇītam || 23 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ (utpaya adhi)
prāmāṇyaṃ karma kā (ṇḍe ) ṇḍasmṛtinayavaśataḥ sātvatasyāpi siddhaṃ pāde'smin saṅgatiśca pratimatadamane nāstyamuṣyeti cenna | pratyarthitvaṃ virodhabhramamapanayatā pañcarātrasya vārya
dusta' dyuttha' toktyā taditarasamayeṣvityabhu' syūta' siddhiḥ || 35 ||

sātvatasyā'pi sātvatāparaparyāyasya pāñcarātratantrasyāpi prāmāṇyam - pramāṇabhūta- (tattvārtha) jñānajanakatvam, karmakāṇḍasmṛtinayavaśataḥ - pūrvamīmāṃsīyāt smṛtyadhikaraṇāt, siddham - labdhameva pratimatadamane – pratipakṣapratikṣepārthake, asmin pāde – dvitīyādhyāyīye dvitīyacaraṇe, amuṣya – pāñcarātraprāmāṇya samarthanaupayikasyāsya utpatyasambhavādhikaraṇasya, saṅgatiśca na – nirūpaṇīyatvaupayikaḥ sambandho'sti nāsti iti cet na evaṃ śaṅkanamapi nocitam pañcarātrasya – sātvatākhyasyāsya tantrasya, virodhabhramam – śrutipratipannārthaviruddhārtha- pratipādakatvabhrāntim apanayatā nivārayitumudyuktena sūtrakāreṇa, pratyarthitvam vedānanu- rodhitvam, vāryam – pariharaṇīyam (asminnadhikaraṇe), taditarasamayeṣu — vaidiketara siddhānteṣu, dustakadyutthatoktyā – apratiṣṭhita duryuktiprabhavatvapratipādanataḥ

pratyarthitvam — vedapratibhaṭana-

- kṣamatvam, vāryam - parihāryam iti uktayā rītyā, anusyūtasiddhiḥ – virodhāpanayarūpasya

anuvṛttasyaikasya pādārthasya lābho bhavati || 35 ||
dṛṣṭāsmin vedanindetyanabhimatamṛṣeḥ sātvate vaidikatvaṃ
maivaṃ vaiśadyamūlastutiparavacane vedavairasyahāneḥ |
saṃgṛhyā''mnāyasāraṃ praṇayati bhagavāṃstaddhi ! bhaktānukampī
śrautasmārtādivacca vyabhaja diha vibhurvaidikaṃ tāntrikaṃ ca || 36 ||

asmin — śrīpāñcarātre, vedanindā - śāntilābhājanakatvavarṇanakṛtā garhā, dṛṣṭeti- upalabdheti hetoḥ, sātvate – pāñcarātratantre, vaidikatvam - vedānurodhitvam ṛṣeḥ anabhi- matam — sūtrakṛto bādārāyaṇasya aniṣṭam ; maivam - īdṛśaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na sādhu, vaiśadyamūlastuti- paravacane tattvārthaviṣayaka viśada pratipattijanakatvaṃ nimittīkṛtya pāñcarātrapraśaṃsārthaṃ pravṛtte vākye (sāṅgeṣu vedeṣu niṣṭha( malabhamānā iti), veda vairasyahāne :- vedavirodhitva tātparyakatvāprasakteḥ, hi-yataḥ,

bhaktānukampī bhagavān – āśritavātsalyaikaśīlaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ, āmnāyasāram – sarveṣu vedeṣu pradhānabhūtamartham, saṃgṛhya — ekatra saṃkṣepata āhṛtya tat prayati - pāñcarātraṃ nirmāti sma, vibhuḥ pāñcarātrapravartako bhagavān, nāgayaṇaḥ, iha – pāñcarātratantre, vaidikaṃ tāntrikaṃ ca- vaidikamiti tāntrikamiti ca śrautasmārtādivacca vaidikeṣu karmasu śrautaṃ karma, smārta karmeti vibhāga karaṇavadeva, vyabhajat vibhaktavān || 36 ||
vedānāṃ mānatoktestadanusaraṇataḥ svasya tanmūlatoktyā

vyāvṛttirbhāti bāhyāgamata iti na tattulyabhāvoktirārṣī |
kā hāni: kṣudra vidyāśabalamiti yathā tādṛśe vedabhāge mokṣasya pratyayārthaṃ tvagaṇiṣata paraṃ sātvate siddhibhedāḥ || 37 ||

vedānām – apauruṣeyagirāṃ ṛgādirūpāṇām mānatokteḥ – pramāṇatāyāḥ śrīpāñcarātre pratipādanāt svasya – pāñcarātrasya, tadanusaraṇataḥ – vedānurodhata:, tanmūlatoktyā — vedamūla- katvavacanena, bāhmāgamata : - veda bahiṣkṛtebhyaḥ āgamebhyaḥ. vyāvṛttiḥ bhāti – pārthakyaṃ pratīyate, iti - ato hetoḥ, tattulyabhāvoktiḥ - pāñcarātrasya bāhyasāmyavācoyuktiḥ, na ārṣī ṛṣeḥ sūtrakartuḥ na saṃmatā, kṣudra vidyāśabalamiti - bubhukṣujanādaraṇīyābhiḥ bahvībhirvidyābhiḥ saṃvalitamidaṃ tantrabhityetāvatā kā hāniḥ kiṃ munukṣujanopādeyatvahānirbhavet ? naiva yathā tādṛśe vedabhāge_raktairviraktaiśca ādaraṇīyābhirvidyābhiḥ (brahmaprāptiphalābhiḥ upāsanābhiḥ, kāmyābhiḥ iṣṭividhirūpābhiśca) saṃcalite śrutīnāmekadeśe yathā na kā'pi pramāṇatvakṣatiḥ tadvat sātvate- pāṃcarātre, siddhibhedāḥ param – bubhukṣujanasevyāḥ siddhiviśeṣāstu, mokṣasya pratyayārtha tu- mokṣaviṣayakaviśvāsopajananārthameva, agaṇita - guḍajihvikānyāyena siddhitvena parigaṇitā

kāraṇatvena vyavaharati, tataḥ — śrutiviruddhānāmeṣāmarthānāṃ pratipādanāt pañcarātram - pañcarātrākhyaṃ santram, na mānam — pramāṇaṃ naiva bhavitumarham; maivam – īdṛśaṃ śaṅkanaṃ nocitam hi yata:, jīvādivācaḥ jīva - mano'haṅkāraśabdāḥ, iha – etadvākyavṛttayaḥ, vibhoḥ - bhagavato vāsudevasya, trīn vyūhabhedān — saṅkarṣaṇa - pradyumnāniruddhākhyān trisaṅkhyān vyūha saṃjñāvirbhāvaviśeṣān, abhidadhati bodhayati, teṣu — ukteṣu vyūhabhedeṣu tattatsamākhyā-sā sā saṃjñā, tattattatvā- bhimānāt — jīva mano'haṅkārākhyānāṃ teṣāṃ teṣāṃ tattvānāṃ sveṣu abhimānapadābhidheyāt sambandhaviśeṣāt niyatiṃ adhigatā – ayogānyayogavyavacchedābhyāṃ tadvācakatvaniyamayuktā vartate || 38 ||

jīvo'trā'nādyanantaḥ kathita iti tadutpattipakṣo na hīṣṭaḥ
śabdaḥ saṅkarṣaṇādirna kathamapi samanveti jīvādimātre |
śrautaḥ sṛṣṭikramaśca svayamanupaṭhitastadviruddhaṃ na kalpyaṃ

tasmācchrutyā mitho vā na vihatiriha tattantratātparyadṛṣṭeḥ || 39 ||

atra – pañcarātre, jīvaḥ - cetanaḥ, anādyanantaḥ utpatti vināśarahitatvena, kathita iti — 'sānādirananta' śceti vacanataḥ spaṣṭamukta iti hetoḥ, tadutpattipakṣa:- jīvasya janma- svīkārapakṣaḥ, na hi iṣṭaḥ – naiva pāñcarātrasaṃmataḥ saṅkarṣaṇādiḥ śabdaḥ – saṅkarṣaṇaprabhṛtikaḥ śabdaḥ, jīvādimātre kevalajīva - mano'haṅkārarūpe'rthe, kathamapi na samanveti kenāpi prakāreṇa pratipādakatayā'nvetuṃ nā'rhati śrautaḥ sṛṣṭikramaśca śrutisiddhaḥ sṛṣṭiprakāra eva (caturviṃśati- tattvānāṃ) svayamanupaṭhitaḥ - atrā'pi kaṇṭhoktyā pratipāditaḥ tadviruddham na kalpyam- kaṇṭhoktakramaviruddhaṃ 'jīvo jāyate' ityādivākyapratipannatayā naiva kalpanārham, tasmāt — evaṃ vivecanataḥ, tattantratātparyadṛṣṭeḥ pāñcarātratantratātparyāvagamāt iha – asmin tantre śrutyā mitho vā vedena saha vā svasminneva vā, na vihatiḥ na vyāhatārthapratipādakatā || 39 || sāṅkhyā vaiśeṣikāśca śrutiparipaṭhitaṃ dharmamaicchanna tatvaṃ

tatvācārau tu buddha kṣapaṇaka - paśupatyuktiṣu śrutyapetau | vedopaskāriviṣṇusmṛtivadavi'tathaṃ ' pañcarātrākhyatantre
tattvaṃ trayyantasiddhaṃ caraṇamapi samaṃ gṛhyabhedādinītyā || 40 ||
sāṅkhyāḥ vaiśeṣikāśca–kāpilāḥ, kāṇādāśca, śrutiparipaṭhitaṃ dharmam-vedoktaṃ

vihitānuṣṭhāna-niṣiddhavarjanādikaṃ śreyaḥ sādhanamartham aicchan – abhyupāgaman, tacvaṃ- śrutyuktaṃ prameyaṃ param, naicchan – nābhyupāgaman buddha- kṣapaṇaka - paśupatyuktiṣu tu – saugatārhata- pāśupatāgameṣu param, tatcācārau – śāstraprameyaṃ anuṣṭhānaṃ cetyubhāvapi śrutyapetau – vedavidūrāveva, vedopaskāriviṣṇusmṛtivat - vedopabṛmhaṇatayā sampratipannāyāṃ viṣṇusmṛtāviva pañcarātrākhya- tantre – pañcarātrākhye vaiṣṇave tantre'pi tavaṃ caraṇamapi śāstraprameyaṃ anuṣṭhānaṃ ceti dvayamapi, trayyantasiddham – vedāntavākyapratipannameva, samam – (iti) aviśiṣṭam, gṛhyabhedādinītyā- āśvalāyanāpastambādigṛhyaviśeṣādigataṃ kramamanurudhya cintyamāne, avitatham – vedoktamyaiva svasminnayuktāvapi na paunaruktatyadoṣabhāk bhavet || 40 ||

smaryante pañcayajñā munibhirapi namaskāramantreṇa śūdre

tatrādhītaṃ haviṣkṛtprabhṛti padamihāpyaṃśato'syā'dhikāraḥ |
yojyā dakṣoktakālakramagatirabhigatyā dibhede tadukte
grāhyaṃ paśviṣṭisamaprabhṛtivadakhilaṃ yuktitaḥ saṅkalayya ||| 41 ||

śūdre - caturthavarṇīye adhikāriṇi, pañcayajñāḥ – devayajña - pitṛyajña - manuṣyayajña bhūtayajña- brahmayajñākhyāḥ mahāyajñāḥ pañca, namaskāra mantreṇa 'nama' ityanena mantreṇaiva, (kāryā iti) munibhirapi smṛtikāraiḥ ṛṣibhirapi, smaryante – 'namaskāreṇa mantreṇa pañcayajñān samāpayet' iti smṛtito jñāpyante, tatra – śūdraviṣaye, haviṣkṛtprabhṛtipadam - yajñīyaha viṣaḥ kartṛtvanimittakaṃ preṣoccāraṇakālikavākye prayogāī 'haviṣkṛt' padam (tathā) agnyāharaṇayogyāgāravidhānavelāyāṃ nānāyāgakartṛtvanimittakaṃ 'bahuyā ji' padaṃ ca adhītam – 'haviṣkṛdādhāneti śūdraspa' iti, 'bahuyājino'gārācchūdravarjam' iti ca kalpasūtrādau paṭhitam, (ataḥ ) ihāpi vaidike karmaṇyapi, asya – śūdravarṇīyasya, aṃśataḥ svavarṇocite eva kvacit karmaviśeṣe, adhikāraḥ – ācaraṇāītā, ( astyeva na sarvatra tathā pañcarātrodite karmaṇyapi traivarṇiketarasya āṃśiko'dhikāro vartata eva ) tadukte - pāñcarātrodite, abhigatyādibhede - abhigamanopādānejyā svādhyāyayogākhyapāñca kālika- kriyākalāpa viśeṣe, dakṣoktakālakramagatiḥ - dakṣasmṛtyuktā ṣṭakā likakramikakarmānuṣṭhāna paddhatiḥ, yojyā - mitho'nupamardena anuṣṭheyatayā vyavasthāpanāha bhavati, akhilam — śrautaṃ, smārta, tāntrikaṃ ca sarve karma, paśviṣṭisomaprabhṛtivat – vaidikāntaḥ pātīnyeva yathā paśu iṣṭi-soma saṃjñakā ni bhinna-bhinnāni karmāṇi, kālaviśeṣe (deśaviśeṣe) adhikāriṇaikenaiva parasparāvirodhenānuṣṭheyāni bhavanti, tathā, yuktitaḥ — upāyataḥ, saṅkalayya grāhyam — samyagyojayitvā jñātavyaṃ

jātāvācārya śabda: kvaciditi na tathā''cāryadevo bhavetyā dyāmnāte tatpratītiḥ smṛtiṣu niyamitāllakṣaṇāt tatpravṛtteḥ | tadvat syāt sātvatādāvagatikaviṣaye rūḍhibhaṅgo na doṣo

viprāderatra śāstra sthitirapi bahudhā bhāratādau prasiddhā || 42 ||

ācāryaśabdaḥ – 'ācāryaḥ' ityayaṃ śabdaḥ kvacit – laukikānāṃ vyavahāraviśeṣe, jātāviti — takṣādijātīye jane prayujyata ityetāvatā, ācāryadevo bhavetyāmnāte - 'ācārya - devo bhava' ityādi vaidikaprayogagocare ācāryaśabde'pi, tathā - laukika prayoge iva- na tatpratītiḥ—na jātiviśeṣavato janasya bodho bhavati, smṛtiṣu niyamitāt lakṣaṇāt- 'upanīya tu yaśśiṣyaṃ....tamācāryaṃ pracakṣate' ityādismṛtiṣu niścitaṃ lakṣaṇamavalambya tatpravṛtteḥ- ācāryaśabdasya svārthabodhane vyāpṛteḥ, tadvat - tāṃ rītimanurudhyaiva, agatikaviṣaye - samudāya - śaktibodhyārthabodhanā hatāśūnye, sācvatādau sāntvatabhāgavatādirūpe etattantraprayogagocare śabde, rūḍhibhaṅgaḥ na doṣaḥ samudāyaśaktiparityāgaḥ vyutpattibhaṅgakṛto doṣo na bhavati, atra śāstra- pāñcarātratantre'pi, viprādeḥ sthitirapi - brāhmaṇādervarṇasyāva sthitirapi, (maryādā'pi), bhāratādau - pramāṇamūrdhanyatayā prasiddhe mahābhāratādau (pāñcarātraprastāve ) bahudhā prasiddhā - bahuvāraṃ prathitā vartate || 42 ||

-0-

pādopasaṃhāraḥ
śārīrakanyāya kalāpasaṅgrahaḥ
smṛtinyāyāvirodhaṃ ca parapakṣaparāhatim |

vidhāya parato dvābhyāṃ kāryabhāvo 'vicintyate ' || 24 ||
smṛtinyāyāvirodham—vedāntapratipādyasya brahmakāraṇatvasya kā pilādismṛtibhiḥ vaiśeṣi- kādinyāyaiśca apratikṣepyatvam, parapakṣaparāhatiṃ ca-vedabāhyasaugatā dipakṣāṇāṃ nirāsaṃ ca, vidhāya — ādyapādābhyāṃ saṃsādhya, parato dvābhyām - paścāttanābhyāṃ tṛtīya caturthapādābhyām, kāryabhāvaḥ – ākāśa-prāṇādeḥ brahmakāryatvam, vicintyate - vicārya sthāpyate || 24 ||

-0-

pāde'smim kāpilasthaiḥ kaṇabhuganugataivṛddhavaibhāṣikādyai—
ryogācārābhidhānaiḥ sugatamataraha : śūnyavādaprasaktaiḥ |
arhatsiddhāntabhaktaiḥ paśupatisamayasthā yibhiścoparodhaṃ

|

kṣiptvā'tha pañcarātre śrutipathamavadat pañcamāmnāyadarśī || 44 ||

|| śrīḥ ||

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ | śriyai namaḥ | śrīdharāya namaḥ |

dvitīyādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ || 2-3 ||
viyadadhikaraṇam || 2-3-1||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||
utpattimanto na yathaiva jīvāḥ
nabhaśca notpattimadaṃśa 'hāne: ' |
iti tvasāraṃ vadati śrutiścet
tathaiva tat syānna tu tarkabhaṅgāt || 25 ||

jīvā:- - pratyagātmānaḥ, aṃśahāneḥ avayavarahitatvāt yathaiva na utpattimantaḥ– yenaiva prakāreṇa janmavanto na bhavanti, tathaiva, nabhaśca – gaganamapi na utpattimat kāryatvavanna bhavati, iti tu–evamāśaṅkanaṃ tu, asāram - sthāpakayuktiśūnyam, śrutiḥ vaidikī vāk, badati cet—utpattirāhityaṃ bravīti yadi tat tathaiva syāt tadā gaganaṃ nityameva bhavet, tarkabhaṅgāt —– 'yanniravayavaṃ tadajanyam' iti svamātra kalpitavyāptivirodhamātrataḥ, na tu — gaganaṃ ajanyaṃ bhavituṃ naivā'īm, (ataḥ ākāśasya brahmakāryatā durapavādā) || 25 ||

vaibhāṣikaprabhṛtayaḥ trayo bauddhā:, akhilamapi —– anavaśeṣeṇa sarvamapi, kṣaṇikaṃ abhidadhati- utpattita: dvitīyakṣaṇe eva vināśi iti vadanti, jinaparipaṭhitām – jinamuniproktām, saptabhaṅgīṃ—aikāntyabhaṅgaparipāṭīm, paṭhantaḥ – anudinamā vartayanto jaināḥ, samastam – sarvamapi, nityānityam - dravyatadavasthātmanā nityatvavadanityatvavadapi ca abhidadhati, vaiśeṣikādyāḥ – kāṇādā : gautamīyāśca nityānitye vibhajyāpi - vastujātaṃ nityatvena, anityatvena ca vivicyoktatvā'pi viparyasya – ( śrutimataṃ nityānityavibhāgaṃ ) anyathā kṛtya, (nityaṃ jñānādikaṃ anityamiti, saṃsthānātmakatayā anityaṃ jātirūpamartha nityamiti ca ) abhidadhati, śrutyutthān tān — śrutyavalambanenaiva labdhasvarūpāniva sthitānetān pakṣān śrutibhiratha nirundhan – samyak cintitaiḥ śrutibhireva nivārayitukāmo bādarāyaṇaḥ, viyat prāṇapādau – viyatpādanāmānaṃ tṛtīyaṃ pāda prāṇapādanāmānaṃ turīyaṃ pādaṃ ca, yunakti - asmin adhyāye yojayati || 45 || pūrvatrādhikriyāsāṃ puruṣajanivaco nityatoktatyā 'nirūḍhaṃ- 1

vyomotpattau tathaiva sthitiriti vacaso hatau vakti kaścit | siddhāntī vyomasṛṣṭirnigamaśatamitā nā'nyathā siddhirasyāṃ

vāyu- vyomāmṛtoktirjanivivivihatetyuktivaiṣamyamāha || 46 ||

pūrvatra adhikriyāyām – pūrvasmin utpatyasambhavādhikaraṇe, puruṣajanitracaḥ – 'jīvo jāyate' iti jīvotpattibodhakamiva bhāsamānaṃ pāñcarātravākyam, nityatoktyā - pāñcarātrastha- jīvanityatvabodhakasya 'sahyanādi : ' iti vākyasya pradarśanena nirūḍhaṃ yuktārthavivaraṇapūrvakaṃ kṛta nirvāhamabhavat vyomotpattau – ākāśasya kāryatāyā viṣaye'pi vacasoḥ sambhūtatvāmṛtatva - pratipādakayorvākyayoḥ vyāhatau —mitho virodhabhāne sati, tathaiva sthitiriti - amṛtatvavacasā sambhūtavacasaḥ aupacārikatvena nirvahaṇameva vāstavikī sthitiriti, kaścit – śrutyarthaparyālocane anipuṇaḥ pūrvapakṣī, vakti bravīti; siddhāntī vedāntatātparya cintana kuśalastu, vyomasṛṣṭiḥ - ākāśotpattiḥ, nigamaśatamitā bahuśrutipratipannā, asyām — utpattivādinyāṃ śrutau, na anyathāsiddhiḥ –nirvāhāntareṇa caritārthatā naiva bhavitumarhā, vāyuvyomāmṛtoktiḥ- 'vāyuścāntarikṣaṃ caitadamṛtam' iti śrutyuktiḥ, jani vidhivihateti utpattividhāyaka vākyena bādhiteti, uktivaiṣamyamāha — jīvotpatti - vyomotpattitracaso: asamānarūpatāṃ vadati || 46 ||

teja : prāthamyadṛṣṭeramṛtavacanato'naṃśakadravyabhāvād

vyomanyutpattivāk syādupahitaviṣayaivātmanīveti cenna | prāthamyasyāśrutatvāt prathamapaṭhanataḥ kalpane'nyena bādhāt
'kiñcā' martyoktitulyaṃ tvamṛtapadamihā'nekavākyai kakaṇṭhyāt ||47||

vyomani - ākāśaviṣaye, utpattivāk - sambhūtatvaśrutiḥ, ātmani iva – 'kumbhakāro jāyate' ityādirūpā ātmaviṣayakotpattibodhikā laukikoktiriva, upahitaviṣayaiva ghaṭapaṭā- dyupādhisambaddhā kā śagocaraiva, na tu śuddhakāśagocarā, ( kuta iti cet ! hetutrayamatra ) tejaḥ prāthamyadṛṣṭeḥ sṛṣṭau tejasa ādyatāyāḥ 'tattejo'sṛjata' iti śrutāvupalabdheḥ ; amṛtavacanataḥ- 'antarikṣaṃ caitadamṛtam' iti ākāśe amṛtatvakaṇṭhokteḥ ; anaṃśakadravyabhāvāt vyomnaḥ niravayavadravyatvāt, (iti) iti cet na - evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ na yuktam, prāthamyasya - tejaḥ sṛṣṭeḥ ādyatāyāḥ, aśrutatvāt - śrutisiddhatvābhāvāt prathamapaṭhanataḥ - sṛṣṭiprastāvasyādau tejaḥ sṛṣṭi- kathanamātrataḥ, kalpane—anumāne kriyamāṇe, anyena bādhāt sambhūtatvapratyakṣavacanena anumānasya bādhāpatteḥ, kiṃ ca nipuṇaṃ cintane, iha amṛtapadam - ākāśaviṣaye amṛtatvavacanam, amatyaktitulyaṃ tu-deveṣu cirasthāyitvataḥ amartyaśabdaprayoga sadṛśameva, anekavākyaikakaṇṭhyāt- utpattibodhakaba huvākya saṃvādāt jñāpakāt || 47 ||

'yaukta: ' satkāryavādaḥ śrutibhiranumato nāvayavyasti sṛjya - stattadravyeṣu nāmāntarabhajanasahāvasthā sṛṣṭivādaḥ |
iṣṭāḥ śabdādyavasthāstava niravayavadravyavarge'pi tasmād-
vyomavyutpattiruktā śrutiṣu taditarotpattitulyā na bādhyā || 48 ||

satkāryavāda:- pūrvaṃ sadeva kārya jāyate' ityayaṃ vādaḥ, yauktaḥ – yuktisiddha:, (pūrva idānīmapi mṛvānapāyāt ) śrutibhiranumataḥ - 'sadeva somye tyādiśrutivākyairapi anujñātaḥ, sṛjyaḥ sarvathā prāgasanneva utpādanīyaḥ, avayavī avayavitvena tārkikairabhyupagataḥ nūtano'rthaḥ na asti naiva pramāṇagocaraḥ, sṛṣṭivādaḥ - utpattigocaro laukikaḥ śāstrīyo vā vyavahāraḥ, tattadravyeṣu mṛt-tantvādiṣu teṣu teṣu drayeṣu, nāmā...vasthayā - abhidhāyakaśabda viśeṣaprāpti- kṣamāgantuka dharma sambandhamātrāyattaḥ, tava vaiśeṣikasya bhavataḥ, niravarge'pi kālātmākāśādi- niraṃśatvenābhimatadravyajāte'pi, śabdādyavasthāḥ ākāśe śabdaḥ, ātmani jñānam,

kāla-paramāṇvādau saṃyogādiścetyādyāḥ āgantukā dharmāḥ, iṣṭāḥ saṃmatā eva, tasmāt — avasthā - viśeṣaprāptereva utpattipadābhidheyatvāt vyomani - ākāśaviṣaye śrutiṣu uktā – vedeṣu kathitā, utpattiḥ —kāryatā, taditarotpattitulya - tatsahacaritavāyvanyādyutpattisameti kṛtvā, na bādhyā - na bādhituṃ yogyā || 48 ||

-0-

tejo'dhikaraṇam || 2-3-2||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ
kāryāṇi sarvāṇi tathā śrutatvāt '
sthānāntarāt kāraṇatī bhavanti |
naivaṃ śruteravyavadhānadṛṣṭyā
tattaccharīrāt parato bhavanti || 26 ||

- kāṭha

kāryāṇi sarvāṇi janyatayā lokavedāvagatānyakhilāni, tathā śrutatvāt - "ākāśā-

vāyoragniḥ "

ākāśāditattadvastukāraṇatayaivoktatvāt, sthānāntarāt – annapadasthāne pṛthivīti padāntaraśravaṇāt ( ? ), kāraṇato bhavanti - svasvapūrva - kāraṇādeva jāyante ; na brahmaṇa:; naivam — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ nocitam śruteḥ – 'sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma' iti śruteḥ, (tathā) 'etasmājjāyate' ityādiśruteśva, avyavadhānadṛṣṭyā svetarāvyavahitatayā brahmaṇaḥ sakāśādeva sarvakāryotpatyavagamena tattaccharīrāt - ākāśā ditattadbhūtaśarīrakāt parataḥ bhavanti – parasmād brahmaṇa eva utpadyante || 26 ||

ityevamādiśrutibhyaḥ

dvāyuḥ,

-0-

sugamam - akleśena yojayituṃ śakyam, ityādiḥ ūhaḥ evaṃprabhṛtikaḥ buddhayā tarkaḥ, atra mohaḥ – jagato brahma kāryatvaviṣaye, viparītādhyavasāya eva, prāṇaḥ kramataḥ - prāṇa- manaḥ prabhṛti śrutipāṭhamātramanurudhya, itarasaṃkṣobhaṇe – saubālaśrutyādyavagatakramacālane, atiprasaṅgāt - vinigamanābhāvataḥ sarvaśruti-smṛtyādivyākulībhāvāpatteḥ ; ( tathā karaṇāyogāt ) īkṣānusyūti- dṛṣṭe : ' tadaikṣata, teja aikṣata, āpa aikṣanta' iti sṛṣṭyaupayika saṅkalpātmakasyaikṣaṇasya avicchede - nānuvṛttidarśanāt prathamamitra - ādyaṃ pradhānatattvaṃ paraṃ brahmaiva yathā sṛjati tathā, param- anantarabhāvi mahadādikamapi sṛjyamartham, tattaccharīri—pradhānā ditattatpūrvapūrvatattvaśarīrakaṃ paraṃ brahmaiva, sauti avyavadhānataḥ svayameva janayati ; ( na tu putrapautrādinītyeti || 49 ||
