adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

tejo'dhikaraṇe - ' vāyoragniḥ' ityādiṣu vāyvādibhya evāgnyādīnāmutpattiśravaṇāt kevalavāyvādibhya evāgnyādīnāmutpattiḥ — ityāśaṅkya,
'teja aikṣata', 'āpa aikṣanta' ityādibhiḥ brahmāsādhāraṇabhūtekṣaṇaśravaṇāt tattaccharīra kā dū brahmaṇa evāgnyādīnāmutpattiḥ - ityuktam ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
dravyaṃ sarvaṃ hi nityaṃ kathitamavayavadravyabhaṅgena pūrvaṃ
nityatvaṃ sūtrakāraḥ punarapi puruṣe kiṃ viśiṣyābhidhatte | satyaṃ nāmāntarāhamiha nudati daśāṃ cetanasyānupādheḥ
pratyaktvajñatvadharmau tadiha niyamitau śāśvatau kṣetriṇo'pi || 50 ||

pūrva mahaddīrghādhikaraṇe, avaya vidravyabhaṅgena – mṛdādyavayavātiriktaghaṭādyavaya vinirākaraṇa-- dvārā, sarva dravyam - cetanācetanā viśeṣeṇa dravyasāmānyam, nityaṃ kathitaṃ hi utpatti-- vināśarahitatayā sthāpitaṃ khalu, ( evaṃ sthite ) sūtrakāraḥ - śārīrakasūtrakṛt bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ, puruṣe - jīvātmabhūte puruṣe, nityatvam - janmādirahitatvarūpaṃ nityatvam, viśiṣya - ātmatvākhyaṃ viśeṣākāramupādāya, punarapi – bhūyo'pi kiṃ abhidhatte – kasmai phalāya a

bravīti ? (punaruktaṃ viphalaṃ cetyarthaḥ) (iti śaṅkane) satyam - svābhāvikamevaitat ; param iha — asminnadhikaraṇe, anupādheḥ cetanasya—karaṇa- kalebarādyupādhyasaṃspṛṣṭasya kevalajñānā- kārasyātmanaḥ, nāmāntarāha daśām – saṃjñāntaraprāśyaupayikīmavasthāmanyām, nudati – nirasyati, tat — uktāccetanācetanavaiṣamyāt, kṣetriṇo'pi - prāpta dehasyāpi jīvasya, pratyaktva- jñatvadharmo- svasmai bhāsamānatvaṃ, jñānāśrayatvaṃ cetīmau dharmoṃ, śāśvatau — yāvadātmabhāvināviti, iha atrādhikaraṇe, niyamitau – dhruvaṃ samarthita prāyau | ( ato na paunaruktatya vaiphalye ) || 50 ||

cchandārthātiriktaṃ janimadakhilamityaitadātmyādisiddhaṃ

proktā sṛṣṭiśca jīve niravayavanayastvambarādau nirastaḥ |
jīvotpattistataḥ syāditi na sadakṛtābhyāgamādiprasaṅgā-
nnityatvā jatva kaṇṭhoktibhirapi jananaṃ tvasya deha | diyogaḥ || 51 ||

sacchabdātiriktaṃ akhilam - satpadabodhya brahma tara dvastujātam, janimaditi - utpatti-- bhāgiti, aitadātmyādisiddham - 'aitadātmyamidaṃ sarvam, ' 'sanmūlāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ' ityādi - śrutibhirjñātam jīve sṛṣṭiśca proktā - jīvātmanyapi utpattibhāktatvaṃ 'toyena jīvān vyasasarja ' iti śrutitaḥ kaṇṭhoktaiva, niravayavanayastu – 'yanniravayavaṃ tadajanyam' iti tarkastu, ambarādau- ākāśādiviṣaye, nirastaḥ - dṛṣṭavyabhicāratayā asādhakatvenoktaḥ, tataḥ — uktāddhetujātāt, jīvotpattiḥ syāditi — jīveṣvapi nāmāntarārddhāvasthāyogātmikā janiḥ, apratihataivetyetat, na sat - na sādhu, akṛtā .... saṅgāt – vihita niṣiddhākhyakarmācaraṇaṃ vināpi sukhaduḥkhādi...

prāptiprasaktaḥ, nityatvājatvakaṇṭhoktibhirapi - 'ajo nityaḥ śāśvataḥ' ityādispaṣṭoktibhirapi jñāpakaiḥ, asya jananam tu – ātmanaḥ utpattistu, dehādiyogaḥ tu karaṇakalecarasambandha eva; - na tu ghaṭādivat svarūpānyathābhāvaḥ || 51 ||

dehātmatve janiḥ syāt tadiha na ghaṭate jātamātrasya rāgāt jñāne kiṇvādi- meghopala- śabalapaṭaprakriyā 'tyanta'duḥsthā |
dehe gehāditulyā mamakṛtiranaghā doṣatastvaikyamoha :
kṣiptaṃ caikyānumānaṃ balavadanumiteḥ śāstratastarkataśca || 52 ||

dehātmatve—ātmatvasya dehaniṣṭhatve, janiḥ syāt — ātmana utpattiryuktā bhavet, tat iha na ghaṭate ātmatvaṃ dehe na yujyate, jātamātrasya - utpannamātrasyaiva śarīriṇaḥ, rāgāt – (stanyapānādau ) icchāpūrvaka pravṛtterderśanāt, gehāditulyā- ' mamedaṃ gṛham ' ityādirūpā gṛhe mamatvadhīsadṛśyeva anubhūyamānā, anaghā - nirbādhā jāgarti, aikyamohastu - 'sthūlo'ham ' 'śuklosham' iti ahamartha - śarīrayorabhedāvagāhī viparyayastu doṣataḥ anādi- pāpavāsanārūpadoṣakṛtaḥ, aikyānumānam - 'deha eva ātmā, ahambuddhigocaratvāt' iti ātmani dehābhedābhimānaṃ tu, balavadanumiteḥ – deha | tiriktatvānumānasya 'mamā'yaṃ deha: ' iti pratyakṣopaṣṭambhanena balavatvāt, śāstrataḥ ātmani dehabhedajñāpakāt ityādiśāstrāt tarkataśca – 'vītarāgajanmādarśanāt' ityādisūtrajñāpitāt tarkādapi, kṣiptam – nirastam, pratihatam || 52 ||
dehaṃ dehātireke tadavadhiniyataprāṇabuddhyakṣarūpaṃ

dhīsantānaṃ ca nityaṃ pralayavilayinaṃ sthāsnumapyāpavargāt | ḍiṇḍīrāmaṃ sadabdhāvavitathavikṛtau jīvamicchanta itthaṃ nirdhūtā dūramatra śrutibhiritaravadvādhadopojjhitābhiḥ || 53 ||

deham — naśvaratve sandehānāspadasvarūpaṃ śarīrameva, jīvamicchantaḥ– jīvātmāna- mabhyupagacchantaḥ, dehātireke – ātmano dehāpekṣayā bhede satyapi tadavadhi... rūpam - dehasattāniyatasattāvatprāṇa-buddhīndriyasvarūpaṃ jīvamabhyupayantaḥ, nityaṃ dhīsantānaṃ ca- nirupaplavaṃ citsantānaṃ jīvamaṅgīkurvantaḥ, pralaya vilayinam - pralayaparyantamavasthāya pralaye vicchedabhāgicitsantānarūpaṃ jīvaṃ svīkurvantaḥ, āpavargāt sthāsnumapi – mokṣaparyantaṃ anāditayā avasthāya, mokṣe vinaśyatsvarūpaṃ jīvamaṅgīkurvantaḥ, (tathā) avitathavikṛtau — avarjanīyakāryopayuktavikārāśraye, sadabdhau — sacchandābhidheyabrahmasāgare, ḍiṇḍīrābham – phena- buddhadādisadṛśasvarūpaṃ jīvaṃ saṃmanyantaḥ ityete ( cārvākādayo yādavaprakāśīya paryantāḥ sarve ) ittham – śrutyādipratipannanityātmasvarūpāṅgīkāreṇa, atra – ātmāghikaraṇe, bāghadopojjhitābhiḥ śrutibhiḥ pramāṇatarkādibādha puruṣadoṣa bhūtabhramādividūrābhiḥ vedavāgbhiḥ sādhakatvenāśritābhiḥ, itaravat – sāṅkhyā dipakṣavadeva, dūraṃ nirdhūtāḥ – samūlaṃ nirastāḥ || 53 ||

-0.

jñādhikaraṇam || 2-3-4|| śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ || vijñānaśabdāt svapaneṣvadṛṣṭeḥ
saṃjñānamāgantuka cetano vā |
naivaṃ śruteḥ saṅkucitaṃ suṣuptau

jñānābhidhā tadguṇasārataḥ syāt || 28||

ātmā – jīvaḥ, vijñānaśabdāt – 'yo vijñāne', 'vijñānaṃ yajñam' iti vijñāna- -śabdābhihitatvāt svapaneṣu — suṣuptyavasthāsu, adṛṣṭeḥ – jñānarūpadharmādarśanādapi, saṃjñānam - cinmātrasvarūpa eva ; na jñānadharmakaḥ, vā- athavā, āgantukacetanaḥ – sāmagryāya ttacaitanya- guṇakatāvān ; na svābhāvikacaitanyāśrayaḥ, (iti śaṅkane), evaṃ na- iti śaṅkā nocitā, śruteḥ - 'nāvijñāturvijñāterviparilopo vidyate' iti śrutipramāṇāt, suṣuttau – nidrāvasthāyām, saṅkucitam – dharmabhūtaṃ jñānam aprāptaprasaram na asat, jñānābhidhā – ātmanaḥ jñānavācaka śabdenā'bhidhānam, tadguṇasārataḥ syāt - yāvadātmabhā vijñānākhyaguṇāśrayatvataḥ - prāptamasti || 28 |

bādarāyaṇaḥ, pūrvanyāyāt – pūrvasmādātmā dhikaraṇāt, amuṣmin - jīvātmani, jani- layarahite -- utpatti vināśābhāvavattayā pratipādite, atra ca asmin jñādhikaraṇe ca, nityabodhe ukte- svābhāvikajñātṛtvavattayā nirṇīte sati asya jīvātmanaḥ, vikṛtivacana- mapi — kāryatvavarṇanamapi, saṅkocādyarhabuddheḥ saṅkoca vikāsāībuddhitvāddhetoḥ, sadvārakaṃ syāt—svadharmabhūtajñānadvārā pravṛttaṃ bhavet ; na tu viyadā divat svarūpānyathābhāvakṛtam || 54 ||

jñātṛtvaṃ jñānatā ca śrutibhirabhidadhe nā'tra pakṣe patāmaḥ

sarvatrātmā na bhāyāt kimiti na nigame'rdehino'ṇutvasiddheḥ | svābhāsaikasvamūrteraviśadamahamaḥ sarvadā bhānamiṣṭaṃ
dhīraṅkocāt suṣuptiprabhṛtiṣu viśadollekha mātroparodhaḥ || 55 ||

atyantāyogasya ca vyāvartakatvarūpaṃ triprakāramapi bhavati yathāyogyam, asya dharmiṇi anvaye- — evakārasya viśeṣye saṅgatatve sati taditaraviṣaye prakṛtetaradharmiviṣaye, tasya dharmasya hāniḥ syāt - taddharmasyā'bhāvasiddhiḥ bhavet, jānātyeveti – 'jānātyevāyaṃ puruṣaḥ' ityayameva- kāraḥ, aboddhā na bhavati - 'ātmā kadā'pi jñānadharmakatvābhāvavān na bhavati' ityarthakaḥ, jaḍatā— ātmani acetanatā, jñānamātroktivāryā- 'vijñānamayaḥ' iti jñānaikā kārasvavacanena vyāvartyā, asau evakāraḥ - ' jānātyeva' iti kriyāsaṅgato'pyevakāraḥ, jñānālopādi .... yatām — 'na hi vijñāturvijñāterviparilopaḥ' ityetadvākyānurodhya bhidheyaka tām, (viṣaya:- abhineyaḥ) yāti prāpnoti || 56 ||

utkrāntispandanāṇūpamitivacanato'ṇīyasaḥ śaktilā bhe
dhībhūmnā yaugapadyaṃ tvavayavanayato'nekamūrtigrahe'pi |
yatrā''mnātaṃ vibhutvaṃ paraviṣayamidaṃ bhāti tātparya liṅga-
jave vyāpitvavādo matimahimaparaḥ svacchatādyāśayo vā || 57 ||

utkrānti .... vacanataḥ — 'niṣkrāmati, sarve gacchanti, vālāgraśata' iti dehādutkramaṇasya, gamanasya, vālāgraśatabhāgarūpasūkṣmārthopamyasya ca śrutau kathanataḥ, aṇīyasaḥ — atyantaṃ sūkṣmasya, jīvasya śaktilā bhe—parakāyāpraveśādisāmarthyaprāptau satyām, anekamūrtigrahe'pi- – nānā- śarīrasvīkāre'pi, yaugapadyam – ekadaiva sarvaśarīrāvacchinnasukhaduḥkhādipratisandhānam avayava- nayataḥ – ekasmin dehe hastanādādyavayaveṣviva dhībhūmnā – dharmabhūtajñānavikāsena (na virudhyate ) yatra—'sa vā eṣa mahānaja ātmā' iti yasmin śrutivākye, ( mahatvāparaparyāyaṃ vibhutvam- sarvagatatvam, (aparicchinnaparimāṇatvam ) āmnātam – upadiṣṭam idam – tadetat śruti- vacanam, tātparyaliṅgaiḥ śrutitātparyanirṇāyakaiḥ arthaprakaraṇādibhiḥ, paraviṣaya bhāti - paramātma- paramityeva sphuṭībhavati, jīve vyāpitvavādaḥ – 'nityo vyāpī pratikṣetramātmā' iti jīvātma- viṣaye vyāpakatā vyavahāraḥ, matimahimaparaḥ - ātmano guṇabhūte jñāne vidyamānasya vyāpakatvākhya- mahimnaḥ āśrayatvakṛtaṃ mahimānam ātmani pratipādayituṃ pravṛttaḥ, svacchatādyāśayo vā- ātmani vidyamānaṃ apratighātena sarvācetanāntaḥpraveśasāmarthya svacchatā para paryāyaṃ pratipādayituṃ pravṛtto vā || 57 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

adhikaraṇasāra valiḥ

jñātṛtvaṃ puṃsa itthaṃ bhavatu tadapi nāmuṣya kartṛtvasiddhiḥ śrutyādyaistanniṣedhād vikṛtivirahataścetyasad dṛṣṭabādhāt | kartṛtvāpahnavoktera vikṛtivacaso'pyānyaparyaṃ hi ! gītaṃ

kartā śāstrārthavatvāt tadayamiha na ca syādaboddhurniyogaḥ || 58 ||
puṃsaḥ jñātṛtvam - jīvasya jñānāśrayatvam, itthaṃ bhavatu – jñā'dhikaraṇoktarītyā sidhyatu nāma, tadapi tathāpi, amuṣya kartṛtvasiddhiḥ na jīvātmana: kartṛtvalābhaḥ pramāṇato naiva vaktuṃ śakyaḥ, śrutyādyaiḥ tanniṣedhāt pramāṇatayā sampratipannaiḥ śruti-smṛtiprabhṛtibhiḥ 'nāyaṃ hanti', 'vimūḍhātmā kartā'hamiti manyate ' iti tasmin kartṛtvābhāvajñāpanāt, vikṛtivirahataśca- nirvikāratvādapi jīvasya, (kartṛtāyā api vikārāntaḥ pātitvāt ) ityasat - evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ asādhu, dṛṣṭabādhāt — pratyakṣataḥ kartṛ-vagrāhakāt tadabhāvagrahasya bādhitatvāt, hi yataḥ, kartṛtvāpahnavokteḥ—kartṛtvaniṣedhakavākyasya, (tathā) avikṛtivacaso'pi - nirvikāratvaprati- pādakavākyasyāpi ca, ānyaparyam – kartṛtvasya prakṛtisaṃsargāyattatve tātparyam, gītam — 'kartā - ramātmānaṃ kevalaṃ tu yaḥ' iti gītābacanato'vagatam tat ayaṃ iha kartā — tasmāt ayaṃ jīvaḥ karmaṇi kartṛtvadharmāśraya eva, śāstrārthavatvāt – vidhiniṣedhaparāṇāṃ śāstrāṇāṃ sāphalyasya avaśyaṃ - bhāvāt, śāstraphalabhāgitvasya jīve varṇanīyatvācca, aboddhuḥ – jñātṛtvānāśrayasya pradhānādeḥ, niyogoṃ na ca syāt – niyojakaṃ śāstram śāstrataḥ siddhaṃ śāsanaṃ vā naiva svarūpaṃ sāphalyaṃ vā labhate || 58 ||

kiñca svoktyādibhano nigaditurahamaḥ kartṛtāyā niṣedho

yadyanyasyā'hamarthāt tadidamupaniṣadvedinaḥ siddhasādhyam |
bhoktṛtvasyāpyabhāve prasajati vitathaṃ bandhamokṣādiśāstra
prāṇādānādvihārāt prakṛtisamadhiko'smīti yogācca kartā || 59 ||

kiñca–api ca, ahamaḥ kartṛtāyā niṣedhaḥ jīvātmana: kartṛtvāsambhavavādaḥ, nigadituḥ vaktuḥ, svoktayādibhagnaḥ – svavacana - svānumānābhyāṃ svakartṛkābhyāṃ vyāhataḥ, ahamarthādanyasya niṣedho yadi – ahamarthabhūta jīvātmetaraviṣayo'yaṃ kartṛtvaniṣedha iti yadyucyeta tarhi tadidam -- ahamarthātiriktasya kartṛtvābhāvavacanam upaniṣadve- dinaḥ vedāntavedinaḥ, siddhasādhyam – niścitārthasādhyakaṃ sat niṣphalameva, bhoktṛtvasyāpya-

bhāve - (vyāpakabhūtasya kartṛtvasyābhāvena tadvyāpyasya) bhoktṛtvasyāpi niṣedhaparyavasāne sati, bandhamokṣādiśāstram – saṃsāra - tannivṛtti tadupāyaprabhṛtyarthāvedakaṃ hitānuśāsanaṃ sarvam, vitathaṃ prasajati—vyarthamāpatati, (sarvasyā'pi tasya bhoktṛtvāyattatvāt ), prāṇādānāt – svabhogārthaṃ indriyāṇāṃ preraṇāt, vihārāt —tadarthameva śarīrapreraṇācca śrutyuktāta, prakṛtisamadhiko'smīti yogācca mokṣasādhanatvenābhimatasya 'prakṛteranyo'hamasmi' ityevaṃrūpasya samādheḥ āśrayatvāya ca, kartā - ( jīvātmaiva ) kartṛtvavān na buddhyādiḥ || 59 ||

vyāpāra - jñāna- vāñchā praśakana yatanā yogayuktistu mandā

kārye sāmagryapekṣe vidhi- taditarayorloka siddhā pravṛttiḥ |
sārtha śāstra hitoktyā niyatiniyamitā śāstrayogyā daśā sā
jñātā kartā ca bhoktā tadayamiha pumān bhāti sarvaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ || 60 ||

vyāpāra.... yuktistu — upādānā disūtroktavyāpāra- tadaupāyikajñātṛtva - tajjanyecchā- tatsādhya kāryanirvahaṇasāmarthya - tatprayojakaprayatnānāṃ jīvātmanyasambhavanvopapādanārthaṃ darśitā yukti:- param, mandā—yuktatyābhāsarūpaiva (vibhutva-nirvikāratvayoḥ abhimatā sādhakatvāt ) vidhi- taditarayoḥ - 'idaṃ kuru' iti vidheḥ, 'idaṃ mā kuru' iti niṣedhasya ca, sāmayyapekṣe kārye— kāraṇasamavadhānāvalambini pravṛttinivṛttirūpe viṣaye, ( tādṛśaṃ sādhyamuddiśya ) pravṛttiḥ tatta-- darheṣvadhikāriṣu vyāpṛtiḥ, lokasiddhā – sārvajanīnā dṛśyate, śāstram – laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ vā śāsanam, hitoktyā – puruṣārthasādhanāvagamanena, sārtham – saphalaṃ bhavati, śāstrayogyā sā daśā —śāstrabodhitahitānuṣṭhānārhā vilakṣaṇā avasthā ca, niyatiniyamitā — daivakṛtavarṇāśramādi- niyamaviśeṣāvalambanādhīnā, tat — uktā detasmāddhetujātāt, ayaṃ pumān - dehendriyādyadhiṣṭhātā cetanaḥ, iha–asmin śāstre, sarvaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ tatvanirṇāyakaiḥ pratyakṣādibhiḥ sādhanaiḥ jñātāḥ kartā bhoktā ca bhāti -jñātṛtva kartṛtva-bhoktṛtvānāmāśrayaḥ san pratipanno bhavati || 60 ||

-0-

'prakṛteḥ kriyamāṇāni' ityādīnāṃ jīvasya sāṃsārika kartṛtvasya guṇasaṃsargāyattatvapratipādanaparatvācca " 'ātmā kartā' ityuktam ||

taccasāraḥ
ādāvīśvaradattayaiva puruṣaḥ svātantryaśaktayā svayaṃ
tattajjñāna- cikīrṣaṇa-prayatanānyutpādayan vartate |
tatro'pekṣya tato'numatya vidadhat tannigrahānugrahau
tattatkarmaphalaṃ prayacchati tataḥ sarvasya puṃso hariḥ || 46 ||

puruṣaḥ – saṃsārī cetanaḥ, ādau -karaṇa kalebarapradānātmaka sṛṣṭisamaye eva, īśvara- dattayaiva paramapuruṣaprasāditayaiva, svātantryaśaktyā svātatryāparaparyāyayā svagatayā kāryanirvahaṇa- śaktyā, svayam—svācchandyenaiva pūrvavāsanāyattena) svataḥ, tattajjñāna .... tanāni - tattadabhimata-- viṣayakaṃ jñānaṃ, tādṛśīmicchām, tādṛśamudyogamapi ca, utpādayan – svasmin janayan, vartate dehādhiṣṭhātā bhavati, hariḥ – sarveśvaro nārāyaṇaḥ, tatra tadīyacchandānurodhini tadīye ādime udyoge, upekṣya – tūṣṇīmbhāvarūpa maudāsīnyamāśritya tataḥ anantarabhāvitattadarthaviṣayaka - dvitīyādyudyoge anumatya tatpracodana tatsāhāyyādinā taṃ poṣayitvā tataḥ tadanantaram, tannigrahānugrahau vidadhat – yathāyogyaṃ tadviṣaye kopaṃ pratīti ca kurvan tattatkarmaphalam - tattadīya karmā svātmapradānādimahāndhatamomaya sthānaprāpaṇaparyantaṃ uttama - madhyamādhamarūpaṃ phalaviśeṣam, sarvasya puṃsaḥ – samastasya cetanavargasya, prayacchati – ( priyadveṣyabhāvavimokena vitarati || 46 ||

duṣkarmasvanivartanānumanane puṃsaḥ karotyacyutaḥ

svātantryeṇa niraṅkuśena saguṇaḥ śrutyā na doṣo hareḥ |
vāriṣu nigraho guṇatayā loke na doṣātmanā

na syādāśrayasiddhiraupaniṣadaṃ no cet pramāṇaṃ vacaḥ || 47 ||

acyutaḥ — bhagavān nārāyaṇaḥ, niraṅkuśena svātantryeṇa — apratihatena svātantryeṇa, puṃsaḥ duṣkarmasu - cetanakartṛkeṣu svājñāviruddheṣu pāpakarmasu anivartanānumanane - pravṛttasyā nivāraṇaṃ tataḥ anujñādānaṃ ca karoti - tanute, saḥ tādṛśaḥ svatantrabhāvaḥ, guṇaḥ - atiśayā pādaka eva iti śrutyā – vedavācā avagamyate, saḥ hareḥ na doṣaḥ - tādṛśaḥ svatantra bhāvaḥ hare: sarvotkṛṣṭatva- vighātako na bhavati, (ityapi śrutyā'vagamyate,) ariṣu nigrahaḥ – śatruviṣaye kāpakṛto daṇḍana- vyāpāraḥ, loke ca—jagatyapi, guṇatayā dṛṣṭaḥ praśaṃsā hetutvenaiva anubhūtaḥ, na doṣātmanā - garhāhetutayā na avagataḥ, aupaniṣadaṃ vacaḥ īdṛśeśvaragrāhakaṃ vedāntavākyam, pramāṇaṃ na cet–tattvayāthātmyāvagamasādhanatayā na svīkriyeta yadi, āśrayasiddhiḥ na syāt – dūṣyatayā

- nāstikābhimatasya īśvarasyaivālābho bhavet, (yadi pramāṇaṃ tadā dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhaḥ upajīvyavirodha eveti ca īdharasya dūṣyateti ) || 47 ||

duṣkarmavyavasāyatastu virato yastasya puṃsaḥ purā-
bhūyojanmasamārjitānyagaṇitānyāgāṃsyanādṛtya yat | tasyānantasukhāptaye ca yatate lakṣmīsahāyaḥ svayaṃ
tatkāruṇyapuraḥsaro guṇagaṇastasyā'yamujjṛmbhate || 48 ||

yastu — ya eva cetanaḥ, duṣkarmavyavasāyataḥ pāpakriyodyamāt virataḥ nivṛttamanāḥ, tasya puṃsaḥ tasya cetanasya, purā... rjitāni prāktanabahubhavasampāditāni, agaṇitāni āgāṃsi saṅkhyātumaśakyāni pāpajātāni, anādṛtya daṇḍana hetutvenā'vigaṇayya, yat- yena hetunā, tasya aparādhino'pi virataduṣkarmodyamasya puṃsaḥ, anantasukhāptaye ca niravadhika- mahānandaprāpaṇāyā'pi, lakṣmīsahāyaḥ – śriyaḥ patirnārāyaṇaḥ, svayaṃ yatate - svayamapi kṛtodyamo bhavati, tat— tasmāt, tasya – bhagavato viṣṇoḥ, kāruṇyapuraḥ saro'yaṃ guṇagaṇaḥ – nirupādhika- dayāprabhṛtiḥ śāstrasiddho guṇasamūhaḥ, ujjṛmbhate – pravṛddhaḥ san pravṛddhaḥ san virājate || 48 ||

tatphalaṃ ca svīkṛtya – kriyāyāṃ svātantryaṃ kriyāphalabhāgitāṃ ca abhyupagamya, śrutiśata vidite ātmeśamātre - bahuśrutipratipanne sarvātmaniyantari paramapuruṣe param itthaṃ dveṣa:- kāraṇatvāsaha virodhabhāva īdṛśaḥ, durantaḥ– duṣpariṇāmakārīti hetoḥ na kāryaḥ (ato jīvaḥ parāyattasvātantryavā neveti ) || 61 ||

sādhāraṇyena hetuḥ salilamitra vibhuḥ sarvakāryākurāṇāṃ
vaiṣamyaṃ tvāviriñcāt pratiniyataphalaiḥ prāṇināṃ karmabījaiḥ | sāmyaṃ svasya svagītaṃ śrutamapi tadihā'ghoninīṣādibheda-
stādṛkkarmānurūpaṃ phalamiti niyato'nādireṣa pravāha: || 62 ||

sarvakāryākurāṇām sarveṣāmapi sarvabīja sambandhināmapi aṅkurāṇāṃ jāyamānānām, sādhāraṇyena sāmānyarūpeṇa, salilaṃ heturiva — jalaṃ yathā kāraṇaṃ tathaiva sarvakāryākurā- ṇām – aṅkurasādṛśyena janimatām sarveṣāmapyarthānām, sādhāraṇyena kāryatvarūpa sāmānyā kā reṇa, vibhuḥ sarveśvaraḥ paramātmā, hetuḥ kāraṇabhūto bhavati, vaiṣamyaṃ tu sṛjyavastuṣu vaicitryaṃ tu, āviriścāt – vyaṣṭisṛṣṭikartuḥ hiraṇyagarbhādārabhya prāṇinām – akhilānāmeva dehinām, pratiniyataphalaiḥ suvyavasthita viśiṣṭaphalavadbhiḥ karmabījaiḥ viśiṣṭabīja sadṛśaiḥ viśiṣṭaiḥ tattatkarmabhireva bhavati ; ( na tu sādhāraṇahetunā paramātmanā ) svasya - bhagavataḥ svasya, sāmyam - sarvasādhāraṇyena niṣpakṣapātitvam svagītam – svena (paramātmanaiva ) spaṣṭaṃ kathitam 'samo'haṃ sarvabhūteṣu' iti, śrutamapi ca - śrutyāpi varṇitam 'śāstā janānāṃ sarvātmā' iti, tat uktāddhetoḥ, iha - bhagavati (vidyamānatayā śruti siddha:), adhoninīṣādibhedaḥ - adholokanayano'rdhvaloka- nayanā disaṅkalparūpo viṣayabhāva:, tādṛkkarmānurūpaṃ phalamiti tattajjīvīyatādṛśa karmānuguṇa- phalātmakamevetyataḥ, eṣa pravāhaḥ – 'vilakṣaṇe sukṛtaduṣkṛte, vilakṣaṇe ca sukhaduḥkhādike' ityevaṃrūpaḥ kāryakāraṇasantānaḥ, anādiḥ niyataḥ - ajñāte prathamatākaḥ mitho'saṅkīrṇatayā atyantavyavasthitasvarūpa svabhāvavāniti jñeyam || 62 ||
kāle duḥkhopaśāntiṃ janayati bhagavān vyājamātrāvalambī yā duḥkhāpācikīrṣā parahitamanasaḥ saiva tasyānukampā |
datte dehādi 'yogaṃ' diśati ca nigamaṃ vakti vedāntasāraṃ
śrī niḥsīmānandayogaṃ nirupadhisamaye sauti puṃsāṃ ‘tayaiva' || 63 ||

bhagavān ṣaḍguṇaiśvarya sampūrṇaḥ sarveśvaraḥ, kāle – sukṛtaparipākasamaye, vyājamātrāva- –ṣaḍguṇaiśvaryasampūrṇaḥ lambī - bhagavatpratyupāyānuṣṭhāna saṅkalpa tatpravṛttyā dikameva (vastutaḥ phalapradānayogyatā rahitamapi ) upāyatvena parigaṇayya, duḥkhopaśāntiṃ janayati — niravaśeṣatayā sarvaduḥ khaprahāṇarūpāṃ śānti cetanānāmutyādayati, parahitamanasaḥ anya hite damparasvāntasya tasya — paramapuruṣasya, duḥkhā- pācikīrṣā yā anyadīyaduḥ khonmūlana viṣayiṇī yā svābhāvikī apekṣā'sti, saitra anukampā – saiva anukampā dayā ityucyate, tayaiva – dayayaiva, puṃsām – cetanānām, dehādi- yogam – (mumukṣā mokṣopāyānuṣṭhānādya) dehendriyādisambandham datte anugṛhṇāti, nigamaṃ ca diśatitatvahita puruṣārthāvedakaṃ vedarūpaṃ dīpaṃ ca prayacchati, vedāntasāraṃ vakti - (avilambena bhagavatprāpyupāyapratipattisādhanabhūtaṃ ) vedāntaparamapradhānārthamapi sadācāryādyantaḥ praviṣṭaḥ san prakaṭī- karoti, nirupadhisamaye -- dehendriyādyupādhivigamakāle nissīmānandayogaṃ sauti – paramānandalābhaṃ ca svābhāvikaṃ vitarati || 63 ||

doṣa: syānniprahāṃśo yama yituriti cennopamardāsatvāt
'svāniṣṭaṃ "neśvare hi prasajati na parāniṣṭamasya pratīpam | kāruṇyaṃ sāvakāśaṃ kvaciditi kathitaṃ sākṣitādyaṃ ca susthaṃ

dṛṣṭe caitatsvabhāve phalada iti dhiyā yujyate tatprapattiḥ || 64 ||

yamayituḥ sarvaniyāmakasvātantryavataḥ sarveśvarasya, nigrahāṃśaḥ - nigrahānugrahayorekataraḥ nigrahaḥ, doṣaḥ syāditi cet — tatsamāśrayaṇavighaṭako bhavet iti cintanam na-na kāryam, upamardāsahatvāt kathaṃ doṣa: ? kīdṛśo doṣa: ? kasya doṣa: ? ityevamādirītyā śodhane doṣarūpatvāsiddheḥ, hi-yataḥ, īśvare — niyantari paramātmani, svāniṣṭaṃ na prasajati nigraha- nibandhanaṃ tadanabhimataṃ kiñcit naiva āpadyate, parāniṣṭam — nigrahataḥ prāptaṃ parakīyaṃ anabhimataṃ yadbhavettat asya na pratīpam – īśvarasya doṣarūpaṃ na bhavati, kāruṇyam – nirhetuka sahetuko bhayarūpamapi bhagavato daya. svarūpam, kvacit sāvakāśamiti sṛṣṭyārambhe ( yathāyogyakaraṇa- kalebarasampattaye) paścāt, (svargāpavargārthopāyānuṣṭhānataḥ tattatphalāvāptaye) ca saviṣayameva vijṛmbhate iti kathitam - pūrvasmin śloke pratipāditam, sākṣitādyaṃ ca - bhagavati jīvakṛta sukṛtaduṣkṛtādikaṃ prati) sākṣitva - anumantṛtva-prerakatva udāsīnatva- sahakāritva- phalapradatva- sarvaphalitvādi akhilam, sustham - doṣānāvahaṃ san acālyamasti etatsvabhāve dṛṣṭe-

etasmiṃśca nikhile bhagavataḥ svabhāve śāstrācāryāditaḥ paricite sati, phalada iti dhiyā -- bhagavān phalapradānasamartha iti buddhayā, tatprapattiḥ tasmin bhagavati śaraṇavaraṇam, yujyate nyāyyaṃ bhavati || 64 ||
pratyaṅḍātmā'hamarthaḥ pramitiparavatāṃ kartṛtādiśca tasmin
svecchā pūrva pravṛtterayamacidadhikastā 'vateśāna ' tulyaḥ |
īśastu svecchayaiva prayatata iti tanninnamanyat samastaṃ
sārathyādikrameṇa pratiniyatagatiḥ syāt trayāṇāṃ pravṛttiḥ || 65 ||
pramitiparavatām - pramāṇaparādhīnānām, ahamartha:- ahamityullikhyamāno'rthaḥ, pratyaṅṅkā- tmā—svasmaibhāsamānasvarūpaścatanaḥ tasmin — tādṛśe cetane, kartṛtādiśca kartṛtva- bhoktṛtva- jñātṛtvādidharmo vartate, ayam – kartṛtvādimān jīvaḥ, svecchā pūrvapravṛtteḥ

pravṛttisvabhāvatvāt acidadhikaḥ – acetanavilakṣaṇaḥ tadatiśayitaśca tāvatā īśānatulyaḥ - etāvatāṃśena īśvarasadṛśaḥ, īśastu svecchayaiva prayatata iti — īśvaraḥ paraṃ svasaṅkalpamātrādhīna- pravṛttimān iti hetoḥ (saḥ jīvādapyadhikaḥ) anyat samastam - īśvarātiriktaṃ sarvam, tanninam — īśvara paratantram, trayāṇāṃ pravṛttiḥ - cidacidīśvarāṇāṃ trayāṇāmapi vyāpṛtiḥ, sārathyādikrameṇa sārathi - rathi sthānāṃ rītyā pratiniyatagatiḥ syāt – suvyavasthitasvabhāvā jāgarti || 65 ||
ra kartṛtvaṃ syāt kadācit karaṇavati parapreraṇā nirvyapekṣa

no cet tannigrahādyaṃ kathamiti yadi na sveṣṭapakṣe'pi sāmyāt | svenā'pathyapravṛttaṃ na hi punarapi tatkārayeyurdayārdrāḥ
"taccet tasya' svabhāvāditaradapi na kiṃ niṣphalo'dhītabhaṅgaḥ || 66 ||
karaṇavati—śarīrendriyādimati jīve, kadācit - ādipravṛttisamaye, parapreraṇā nirvya- pekṣaṃ kartṛtvam - paramātmapracodanānadhīnaṃ kevalaṃ svāyattaṃ kartṛtvam, (sambhavati), no cet- ādimapravṛttāvapi paramātmāyattaṃ yadi syāt tarhi ! tannigrahādyam – svapreraṇāmātrāyattapravṛtti- mataścatanasya nigraho vā anugraho vā, kathamiti yadi - ( svenaiva preritamuddiśya daṇḍanādeḥ unmattavyā pāratāprasaktyā) kathaṃ vā ucitaḥ syāt ? ityevaṃ yadi śaṅkyeta tadā, na-nedaṃ śaṅkanaṃ yujyate, sveṣṭapakṣe'pi sāmyāt - dvitīyādipravṛttiṣu īśvarāyattatvamiti svasaṃmate'pi pakṣe,

asya doṣasya samānatvāt svena parapreraṇāṃ vinā svata eva apathya pravṛttam — ahitaphale karmaṇyudyuktaṃ puruṣaṃ, dayārdrāḥ paraduḥkhāpācikīrṣā tatparamṛduhṛdayāḥ santaḥ punarapi - dvitīya- vāramapi vā tat na hi kārayeyuḥ tādṛśamapathyaṃ karma kartuṃ naiva khalu prerayeyuḥ, tat tasya svabhāvāt cet — dvitīyādi vṛttau prerakatvaṃ bhagavataḥ svabhāvakṛtamityataḥ na ākṣepārhamiti samādhīyate tadā, itaradapi kiṃ na ādyapravṛttau tadīyaprāgvāsanā sāpekṣapravartakatvam dvitīyādi- pravṛttāvanumatya nigrahādikamapi ca tasya svabhāvakṛtamiti kuto na samādhīyate ? adhītabhaṅgaḥ- ( evaṃ samādhāne sugame) śrutyādyukta sarva kāraṇatvādeḥ sarveśvare bhañjanaprayatnaḥ, niṣkalaḥ - niṣprayojana eveti jīvasya sārvadikaṃ parāyattakartṛtvaṃ nirābādham || 66 ||

kṣetrajñānāṃ samānāṃ viṣamayatanatā tādṛśādṛṣṭabhedā-
nnādṛṣṭaṃ tvanyadiṣṭaṃ niyamana bhidayā 'śāsitustatra 'bhāvyam |
sākṣitvā ca netuḥ samanigamamitaṃ prerakatvaṃ na rundhe
bhāṣyādigranthaleśo'pyavahitamanasā maidamarthyaṃ bhajeta || 67 ||
kṣetrajñānām - jīvātmanām, samānām - svarūpataḥ svabhāvataśca aviṣamāṇāmapi satām, viṣamayatanatā — pravṛttivaiṣamyam, tādṛśādṛṣṭabhedāt tadanuguṇādṛṣṭavaiṣamyāt, (iti prathama pravṛttau jīvasya adṛṣṭāyattakartṛtvameva ; na paramātmāyattakartṛtvam - iti śaṅkane) adṛṣṭaṃ tu - adṛṣṭapadābhi- dheyaṃ tu, anyat — vaiśeṣika mīmāṃsakādyabhimatasvarūpam, na iṣṭam vedāntibhirnābhyupagatam, tatra - sargādyakāle, śāsituḥ sarvaniyanturīśvarasya, niyamanabhidayā – niyamanapadavācyasaṅkalpa- viśeṣeṇaiva, bhāvyam – adṛṣṭātmanā bhavitavyam ; nānyena, (ataḥ niyamanadvārā īśvarakāraṇatve eva viśrāntam ) netuḥ pravṛttihetorīśvarasya, sākṣitvādyaṃ ca – prathamapravṛttau kevaladraṣṭṛtvā dikaṃ ca, samanigamamitam - sākṣitvapramāpakasadṛśapramāṇapramitam prerakatvam – nirupādhikaṃ prayojaka- kartṛtvam, na rundhena vārayati, bhāṣyādigranthaleśo'pi etadadhikaraṇīyaśrībhāṣya- tatvasārādiprāktanapranthāna| maikaikoṃḍaśo'pi, anucintyamānaḥ, avahitamanasām — tadekacittānām, aidamarthyaṃ bhajeta etasminnukte'rthe eva tātparya prāpnuyādeva, (na ānyaparyam ) || 67 ||

sarvaśrutyādikopānna bhavati na phalaṃ karmaṇaḥ pāpacaryā | karmādhīnaṃ tu cintādyapi hi bhavabhṛto bhāṣitaṃ bhāṣyakāre- jantūnāṃ devatānāmapi karaṇagaṇādhiṣṭhitaṃ vakṣyatīttham || 68 ||

deva: sarveśvaraḥ, phalānāṃ kartā-karmaphalānāṃ pradātava, na tu karaṇabhṛtaḥ prerakaśva- na tu dehendriyādimataḥ cetanasya karmaṇāṃ prayojaka kartā'pi ityayuktam — evaṃ śaṅkanamasādhu ; sarvaśrutyādiko pāt– 'śāstā janānām karaṇādhipādhipaḥ, īśvarapreritaḥ' ityevamādisakala śruti- smṛtivirodhāt, karmaṇaḥ prāgbhavīyasya karmaṇaḥ, pāpacaryā etadbhavīya duṣkṛtānuṣṭhānam, phalaṃ na bhavatīti na - paripākaprayojanarūpaṃ na bhavatīti vaktuṃ na śakyate, hi-yataḥ, bhavabhṛtaḥ- baddhajīvasya, cintādyapi—sādhvasādhuviṣayālocanādikamapi, karmādhīnaṃ tu prāktanakarmaviśeṣā- yattameva, na tādātvikam (iti) bhāṣyakāraiḥ bhāṣitam - śrībhāṣyakāraiḥ bhagavadrāmānujai: viśadīkṛtam, jantūnāṃ devatānāmapi sarveṣāṃ prāṇabhṛtām, indrādīnāmapi ca, karaṇa- gaṇādhiṣṭhitam - dehendriyādyadhiṣṭhānam, (agnyādidevatānāṃ sakalaprāṇisambandhivāgādyadhiṣṭhānaṃ ca) ittham – īśvarapreraṇāyattamityeva, vakṣyati - bhāṣyakāraḥ prakaṭīkariṣyati || 68 ||

-0-

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ

parāyattādhikaraṇe jīvasya svataḥ pravṛttiśaktayabhāve, vidhiniṣedhaśāstrānarthakyaprasaṅgāt, nīvasya kartṛtvaṃ svātantryeṇa — ityāśaṅkhaya |

'antaḥ praviṣṭaḥ' ityādinā paramātmādhīnatvaśravaṇāt, tadadhīnatve prathamapravṛttau jīvasya svatantratayā śāstrānarthakyābhāvācca, parādhīnameva kartṛtvam — ityuktam ||

bhedāt — jīvaparayoḥ pṛthaktvasya śrutitaḥ siddhe, jīvaḥ na aṃśa iti — jīvātmā "paramātmanaḥ aṃśātmako na bhavatītyeva citam śrutīnāṃ tu - jīva- parasvarūpa vedanapravṛttānāṃ veda girāṃ tu, itaretareṇa virodhaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ– anyonyaṃ anaikakaṇṭhyam, abhiprāyabhedaśca spaṣṭapratipannaḥ (iti pūrvapakṣe ) pādatayā upadeśāt - ' pādo'sya' iti śrutyā sarvasyā'pi pādatvabodhanāt, aṃśasmṛte:- 'mamevāṃśaḥ' iti gītasmṛtisattvāt, asya parasya – prakrāntasya paramātmanaḥ, jīvaḥ aṃśa eva syāt — jīvātmā viśiṣṭavastvekadeśatvarūpāṃśatvavāneva bhavati || 31 ||

}

-0-

kimanyat upaniṣatpratipādyam ? iti praśna, advaitam – pratyakṣaviditadvaitaviruddhaṃ abhedarūpamadvaitameva, ananyagocaratayā - śrutītarāgamyatvena tadvedyam āsthīyatām — upaniṣadvedyamiti svīkriyatām, aprāpte khalu – pramāṇāntarāvedye hi viṣaye, śāstra arthavat - śāstrākhyaṃ pramāṇaṃ saprayojanatāmaśnute, iti prayāsaḥ evamādivyavahārarūpaḥ prayatnaviśeṣaḥ, vyarthaḥ - svābhimatādvaitasādhanena phalavān na bhavati, yataḥ - yasmāddhetoḥ, prakhyātādaparaḥ bheda: – loka siddhādbhinnaḥ niyāmya-niyāmaka - bhāvādiprayojakaḥ jīvaparayorbhedaḥ, tvadadvaitavat – tvadabhimatābhedavat śāstraviṣayastu - lokā- viditatvāt śāstraikalabhya eva || 50 ||

yacchvetāśvataraśrutirnṛbahutā ko bahūnāmiti
brūte tatra vidhirviśiṣṭaviṣayastvaṣṭākapālādivat |
nityatvātma bahutvamatra nigamādanyatra vidmaḥ kathaṃ ?
pratyaktvena parāktayā svaparayāmuktau ca bhedaḥ sphuṭa: || 51 ||

śvetāśvataraśrutiḥ – 'nityo nityānāṃ cetanaścetanānāmeko bahūnāṃ yo vidadhāti kāmān' itīyaṃ śrutiḥ, eko bahūnāmiti – 'eko bahūnā 'miti mantraikadeśena nṛbahutāṃ brūte yat - cetanānāṃ nānātvaṃ pratipādayatīti yadasti tatra vidhiḥ - cetanabahutve ajñātajñāpanavyāpāraḥ, aṣṭākapālādivat – 'yadāgneyo'ṣṭākapāla:' ityādivākyena aṣṭasaṅkhyā ka kapāla saṃskṛtāgnidevatāka- puroḍāśaviśiṣṭāmāvāsyā paurṇamāsyātmaka kāla viśiṣṭayāgaviśeṣavidhiriva viśiṣṭaviṣayastu — nityatva-bahutvaviśiṣṭacetana sambandhiphala vidhāyitva viśiṣṭa nityaika cetanaviṣayaka eva, (pūrṇavākya- bodhyaviśiṣṭārthagocara eva) atra — vākye vidheyatvābhimatam, nityatvātma bahutvam – nityatva- viśiṣṭaṃ (sāmānādhikaraṇyena) ātmanānātvam, nigamādanyatra – vedaṃ vihāya pramāṇāntarāt, kathaṃ vidmaḥ 1– kena vā sādhanena avagacchāmaḥ ?, muktau ca - atikrāntasaṃsāratādaśāyāmapi jīvagatāyām, sva-parayoḥ – jīvātma- paramātmanoḥ, pratyatvena parāktayā ca bhedaḥ - yathākramaṃ ahambuddhi- idambuddhi. bodhyatvakṛtaḥ pṛthagbhāvaḥ, sphuṭa: - atirohitaḥ jāgarti || 51 || sāmānādhikaraṇyabhāgiṣu padastomeṣu sarveṣu kiṃ

pratyāyyaṃ vyatiricyate pratipadaṃ vaktavyamadvaitinā |
yadvā neti na cedanarthakatayā naikātiriktaṃ padaṃ
paṭhyeta prativādinastvitarathā yuktiḥ svamāsīdati || 52 ||
sāmānādhikaraṇyabhāgiṣu —– samānavibhaktikeṣu, padastomeṣu sarveṣu - 'satyaṃ jñānam', 'tattvamasi', ‘ayamātmā brahma', 'sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ' ityādiṣu padaja | teṣu nikhileṣu pratyāyyam-

- svena svena bodhyam, pratipadam - pratyekasminnapi pade, vyatiricyate yadvā na iti - bhidyate uta na iti praśna, kiṃ vaktavyamadvaitinā-kiṃ vā prativacanīyaṃ bhavati nirviśeṣabrahmātmaikatva- bādinā ? (naiva kimapi), na vyatiricyate cet — na bhidyate iti yadyuttaraṃ dīyate tadā, anarthakatā – ekādhika padajātasya bodhyavailakṣaṇyakṛtasā phalyābhāvena, ekātiriktaṃ padaṃ na paśyeta ekādhikaṃ padajātaṃ naiva śrutau āmnāyeta, itarathā - 'pratipadaṃ vyatiricyate' iti yadyuttaraṃ dīyeta tadā, prativādino yuktiḥ – prativādibādhakatayā bhavadupanyastā samānādhikaraṇya- - phalabhūtākhaṇḍārthāsiddhirūpā yuktiḥ, svam āsīdati – ātmānamevā'nudhāvati ; akhaṇḍārtha- - sādhanāśakteḥ iti || 52 ||

dṛśyatvādanṛtaṃ vigītamiti yad dṛṣṭāntayanto jaguḥ
śuktyāropitarūpyamatra katicit tatredamācakṣmahe | dharmagrāhakamāna (bādhita) dhikkṛtamidaṃ sopādhikatvaṃ punaḥ
sautraṃ vyaktamabhaṅguraṃ vyabhicaralliṅgaṃ ca bhaṅgāya vaḥ || 53 ||

katicit — advaitisāmayikāḥ, atra – svokte viśvamithyātve, śuktyāropitarūpyam - śuktikākhye dharmiṇyadhyastaṃ rajatam dṛṣṭāntayantaḥ - sādhanīye mithyātve nidarśanī kurvantaḥ, vigītam - satyatva-mithyātvābhyāṃ vipratipattiviṣayabhūtaṃ jagat, dṛśyatvāt - pratyakṣagocaratvāt, anṛtamiti – mithyeti, yat jaguḥ – sādhayāmāsuriti yadasti, tatra idamācakṣmahe tasmin viṣaye doṣajātaṃ pratipādayāma:, (kimiti cet ) idam – dṛśyatvahetukaṃ mithyātvānumānam, dharmigrāhaka mānadhikkṛtam - midhyātvadharmibhūtajagaddma | haka pratyakṣavādhitaṃ bhavati, ('vahniranuṣṇa: 'pratyakṣatvāt jalavat' ityanumānasya uṣṇatvagrāhiṇā tvācapratyakṣeṇa bādhitatvamiva) sopādhikatvaṃ punaḥ - anumānasya bādhitatva khyopā ghigrasta tvaṃ tu, sautram — 'vaidharmyācce 'ti sūtrapratipannam, abhaṅguraṃ vyaktam - aparihāryamityetat suspaṣṭam liṅgaṃ ca hetutvenopāttaṃ dṛśyatvamapi, vyabhicarat – 'dṛśyate tvayyayā' iti śrutisiddhadṛśyatvavati brahmaṇi mithyātvānabhyupagamena prāptavyabhicāradoṣaṃ sat, vaḥ bhaṅgāya - yuṣmākaṃ abhimatārthavighaṭanāya parājayāya ca bhavati || 53 ||

mithyātvaṃ bhrāntisiddhaṃ yadi khalu jagataḥ sādhyate siddhasādhyo
hetuḥ satyaḥ prapañco bhavati ca yadi vā satyamadvaita hāni: sādhyaṃ brahmasvarūpaṃ yadi bhavati tadā siddhasādhyatvameva
syādevaṃ ca prapañcavyudasanasaraṇirdūrataste nirastā || 54 ||

jagataḥ - prapañcasya, bhrāntisiddhaṃ mithyātvam – mithyābhūtaṃ mithyātvam, yadi khalu sādhyate dṛśyatva hetu kānumitividheyatāmāpādyate yadi tarhi hetuḥ – sādhanatvābhimataḥ dṛśyatva- rūpo'rthaḥ siddhasādhyaḥ – pakṣatā vaikalyavān (bhrāntisiddhatvena sandehānavakāśāt ) prapañcaḥ - sādhya mithyātvadharmibhūto'rthaḥ (jagadrūpo'rthaḥ ), satyo bhavati — satya eva syāt, yadi vā satyam - pāramārthikameva mithyātvaṃ yadyanumitividheyīkriyate tarhi, advaitahāniḥ brahmātirikta- mithyātvabhaṅgaḥ, (mithyātvasya paramārthasyābhyupagamāt ) sādhyam – anumeyabhūtaṃ mithyātvam, yadi brahmasvarūpaṃ bhavati tadā – brahmānatiriktasvarūpameva abhyupagataṃ bhavedyadi tarhi, siddhasādhyatvameva syāt śrutyādibhirniścita svarūpasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ anumeyatvābhyupagamakṛtaḥ siddhasādhanākhyo doṣaḥ anumānasyāpatati, evañca – ukta vikalpataḥ mithyātvānumānadau. sthye sati te prapañca vyudasana- saraṇiḥ - advaitinastatra brahmātiriktanirākaraṇarītiḥ, dūrato nirastā syāt — sarvathā asiddha- svarūpaiva bhavet || 54 ||

cinmātravyatireki vastu sakalaṃ mithyeti joghuṣyate
yogācāramate yadaupaniṣadīkartuṃ tadākṛṣyate |
māyāvādibhirapratīta nigama nyāya pramāṇāntara-
prasthānairapahastayanti hi ! saha nyāyaiḥ pramāṇāni tat || 55 ||

yogācāramate_yogācārākhyabauddhasiddhānte, cinmātrāvyatireki kevalāyā: saṃvi- do'tiriktaṃ jñātṛ- jñeyātmakam, sakalaṃ vastu – nikhilamapi pāramārthikam, mithyeti joghuṣyate yat — aparamārthamityeva śabdaughena mahatā prasāryata iti yadasti, tat — cinmātrātireki mithyātvam aupaniṣadīkartum - śrutyantārūḍha tāmāpādayitum, apratīta.... prasthānaiḥ śruti- tarka - pratyakṣādiṣu vidyamāne svābhāvike arthaniścāyakatve alabdhaviśvāsaiḥ, māyāvādibhiḥ— advaitisamayanirvāhakaiḥ, ākṛṣyate śrutyādiḥ balāt tatparatayā netuṃ prayatyate, tat - yogācāra- matam, pramāṇāni - pratyakṣānumānāgamarūpāṇi sarvāṇyapi, nyāyaiḥ saha – svānugrāhaka tarka saha- kṛtāni bhūtvā apahastayanti pramāṇapathāt dūrato niṣkāsayanti || 55 ||

nānātva- kṣaṇikatvayorupagate rekatva - nityatvayo.
rapyekatra mate paratra ca dhiyo vaiṣamyamastīti cet |
māyāvādimatasya saugatamatādetanna yat tāttviko

dharmastatra matadvaye'pi na dhiyaḥ kaścit svarūpādyate || 56 ||

ekatra mate – yogācāramate, dhiyaḥ-saṃvidaḥ, nānātvakṣaṇikatvayorupagate:- (vijñānasantateḥ kṣaṇikāyāḥ svīkāreṇa ) bahutva - kṣaṇaika vṛttitvayorabhyupagamāt paratra ca - māyāvādināṃ mate ca, ekatvanityatvayoḥ upagaterapi - akhaṇḍaikasvarūpatāyāḥ avināśitāyāśca aṅgīkārāt, saugatamatāt māyāvādimatasya vaiṣamyamasti iti cet — bauddhamatāpekṣayā advaitimatasya bailakṣaṇyaṃ vartata eveti śaṅkane, etat na - idaṃ vaiṣamyakathanaṃ na suṣṭhu yat - yena hetunā, tatra matadvaye'pi saṃvinmātrasatyatāva disau gatavedāntyekadeśi siddhāntadvaye'pi, dhiyaḥ svarūpāhate—vijñānātmakamekaṃ dharmiṇaṃ vinā, kaścit tātviko dharmaḥ yaḥ ko'pi pāramārthikaḥ vijñānāśritaḥ dharmapadābhidheyo'rthaḥ, na naivābhyupagata iti, vaiṣamyāyogāt (nityatvaikatvādi tu adhikaraṇānatiriktam ) || 56 ||

svarūpānāditva - svaparaghaṭane durghaṭanatā
pravāhānāditvaṃ punariti catasro hi gatayaḥ |
asatyā vidyāyā gaditumanavasthā parihṛtau
matāstvetāmtulyāḥ sphuṭama sadadhiṣṭhāna saraṇau || 57 ||

re re khaṇḍanakāra ! -- he nikṛṣṭa ! khaṇḍanākhyagranthakartaḥ !, khaṇḍaya ! – sarvapāramārthyaṃ- vādimataṃ dūṣaya (nirbharam ), bhavatpakṣaḥ pratikṣipyate - bhavadīyaḥ pāramārthyavighaṭanodyataḥ pakṣaḥ nirasyate ; (katham ?) tvatkathitaiḥ tarkaiḥ – mādhyamikanirāsakatayā bhavaduktaireva tarkaiḥ sādhanabhūtaiḥ, - tataḥ kimiti cet — khaṇḍanagranthanirasanena bhavatāṃ ko lābha iti cet ! asmanmataṃ tiṣṭhati - asmadīyaṃ jagatpārarthyamatam abādhitamavasthitaṃ bhavati tena yadvat yathā vā, mādhya.... tyāsata:- mādhyamika bauddhavarṇitasaṃvinnirasanapratikṣepāt tvanmataṃ tiṣṭhatyeva bhavadīyaṃ jagadbhamādhiṣṭhāna- saṃvitpāramarthyamataṃ nirbādhamavatiṣṭhate, yathā ca yathaiva, loke - laukike sasyādau, bādhakahatau- tannāśaka vighāte kriyamāṇe, svarūpasthitiḥ– sasyādeḥ svarūpeṇāvasthānaṃ sidhyati ; tadvat ityavehi || 58 ||

adhītvaṃ vedyatve prasajati dhiyaḥ kumbhavaditi
svasiddhiḥ sā dhītvāditi yadiha ko'pyāha tadasat |
svasiddhiḥ svādhāraṃ prati hi | na paraṃ pratyapi naraṃ

parajñeyā seyaṃ tadapi na matitvaṃ vighaṭate || 59 ||

dhiyaḥ vedyatve – saṃvidaḥ (jñānasya ) jñānāntara prakāśya svarūpatve, kumbhavat - ghaṭavat, adhītvam - saṃviditaratvam (jaḍatvam ) prasajati - āpadyate iti — evaṃrītyā, (tathā) sā- saṃvit, dhītvāt — saṃviśvāddhetoḥ, svasiddhiḥ – (anyānapekṣa) svaprakāśā, iti – evaṃ rītyā ca, iha — brahmaṇi, (saṃvinmātraṃ pakṣīkṛtya ) ko'pi - advaitisāmayikaḥ kaścit, āha - ( saṃvidrūpasya ) brahmaṇaḥ avedyatvasvaprakāśatve uktavān, tadasat - tadetat viṣayasvarūpāvivekakṛtam, hi—yataḥ, svasiddhi:- (dhiyaḥ) svaprakāśatā, svādhāraṃ naraṃ prati — dhiyaḥ āśrayabhūtaṃ cetanaṃ pratyeva, paraṃ naraṃ pratyapi na – svasyā anāśrayaṃ cetanāntaraṃ pratyapi na bhavati ; (ataḥ) seyam - prastuteyaṃ saṃvit (anubhūtiḥ) parajñeyā jñānāntaravedyā ( anubhāvyeva), tadapi tathā'pi, (anubhāvyatve satyapi) matitvam – (saṃviśvam - anubhūtitvam ) dhītvam, na vighaṭate- vighaṭitaṃ na bhavati, anubhūtitvānubhāvyatvayoḥ mitho'virodhāt || 59 ||

tathā hi pratyakṣa prabhṛtimitivargaprakaṭitaḥ
svabhāvo bhāvānāmapasarati vedyāsta iti kim | svabhāvapratyāso yadi bhavati vedyatvavaśataḥ
parasya jñeyatve ghaṭavadapaṭatvaṃ prasajati || 60 ||

tathā hi-uktametattathaiva ; ( na viparyasyati ), te vedyā iti-bhāvā : vedyā iti hetoḥ, (vedyatvamātreṇa) bhāvānām - teṣāṃ teṣāmarthānām, pratyakṣa prakaṭitaḥ pratyakṣādipramāṇajāta- siddhaḥ, svabhāvaḥ apasarati kim – svāsādhāraṇo dharmaḥ tasmādbhāvāt dūrībhavati kim ? (naiva), vedyatvavaśataḥ — jñeyatvamātrataḥ, svabhāvapratyāsaḥ – svāsādhāraṇadharmaviparyāsaḥ, bhavati yadi- bhavatītyetadyadi svābhāvikaṃ syāttarhi, paṭasya jñeyatve – paṭarūpasyārthasya jñānagamyatve prāpte sati, ghaṭavat – jñānagamyasya ghaṭasyeva, apaṭatvam - paṭetaratvam, paṭatvābhāvo vā prasajati - prāpnuyāt, (dharmasyaikasya dharmāntarasāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ na hi ! tadāśritasya atattāmāpādayitumalam ; vyāpya- vyāpakabhāvādivilopaprasaṅgāt ) || 60 ||

prakāśaḥ kumbhādau jaḍimavati tadgocaramiti - stirodhiścaitasyā viratirajaḍe brahmaṇi punaḥ |
svarūpaṃ cinmātraṃ sphuraṇamapidhiśca svanidhanaṃ
jaḍaṃ vā brahma syādapidhividhuraṃ vāpyaparathā | 61 ||

jaḍimavati - jaḍatvavati, kumbhādau— ghaṭādāvarthe, (vidyamānaḥ ) prakāśaḥ – 'prakāśa'- padavācyā avasthā nāma, tagocaramiti - tad (ghaṭādi) viṣayiṇī pramitireva, tirodhiśca - (ghaṭādau) tirodhānapadavācyā avasthā tu etasyāḥ - tadviṣayaka pramiteḥ, viratiḥ– abhāva:, ajaḍe brahmaṇi punaḥ – 'ajaḍa' padavācye brahmaṇi tu, sphuraṇam – prakāśātmikā sthitiḥ, cinmātraṃ svarūpam – nirviśeṣacaitanyasvarūpaṃ brahmaiva na pratītirūpaṃ tat (avedyatvāt tasya ), apidhiśca – brahmaṇaḥ tirodhānarūpā sthitistu, svanidhanam - brahmānatiriktaḥ yaḥ prakāśaḥ tasya vināśa eva (brahmavināśa paryavasitaḥ ), aparathā – ghaṭādāviva svarūpaprakāśa- tattirodhānayorabhyupagame, brahma jaḍa vā syāt paraṃ brahmāpi ghaṭādivat jaḍameva bhavet, apidhividhuraṃ vāpi - asaṃspṛṣṭatirodhānameva vā bhavet ; jaḍatva-svarūpanāśapakṣayoraniṣṭatvāt || 61 ||

purovartidravyasphuraṇamapi rūpyabhramasahaṃ
na śuktitvasphūrtirbhramavihataye yā prabhavati |
yadi brahma prāgvanna khalu jagadadhyāsaviratiḥ
kva sidhyedadhyāso yadi bhavati śuktitvasadṛśam || 62 ||
puro.... sphuraṇam – cakṣuḥ saṃnikṛṣṭaśuktyā didharmiṇāṃ bhānam, rūpya śramasahamapi — svaviṣayabhūtaśuktyā didharmiṇāṃ rajatatvaprakārakaṃ rajatā bhedaprakārakaṃ vā bhramaṃ ruhata eva ; na niruṇaddhi,

na śuktitvasphūrtiḥ– purovartini śuktitvasya pratītiḥ paraṃ na rajatabhramaṃ sahate, (kuta: ?), yā - śuktitvasphūrtiḥ, bhramavihataye prabhavati - rajatabhramānutpādāya samarthā bhavati, (viśeṣadharmadvayasya virodhāt; sāmānya viśeṣātmaka dharmadvayasya avirodhācca ) brahma yadi prāgvat-sa __svaprakāśaikarasaṃ sphuraṇātmakaṃ brahma yadi idaṃ tvarūpasāmānya dharmasphuraṇatulyam, tarhi (tasya bhramasahatvāt ) na khalu jagadadhyāsaviratiḥ - prapañcādhyāsarūpasya saṃsārabandhasya viśrānteraprasaṅga iti mokṣānu- papattireva, yadi bhavati śuktitvasadṛśam – purovartini śuktitvarūpa viśeṣadharmasphuraṇatulyaṃ bhavati cet, sphuraṇābhinnaṃ svaprakāśakarasaṃ brahma, tarhi !; adhyāsaḥ – saṃsārātmakaḥ jagadadhyāsaḥ, kva sidhyet ?–kasmin adhiṣṭhāne utpadyeta ? ('śuktiḥ, rajatam' ityevaṃ bhramaḥ yathā asambhāvitaḥ, tathā viśeṣasphuraṇe, 'brahma jagat' ityevaṃ śramānudayāt ) || 62 ||

idaṃ māyāvāde vividhamuditaṃ dūṣaṇa' miha

svayaṃjyotirbrahma vyapagataviśeṣaṃ yadi katham ? |
mate tasminnasmadṛśi tu saviśeṣaḥ puruṣa i-
tyatadrūpāropastadupara tirityādi ghaṭate || 63 ||

idamuditam - etāvadbhiḥ katipayaiḥ ślokairvarṇitam vividhaṃ dūṣaṇam - nānāprakārāśritaṃ doṣapradarśanam, māyāvāde – advaitasamayaṃ viṣayīkṛtya pravṛttamabhavat iha – asminneva darśane (anyadapi dūṣaṇajātaṃ samasti ) svayaṃ jyotiḥ brahma - svaprakāśakarasaṃ paraṃ brahma, yadi vyapagata - viśeṣam - nirviśeṣacinmātrātmakaṃ yadi syāt tarhi ! asmadūhaśi tu - ahantvena sphuraṇarūpe brahmaṇyeva, saviśeṣaḥ puruṣa iti – viśeṣadharmāvagāhī 'puruṣa' ityullekhayogya: ( 'puruṣatva' rūpa- viśeṣaviṣayakaḥ) atadūpāropaḥ – brahmaṇyavidyamānadharmāropaḥ, (saṃsāraḥ) taduparatiḥ – tādṛśāropa- nivṛtti:, (mokṣaḥ) ityādi - evamādikaṃ bandhamokṣopapādakaṃ sarva vyavahārajātam, tasmin mate - māyāvādapakṣe, kathaṃ ghaṭate kathaṃ saṅgataṃ bhavet ? (mitho vyāhatimayatvāt sarva- vyavahārasya) || 63 ||

adhiṣṭhāne puṃsi svayamahamiti sphūrjati vapuḥ
prakārādhyāropaḥ paśurahamahaṃ mānuṣa iti |
aṇuvāda dehavyatikaravidūre ca ' vihite '
tirodhi ḥ saṅkoco nanu bhavati tadgocaradhiyaḥ || 64 || 2 pihite - vā

ahamiti—ahaṃtvena rūpeṇa, svayaṃ sphūrjati – prakāśakāntaranairapekṣyeṇa svata eva bhāsa- māne, puṃsi adhiṣṭhāne cetanākhye āśraye, ahaṃ paśuḥ ahaṃ mānuṣa iti – 'paśuraham, manuṣyo'hamiti, vapuḥ prakārādhyāropaḥ – dehagatapaśutvamanuṣyatvādidharmāṇāṃ vastuto'hamarthe'vidya- mānānāṃ bhramaḥ (sambhavati), vihite - āgamaikajñāpye, deha.... vidūre ca – sarvathā dehāśraya- svāna ca, aṇutvādī - aṇusva-nityatva- niravayavatvādidharme ātmagate, tirodhiḥ- tirodhānam ( sphurato'hamarthasya niyataprakārabhāvenā'prakāśaḥ ) ityetat, tadgocaradhiyaḥ- tattaddharmaviśiṣṭātma- viṣayiṇyāḥ ātmadharmabhūtāyāḥ buddheḥ, saṅkoco bhavati nanu – saṅkocapadavācyo'vasthā viśeṣa eva bhavitumarhati || 64 ||

ahaṃsva-jñātṛtve nanu puruṣadharmau na tu vapu-
guṇa vastusthityā tadapi na tathātvaṃ nanu tayoḥ | anāderadhyā sādavagatamidānīṃ śruti-naya-

svayogācairanyaḥ sphurati vapurādestu puruṣaḥ || 65 ||

ahaṃtva-jñātṛtve – pratyaktvaṃ jñānadharmitvaṃ ca puruṣadharmau nanu - cetanāyau dharmāveva, vastusthityā vastutaḥ, na tu vapuguṇau naiva dehadharmoṃ, tadapi - tathāpi, tayoḥ ahaṃtva - jñātṛtvayordvayorapi tathātvam – dehaguṇatvarāhityam, puruṣaikāśrayatvaṃ vā anāderabhyāsāt - anādisaṃsaraṇataḥ prāptāt dehātmabhramāt hetubhūtāt, na avagataṃ khalu – naiva jñātamāsīt, idānīṃ sukṛtaparipākena labdhe viśiṣṭe'smin janmani, puruṣaḥ – cetanaḥ, śruti-naya- svayogādyaiḥ –viviktatatvagrāhikābhiḥ śrutibhiḥ, tadanurodhibhiḥ samīcīnaistarkaiḥ, (tathā) jñāna- yogena cātmāvalokanasādhanena vapurāderanyaḥ – dehendriyamanaḥ - prāṇa- ghībhyo viviktaḥ san, sphurati - prakāśate || 65 ||

jīvanmuktimataṃ na jīvati yataḥ śastreṇa śāstrātmanā
lūnaṃ lokaviruddhasiddhi ca yatastenedamādāvapi |
āpastambanirastamaupaniṣadaṃ prasthānamātasthuṣā-
mācāryo'pi nirācakāra khalu tad dvaipāyanākhyo muniḥ || 66 ||

jīvanmuktimatam – ( labdhaṃjñānasya ) 'jīvaddaśāyāmeva mokṣo labhyate' itīdṛśamadvaitināṃ matam, na jīvati naiva svarūpaṃ labhate yataḥ yasmāddhetoḥ, śāstrātmanā śastreṇa dehapāta - mātrāvadhikavilambabodhakaśāstrarūpeṇa śastratulyena sādhanena, lūnam - bhamasvarūpam, yataḥ loka- viruddhasiddhi ca pratyakṣapratibaddhaniścayaṃ ca (tasyāpi tāpatrayāmiha timatvāt ), tena - tadeva

kāraṇatvena pradaśya, ādāvapi - idānīṃ upalabhyamānatayā rītyā darśanānāṃ pravṛtteḥ pūrvameva, idaṃ āpastambanirastam — jīvanmuktimataṃ 'buddhe cet kṣemaprāpaṇam ; ihaiva na duḥkhamupalabheta iti sūtritavatā āpastambākhyena muninā nirākṛtam, aupaniṣadaṃ prasthānamātasthuṣāmācāryaḥ - śrutyantamārgāvalambināṃ vaidikānāṃ pravartakaḥ, dvaipāyanākhyo munirapi - kṛṣṇadvaipāyananāmā ṛṣirapi, tat — jīvanmuktimatam, nirācakāra khalu – ' tadā''pīteḥ saṃsāravyapadeśāt' iti svāsammataṃ spaṣṭīcakāra khalu || 66 ||

ka vedāntāḥ kāmī nihitamatayaḥ śaṅkaramate
vadantaḥ prāmāṇyaṃ bhramavilasitaṃ vedavacasām |
aho yadvat buddhagrathitaphaṇitīnāmatha ca te
kaligrāhamastairagaṇiṣata vedāntina iti || 68 ||

vedāntāḥ kva ? – trayyantagiraḥ kva ? vedavacasām - śrutivacanānām, bhramavilasitam — (bhramādhīnam ) avidyā vijṛmbhaṇameva, prāmāṇyaṃ vadantaḥ – pramāṇapadavācyatvaṃ kathayantaḥ, śaṅkaramate nihitamatayaḥ śāṅkare māyāvāde viśvastabhāvāḥ, amī va 1 – advaitinaḥ kva ?, (tanmate vedāntaprāmāṇyaṃ duḥsthameva) buddha grathitakaṇitīnāṃ yadvat – buddhasaṅkalitānāṃ vācāṃ yādṛśaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ tādṛśameva vedavacasāmapi, aho - viṣādāvahamevaitat atha ca – evaṃ sthitāvapi te vedāntina itiadvaitinaḥ vedāntavādina ityeva, kaligrāhagrastaiḥ kalirūpeṇa tatvatirodhāyakena āvṛta -- matibhiḥ kaiścit, agaṇiṣata — parigaṇanāṃ prāptāḥ vijṛmbhante || 67 ||

-0-

( jīvaparayoḥ) mithobhedasya abhedasya ca bodhakānāṃ vedagirām, śātravaṃ ca — āpātataḥ pratipannaṃ viruddhatvamapi, ghaṭaka nigamataḥ - antaryāmitrāhmaṇa saucālādiśrutivacāṃsyādāya, vyapoḍham - (sūtra- kāreṇa 'avasthiteriti kāśakṛtsna:' ityādinyāyapradarśanataḥ) parihṛtamabhavat, uktākṣepe samādhā- piprā nirūpita pūrvapakṣa - siddhāntasvarūpe ca samadhikaḥ hetuḥ – uktāpekṣayā atiśayitaḥ atirikto vā hetuḥ (yuktiviśeṣa: ), atra nāsti - asminnaṃśādhikaraṇe na pradarśito vartate, ( evaṃ sati adhikaraṇārambhaḥ kutaḥ iti śaṅkane) satyam - yadyapyākṣepo'yaṃ yujyate ; athāpi, pādāṃśā.... kṣiptaye tu jīvaviṣaye śrutau 'pāda' śabdasya, (tathā) gītāyāṃ 'aṃśa' śabdasya ca darśanena bhrāmyatāṃ anyamatīyānāṃ mateṣu doṣajñāpanāyaiva, aṃśacintā – aṃśādhikaraṇīyaḥ aṃśasvarūpasya vicāraḥ kṛtaḥ || 69 ||

aṃśatvaṃ rāmakṛṣṇaprabhṛtiṣu ghaṭatāṃ vigrahāṃśāvatārā-
jjīve brahmāṃśatoktirna tu niravayavaṃ brahma vakturdhaṭeta |
brahmā'taścitsamaṣṭiḥ pratipuruṣamiha tvaṃśatā cetyayuktaṃ

viśvasraṣṭurbahu syāmiti bahubhavanadhyātu rekatvasiddheḥ || 70 ||

aṃśatvam - ekadeśatvarūpaṃ aṃśatvam, rāmatiṣu - rāma kṛṣṇādibhagavadvibhavamūrti- bhedeṣu vigrahāṃśāvatārāt — paramapadastha para vāsudeva divya mūrtyaikadeśāvatārarūpatvāt, ghaṭatām- yujyatāṃ nāma, brahma niravayavaṃ vaktuḥ – brahmaṇo niraṃśatāvādinoṃ vedāntinaḥ, jīve brahmāṃśa- toktiḥ - nitye pṛthaktatvabhūte pratyagātmani brahmaikadeśatvavarṇanā, na tu ghaṭeta naiva sarvathā yujyeta, ataḥ – aṃśatvavacanasya saṅgamanīyatvāt, brahma - jīvanirūpitāṃśitvāśrayabhūtaṃ brahmapadābhi- dheyaṃ vastu, citsamaṣṭiḥ sakalacetanasaṅghātarūpameveti nyāyyam, pratipuruṣam ekaikasminnapi jīvātmani, iha aṃśatā ca samaṣṭibrahmanirūpitaṃ ekadeśatvamapi (senaikadeśe senāṃśatvamiva vanaikadeśe vṛkṣādau vanāṃśatvamiva ca ) ityayuktam — evaṃ pūrvapakṣīkaraṇaṃ na sādhu, bahu syāmiti bahubhavanadhyātuḥ — 'bahu syām' iti svasyaiva bahudhā bhavanaṃ yathā syāttathā saṅkalpayituḥ, viśvasraṣṭuḥ – jagatkāraṇasya brahmaṇaḥ, ekatvasiddheḥ – ekasaṅkhyāśrayatāyā eva śrutito'vagamāt (samaṣṭirūpatvādeḥ śrutyādivirodhāt ) || 70 ||

vyomaika syād ghaṭādyaiḥ pṛthagupacigaṇairbrahma bahvaṃśa meva
tatropādhivyapāye bhavabhṛdayamiyād brahmatāmityayuktam |
svānarthārambhadauḥsthyāt pratiniyataguṇapratyabhijñādyadṛṣṭeḥ

ekaṃ vyoma vastuto nānātvarahitamapi gaganam ghaṭādyaiḥ upadhigaṇaiḥ ghaṭa- maṭhādirūpaiḥ nānātmakairupādhistomaiḥ pṛthak syāt – ghaṭākāśa maṭhākāśādirūpaṃ prāptamiva sat nānāvyavahārāspadaṃ bhavet, evaṃ ākāśe pradarśitayā rītyā, brahma - paraṃ brahmāpi, bahvaṃśam - vastuto niraṃśamapi upādhikṛtāṃśa bahutvavat, tatra – aupaudhikāṃśa bahutvavati brahmaṇi, upādhi- vyapāye—nānājīvatryavahārajanaka dehādyupādhivigame sati, ayaṃ bhavabhṛt — brahmāṃśarūpo'yaṃ saṃsārī jīva:, brahmatāṃ iyāt - nirupādhikaṃ brahmarūpatvaṃ prāpnuyāt iti brahmajīvavādināṃ śaṅkana asādhu, svāna.... dausthyāt – 'svasya brahma, tādṛśaṃ sṛṣṭikarma kathaṃ prārabheta ?' ityasya aparihārāt tattaddehopahite (brahmaṇi) tattadeha | diniyatasukhaduḥkhādyanubhavāsiddheḥ (saubharyādiṣviva ) ( idaṃ mata- mayuktam ) chinnā .... vihateḥ - jīvarūpo'rthaḥ pūrṇasya brahmaṇaḥ khaṇḍitaḥ aṃśo vā ? akhaṇḍito'- pyaṃśo vā ? ityevamādivicārāyattanaikavidhavyāghātaprasaṅgādapi (matamidamanucitam) muktau- jīvasya mokṣakāle, sāmyaśabdācca - jīvasya brahmasamatāpatteḥ niyatabhedaghaṭitāyā eva 'paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti' iti śrutāvukterapi (upahita brahmajīvavādo'yamayuktaḥ || 71 ||

ayuktam — evaṃ aupādhika- saṃsāritāprāptirūpamanarthaṃ jānad pratidyadṛṣṭeḥ – nānāvidha-

māyodanvatyapāre pratiphalati mṛṣāvīciṣu brahmacandraḥ

chāyāṃśāstasya jīvā iti katiciduśantyeta 'dālocanīyam ' |

na brahma draṣṭṛ teṣāmacidapi 'hi' tathā svena kalpyo na jīvaḥ klṛpteḥ : prāk svātmahānestritayasamadhikaḥ kalpakastatra mṛbhyaḥ || 72 || apāre māyodanvati–alabhyatīre māyātmake sāgare, mṛṣāvīciṣu – (pratibimbanārhāsu) vīcivat dṛśyamānāsu mithyābhūtāsu buddhipariṇatiṣu brahmacandraḥ - candrabimba sadṛśaṃ brahma, pratiphalati pratibimbabhāvena bhāsate, tasya – pratiphalato brahmaṇaḥ, chāyaśāḥ praticchāyā- sadṛśāṃśatāvantaḥ, jīvāḥ - jīvātmānaḥ ( saṃsāriṇa:) ; ( na vastutonye, brahma- jīvayoḥ bimba- pratibimbabhāvaḥ ityarthaḥ) iti katicit uśanti evaṃ kecana advaitinaḥ aṅgīkurvate, etat ālocanīyam - idaṃ mataṃ cintyam, na tu abhyupagamāīm; hi-yata:, teṣāṃ draṣṭṛṭṭū - praticchāyārūpāṇāṃ brahmāṃśānāṃ kalpakam, na brahma – bimbabhūtaṃ brahmaiva na bhavitumarham, (nirviśeṣa- śuddhacaitanye tasmin kalpanāyogāt ) acidapi na - acidbhūtamapi kiñcit na draṣṭṛ bhavitumalam, (draṣṭṛtvasya cetanaikadharmatvāt ) tathā — brahmācidbhayāmiva yathā tathaiva, svena – praticchāyābhūtena jīvenāpi, jīvaḥ kalpyo na-svātmā jīvaḥ kalpanā naiva syāt, svātmahāneḥ - kalpanātaḥ

pūrva kalpakasyāsiddhayā kalpanāyā aniṣpatteḥ, tritayasamadhikaḥ kalpakastu-- etantrayātiriktaḥ jīvakalpakaḥ param, tatra mṛgyaḥ tasmin mate anveṣya eva; ataḥ idaṃ matamapi duḥsthameva || 72 ||
sanmātraṃ brahma sarvānugatamiha punarnityasiddha strayoṃ'śāḥ
jīveśā citprabhedāditi ca katicanedaṃ ca nodañcanīyam | sattāmātrānuvṛttestadadhikavapuṣaḥ śāsiturbrahmatokte-
brahmatraividhyavākyaṃ niravayavatayā niścite'nyāśayaṃ syāt || 73 ||

sanmātraṃ brahma - śrutiṣu sadekākāratayā pratipannaṃ brahmatastram sarvānugatam — sarvatattveṣu anuvṛttatayā bhāsamānama, iha punaḥ - asminneva brahmatattve, jīveśācitprabhedāt- jīva īśvarā- cetanākhyaṃ viśeṣaṃ (mithovilakṣaṇaṃ rūpam ) samāśritya trayaḥ aṃśāḥ trayo bhāgāḥ, nityasiddhā: - kasyāpi kalpanāṃ kāraṇatāṃ vā anapekṣyaiva anāditaḥ klṛptāḥ, iti ca katicana — evamapi kecidadvaitinaḥ abhiprayanti, idañca na udañcanīyam etanmatamapi naiva saṃmānyam, sattāmātrānu- vṛtteḥ—astītivyavahāraviṣayatā rūpasya sattārūpasya dharmasyaiva kevalaṃ triṣvapyanugateḥ, (yuktatvāt ), tadadhikavapuṣaḥ- — sattā sadubhayātiśayita svarūpasya śāsituḥ sarvaniyanturātmaviśeṣasyaiva brahma- tokteḥ brahmapada | bhidheyatāyāḥ śrutyanteṣu pratipādanāt niravayavatayā niścite - niraṃśatayā nirṇīte antrayi, brahmatraividhyavākyam – 'trividhaṃ brahmametat' iti vākyam, anyāśayaṃ syāt — viśiṣṭavastvekadeśatvābhiprāyakaṃ bhavet || 73 ||

meroraṃśa: kirīṭaprabhṛtibhiriti nayā nnityabhinne'śatoktiḥ
sājātyālpatvamūlā gamayitumucitetyāgamāsannapakṣe |
aṃśoktiḥ syādamukhyā sa hi | nipuṇadhiyāmekavastvekadeśaḥ
tasmājjīvo viśiṣṭa bhagavati guṇavat tatprakāroṃśa uktaḥ || 74 ||
kirīṭaprabhṛtiḥ - sauvarṇaḥ kirīṭā dirarthaḥ, meroraṃśaḥ- sauvarṇasya meruparvatasyāṃśabhūtaḥ, iti nayāt — ityevaṃvidhavyavahārarītimanurudhya cintane, nityabhine – svābhāvikātyantika- bhedavati vastuni vyavahniyamāṇā, aṃśatoktiḥ– aṃśasvagīḥ, sājātyālpatvamūlā - tatsamāna- jātīyatāṃ tadapekṣayā alpatāṃ ca nimittīkṛtya pravṛtteti, gamayitumuciteti kathaṃcinnetumarhā ityevam, āgamāsannapakṣe - bhagavadguṇacintanataḥ śrutinikaṭavartināṃ mate, aṃśoktiḥ ' jīvoṃ'śaḥ ' iti śrutyādigatā bhāṣā, amukhyā syāt – gauṇī syāt, hi yataḥ, nipuṇadhiyām - kuśalamatīnāṃ mate, saḥ - aṃśapadārthaḥ, ekavastvekadeśaḥ - viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭasyaikasya vastunaḥ

ekadeśarūpa eva, tasmāt — uktāddhetoḥ, viśiṣṭe bhagavati – cetanā cetanākhyā pṛthaksiddhaviśeṣaṇa- te pare brahmaṇi, guṇavat tatprakāra :- dravye guṇa iva niyataprakārabhūto'rtha eva, aṃśa uktaḥ- aṃśabdena vyavahṛtaḥ || 74 ||

uktaṃ nityopalabdhiprabhṛti paramate pūrvameva hyaniṣṭaṃ

bhūyastādṛk prasaṅgaḥ prakathita iha kiṃ bhogasaṅkīrṇa 'tādeḥ ' |
maivaṃ pūrva hi bāhyaprasṛtimaśamayat sāmprataṃ brahmavāda-

I

vyājotsiktān kudṛṣṭīn parihṛtiriti ca syād bhaviṣyannayeṣu ||75|| paramate – ātmano vibhutvavādināṃ mate, nityopalabdhiprabhṛti - ahamiti sarvadā bhānaṃ sarvatra bhānamityādi, aniṣṭam – anabhimatārthaprasañjanam, pūrvamevoktam – jñādhikaraṇe eva varṇitam, , bhūyaḥ – punarapi, iha – aṃśādhikaraṇe, tādṛk- paramatanirāsaparaḥ, bhogasaṅkīrṇatādeḥ prasaṅgaḥ – bhogasāṅkaryāpādanarūpa: (dūṣaṇavyāpāraḥ ) kiṃ prakathitaḥ kimarthaṃ spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ, (pauna- ruktyāt) iti śaṅkane, maitram – evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ nocitam hi yataḥ, pūrvam – jñādhikaraṇe, bāhyaprasṛtim - śrutyananurodhināṃ sāṅkhyādīnāṃ prasāram, aśamayat - kṣapayāmāsa, sāmpratam - idānīṃ tu, brahmavādavyājotsiktān vyājamātrataḥ aupaniṣadaṃ brahmavādamavalambya 'vedāntino vayam' iti prāptadarpān kudṛṣṭīn śrutiviruddhābhiprāyavataḥ advaitamatīyān aśamayat (ato na paunaruktayam ), iti parihṛtiśca ityevaṃ parihāravidhānamapi bhaviṣyannayeṣu ca syāt- āgāmiṣvapyadhikaraṇeṣu anvayārhaṃ bhavet || 75 ||

mithyājñānādicake maruti bhagaṇavad ghūrṇamānasya jantoḥ
"pratyaktatvapra' bādhād bhavapariharaṇe sarvatantrāvigīte |
śuddhātmajñānagarbhā parabhajana bhidāmaṅgabhedāṃśca vakṣyan
mīmāṃsārambhasidhyadvapuṣamapi punaḥ śodhayāmāsa jīvam || 76 ||
mithyājñānādicakre - cakravat sāntatyena parivartamāne atasmiṃstadbuddhi - tadadhīnakarma- tadrdhīnavāsanā-tajjanyanirvitrekaruci viśeṣa tannibandhanā va jainīya prakṛti sambandhaparamparātma ke kārya- kāraṇastome, maruti bhagaṇavat — vāyucakre grahanakṣatrādijyotirmaṇḍalamiva, ghūrṇamānasya – avaśa tayā bambhramyamāṇasya jantoḥ - saṃsarato jīvajātasya, pratyaktatvāvabodhāt – svasvarūpayāthātmya- vijñānarūpāt hetoḥ, bhavapariharaṇe saṃsaraṇacakraprahāṇe, sarvatantrāvigīte - sakaladarśanānāṃ vipratipatteranāspade sthite satyapi śuddhātmajñānagarbhām — acidviyuktātmacintanaghaṭitām, Panja

parabhajanamidām ---vaividhyavatīṃ paramātmopāsanām, aṅgabhedāṃśca - tadupayogyaṅgakalāpaviśeṣāṃśca, vakṣyan - tṛtīye adhyāye nyāyavyutpādanapūrvakaṃ vicārayiṣyan, bādarāyaṇaḥ, mīmāṃsā ... vaṣamapi jīvam — karmamīmāṃsārambhe eva yajñādiphalabhoktṛtvāya dehādyatirekeṇa niścita- svarūpamapi jīvātmānam, punaḥ śodhayāmāsa – asmin śārīrake'pi apavargārhatā niścaya sampattaye bhūyo vicāritavān || 76 ||

kartutāyāḥ pararāpekṣā — jīvagata kartṛtvasya paramātmādhīnatā, 7) paraṃ prati jīvasya aṃśatā'pi ca—paramātmanirūpitaṃ jīvasya aṃśatvamapi, ittham — anayā rītyā, amī sarve arthāḥ uktāḥ sarve sapta arthāḥ, anena pādena - dvitīya tṛtīyena caraṇena, cintitāḥ- nyāyato nirṇītāḥ || 32-33 ||
prathamaśva dvitīyaśca tṛtīyāścā'tra saṅgatāḥ |
tṛtīyaśeṣāścatvāraḥ prasaṅgā ' viha" cintitāḥ || 34 ||
ādimāstrayo nyāyāḥ asmin sākṣātsaṅgatimantaḥ, avaśiṣṭāścatvāro nyāyāḥ tṛtīyasya nyāyasya upakārakāḥ santaḥ prasaktānuprasaktyā nirūpitāḥ sākṣātsaṅgatirahitāḥ || 34 ||

6)

-0-

adhikaraṇamārāvaliḥ

klṛptivyamā dike'pi kramabhuvi ca vibhoḥ prācya tattvairviśiṣṭā- jjīvasyopādhikau tu prajananavilayau ciddhanajñātṛtā'sya |
kartṛtvaṃ pāratantryaṃ guṇatanunayato viśvarūpāṃśatā ce-
tyāmnāyānyonyabādhavyapanayana viyatpā dasādhyāni sapta || 77 ||

1) vyomādike'pi - ākāśādāvapi vibhoḥ klaptiḥ paramātmana evotpattiḥ, 2) kramabhuvi ca - pāramparikasṛjyatayā śrayamāṇe'pi vāyvanyādau tattvajāte, prācyatastva- viśiṣṭāt vibhoḥ klaptiḥ – pūrvaśrunā kāśa | ditastvaśarīrakāt paramātmanaḥ uttaratattvānāmutpattiḥ,, 3) jīvasya - pratyagātmanaḥ, aupādhikau prajananavilayau dehāgama vigamarūpopādhyāyattau utpatti vināśavyavahārau, 4) ciddhanajñātṛtā asya – jñānakā kāratvasahakṛtaṃ jñānāśrayatvamasya- jīvasya, 5) kartṛtvam — jīvātmanaḥ prāptakriyāyāṃ svātantryam, 6) pāratantryam — jīvātmanaḥ paramātmanighnaṃ kartṛtvam, 7) guṇa - tanunayataḥ - dravyaṃ prati guṇasyeva, ātmānaṃ prati dehasyeva ca, viśvarūpāṃśatā ca jīvasya viśvaśarīriṇaṃ paramātmānaṃ pratyaṃśatvamapi iti uktarītyā, āmnāyā.... sādhyāni - śrutiṣu bhāsamānamithovirodhaparihārapareṇa viyatpādena sādhanīyāni viṣayasvarūpāṇi, sapta—– saptasaṅkhyāni vartante || 77 ||

|| T: ||

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ | śriyai namaḥ | śrīdharāya namaḥ |

abrahmakāryaṃ kathayatām – yatkiñcidekamapi vā paramātmano'janyamiti vādinām bādhanena- nirākaraṇena, arthavatvāt - pādārambhasya saprayojanatvasiddheḥ, tat — tasmāt kāryatāyā: samarthanāt prathama- caramayoḥ - ādyā (prāṇotpattyadhikaraṇa) ntimādhikaraṇa (saṃjñāmūrtiklapti) yo:, sākṣātsaṅgatiḥ syāt—avyavahitaiva pādasaṅgatirbhavet, madhyamānām – etadantarvartināmitareṣāmadhikaraṇānāṃ tu, prasaṅgāt saṅgatiḥ syāt prāsaṅgikī saṅgatirbhavet ; na sākṣāt || 1 || aṣṭāvatrādhikārā: prathama iha viyannītiruktendriyāṇāṃ tejo'bannoktanītiṃ draḍhayati caramevyaṣṭibhedasya sṛ |
saṅkhyā-mānādicintāsvapi tadupahitopāsanādyaiḥ phalaṃ syāt
prāṇādibhyaḥ pramātā pṛthagiti viśadīkartumapyeṣa pāda: || 2 ||
atra - dvitīyaturye prāṇapāde, aṣṭau adhikārāḥ– aṣṭasaṅkhyā nyadhikaraṇāni santi, (teṣu madhye) iha – prathame saṃnikṛṣṭe ādye'dhikaraṇe, indriyāṇām - sātvikāhaṅkāropādānakānāṃ karmajñānendriyāṇāmubhayeṣāmapi, viyannītiḥ uktā - viyadadhikaraṇataḥ siddhā nīti: (ākāśasya brahmakāryatā) atidiṣṭā – ' tathā prāṇā:' iti ), carame etatpādāntime (saṃjñā mūrtiklasyadhi- karaṇe) vyaṣṭibhedasya sṛṣṭau – devamanuṣyādivyaṣṭiviśepotpattiviṣaye, tejo'vanoktanītim- tejo'dhikaraṇasiddhāṃ tajo-jala- pṛthivītattvānāṃ brahmakāryatvarītim, draḍhayati - caturmukhādiśarīra ka- brahma kāryatvātideśataḥ sthirīkaroti, saṅkhyā. svapi – saptatva - paṭtva - ekādaśatvā dibhiḥ indriya- gaṇanāyāḥ aṇutvādiprakārataḥ tatpariṇāmasya ca vicāraṇāsu kriyamāṇāsvapi, tadupahitopāsa- nādyaiḥ- tatsaṅkhyā katatpariṇāma kendriyādiviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭatayā bhagavato bhajanāditaḥ, phalaṃ syāt- prayojanaṃ labhyata eva, pramātā – dharmabhūtajñānāśrayo jīvaḥ, prāṇādibhyaḥ pṛthagiti - prāṇendriya- prabhṛtibhyaḥ svopakaraṇabhūtebhyaḥ vibhinna iti, viśadīkartumapi spaṣṭayitumapi eṣa pādaḥ - ayaṃ turyaḥ prāṇapādaḥ upayukto bhavati || 2 ||

agre sattā mṛṣīṇāṃ śrutirabhidadhatī prāṇatāṃ vakti teṣāṃ
nāstra brahmaiva vācyaṃ bahuvacana testena nityākṣasiddhiḥ |
naivaṃ tatsṛṣṭidārthyāt bahuvacanamidaṃ pāśanītyaiva neyaṃ
prāṇarṣitve parātmanyapi hi ! sughaṭite tanniruktyādisāmyāt || 3 ||
śrutiḥ - 'asadvā idamagra āsīt... prāṇā vāva ṛṣayaḥ' itīyaṃ śatapathaśrutiḥ, ṛṣīṇām- ṛṣipadabodhyānāmarthānām, prāṇatāṃ vakti-prāṇasvarūpatāmeva kathayati, atra brahmaiva vācyaṃ na. ‘ṛṣi’-‘prāṇa’śabdayoḥ parabrahmaiva vivakṣitamiti vaktuṃ na śakyam, bahuvacanahateḥ - bahutvā nanvayena - bahutvānanvayena

( brahmaṇa ekatvāt ) bahuvacana vyāghātāpatteḥ tena uktenānena hetunā, nityākṣasiddhi:- indriyeṣu bahutvānvayāt nityendriyasiddhirbhavati ( iti śaṅkane) maivam — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ māstu, tatsṛṣṭidāyat-indriyāṇāṃ brahmasṛjyatvasya asandigdhatvāt (etasmājjāyate prāṇo manassarvendra - -yāṇi ca' iti śruteḥ), idaṃ bahuvacanam - 'prāṇāḥ ' 'ṛṣaya:' iti bahuvacanam, pāśanītyaiva- pāśādhikaraṇa nyāyenaiva, neyam – upacārato nirvāhyam, hi yataḥ, parātmanyapi sṛṣṭeḥ prāgavipratipannasattā ke parabrahmaṇyapi, prāṇarṣitve - prāṇatvaṃ ṛṣitvaṃ cetyubhe, tanniruktyādisāmyāt – 'prāṇayati sarvān' iti prāṇaḥ, iti, 'ṛṣa jñāne' iti ca prāṇanasādhanatva. sarvārthadraṣṭṛtvayoḥ avayavārthayoḥ tathā tacchandasamudāyaśakteśca samānatvāt, - sughaṭite - susaṅgate eveti || 3 ||

-0-

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

evaṃviyadādīnāmutpattirnirūpitā, anantaraṃ jīvopakaraṇānāmutpattirnirūpyate – caturthena

prāṇapādena |

tatra prāṇotpantyadhikaraṇe 'asadvā idamagra āsīt' ityādivākye, 'prāṇa' śabda nirdiṣṭā- nāmindriyāṇām pralayakāle'pyavasthānapratīteḥ indriyāṇi na utpadyante – ityāśaṅkaya,

'etasmājjāyate' ityādivākye, indriyāṇāmutpattiśravaṇāt 'asadvā....' ityādiva| kye prāṇaśabdasya paramātmaparatvācca, indriyāṇi utpadyante ityuktam ||

yadeti vākyāt – 'jñānāni' iti viśeṣanirdeśayutāt 'yadā pañca.... paramāṃ gatim ' iti kaṭhavallīgatāt vākyāt sapta prāṇā iti śruteścāpi – 'sapta prāṇāḥ prabhavanti .... sapta sapta' iti muṇḍakaśrutivākyādapi jñāpakāt sapta tu — jīvopakaraṇabhūtāni indriyāṇi saptaiva, naivam — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ nocitam pradhānabhāvāt saptānāṃ pradhānato jīvopakaraṇatvataḥ saptasaṅkhyā- nirdeśāt, daśema iti vākyataḥ smaraṇāt – 'daśeme puruṣā prāṇāḥ ātmaikādaśa:' iti vākyataḥ ekādaśasaṅkhyatvasya jñāpanāt, ( ekādaśasaṅkhyatāyā abādhāt ) || 36 ||

katicit – dārśanikaikadeśinaḥ, dehavyāpi – samagraṃ śarīramabhivyāpya vartamānam, bhāgataḥ bhinnakṛtyam—–śrotrādisthānākhyopādhitaḥ svīyaikadeśena vacana śravaṇādikāryabhedavat, ekam akṣam - svarūpato'bhinnamevendriyam akathayan - pratipādayāmāsuḥ, śrutipathavimukhāḥ kecit – vedamātrānavalambinaḥ vaiśeṣikādayaḥ, kṣudratarkaiḥ – alpameva tarka niścayasādhanatayā avalambya, karmendriyāṇi vāgādīni pañca, tatyajuḥ – nirākṛtavantaḥ, eke—– paurāṇikāḥ, kṣetrajñasya kāraṇagaṇam - jīvopakaraṇabhūtaṃ indriyastomam, buddhayahaṅkāracittaiḥ saha buddhi, ahaṅkāraṃ, cittaṃ ca saṃmelya, āhuḥ - (āhatya caturdaśasaṅkhyānīndriyāṇi iti) varṇayanti, anye—kecatra, (sāṅkhyāḥ) tam - indriyastomam, cittavarjam — cittaṃ varjayitvā, (manobuddhya- haṅkārākhyāntaḥkaraṇena saha) nijagaduriti — kathayāmāsuriti hetoḥ, tānū - evamevaṃ śrutiviruddha- prameyavādinaḥ sarvān, arthataḥ tatrarthabodhanataḥ, atra vyudāsa - saptatyadhikaraṇe avaidikatva - jñāpanena nirākāra || 5 ||

tāni - indriyāṇi prāṇākhyāni vibhūni tu yuktam - sarvagatānītyeva sādhu, sarve'nantāḥ samā iti śravaṇāt – 'ta ete sarva eva samāḥ sarve'nantāḥ' iti ānantyāparaparyāyasya vibhutvasyaiva śrutyuktatvāt naivam - evaṃ śaṅkanamayuktam utkramaṇaśrutita eva – 'tamutkrāmantaṃ sarve prāṇā anutkrāmanti' iti jīvotkramaṇa pratipādakaśrutyaiva, utkramaṇe ca – indriyāṇāṃ jīvena saha gamane tena paricchinnatvagarbhe aṇutve ca (aṇutvam paricchinnatve sati atisvaccha (sūkṣma) - svam ) siddhe sati, upāstyarthatvāt – ānantyopadeśasya kāryabāhulya viśiṣṭaveṣeṇa ) prāṇopāsana- kartavyatājñāpanaphalakatvāt || 37 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ (prāṇa adhi )

prāṇātvādhikaraṇe 'sarva eva samāḥ sarve'nantāḥ' ityādinā ānantyaśravaṇāt indriyāṇi vibhūni ityāśaṅkaya,

'prāṇamutkrāmantaṃ sarve prāṇā anūskāmanti' iti utkrāntiścavaṇāt utkrāntikāle pārśvasthaiḥ anupalabhyamānatvāt, ānantyaśruteśca upāsanārthakāryānantyaparatvācca aṇūni- ityuktam ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ (vāyu- adhi)
prāṇaḥ prāguktanītyā parajanita iti sthāpito vāyumātraṃ
dehāntastatkriyā vā sa iti na pṛthagutpattivādāt sahā'sya | dravyatvaṃ dravyavarge paṭhanata ucitaṃ naiṣa tatvāntaraṃ syāt
tejastve vāyavavasthātyajanavadiha tatyāgahāneḥ kadāpi || 7 ||

prāṇaḥ - śreṣṭhaḥ prāṇaḥ, prāguktanītyā pūrvoktaviyadadhikaraṇanyāyena, parajanita iti- parasmād brahmaṇa evotpanna iti, sthāpitaḥ prāṇādhikaraṇe, dṛḍhīkṛtaḥ, saḥ parabrahmakāryabhūtaḥ prāṇaḥ, dehāntarvāyumātram - śarīrodarasañcaraṇasvabhāvaḥ kevalo vāyuḥ ? dehāntastatkriyā vā- śarīra garbhe satata pravartanasvabhāvā ucchavāsaniśvāsarūpā vāyavīyā kriyā vā :, iti na- śaṅkitānyatarakoṭinirṇayo na samīcīnaḥ asya – vipratipattigocarasya prāṇasya, saha - etadabhinnatva- sambhāvanāviṣayeṇa vāyunā sārdham pṛthak viśiṣya nāmollekhataḥ, utpattivādāt – 'etasmājjāyate prāṇaḥ....khaṃ vāyuḥ' iti śrutita eva utpatteḥ kathanāt dravyatvam - kriyā rūpatvaṃ vihāya kriyāśrayadravya svarūpatvam, dravyavarge paṭhanataḥ – 'khaṃ vāyujrjyotirāpaḥ pṛthivī' iti dravyastomāntaḥ- pātitayā śrutau āmnā tatvataḥ, ucitam – avaśyābhyupagamārham eṣaḥ - prastuto'yaṃ śreṣṭhaḥ āmnātatvataḥ, prāṇaḥ, taccāntaraṃ na syāt tattvavibhājakopādheḥ na sākṣādāśrayo bhavitumarhati (pṛthaktatvatayā parigaṇanāṃ nārhati ), tejastve vāyoḥ tejastvāvasthāprāptau satyām vāyvavasthātyajanavat- prāsthitāyāḥ vāyutvāvasthāyāḥ prahāṇavat, iha prāṇe, kadāpi yadā kadācidapi, iha-prāṇe, tatyāgahāne :- vāyuvāvasthā prahāṇasya adarśanāt, prāṇaḥ na tattvāntaramapi ; kintu avasthā viśeṣa prāpto vāyureva || 7 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā (vāyu-adhi)
mukhyaprāṇasvarūpe'pi vivādānāṃ bahutvataḥ |
vāyu kriyādhikaraṇe prasaṅgāt tattvanirṇayaḥ || 106 ||

.

iti vāyu kriyādhikaraṇam || 4 ||

bhavatu, ityayuktam - evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ asādhu, jīvākṣanyāyataḥ - jīvātmanāṃ tadīyendriyāṇāmiva ca, tatsaha .... bādhāt -- jīva tadindriyasāhacaryeṇa śrutyuktasya prāṇotkramaṇa-tadadhīna gatāgatādeḥ virodha- prasaṅgāt, karaṇa.... hetau – indriyāṇāṃ dehasya ca dhāraṇādisādhanabhūte, iha-prāṇe, sarvasāmya- śrutiḥ - 'samo'nena sarveṇa lokena' iti sarvaloka samānatvaśrutiḥ, stutyarthā — praśaṃsāvabodhaphalā, na tu mahāparimāṇatvapratipattiphalā, dehe - śarīre cā'smin vṛttibhedaiḥ tadīyaiḥ vyāpṛti- viśeṣaḥ, vibhaktaḥ suvyavasthitaḥ, daśadhā - prāṇādipañcakātmanā nāgādipañcakātmanā ca daśarūpaḥ, analpopakāraḥ – avarṇanīyo mahān prayojanalābha: ( sarvapuruṣārthaupayikaḥ ) sphurati— lokavedgatā vijṛmbhate || 8 ||

iha — asmin śerīre, jvalanādikānām – agnyādirūpāṇāṃ abhimānidevatānām, jīvena sārdham - jīvātmanā saha vāgādyadhiṣṭhānam vākprabhṛtikarma jñānendriyapravartakatvarūpaṃ adhiṣṭhānam, svataḥ syāt-svāyattameva bhavet, na tu paramātmāyattam, maivam - evaṃ kevala svāyattatva- varṇanaṃ na sādhu, amīṣām – agnyādidevānāṃ jīvasya ca etat uktamadhiṣṭhānam, parātmāmana- nāttu — paramapuruṣasaṅkalpa deva bhavati na svataḥ, yo'gnau iti ca śrutatvāt – 'yo'mau tiṣṭhan ' yo'gnimantaro yamayatī 'ti teṣvavasthānasya teṣāṃ niyantṛtvasya ca śrutyā'vagateḥ || 40 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ ( jyoti - adhi )
jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānādhikaraṇe - jīvasya agnyādidevatānāṃ ca prāṇaviṣayaṃ adhiṣṭhānaṃ svādhīnam ;

nairapekṣyapratīteḥ—ityāśaṅkya,

'yasyābhiḥ śarīram' ityādau agnyādīnāṃ paramātmādhīna svarūpatvapratipādanāt teṣāmadhiṣṭhānaṃ paramātmādhīnam - ityuktam ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ (indriyādhi)
prāṇo'pi syāduddhṛṣīkaṃ bhṛśamupakaraṇāt teṣu mukhyatvavādā- dukāntyādau ca sāhyāditi yadi na pṛthakchandanaistasya bhedāt | kaṇṭhoktā dindriyatvānmanasi tu ghaṭate go-balīvardanīti-
rna prāṇe sātvikāhaṅkaraṇavikṛtitā śabdasāmyādi mandam || 10 ||

prāṇopi — mukhyaḥ prāṇo'pi hṛṣīkaṃ syāt – ( jīvopakaraṇatvādisāmyataḥ ) indriyameva bhavitumarham, bhṛśamupakaraṇāt teṣu mukhyatvavādāt - atiśayena jīvasya upakāritvataḥ indriyeṣu madhye tasya mukhyatvena nirdhāraṇasya tatkathanasya ca pravṛttatvāt, utkrāntyādau ca sāhyāt- dehādutkramaṇe gatāgatādāvapi sāhāyyakaraṇādapi iti yadi na evaṃ cet śaṅkanaṃ kriyate na tatsādhu, pṛthakśabdanaiḥ—śrutigataiḥ prāṇa-mana indriyākhyairviviktairyapadeśaiḥ, tasya bhedāt- prāṇasya indriyebhyaḥ pārthakyāt, manasi — manoviṣaye tu kaṇṭhoktāt – 'manaḥ ṣaṣṭhānīndriyāṇi ' iti spaṣṭoktisiddhāt, indriyatvāt - manasaḥ indriya koṭipraveśāt go balīvardanītiḥ - vaiyarthya parihārāya śabdopāttavyatiriktaviṣaye sāmānyaśabdasya saṅkocalakṣaṇaḥ sāmānya viśeṣaśabdayoḥ sahaprayuktayoḥ apaunaruktayāba ho nyāyaḥ, (manaḥ sarvendriyāṇi ca iti ) ghaṭate - yujyate, (kiṃ ca) prāṇeśreṣṭhaprāṇe, sātvikāhaṅkaraṇa vikṛtitā - indriyalakṣmabhūtaṃ sātvikāhaṅkāropādānaka dravyatva- mapi, na ghaṭate ---naiva yujyate, śabdasāmyādi - indriye mukhyaprāṇe ca prāṇaśabdekyādirūpaṃ nidānaṃ tu, mandam – (nānārthasthale vyabhicaritatvataḥ tasya abhimatā sādhakatvāt ) nirarthakaṃ tat || 10 ||

-0-

nayasaṅgatimālikā (indriyādhi)
prāṇendriyāvibhāgena vicāro viphalastviti | ākṣepaparihārārthaṃ syādindriyanayodayaḥ || 109 || iti indriyādhikaraṇam || 7 ||

svīyāsu smṛtiṣu jñāpyate jīvenā'nu- praviśyetyapi kathitam -- ' jīvenā''tmanā'nupraviśye' tyapi ca śrutau kathitaṃ vartate, ataḥ - śruti smṛtibhyāṃ jīvakartṛkatvokte:, atra vyaṣṭisṛṣṭiviṣaye, preṣyakṛtyakramaḥ - svāmipreritena bhṛtyena kāryanirvāhakrama eva vivakṣitaḥ, iti śaṅkāyām, tanna – tathāvidhaṃ śaṅkanaṃ asādhu, hi-yataḥ, eka: – eka eva puruṣaḥ, praveṣṭā- anupraveśakartā, trivṛtamakṛta - pṛthivyatejāṃsi trivṛtkṛtavān, vyākarot ca - nāmarūpe viśeṣataḥ sṛjyeṣu yathāyogyaṃ yojitavāṃśca ityadhītaḥ - iti śrutā - vāmnātaḥ, (tasmāt ) bhagavān - ādikartā paramātmaiva tattajjīvāntarātmā brahmādīnāṃ teṣāṃ teṣāṃ jīvānāmantaḥ praveśato niyāmakaḥ san tādṛśaṃ kāryajātam – tattajjīvanirvyūḍhatayā śrutismṛtyādipratipannaṃ kārya stomam, sṛjati –nirmāti, ( na tu preṣyeṇeva anyena sṛṣṭiḥ ) ataḥ sarvaṃ paramātmakartṛkameveti || 11 ||

_

yā jīvenā''tmaneti śrutiriyamapi na brahmajīvaikyamā ha

prāgevaiko'ntarātmā vapuritara iti sthāyibhedābhidhānāt | teneśastadviśiṣṭa(:)svakaraṇakatayā'nupraveśe'pi kartā
jīve tatkartṛtāyāmiha na hi ghaṭate 'ktatvā' śrutiḥ kartṛbhedāt || 12 ||

jīvenātmaneti yā śrutiḥ - 'aneva jīvenā''tmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi " iti yā śrutirasti, iyamapi - iyaṃ kaṇṭhoktā śrutirapi brahmajīvaikyaṃ nā''ha — para - pratyagātmanoḥ abhedaṃ naiva vakti, prāgeva pūrvameva ekaḥ paramātmā eka, antarātmā –nirupādhika- sarvāntaryāmitvavān, itaraḥ — tadanyaḥ sarvo'pi cetanācetanastomaḥ, vapuriti tattaccharīrasvabhāva iti, sthāyibhedābhidhānāt - nityasiddhasya śāśvatikasya pṛthaktvasya sapramāṇaṃ śārīrake pratipādanāt tena – uktena hetunā, īśa:- paramātmā, tadviśiṣṭasvakaraṇakatayā - jīva viśiṣṭa - svātmakaraṇakatvena (sampadyamāne ) anupraveśe - anupraveśa karmaṇi, kartā'pi kartṛkāraka bhūto'pi bhavati, (ākārabhedena) kartṛtva karaṇatvayoḥ samāveśe'virodhāt, ('anupraveśe'pi kartā' ityeva vā anvayaḥ) jīve tatkartutāyām – jīvātmani nāmarūpavyā kartṛtvā'bhyupagame, iha tvāśrutiḥ. etatprakaraṇagatā 'tvā' pratyayarūpā śrutiḥ, na hi ghaṭatenaiva saṅgatā bhavati khala, kartṛbhedāt – (samānakartṛkayoreva ttatvāpratyayasya anuśiṣṭatvāt ) anupraveśakartuḥ nāmarūpavyā kartuśca mithobhedāt || 12 |

abhnyādāvaṇḍamadhyamthitimati kathitaṃ rūpabhedaistrivṛttvaṃ tejo'vannā śitoktāvapi viśadamidaṃ tena dedhāstrivṛtkṛt | maivaṃ brahmāṇḍasṛṣṭirbhavatu kathamasāvatrivṛtkārapūrvā
bhukte'nnādaiḥ tridhoktā pariṇatiritarat saṃnikṛṣṭe pradṛṣṭiḥ || 13 ||
aṇḍamadhya sthitimativyaṣṭisṛṣṭigocara brahmāṇḍāntaḥ pātini, pṛthivyākhye bhūtatraye, rūpabhedaiḥ – rohita śukla kṛṣṇākhyairūpaviśeṣaiḥ,

agnyādau – tejo-jala-- trivṛtvam — trivṛtkaraṇā-

kathitam —'yadane rohita' mityādiśrutau varṇitam, tejo'vannāśitoktāvapi— 'tejo'śitam', 'āpaḥ pītāḥ', 'annamaśitam' iti śrutivacasyapi idaṃ viśadam - trivṛtkaraṇaṃ sphuṭamasti, tena – uktahetunā, vedhāḥ caturmukha eva trivṛtkṛt - trivṛtkaraṇanirvoḍhā, (iti śaṅkane) maitram — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ asādhu, asau brahmāṇḍasṛṣṭiḥ brahmāṇḍatadantargatānāṃ sṛṣṭiriyama, atrivṛtkārapūrvā kathaṃ bhavatu svasṛṣṭeḥ prāk trivṛtkaraṇaṃ vinā sambhāvayitumapi nāha, bhukte annādau bhukta. pītādyātmakamanna jalādikaṃ prastutya tridhoktā – ' tredhā vidhīyate' iti śrutyuktā tu, pariṇatiḥ bhojana - pānādyanantarakālikā pariṇāmarūga avasthaiva na tu trivṛtkaraṇam, itarat - agnyādau rohinādirūpabhedavarṇanādikamanyat, saṃnikṛṣṭe bhūtāntareṇa sambaddhe ekaikasmin bhūte, pradṛṣṭiḥ prakarṣeṇa vaiśadyena vivicya sūkṣmāṃśapradarśanamātram | ( ataḥ trivṛtkaraṇaṃ paramātma- kartṛkameva ; na jīvakartṛkamiti || 13 ||

-0-

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ (saṃjñā- adhi)

saṃjñāmūrtikḷtyadhikaraṇe— 'anena jīvenātmanā'nupraviśya' iti nāmarūpavyākaraṇasya jīvakartṛkatvaśravaṇāt, vyaṣṭisṛṣṭiḥ hiraṇyagarbhakartṛkā – ityāśaṅkaya,

'nāma-rūpaṃ vyākaravāṇi' 'tāsāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtamekaikāṃ karavāṇi' iti trivṛtkurvata eva nāmarūpavyākaraṇopadeśāt trivṛtkaraṇasya hiraṇyagarbhakartṛkatvānupapatteḥ, 'anena jīvene 'ti 'jīva śabdasya jīvaśarīrakaparamātmaparatvācca hiraṇyagarbhakartṛkaparamātmakartṛkaiva vyaṣṭisṛṣṭiḥ ityuktam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā (saṃjñā- adhi )
samaṣṭisṛṣṭau hetutvaṃ syādeva paramātmanaḥ | saṃjñāmūtritraye vyaṣṭau jīvasya tadapodyate || 110 ||
iti saṃjñā mūrtikaḷtyadhikaraṇam || 8 ||

pādopasaṃhāraḥ

śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ
utpacyā saṅkhyānaṃ parimāṇaṃ caiva kāraṇānām |
prāṇasya ca svarūpaṃ parimāṇa (madhiṣṭhitirnighnā ?) manindriyatvaṃ ca || 43 ||
( etasyānindriyatā) eteṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ sargakaro vyaṣṭi (sṛjyanāmādeḥ ) bhūtānām | ityetadakhilamamunā pādena vicintitaṃ samyak || 44 ||

(ā) karaṇānāṃ utpattyā – indriyāṇāṃ paramātmakāryatā nirṇayena saha (dvitīye) saṅkhyānam — indriyāṇāṃ śrutyādisiddha gaṇanam, (tṛtīye) parimāṇaṃ caiva indriyāṇāṃ sarvagatatvanira sena aṇutvasthāpanam (caturthe) prāṇasya ca svarūpam - prāṇa vāyoḥ vāyusāmānya- tatkriyā rūpatvayornirasanena avasthā viśeṣāpannavāyusvarūpatvanirṇayaḥ, (pañcame ) parimāṇam - mukhyaprāṇasyāpyaṇuparimāṇatvam, (ṣaṣṭhe ) nighnā paramātmāyattatā, adhiṣṭhitaḥ cetanena saha nighnā-paramātmāyattatā, agnyādidevatānāṃ vāgādyadhiṣṭhātṛtvam, etasyānindriyatā - (saptame ) prāṇavāyoḥ indriya koṭya- praveśaḥ, (aṣṭame ) vyaṣṭisṛjyanāmādeḥ vyaṣṭisṛṣṭigocarasya jagato nāma-rūpādeḥ, sargakaraḥ - sraṣṭā paramātmaiveti nirṇayaḥ, ityetadakhilam – uktametat sarvam, amunā pādena - dvitīya- turyeṇa caraṇena, samyak vicintitam - nipuṇaṃ vicārya sthirīkṛtam || 43 - 44 ||

prathame prasaktarūpāḥ pañcā'pi vicintitā dvitīyādyāḥ |
tasmāt saṅgatamasmin pāde prathamaṃ ca caramaṃ ca || 45 ||
prāṇasvarūpa cintā prāṇatayā śabdanena karaṇānām | prāsaṅgikaprasiddhā prasaṅgatastadviśeṣacintā ca || 46||
aṇḍabahirbhūnānāṃ pūrveṇa vicintitā sṛṣṭiḥ |
sṛṣṭistu cintitā syādaṇḍāntarvartinā manenā'pi || 47 ||

prathame - turīyādyādhikaraṇe, prasaktarūpāḥ smṛtā anupekṣaṇīya svarūpāḥ, dvitīyādyāḥ- dvitīyādhikaraṇārtha prabhṛtikāḥ, pañcā'pi indriyasaṅkhyā parimāṇādyāḥ pañcavidhā api, vicintitāḥ – vicārya nirṇītāḥ santi, tasmāt — uktāddhetoḥ, asmin pāde – dvitīyaturye caraṇe, prathamaṃ ca caramaṃ ca ādyaṃ prāṇotpattyadhikaraṇam, antimaṃ saṃjñāmūrtiklatyadhikaraṇaṃ ca saṅgatam - sākṣādeva saṅgatimat || 45 ||

karaṇānām - jīvopakaraṇabhūtānāmindriyāṇām prāṇatayā śabdanena - mukhyaprāṇasyāpi anvayinā prāṇatvena rūpeṇa śrutau kīrtanena, prāṇasvarūpacintā - mukhyaprāṇasya svarūpaviṣayiṇī vicāraṇā, prāsaṅgikaprasiddhā - prasaṅgāyattaprasiddhimatī, tadviśeṣacintā ca - prāṇagatasya viśeṣasya parimāṇādeḥ cintā'pi prasaṅgataḥ - prasaṅgasaṅgatyaiva bhavati || 46 |
pūrveṇa - dvitīya tṛtīyena viyatpādena, aṇḍabahirbhūtānāṃ sṛṣṭiḥ – brahmāṇḍākhyavyaṣṭisṛṣṭeḥ prāgbhāvināmākāśāditattvānāṃ samaṣṭisṛṣṭiḥ, vicintitā - viśeṣataścintitā'bhavat anena

tu — dvitīya- turyeṇa prāṇapādena tu, aṇḍāntarvartināmapi brahmāṇḍāntaḥ pātināmapi, (vyaṣṭi ) sṛṣṭi: – nāmarūpavyākaraṇarūpā nirmitiḥ, cintitā syāt — vicāragocarīkṛtā bhavati || 47 ||

śrīrāmabhadrakalitā ṭippanyeṣā samāpyata dvitīyasya || ]

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ

akṣāṇāṃ janma. saṅkhyā parimitaya iha prāṇavāyoḥ svarūpaṃ tatsaukṣmyaṃ devatādestadubhayaviṣayādhiṣṭhitau pāratantryam | prāṇasyānindriyatvaṃ bahuvidhacidacidvyaṣṭināmādi cā''dyāt pañcīkartuḥ svanāmaprabhavakavacitā dūcire prāṇapāde || 14 ||

iha prāṇapāde – dvitīya - caturthe prāṇākhye caraṇe, akṣārṇā janma saṅkhyā parimitayaḥ- indriyāṇāmutpattiḥ, śāstrābhimatā gaṇanā tādṛśaṃ parimāṇam, prāṇavāyoḥ svarūpam — mukhya. prāṇapadavācyasya nyāyyaṃ svarūpam, tatsaukṣmyam — mukhyaprāṇasya aṇurūpatvam, devatādeḥ- agnyādidevatānāṃ jīvātmanāṃ ca tadubhayaviṣayādhiṣṭhitau – mukhya prāṇa indriyātmakajīvopakaraṇa- viṣayake sva-svakāryapravartane paramātmasaṅkalpādhīnatvam, prāṇasya anindriyatvam - mukhyaprāṇasyaḥ indriyavargāpraveśaḥ, ādyāt pañcīkartuḥ – samaṣṭisṛṣṭirūpapañcīkaraṇa nirvoḍhuḥ parasmād brahmaṇa eva, svanābhiprabhavakavacitāt— svanābhyutpannahiraṇyagarbhaśarīrakāt sataḥ, bahuvidhacidacidvayaṣṭi- nāmādi ca nānāvidhacetanācetanavyaṣṭiviṣayaka nāmarūpavyākaraṇamapi cetyete'rthāḥ, ūcire—

nityatvaṃ vyomti vātādyaṇuṣu ca puruṣe'pyajñatādīnapārthā-
chotrādau bhūtatādyaṃ manasi ca vibhutāṃ nityatattvāntaratvam | prāṇeṣvātmādibhāvaṃ svapada niyamanasvairitāṃ svagivṛnde
vedhasyunmuktayantrakramamapi vadatāṃ pādayugme'tra bhaṅgaḥ || 15 ||

vyomni ākāśe, vātādyaṇuṣu ca vāyu-tejo- pṛthivīparamāṇuṣvapi nityatvaṃ vadatām — utpattyā dirā hityavādināṃ (vaiśeṣikāṇām ), puruṣe'pi - jīvātmanyapi, ajñatādīn - apārthān – jātṛtvābhāvaṃ kartṛtva- bhoktṛtvayorabhāvaṃ ca aśrautānarthān nirūpayatām, śrotrādau- āhaṅkārike śravaṇādāvindriyagaṇe, bhūtatādyam – ākāśādibhūtasvarūpatvādikam, manasi— manorūpe indriye aṇubhūte, vibhutām - sarvagatatvam, nitya tattvāntaratvaṃ ca – ajanyāvinā śi- pṛthaktatvarūpatāṃ ca vadatām ātmādibhāvam - bhoktṛbhūtātmarūpatām, upakaraṇabhūtendriyarūpata ca, prāṇeṣu - śarīradhāraṇaupayike mukhyaprāṇe varṇayatām svargivṛnde – svargasthānaṃ prāpya virājatāṃ devānāṃ gaṇe, svapada niyamana svairitām - labdhasvasthānaniyantraṇaviṣaye nirapekṣa- svātantryam, vedhasisṛṣṭinirvahaṇāya bhagavatā niyukte caturmukhe, unmuktayantrakramam - visṛṣṭe yantre visraṣṭuḥ asvātantryamiva paramātmanaḥ asvātantryaṃ ca vadatāṃ teṣāṃ teṣāṃ vādinām, atra pādayugme asmin pādadvaye, bhaṅgaḥ pratikṣepo bhavati, (vādināṃ pādadvayasya bhaṅgo bhavatīti teṣāṃ gatināśaḥ ityapyarthaḥ syāt || 15 ||

tarkairāpāta satyairavihatikathane vyāhatiḥ sthāpitā syāt

samyagbhirvastuvṛtyā taditi yadi tadā svābhimānoparodhaḥ | tenā'dhyāyo virodhapraśamanakṛdasau bauddhabandhorviruddhaḥ
sautrī tarkāpratiṣṭhā śrutipathavimukhasvairavādeṣu yojyā || 16 ||

āpāta satyaiḥ sthūladṛṣṭeḥ satyavadava bhāsamānaiḥ, tarkaiḥ –tarkaiḥ sādhanatayā''śritaiḥ, avihatikathane - etadadhyāyasādhyasya virodhasya samarthane kṛte vyāhatiḥ — avirodhapratiyogibhūto virodha eva, sthāpitā syāt — sthiratāmāpādito bhavet, samyagbhiḥ samīcīnaireva tarkaiḥ sādhanatayā''śritaireva vastuvṛcyā taditi yadi vāstavikameva avirodhakathanamiti yadyucyate, tadā tarhi, svābhimānoparodhaḥ – svairabhimanyamānasya nirviśeṣacinmātrasatyatvasya pratighātaḥ avarjanīyaḥ, tena—uktenā'nena hetunā, virodhapraśamanakṛt — vedāntārthasya tarka tantrādivirodha- nirāsāya pravṛttaḥ, asau adhyāyaḥ - dvitīyo'yaṃ śārīrakādhyāyaḥ, bauddhabandhoḥ – jaganmithyātva - vādena bauddhasaṃnikṛṣṭasya advaitinaḥ, viruddhaḥ pratikūlaḥ, sautrī- 'tarkāpratiṣṭhānāt iti śārīrakasūtroktā, tarkāpratiṣṭhā tarkāṇāmanavasthittvāyattā viṣayāsādhakatā, śrutipatha- vimukhasvairavādeṣu yojyā - vedamārgavirodhiṣu svacchandavādeṣu yojanīyā, (tadviṣayiṇī bodhyā) na tu śrutyanuguṇata ke viṣayiṇī jñeyā; tasya suvyavasthitatvāt || 16 ||

ātmā dhikaraṇīyaḥ ślokaḥ
lokasaṃkhyā toyena jīvāniti vākyabhāvāt
pratijñayā jāyata eva jīvaḥ |
iti tvasāraṃ kṛtavipraṇāśa-
prasaṅgato nitya iti śruteśca || 27 ||

toyena ....bhāvāt — taittirīya ke 'yataḥ prasūtā jagataḥ prasūtī toyena jīvān vyasasarja bhūmyām' iti sarvajīvasṛṣṭipratipādakavākyasadbhāvāt pratijñayā - ' yasmin nu khalu vijñāte sarvamidaṃ vijñātaṃ bhavatī'ti eka vijñānataḥ sarvavijñāna siddhipratijñayā ca jñāpakabhūtayā, jīvaḥ- pratyagātmabhūtaścatanaḥ jāyata eva utpādyata evetyabhyupagamaḥ sādhurbhavati, (iti śaṅkane), idaṃ tu asāram - ātmotpattisādhakatayā uktayukticintanaṃ paraṃ durbalameva, kṛtaviṅgataḥ - jīvasṛṣṭyabhyupagame, utpadyamāne jīve ( phalaviśeṣamanubhavati, pūrvaṃ phalakāraṇa siddhayā akṛtābhyā- gamasya) tathā janyabhāvasya vināśaghrauvyāt tasmin vinaśyati jīvanakāle kṛtānāṃ karmaṇāṃ phalamadantvaiva nāśena kṛtavipraṇāśarūpadoṣasya ca prasaṅgāt nitya iti śruteśca jīvanityatva- pratipādikāyāḥ 'ajo nityaḥ śāśvataḥ' 'nityo nityānām' ityādikāyāḥ śruteḥ sadbhāvācca, ātmanaḥ anutpattereva yuktipramāṇābhyāṃ nirṇeyatvāditi || 27 ||
sārāvaliradhikṛtīnāṃ vedāntāyairvinirmitā sādhvī | lavyā cārthavivṛtyā suyojitā dvitīyake'dhyāye ||
śrīrāmabhadraviduṣā katunāthāryānvayāptaśubhajanuṣā |
adhyetṝṇāṃ ca kṛte śabdayathāśrutārthadhāraṇārthāya ||

anubandhaḥ – 2

ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyate'

paramātmā hi janmapadābhilapya karmanibandhanaśarī ra sambandha ra hito'pi, icchavigrahaparigraheṇa nānāvidhāvirbhāvāśrayo bhavati || 'ajo nityaḥ śāśvato'yaṃ purāṇo na hanyate hanyamāne śarīre'
ayaṃ jīvākhya ātmā, jñānasaṅkoca vikāsakaradeha saṃśleṣātirikta- svarūpotpatti-vināśā khyajanma maraṇa vidūraḥ, aviśada satata pariṇāmairapya- nanvīyamānatayā prakṛtivisajātīyaḥ, sarvakālepyapūrvavadeva aparyuṣitānu- bhavāhaḥ, śarīracyutāvapi apracyutasvabhāva eva jāgarti ||

'atha yo vededaṃ jighrāṇīti, sa ātmā'

yaḥ punaḥ ghrātavyamartha ' jighrāṇi' iti ghrāṇajena jñānena viṣayī karoti
saḥ gandha tadāśrayaviṣayakajñānavāneva jīvapadābhilapya ātmā ||

atha yo tānanantānupāste'

'kāmaḥ saṅkalpo vicikitsā śraddhā'śraddhā dhṛtiradhṛtirhīīṃrdhīrbhīḥ ' ityetat sarvaṃ mana eva'

kāmādikaṃ sarvaṃ manaāyattameva na tu darśana-śravaṇādimātram | śabdādau viṣaye abhilāṣaḥ, svakartavyārthaviṣayako vyavasāyākhyo vṛttiviśeṣaḥ, saṃśayaḥ, karmādiṣu śāstrīyeṣu āstikyabuddhiḥ, nāstikya- buddhiḥ, bhogyavastuṣu prītiḥ, dveṣyeṣvaprītiḥ, niṣiddhācaraṇe lajjā, śāstrādyāyattaṃ jñānam, āgāmiduḥkha taddhetu viṣayiṇī ākulatā cetye- tāni sarvāṇi mano'dhīnānyeva ||
'kiṃsvidvanaṃ ka u sa vṛkṣa āsīt yato dyāvāpṛthivī niṣṭatakṣuḥ | manīṣiṇo manasā pṛcchatedu tad yadadhyatiṣṭhad bhuvanāni dhārayan ||'
bho mantāro vācakāḥ ! loke hi gṛhanirmāṇe pravṛttā janāḥ, vanaṃ. tadgataṃ vṛkṣa, tadīyāni dārūṇi ca gṛhanirmāṇāyā'pekṣante | tadvan jagannirmāṇa pravṛtto (bhagavān) divampṛthivīṃ ca nirmātuṃ kiṃ vanaṃ, kaṃ vā vṛkṣamāśritavān ? kiṃ vā tadā avartata ? īdṛśajagannirmātṛ apāra mahima- sampannaṃ ca vastu kīdṛśam ? yat sabai viśvaṃ svayaṃ dhārayat tadadhiṣṭhātṛ bhavati' idaṃ sarvaṃ mananīyamarthajātaṃ gurusakāśe pṛṣṭvā samyagvijānīta || 'kutastu khalu somyevaṃ syāt'
he somā ! ' asato hetubhūtāt sadvastu jāyate' ityayamarthaḥ kena vā pramāṇena siddho bhavati ? sarvathā asambhāvitamidam — ityarthaḥ || 'jānātyevā'yaṃ puruṣaḥ '
ayaṃ cetanavargaḥ sarvo'pi, sarvadeśa- sarvakāla- sarvāvasthāsvapi jñāna- dharmaka eva ; na tadrahitaḥ ||

krama saṃkhyā

'taṃ pṛthivyatravīt '
cayanākhye śraute karmaṇi pravṛttaṃ tadadhikāriṇamuddiśya pṛthivī ukta- vatīva | (yadvā) pṛthivyabhimāninī devatā uktavatī ||
'ta etasyaiva sarve rupamabhavan | tasmādeta etenākhyāyante'
