bho tapasvivarya ! dṛśyādṛśyātmakānāṃ sarveṣāmapyarthānāṃ kāryotpanyaupa yikyaḥ sarvā api śaktirūpā dharmāḥ, atīndriyāḥ santo divyaprajñā- viṣayā eva | ataḥ tāḥ paramātmaikasambandhinyaḥ | lokepi auṣṇyātmikā śaktiryathā vahnimātragatā ; nā'nyasambandhinī, tadvat ||
'śaktau hasti kapā (vā) ṭayo:'
'hasti kapāṭa' śabdayorupapadayoḥ karmavācakayoḥ, han dhātoḥ paraḥ ṭak pratyayo bhavati, śaktau gamyamānāyām | hastinaṃ hantuṃ śaktaḥ hastinaḥ manuṣyaḥ | kapāṭantaḥ coraḥ ||

343 I ' śāstrayonitvāt '

II ' śāstrayonitvāt '

parasya brahmaṇaḥ pramāṇāntarāgocaratvena śāstrakapramāṇatvāt brahma

vedāntavedyameveti, tadvicāraparaṃ śārīrakaṃ śāstramārambhaṇīyameveti ||

'saṅkarṣaṇāt pradyumnasajña mano jāyate'

vivṛtiḥ pra. saṃ. 331 'vibhavācanāt' ityatra ) 'saṅghātaparārthatvāt triguṇādi viparyayādadhiṣṭhānāt | puruṣo'sti bhoktṛbhāvāt kaivalyārthaṃ pravṛtteśca ||' saṅghātabhāvaṃ prāpya dehādibhāvaṃ bhajataḥ prākṛtārthastomasya para prayojana- niṣpādanaikasvābhānyāt tādṛśaprayojanabhāginaḥ kasyacana āvaśya- katvāt, triguṇātmaka prakṛti vilakṣaṇasyaiva kalpanīyatvāt, adhiṣṭhāna- bhūtāyāḥ prakṛteradhiṣṭhātuḥ acetanatva - bhogyatvayordarśanena tadadhiṣṭhātuḥ cetanatva bhoktṛtvayoḥ avarjanīyatvāt, pradhāna (prakṛti) pravṛtteḥ kaivalyārtha - tvena kaivalyasyaiva kalpyatvācca puruṣasiddhiḥ ||

'sametyā'nyonyasaṃyoga parasparasamāśrayāḥ |
mahadādyā viśeṣāntā hyaṇḍamutpādayanti te ||' pūrvoktā: mahadādipañcabhūtāntāḥ, nyūnādhikabhāvena militāḥ santaḥ, anyonyamāśritāśrayabhāvaṃ prāpya, aṇḍotpattau kāraṇatāṃ bhajante || ‘sambhogaprāptiriti cenna vaiśeṣyāt'
paramātmanaḥ hṛdaya|khyade haikadeśavatitve abhyupagate, parasyāpi jīvasyeva sukhādibhoktṛtvāpacyā, nedaṃ yuktamiti śaṅkane; dehasambandhādanyasya karmavaśatvasyaiva bhoktṛtāprayojakatvānna doṣaḥ ||
'sarvasya cā'haṃ hṛdi saṃniviṣṭo mattaḥ smṛtirjñānamapohanaṃ ca'

pra. saṃ.

anubhūtārthasmaraṇaṃ, anubhavātmakaṃ pramāṇādhīnaṃ jñānaṃ, tattirodhānaṃ, (ūha para- paryāyaṃ jñānaṃ vā ) sarva madekādhīnameva ||

'sarveṣu ca nṛpaśreṣṭha jñāneṣveteṣu dṛśyate |

he rājarṣe ! satarkānugṛhītavedāgamānurodhena tattvārthe cintyamāne, jñānadāyakeṣu eteṣu sarveṣvapi śāstreṣu dhyeyatayā ārādhyatayā ca paryavasānabhūmiḥ nārāyaṇa eveti niścīyate ||
sahopalambhaniyamādabhedo nīla- taddhiyoḥ' (vivaraṇaprameya saṃgrahaḥ ) viṣaya- viṣayiṇoḥ ghaṭa tabuddhyādyo:, mithaḥ upalambhe samanaiyatyāta, abheda eveti ||
'sāṃkhyaṃ yogaḥ paṃcarātraṃ (vedāraṇyakameva ca ) vedāḥ pāśupataṃ tathā | kimetānyekaniṣṭhāni pṛthaṅ niṣṭhāni vā mune |'
kāpila - hairaṇyagarbha vaiṣṇava- pāśupatākhyāni imāni tantrāṇi, catvāraśca vedā ṛgādyāḥ, sarvāṇyetāni kim ekatatvapratipādanapravṛttāni ? uta pṛthaktatvapratipādanaparāṇi? kiṃ caikaṃ tattvam ? kathaṃ vā eteṣāṃ anyonyamabādhaḥ sarvamasaṃśayaṃ vivṛṇu ||
'sāṃkhyaṃ yogaḥ pañcarātraṃ vedāḥ pāśupataṃ tathā | ātmapramāṇānyetāni na hantavyāni hetubhiḥ ||'

8 apahatapāmmanastu paramātmanaḥ, sthūla sūkṣmarūpakṛtsna jagaccharīratve'pi, karma- sambandhagandho nā'stīti, natarām apuruṣārthagandhaprasaṅgaḥ | yathā loke rājaśāsanānuvartināṃ tadativartināṃ ca rājānugraha- nigraha kṛtasukhaduḥkhayoge'pi, na śarīritvamātreṇa śāsake rājyapi śāsanānuvṛttyativṛttinimittasukha-duḥkhayoḥ bhoktṛtvaprasaṅgaḥ ||
9 atredaṃ tattvam – cidacidvastuśarīratayā tatprakāraṃ brahmaiva sarvadā sarvaśabdābhi- dheyam | tat kadācit svasmāt svaśarīratayā'pi pṛthagvyapadeśā naī sūkṣma- daśāpannacidacidvastuśarīram | tat kāraṇāvasthaṃ brahma | kadācicca vibhaktanāmarūpavyavahārārhamthūladaśa | pannacidacidvastuśarīram | tacca kāryā - basthamiti, kāraṇāt parasmād brahmaṇaḥ kāryarūpaṃ jagat ananyat || śarīrabhūtacidacidvastunaḥ śarīriṇo brahmaṇaśca kāraṇāvasthāyāṃ kāryāvasthāyāṃ ca, śrutiśata siddhayā svabhāvavyavasthayā guṇa doṣavyavasthā || 10 sarvā hi ! śrutayaḥ sasmṛtītihāsapurāṇāḥ sarveśvaraṃ sadaiva sarvajñaṃ sarvaśaktiṃ satyasaṅkalpaṃ niravadyaṃ deśakālānavacchinnānavādhikātiśayānandaṃ paramakāraṇaṃ brahma pratipādayanti || na puna: īśvarādapi param īśvarāśi sanmātram || 11

36 ataḥ, sa bhagavān vedaikavedyaḥ parabrahmābhidhāno vāsudevaḥ, nikhila heya pratyanīka- kalyāṇaikatānānantajñānānandādyaparimitodā raguṇasāgaraḥ, satyasaṅkalpaḥ, cāturvarṇya-cāturāśramyavyavasthayā avasthitān dharmārthakāmamokṣākhyapuruṣārthābhi- mukhān bhaktānavalokya, apārakāruṇya sauśīlya vātsalyaudāryamahodadhiḥ, svasvarūpa svavibhūti-svārādhana- tatphalayāthātmyāvabodhino vedān ṛgyaju:- sāmātharvabhedabhinnān aparimitaśākhān vidhyarthavādamantrarūpān, svetara- sakalasura-naraduravagāhāṃśca avadhārya, tadarthayāthātmyāvabodhi pāñcarātraśāstra svayameva niramimīteti sarvaṃ niravadyam ||

37 pauruṣeṇāṇāṃ tantrāṇāṃ parasparaviruddha vastuvāditayā, apauruṣeyatvena nirastapramādādi- nikhiladoṣagandhavedavedyavastuviruddhābhidhāyitvācca, yathāvasthitavastuni prāmāṇyaṃ durlabham | vedavedyaśca parabrahmabhūto nārāyaṇaḥ | ataḥ tattattantrābhihita- pradhānapuruṣapaśupatiprabhṛtitattvasya vedāntavedyaparabrahmabhūtanārāyaṇātmakatayaiva vastutvamabhyupagamanīyam | sarvasya brahmātmakatāmanusandadhānasya, tattattantrābhi- hitānāṃ tattvānāṃ nārāyaṇa eva niṣṭheti pratīyate || ataḥ vedāntavedyaḥ parabrahma- bhūto nārāyaṇaḥ svayameva pāñcarātrasya kṛtsnasya vakteti, tatsvarūpa tadupāsa- nābhidhāyi tattantramiti ca tasmin itaratantrasāmānyaṃ na kenacidudbhāvayituṃ śakyam ||
